自显论·工篇·势引组式第二十八 v1.0

# 势引组式资源

## 第一节　本篇所收与读法

### 1.1　所收

本篇收势引组式各篇式文所依据的最终模型、源码与测试结果，共 77 项：源码 40 项，测试结果 37 项。资源全文都在本篇第三节。  
各篇的模型（宿主、事件运行与判法）以源码给出，测试所用的输入与参数都写在源码内，故不另列测试数据。几篇共用的模型只收一次，由各篇分别引用。  
第一《势引一般式》、第二《调宿一般式》无资源。

### 1.2　编号与引用

资源编号为三位数，组式内唯一。各篇附录按下式引用：  
　　势引组式第二十八　资源编号 007　类型：源码  
　　势引组式第二十八　资源编号 009　类型：测试结果

### 1.3　哈希

每项资源的全文在第三节，起于「【资源 xxx 起】」的下一行，止于「【资源 xxx 止】」的上一行（含该行行末换行）；以 UTF-8 编码计其 SHA-256，应与登记值相同。不同即不是本篇所收的资源。

### 1.4　复算

源码之间按「存名」相互导入或前缀执行。复算时把各项全文按其存名存入同一目录，执行 python3 \<存名\>，写出同名测试结果（资源 001、003、005 的结果输出到标准输出），与本篇所收者逐项比对。环境：Python 3；资源 072—077 另需 numpy。

【资源 001 起】源码　tiaoxie\_checks.py　SHA-256 f34bd7ae6043fc45015b326acf7c4af6ca4473fa463171932ba96bc6f5987708

~~~python
"""《调谐一般式》正文构造核算（G₀，精确分数；S5 分记 after_1 与 after_3）"""
from fractions import Fraction as Fr
import json
E0={('x','h1'):Fr(1),('h1','y1'):Fr(1),('x','h2'):Fr(1),('h2','y1'):Fr(2),('x','h3'):Fr(1),('h3','y2'):Fr(1)}
P1=[[('x','h1'),('h1','y1')],[('x','h2'),('h2','y1')]]
P2=[[('x','h3'),('h3','y2')]]
eta=Fr(1,10)
def pv(w,m,p): 
    v=Fr(1)
    for e in p: v*=w[e]*m.get(e,Fr(1))
    return v
def phi(w,m): return sum(pv(w,m,p) for p in P1)
def inp(w,m,s=1):
    f={e:Fr(0) for e in w}
    for p in P1+P2:
        v=s*pv(w,m,p)
        for e in p: f[e]+=v
    return {e:w[e]+eta*f[e] for e in w}
r={}
# W_q
mq={('x','h1'):Fr(0),('x','h2'):Fr(0),('x','h3'):Fr(1)}
wq=inp(E0,mq); r['Wq']={str(k):str(v) for k,v in wq.items() if v!=E0[k]}
r['Wq_phi']=[str(phi(E0,mq)),str(phi(wq,mq))]
w0=wq; mr={('x','h1'):Fr(1),('x','h2'):Fr(0),('x','h3'):Fr(1)}
r['start_phi']=str(phi(w0,mr))
# N-dw
w=dict(w0); w[('h1','y1')]=Fr(5); r['Ndw']=str(phi(w,mr))
# N1s
w=inp(w0,mr); r['N1s']=str(phi(w,mr))
# N-dead
md=dict(mr); md[('x','h3')]=Fr(0)
r['Ndead']=[str(phi(inp(w0,md),md)), 'no gate',str(phi(inp(w0,mr),mr)),'no input',str(phi(w0,md))]
# S1
m1=dict(mr); m1[('x','h1')]=Fr(0); m1[('x','h2')]=Fr(1); r['S1']=str(phi(w0,m1))
# S5'
w1s=inp(w0,m1); r["S5_after_1"]={"phi":str(phi(w1s,m1)),"h2":[str(w1s[("x","h2")]),str(w1s[("h2","y1")])],"allopen":str(phi(w1s,{}))}
w=dict(w0); seq=[]
for _ in range(3): w=inp(w,m1); seq.append(phi(w,m1))
r['S5']=[str(x) for x in seq]; r['S5_float']=[float(x) for x in seq]
r['S5_after_3_h1']=[str(w[('x','h1')]),str(w[('h1','y1')])]; r['S5_after_3_h2']=[str(w[('x','h2')]),str(w[('h2','y1')])]
r['S5_after_3_allopen']=[str(phi(w0,{})),float(phi(w,{}))]
# S4'
alt=lambda s:{('x','h1'):Fr(1),('x','h2'):Fr(0)} if s==0 else {('x','h1'):Fr(0),('x','h2'):Fr(1)}
s=1;m=dict(mr);sq=[]
for k in range(200): m.update(alt(s)); sq.append(phi(w0,m)); s=1-s
r['S4']=[str(x) for x in sq[:6]]; r['S4set']=sorted(set(str(x) for x in sq))
# S4 flow contrast
w=dict(w0);s=1;fl=[]
for k in range(6):
    m=dict(mr); m.update(alt(s)); w=inp(w,m); fl.append(float(phi(w,m))); s=1-s
r['S4flow']=fl
# N-hj
mh=dict(mr); mh[('x','h1')]=Fr(0); mh[('x','h2')]=Fr(0); r['Nhj']=str(phi(w0,mh))
print(json.dumps(r,ensure_ascii=False,indent=1))
~~~

【资源 003 起】源码　qishi\_checks.py　SHA-256 a25a605f26043a26fda8438e27e2276df3559f45419686fc1409e5f98c8e1b65

~~~python
"""《起势一般式》正文构造核算（宿载 Q，精确分数）"""
from fractions import Fraction as Fr
import json
A=[('x','h1'),('h1','y')]; B=[('x','h2'),('h2','y')]
def paths(E): return [p for p in (A,B) if all(e in E for e in p)]
def phi(E,w,sg,m={}):
    t=Fr(0)
    for p in paths(E):
        v=Fr(1)
        for e in p: v*=sg.get(e,1)*w[e]*m.get(e,Fr(1))
        t+=v
    return t
def inp(E,w,sg,s=1,m={},write=True):
    if not write: return dict(w)
    f={e:Fr(0) for e in E}
    for p in paths(E):
        v=Fr(s)
        for e in p: v*=sg.get(e,1)*w[e]*m.get(e,Fr(1))
        for e in p: f[e]+=abs(v)
    return {e:w[e]+Fr(1,10)*f[e] for e in E}
E4=sorted(set(A+B)); r={}
wpre={('x','h1'):Fr(1),('h1','y'):Fr(1),('x','h2'):Fr(1),('h2','y'):Fr(2)}
r['Kpre_phi']=str(phi(E4,wpre,{}))
w1=inp(E4,wpre,{}); r['Kpre_after1']={str(k):str(v) for k,v in w1.items()}; r['Kpre_after1_phi']=str(phi(E4,w1,{}))
w2=inp(E4,w1,{}); r['cont_R1_step1_phi']=str(phi(E4,w2,{}))
# K-self: save w1, clear, restore -> same
saved=dict(w1); restored=dict(saved); w2r=inp(E4,restored,{}); r['Kself_same']=(w2r==w2)
wr={('x','h1'):Fr(3,5),('h1','y'):Fr(7,5),('x','h2'):Fr(6,5),('h2','y'):Fr(4,5)}
r['Krand_phi']=str(phi(E4,wr,{}))
sg={('h2','y'):-1}; wc={e:Fr(1) for e in E4}
r['Kcancel']=[str(phi(E4,wc,sg)), str(phi(E4,{**wc,('h2','y'):Fr(2)},sg)), str(phi(E4,wc,sg,{('x','h2'):Fr(0)}))]
r['Nempty']=[str(phi(set(),{},{})), str(inp(set(),{},{}))]
r['Nfrz']=[str(phi(E4,wpre,{})), str(phi(E4,inp(E4,wpre,{},write=False),{}))]
wu={e:Fr(1) for e in E4}; r['Nmid']=[str(phi(E4,wu,{})), str(phi(E4,{**wu,('h2','y'):Fr(2)},{}))]
print(json.dumps(r,ensure_ascii=False,indent=1))
~~~

【资源 005 起】源码　zaochang\_checks.py　SHA-256 127569ea867dea0976b61e373e3c0780bb241c54c9a4306ee66854d71add4967

~~~python
"""《自显场势引造场一般式》正文构造核算（宿载 Z，精确分数）
相位标注：0⁻ 为 W 入口（report 的 w_before / 未接入），0⁺ 为事件完成后（run 的第 0 项），k 为第 k 拍后；两目标分支自同一入口态出发。
宿载 Z：x→h1→y、x→h2→y、x→h3→z；M_sed：每拍环境输入 s=1 进入 x，按通路通量写回 w += (1/10)·Σ f_p。
P_i = w(x→h_i)·w(h_i→出)。τ_h2：P2 ≥ 2 且 P2 > P1；τ_h1 对称。形成：W 起点不成立、W 内某时起成立。
"""
from fractions import Fraction as Fr
import json
E=[('x','h1'),('h1','y'),('x','h2'),('h2','y'),('x','h3'),('h3','z')]
PA=[[('x','h1'),('h1','y')],[('x','h2'),('h2','y')],[('x','h3'),('h3','z')]]
STEPS=6; eta=Fr(1,10)
def P(w,i): p=PA[i-1]; return w[p[0]]*w[p[1]]
def tau(w,g='G_h2'):
    a,b=(2,1) if g=='G_h2' else (1,2)
    return P(w,a)>=2 and P(w,a)>P(w,b)
def step(w):
    f={e:Fr(0) for e in E}
    for p in PA:
        v=w[p[0]]*w[p[1]]
        for e in p: f[e]+=v
    return {e:w[e]+eta*f[e] for e in E}
def run(w0,target='G_h2'):
    w=dict(w0); tr=[('0+',P(w,1),P(w,2),tau(w,target))]
    for t in range(1,STEPS+1):
        w=step(w); tr.append((t,P(w,1),P(w,2),tau(w,target)))
    return tr
def formed(tr,start_tau=False):
    hits=[t for (t,_,_,k) in tr if k]  # '0+' 表示事件完成后、第 1 拍之前
    return {'formed': (not start_tau) and bool(hits), 'first': hits[0] if hits else None}
def fmt(x): return f"{str(x)} (≈{float(x):.6f})"
ones={e:Fr(1) for e in E}; r={}
# W 起点（预搭/先在）在 t=0 读；起势/调宿事件在 t=0 第一拍之前；"形成"相对 W 起点（事件之前）
def report(name, w_before, w_after_events_G2, w_after_events_G1, extra=None):
    trA=run(w_after_events_G2); trB=run(w_after_events_G1)
    st=tau(w_before) if w_before is not None else False
    fa=formed(trA,st); fb=formed(trB,st)
    d={'start_tau':st,'G_h2':fa,'G_h1':fb,'GoalResp':fa['formed'] and not fb['formed'],
       'last_G_h2':[fmt(trA[-1][1]),fmt(trA[-1][2])]}
    if extra: d.update(extra)
    r[name]=d; return trA
# P-mix: 起势 全1；调宿按目标直写非目标支 x→h_other := 3/10
def mix(G):
    w=dict(ones); w[('x','h1') if G=='G_h2' else ('x','h2')]=Fr(3,10); return w
report('P_mix',None,mix('G_h2'),mix('G_h1'))
# C-coin: 先在 w(x→h1)=1/2；b′ 直写 w(h3→z):=1/5，不随 G
pre=dict(ones); pre[('x','h1')]=Fr(1,2)
wc=dict(pre); wc[('h3','z')]=Fr(1,5)
tr_with=run(wc); tr_without=run(pre)
r['C_coin']={'z_readout':[str(P(pre,3)),str(P(wc,3))],'with':formed(tr_with,tau(pre)),'without':formed(tr_without,tau(pre)),
 'y_side_equal_with_without':[ (a[1],a[2])==(b[1],b[2]) for a,b in zip(tr_with,tr_without)]}
# C-chance: 起势伪随机（固定列），规程不读目标
wr={('x','h1'):Fr(3,5),('h1','y'):Fr(4,5),('x','h2'):Fr(1),('h2','y'):Fr(6,5),('x','h3'):Fr(7,10),('h3','z'):Fr(9,10)}
report('C_chance',None,wr,wr,{'start_P1P2':[str(P(wr,1)),str(P(wr,2))]})
r['uniform_start']=formed(run(ones),False)
# C-sub: 起势按目标只写 h3 标记 w(x→h3)：G_h2→1/3，G_h1→9/10
def sub(G):
    w=dict(wr); w[('x','h3')]=Fr(1,3) if G=='G_h2' else Fr(9,10); return w
report('C_sub',None,sub('G_h2'),sub('G_h1'))
# W-pre: 先在全 1（τ_h2 起点不成立）；调宿同 P-mix
report('W_pre',ones,mix('G_h2'),mix('G_h1'))
# W-preset: 起势按目标预搭 x→h_target := 13/10
def preset(G):
    w=dict(ones); w[('x','h2') if G=='G_h2' else ('x','h1')]=Fr(13,10); return w
report('W_preset',None,preset('G_h2'),preset('G_h1'))
# W-ffn-sed: 先在全 1；调宿 t_w=0 直写 h_target→y := 5/2
def ffn(G):
    w=dict(ones); w[('h2','y') if G=='G_h2' else ('h1','y')]=Fr(5,2); return w
tr=report('W_ffn_sed',ones,ffn('G_h2'),ffn('G_h1'))
r['W_ffn_sed']['tau_at_write']=tau(ffn('G_h2'))
r['W_ffn_sed']['y_readout_seq']=[f"{float(a+b):.6f}" for (_,a,b,_) in tr]
r['W_ffn_sed']['G_h1_branch_tau_h2_ever']=any(k for (*_,k) in run(ffn('G_h1')))
# W-frozen-preset-sed: t=0 未接入；t*=1 接入 ffn(G) 组织；之后运行
trf=run(ffn('G_h2'))
r['W_frozen_preset_sed']={'tau_at_attach':tau(ffn('G_h2')),'P1_P2_last':[fmt(trf[-1][1]),fmt(trf[-1][2])],'phys_changed':trf[-1][1]!=1}
# W-sym2-sed：x→a→u、x→b→v；W 起点全 1；t=0 写入（若有），之后第 1…5 拍各运行一次（环境输入 s=1，按通量写回）
def run2(write):
    s={'xa':Fr(1),'au':Fr(1),'xb':Fr(1),'bv':Fr(1)}; s.update(write); seq=[]; first=None
    for t in range(0,6):
        if t>0:
            fu=s['xa']*s['au']; fv=s['xb']*s['bv']
            s={'xa':s['xa']+eta*fu,'au':s['au']+eta*fu,'xb':s['xb']+eta*fv,'bv':s['bv']+eta*fv}
        pu=s['xa']*s['au']; pv=s['xb']*s['bv']; ok= pu+pv>=4 and pu==pv
        if ok and first is None: first=t
        seq.append((t,pu,pv,ok))
    return seq,first
for name,wt in [('G_pair',{'au':Fr(9,4),'bv':Fr(9,4)}),('G_u',{'au':Fr(9,4)}),('keep',{})]:
    seq,first=run2(wt)
    r['W_sym2_'+name]={'first':first,'seq':[(t,f"{float(a):.6f}",f"{float(b):.6f}",k) for t,a,b,k in seq]}
print(json.dumps(r,ensure_ascii=False,indent=1,default=str))
~~~

【资源 007 起】源码　shiyin\_core.py　SHA-256 97d3aaeefee5c854240035e1eab894ae01d97c287ba778b5820bca96c9b22a7e

~~~python
"""势引数理式 · 最小形式接口的有限可执行核心（纯 Python，精确分数，确定性）
- 依赖判定只在可证完备的类中给出 0：变量申报有限值域（全枚举），或函数申报对该变量的多项式次数上界 d（取 d+1 个不同值）；
  否则只能给出依赖=1（发现差异）或 ⊥。⊥ 沿祖先链与读数端传播；承担求值为三值。
  替代值按精确有理数规范化、筛合法且异于当前值、保序去重；完备性按不同点数计；
  次数元数据须为非负整数或未申报，替代序列须为有限列表，否则 INVALID；局部/全窗承担分写；phase 为调用参数、deg 可省。
- 每个原子事件独立一个子相位 (k, i)，事件后即存快照；相位严格前进，倒退者 INVALID。
- 写入只有在实际改变了值时才成为新的来源；同值写入不生成来源、不改祖先；Rewrite/Gate 的形式像取「有实际改变的直写/门控」，原始指令另记。
- 数据契约：变量、层、值域、门控支撑、施作端口、F_M 分项在模型中申报；事件 id 唯一、part 必填、读集已申报且有值、
  实际输出键 = 声明写集、实际输出按层、值域、支撑、端口逐项核验；任何不合 → INVALID。
结果三类：'INVALID'、None（⊥）、True/False。kind 不授事件资格。
"""
from fractions import Fraction as Fr
import json, sys, itertools

KINDS = {'In', 'Run', 'Sed', 'Rw', 'Gt', 'Acq'}
OK_LAYER = {'In': {'ext', 'r'}, 'Run': {'r', 'ext'}, 'Sed': {'theta'}, 'Rw': {'theta'}, 'Gt': {'m'}, 'Acq': {'theta'}}


class Invalid(Exception):
    pass


def check_deg(degs, where):
    for v, d in degs.items():
        if d is not None and not (isinstance(d, int) and not isinstance(d, bool) and d >= 0):
            raise Invalid(f'{where}: degree of {v} must be a non-negative integer or undeclared')


class Model:
    """layer: 变量→层；dom: 变量→('fin', 值集) 或 ('pred', 谓词, 替代值序列)；
    readout: (Φ, {变量: 次数上界或 None})；support: m 变量→连接变量；ports: kind→可写变量集；FM: 分项名→(kind, 写集)。"""
    def __init__(self, layer, dom, readout, support=None, ports=None, FM=None):
        self.layer, self.dom = dict(layer), dict(dom)
        self.phi, self.phi_deg = readout
        self.support = dict(support or {})
        self.ports = {k: set(v) for k, v in (ports or {}).items()}
        self.FM = dict(FM or {})
        for v, L in self.layer.items():
            if L not in {'theta', 'm', 'r', 'ext'}: raise Invalid(f'bad layer {v}')
            if v not in self.dom: raise Invalid(f'no domain for {v}')
            d = self.dom[v]
            if d[0] == 'fin' and not isinstance(d[1], (set, frozenset)): raise Invalid(f'finite domain of {v} not a set')
            if d[0] == 'pred' and not isinstance(d[2], (list, tuple)): raise Invalid(f'alt sequence of {v} not a finite list')
            if L == 'm' and v not in self.support: raise Invalid(f'gate var {v} without declared support')
        check_deg(self.phi_deg, 'readout')

    def in_dom(self, v, x):
        d = self.dom[v]
        return x in d[1] if d[0] == 'fin' else d[1](x)

    def probes(self, v, x, deg):
        """返回 (替代值列表, 是否完备)。有限值域：全枚举，完备。多项式次数 ≤ deg：取 deg 个异于 x 的值，完备。否则不完备。"""
        d = self.dom[v]
        if d[0] == 'fin':
            return [y for y in d[1] if y != x], True
        seen, alts = set(), []
        for y in d[2]:
            y = Fr(y)
            if y != x and d[1](y) and y not in seen:
                seen.add(y); alts.append(y)
        if deg is not None and len(alts) >= deg:
            return alts[:deg], True          # deg 个彼此不同、均异于当前值的合法点 + 当前值 = deg+1 个不同点
        return alts, False


def depends(f, args, v, model, deg):
    """f 在 args 上对 v 的结构依赖：True（有差异见证）、False（完备检验无差异）、None（不完备未见差异）。"""
    base = f(args)
    alts, complete = model.probes(v, args[v], deg)
    for y in alts:
        a2 = dict(args); a2[v] = y
        if f(a2) != base:
            return True
    return False if complete else None


def merge(a, b):
    """祖先映射合并：事件→确定性（True 确定、None 未决）；确定优先。"""
    out = dict(a)
    for k, s in b.items():
        out[k] = True if (out.get(k) is True or s is True) else None
    return out


class Run:
    def __init__(self, model, state):
        self.M = model
        for v, x in state.items():
            if v not in model.layer: raise Invalid(f'undeclared var {v}')
            if not model.in_dom(v, Fr(x)): raise Invalid(f'init {v}={x} outside domain')
        for g, e in model.support.items():
            if g in state and e not in state: raise Invalid(f'gate {g} on unattached {e}')
        self.s = {k: Fr(v) for k, v in state.items()}
        self.anc = {k: {} for k in state}
        self.trace = [((0, 0), dict(self.s), {k: dict(v) for k, v in self.anc.items()})]
        self.ids, self.events, self.last = set(), [], (0, 0)

    def ev(self, phase, e):
        if tuple(phase) <= self.last: raise Invalid(f'phase {phase} not after {self.last}')
        check_deg(e.get('deg', {}), e.get('id', '?'))
        for k in ('id', 'kind', 'reads', 'writes', 'fn', 'part', 'src'):
            if k not in e: raise Invalid(f'event missing {k}')
        if e['id'] in self.ids: raise Invalid(f'duplicate id {e["id"]}')
        if e['kind'] not in KINDS: raise Invalid('unknown kind')
        # 来源申报：Sed/Run 须引用 F_M 分项；Rw/Gt/Acq/In 须经申报端口
        if e['kind'] in ('Sed', 'Run'):
            if e['src'] not in self.M.FM or self.M.FM[e['src']][0] != e['kind']: raise Invalid(f'{e["id"]}: undeclared F_M component')
            if not set(e['writes']) <= self.M.FM[e['src']][1]: raise Invalid(f'{e["id"]}: writes outside F_M component')
        else:
            if not set(e['writes']) <= self.M.ports.get(e['kind'], set()): raise Invalid(f'{e["id"]}: writes outside declared port')
        for v in e['reads']:
            if v not in self.M.layer or v not in self.s: raise Invalid(f'{e["id"]}: read {v} undeclared or absent')
        args = {v: self.s[v] for v in e['reads']}
        out = {k: Fr(x) for k, x in e['fn'](args).items()}
        if set(out) != set(e['writes']): raise Invalid(f'{e["id"]}: output keys {sorted(out)} != declared writes')
        for v, x in out.items():
            L = self.M.layer.get(v)
            if L not in OK_LAYER[e['kind']]: raise Invalid(f'{e["id"]}: {e["kind"]} writes layer {L}')
            if not self.M.in_dom(v, x): raise Invalid(f'{e["id"]}: {v}={x} outside domain')
            if L == 'm' and self.M.support[v] not in self.s: raise Invalid(f'{e["id"]}: gate outside E')
        degs = e.get('deg', {})
        newanc = {}
        for x, val in out.items():
            if x in self.s and self.s[x] == val:          # 同值写入：不成为来源，祖先不变
                continue
            a = {e['id']: True}
            for v in e['reads']:
                d = depends(lambda ar, x=x: Fr(e['fn'](ar)[x]), args, v, self.M, degs.get(v))
                if d is True:
                    a = merge(a, self.anc[v])
                elif d is None:
                    a = merge(a, {k: None for k in self.anc[v]})
            newanc[x] = a
        changed = {x for x in newanc}
        for x in changed:
            self.s[x] = out[x]; self.anc[x] = newanc[x]
        self.ids.add(e['id']); self.last = tuple(phase)
        self.events.append({'id': e['id'], 'kind': e['kind'], 'part': e['part'], 'changed': sorted(changed)})
        self.trace.append((tuple(phase), dict(self.s), {k: dict(v) for k, v in self.anc.items()}))

    def phi(self, st=None):
        return self.M.phi(st if st is not None else self.s)

    def dep_phi(self, st):
        out = {}
        for v in st:
            if self.M.layer[v] not in ('theta', 'm'): continue
            out[v] = depends(lambda ar: self.M.phi({**st, **ar}), {v: st[v]}, v, self.M, self.M.phi_deg.get(v))
        return out


def safe(fn):
    def g(*a, **k):
        try:
            return fn(*a, **k)
        except Invalid as ex:
            return 'INVALID: ' + str(ex)
    return g


def eff_label(run, part, kind):
    """有实际改变的该类事件存在（Rewrite/Gate 的形式像）；原始指令另记。"""
    return any(e['part'] == part and e['kind'] == kind and e['changed'] for e in run.events)


def bear(run, part, kinds):
    ids = {e['id'] for e in run.events if e['part'] == part and e['kind'] in kinds and e['changed']}
    res = False
    for ph, st, anc in run.trace:
        for v, d in run.dep_phi(st).items():
            if d is False: continue
            hit = [anc[v][i] for i in ids if i in anc.get(v, {})]
            if not hit: continue
            if d is True and any(h is True for h in hit):
                return True, ph, v
            res = None
    return res, None, None


~~~

~~~python
"""势引数理式 · 核算（纯 Python，精确分数，确定性）
核心：shiyin_core.py。扩展层 X：
X1 界外过程分项：src 以 ext_ 开头者须为 Run 分项、写集只含已申报 ext 变量（模型构建时校验）。
X2 重定传播：In、Run 的同值写入，按写前状态与写前来源快照逐输出重算来源，全部算完后统一提交；
   写入目标变量者（任何途径）按同值规则（来源不变）；同值重定登记来源刷新节点（变量, 序号~, 事件），版本路径可经刷新节点回溯。
X3 目标声明与来源登记：GoalDecl/TargetDecl/fid 三方一致（带 GoalDecl 的运行缺登记或不一致 INVALID）；
   来源域为事件标识与持有锚标识的不交并，事件标识不得以 HOLD: 开头（INVALID）。
X4 变量版本来源：每次实际改变产生版本 (变量, 序号, 事件)；版本来源只沿同一输出的依赖并入；路径关系建在版本上；
   路径边分标「写值」「控制」；路径无法复原时 MechC 不给肯定见证。
X5 方面：规程事件申报方面（操作、位置、时刻、强度、发生）；方面标签只对端口规程事件（Rw、Gt、Acq）有效，同一分支内方面名须唯一；
   A_G 逐输出、按方面签名比较，联合方面另核；通用 D2 一条路线；成对探针只取合法值。
X6 决定方式申报 ctrl：事件可申报其发生、位置、时刻由哪些变量决定（受信任申报），其来源并入该事件各输出；ctrl 不升级上游未决。
MechC 为见证：在申报的有限见证协议上核三项职责（同一 F、同一迹应变量），协议须整条落在申报机制路线与显式允许的输入之内，
  读入见证绑定同一实际改变输出，历史见证带版本路径；读写登记只作候选筛选。
比较对象：GoalResp 返回经准入的比较对象（绑定目标族、判据读集与判据规格签名、W、各分支形成结论、轨迹与准入结论）；
  准入证据另存于比较对象之外的登记表（读集、判据签名、形成结论、分支集合、各支协议与指纹）；
  比较分量在准入时规范化为独立的不可变值——W 规范化为相位对元组（非法表示 INVALID），各分支协议（含环境、随机源）深度规范化为内容摘要；
  轨迹指纹覆盖 D2 求值读取的全部证据（事件记录全部字段、状态与来源快照序列、版本来源快照序列、现行版本表与目标来源登记）；
  GoalForm 只接收准入有效的比较对象，求值前逐支重核分支集合、窗覆盖、协议与指纹，否则 INVALID；未绑定读集的比较对象只供 P15。
  比较协议固定模型内容（端口、分项、支撑、值域、读数）与场规则、输入事件的函数代码；规则签名含默认参数、关键字默认、闭包与所读全局量；
  共同规格逐项核对窗内实际外源输入；输入事件一律入共同运行规格；形成判定只在 W 内。
窗化：施作事件取窗内相位，τ 取窗内快照，窗外事件可作上游来源；Ev 只对窗内事件求值，窗外事件取「不适用」、不入聚合，
  按事件类只核适用项并给强 Kleene 聚合；Ev 逐相位保持、Rewrite/Gate 分列；保持区间须 t1 ≤ t0 且两端都有快照（Ev 与 Acq 共用）；
  单事件承担按事件 ID 投影，取事件与快照双重窗；GoalCond 可申报事件过滤（种类、层）；目标来源统一读法。
三类结果：'INVALID'、None（⊥）、True/False。形式值 1 只到必要项，不授事件资格；0 的读法见式文对应表。
"""
import sys, json, itertools, hashlib
sys.path.insert(0, '.')
import shiyin_core as C
from fractions import Fraction as Fr

Q = lambda lo=None: ('pred', (lambda x: True) if lo is None else (lambda x: x >= lo), [Fr(k, 7) for k in range(-9, 10)])
B01 = ('fin', {Fr(0), Fr(1)})


def attempt(f):
    try:
        return f()
    except C.Invalid as ex:
        return 'INVALID: ' + str(ex)


class Mx(C.Model):
    def __init__(self, *a, fid='F', goal=None, target=None, mech_routes=None, mech_exhaustive=False, **k):
        super().__init__(*a, **k)
        self.fid, self.goal, self.target = fid, goal, dict(target or {})
        self.mech_routes = None if mech_routes is None else set(mech_routes)   # None：机制未申报
        self.mech_exhaustive = mech_exhaustive
        for n, (kd, ws) in self.FM.items():                                    # X1
            if n.startswith('ext_') and (kd != 'Run' or not all(self.layer.get(v) == 'ext' for v in ws)):
                raise C.Invalid(f'ext component {n} must be Run writing only declared ext vars')


def vkey(x): return x if isinstance(x, str) else '|'.join(map(str, x))


class RunX(C.Run):
    def __init__(self, model, state, hold=False):
        super().__init__(model, state)
        self.goal_src, self.vanc, self.vcount, self.vtrace, self.curv = {}, {v: {} for v in state}, {}, [], {}
        g = model.goal
        if g:                                                                   # X3 运行入口
            td = model.target.get(g['var'])
            if td is None or td['field'] != g['field'] or g['field'] != model.fid:
                raise C.Invalid('goal decl inconsistent with TargetDecl/model (or TargetDecl missing)')
        if hold:
            if not g or g['var'] not in state or model.layer.get(g['var']) != 'ext':
                raise C.Invalid('hold not bound to declared goal var')
            aid = f"HOLD:{g['role']}:{g['var']}"
            self.goal_src[aid] = 'hold'; self.anc[g['var']] = {aid: True}; self.trace[0][2][g['var']] = {aid: True}
            self.vanc[g['var']] = {('HOLD', g['role'], g['var']): True}
        self.vtrace.append({v: dict(a) for v, a in self.vanc.items()})

    def ev(self, phase, e):
        if isinstance(e.get('id'), str) and e['id'].startswith('HOLD:'):
            raise C.Invalid('event id uses reserved hold-anchor namespace')
        for v in e.get('ctrl', []):
            if v not in self.s: raise C.Invalid(f'{e.get("id")}: ctrl var {v} undeclared or absent')
        reads = [v for v in e['reads'] if v in self.s]
        args = {v: self.s[v] for v in reads}
        before = {v: self.s.get(v) for v in e['writes']}
        anc_b = {v: dict(self.anc[v]) for v in set(reads) | set(e.get('ctrl', []))}       # 写前来源快照
        vanc_b = {v: dict(self.vanc.get(v, {})) for v in set(reads) | set(e.get('ctrl', []))}
        cur_ver = {v: self.cur_version(v) for v in reads}
        ctrl_ver = {v: self.cur_version(v) for v in e.get('ctrl', [])}
        super().ev(phase, e)
        last = self.events[-1]
        out = {k: Fr(x) for k, x in e['fn'](args).items()}
        deps = {(x, v): C.depends(lambda ar, x=x: Fr(e['fn'](ar)[x]), args, v, self.M, e.get('deg', {}).get(v)) for x in out for v in reads}
        last.update({'src': e['src'], 'phase': tuple(phase), 'out': {x: str(y) for x, y in out.items()}, 'reads': reads,
                     'read_versions': cur_ver, 'ctrl_versions': ctrl_ver, 'deps': deps, 'asp': e.get('asp'), 'ctrl': list(e.get('ctrl', [])), 'carrier': self.M.fid,
                     'fnsig': fn_sig(e['fn']), 'deg': dict(e.get('deg', {}))})
        g = self.M.goal
        if g and e['src'] == 'ext_goal' and e['part'] == g['role'] and e['writes'] == [g['var']] and last['changed'] == [g['var']]:
            self.goal_src[e['id']] = 'event'
        new_anc, new_vanc = {}, {}
        for x in out:
            def gather(src_map, x=x):
                a = {}
                for v in reads:
                    d = deps[(x, v)]
                    if d is True: a = C.merge(a, src_map[v])
                    elif d is None: a = C.merge(a, {k: None for k in src_map[v]})
                for v in e.get('ctrl', []): a = C.merge(a, src_map[v])         # X6：申报的决定方式（受信任）
                return a
            if x in last['changed']:
                n = self.vcount.get(x, 0) + 1; self.vcount[x] = n
                new_vanc[x] = C.merge({(x, n, e['id']): True}, gather(vanc_b)); self.curv[x] = (x, n, e['id'])
                if e.get('ctrl'): new_anc[x] = C.merge(self.anc[x], gather(anc_b))
            elif e['kind'] in ('In', 'Run') and before.get(x) is not None and not (g and x == g['var']):   # X2
                new_anc[x] = gather(anc_b); new_vanc[x] = gather(vanc_b)
                self.curv[x] = (x, f'{self.vcount.get(x, 0)}~', e['id'])          # 来源刷新节点（数值版本不变）
        for x, a in new_anc.items(): self.anc[x] = a; self.trace[-1][2][x] = dict(a)       # 统一提交
        for x, a in new_vanc.items(): self.vanc[x] = a
        self.vtrace.append({v: dict(a) for v, a in self.vanc.items()})

    def cur_version(self, v):
        return self.curv.get(v, (v, 0, 'entry'))


def E(eid, kind, src, part, reads, writes, fn, deg=None, **kw):
    return {'id': eid, 'kind': kind, 'src': src, 'part': part, 'reads': list(reads), 'writes': list(writes), 'fn': fn, 'deg': deg or {}, **kw}


def K_and(vals):
    vals = list(vals)
    return False if any(v is False for v in vals) else (True if all(v is True for v in vals) else None)


def K_or(vals):
    vals = list(vals)
    return True if any(v is True for v in vals) else (False if all(v is False for v in vals) else None)


def snap(run, ph):
    return next((s, a) for p, s, a in run.trace if p == tuple(ph))


# ---------------- 形式谓词 ----------------
def attached_theta(run, ph):
    st = snap(run, ph)[0]
    return sorted(v for v in st if run.M.layer[v] == 'theta')


def dep_phi(run, st, v):
    return C.depends(lambda ar: run.M.phi({**st, **ar}), {v: st[v]}, v, run.M, run.M.phi_deg.get(v))


def Org0(run, ph=(0, 0)):
    """接入的 θ 非空（辅助检查）；承担势的部分见 Pot 的 (c)。"""
    return bool(attached_theta(run, ph))


def Eff(run, part):
    return any(e['part'] == part and any(run.M.layer[v] in ('theta', 'm', 'r') for v in e['changed']) for e in run.events)


def DPhi(run): return run.phi(run.trace[-1][1]) - run.phi(run.trace[0][1])
def DPhi_ne0(run): return DPhi(run) != 0
def Bear(run, part, kinds=C.KINDS): return C.bear(run, part, kinds)[0]
def Rewrite(run, part): return C.eff_label(run, part, 'Rw')
def Gate(run, part): return C.eff_label(run, part, 'Gt')


def GoalCond(run, part, kinds=None, layers=('theta', 'm', 'r'), W=None):
    """覆盖：目标经写值链或申报的决定方式（ctrl）决定 part 的某一实际改变；目标决定发生、位置、时刻而未申报 ctrl 者不在覆盖内。
    事件过滤 Π = (kinds, layers) 可申报；W 给定时只取窗内相位的事件。"""
    if not run.goal_src: return None
    res = False
    for (ph, st, anc), e in zip(run.trace[1:], run.events):
        if e['part'] != part: continue
        if kinds is not None and e['kind'] not in kinds: continue
        if W is not None and not in_W(e['phase'], W): continue
        for v in e['changed']:
            if run.M.layer[v] not in layers: continue
            hits = [anc[v][k] for k in anc[v] if k in run.goal_src]
            if any(h is True for h in hits): return True
            if hits: res = None
    return res


def S(run):
    """W 内承担势的组织：逐相位三值（某 θ/m 依赖为 1 → 1；全部完备为 0 → 0；否则 ⊥），跨相位强 Kleene 析取。"""
    per = []
    for ph, st, anc in run.trace:
        per.append(K_or(dep_phi(run, st, v) for v in st if run.M.layer[v] in ('theta', 'm')))
    return K_or(per)


def T_star(run, o, t_star):
    """o 在 t* 及以前的实际取得所构成的组织：o 的 Acq 事件实际改变的变量，且其在 t* 时的现行版本由 o 的 Acq 事件生成。"""
    evs = [e for e in run.events if e['part'] == o and e['kind'] == 'Acq' and e['changed'] and e['phase'] <= tuple(t_star)]
    if not evs: return {}
    ids = {e['id'] for e in evs}; st = snap(run, t_star)[0]
    idx = [p for p, _, _ in run.trace].index(tuple(t_star))
    vs = run.vtrace[idx]
    T = {}
    for v in {x for e in evs for x in e['changed']}:
        cur = [k for k in vs.get(v, {}) if isinstance(k, tuple) and len(k) == 3 and k[0] == v and k[1] == max(
            kk[1] for kk in vs[v] if isinstance(kk, tuple) and len(kk) == 3 and kk[0] == v)]
        if cur and cur[0][2] in ids: T[v] = st[v]
    return T


def Acq(run, o, t_star, t0):
    """o 的实际取得构成 T*（t* 时的现行版本由 o 生成），且对 [t*, t0] 内每一相位，T* 的每个变量保持 t* 时的值；只查 T* 所属变量。"""
    T = T_star(run, o, t_star) if interval_ok(run, t_star, t0) else {}
    return bool(T) and held(run, T, t_star, t0)


def Init(run, o, t_star, t0, start):
    """t* ≤ t0，t0 为申报运行 R 的起点，T* 在 t0 时已接入且取 t* 的值。"""
    if tuple(t_star) > tuple(t0) or tuple(t0) != tuple(start): return False
    T = T_star(run, o, t_star); st = snap(run, t0)[0]
    return bool(T) and all(v in st and st[v] == x for v, x in T.items())


def Pot(run, T, t0):
    """(a) T 在 t0 已接入（键存在）；(b) 非空；(c) 某 v ∈ T 对 Φ 局部依赖为 1 → 1；全部完备为 0 → 0（只报形式值）；否则 ⊥。"""
    st = snap(run, t0)[0]
    if not T or not all(v in st for v in T): return False
    return K_or(dep_phi(run, st, v) for v in T)


# MechC 见证：协议须整条落在申报机制路线（与显式允许的输入）之内；三项在同一 F、同一迹应变量版本上核
def route(e): return e['src'] if e['src'] != 'port' else f"port:{e['kind']}"


def parents(run, k):
    """版本或刷新节点 k = (变量, 序号, 事件) 的直接来源：写值边——该事件中对 k 的输出依赖为 1 的读入变量的写前现行节点；
    控制边——该事件申报 ctrl 变量的写前现行节点。"""
    e = next(x for x in run.events if x['id'] == k[2])
    return ([(e['read_versions'][v], '写值') for v in e['reads'] if e['deps'].get((k[0], v)) is True] +
            [(cv, '控制') for cv in e.get('ctrl_versions', {}).values()])


def version_path(run, src, dst):
    stack, seen = [(dst, [dst])], set()
    while stack:
        k, path = stack.pop()
        if k == src:
            nodes = list(reversed(path)); out = [vkey(nodes[0])]
            for u, w in zip(nodes, nodes[1:]):
                lab = next(l for p_, l in parents(run, w) if p_ == u); out += [f'—{lab}→', vkey(w)]
            return out
        if k in seen or (isinstance(k, tuple) and k[2] == 'entry'): continue
        seen.add(k)
        for p, lab in parents(run, k): stack.append((p, path + [p]))
    return None


def MechC(build, protocols, allowed=('port:In',)):
    m = build().M
    if m.mech_routes is None: return None, 'mechanism undeclared'
    th = {v for v, L in m.layer.items() if L == 'theta'}
    writers = {n for n in m.mech_routes if n in m.FM and (m.FM[n][1] & th)} | {n for n in m.mech_routes if n.startswith('port:') and (m.ports.get(n[5:], set()) & th)}
    if not writers:
        return (False, 'declared exhaustive; no mechanism route writes θ') if m.mech_exhaustive else (None, 'no θ-writing route among declared (not exhaustive)')
    notes = []
    for name, steps in protocols.items():
        bad = [ev['id'] for ph, ev in steps if route(ev) not in m.mech_routes and route(ev) not in allowed]
        if bad: notes.append(f'{name}: undeclared route in {bad} (protocol not admitted)'); continue
        r = build()
        res = attempt(lambda: [r.ev(ph, ev) for ph, ev in steps])
        if isinstance(res, str): return res, name
        ids_in = {e['id'] for e in r.events if e['kind'] == 'In'}
        for e1 in r.events:
            if e1['kind'] == 'In': continue
            for x in e1['changed']:
                if r.M.layer[x] != 'theta': continue
                ver = next(k for k in versions_of(r, e1) if k[0] == x)
                trace_ok = any(isinstance(k, tuple) and len(k) == 3 and k[2] in ids_in and s is True for k, s in vanc_at(r, e1)[x].items())   # (1)
                later = r.events[r.events.index(e1) + 1:]
                reader = next(((e2['id'], y) for e2 in later if e2['read_versions'].get(x) == ver
                               for y in e2['changed'] if e2['deps'].get((y, x)) is True), None)                                        # (2) 同一输出：有依赖且实际改变
                hist = next(((e3, y) for e3 in later for y in e3['changed']
                             if r.M.layer[y] == 'theta' and vanc_at(r, e3)[y].get(ver) is True), None)                                  # (3)
                if trace_ok and reader and hist:
                    e3, y = hist; tgt = next(k for k in versions_of(r, e3) if k[0] == y)
                    if version_path(r, ver, tgt) is None:
                        notes.append(f'{name}: path from {vkey(ver)} to {vkey(tgt)} not reconstructible'); continue
                    return True, {'protocol': name, 'trace_version': vkey(ver), 'reader': list(reader), 'history_version': vkey(tgt),
                                  'path': version_path(r, ver, tgt)}
    return None, 'no admitted witness (pending)' + ('; ' + '; '.join(notes) if notes else '')


def vanc_at(run, e):
    return run.vtrace[run.events.index(e) + 1]


def versions_of(run, e):
    vs = vanc_at(run, e)
    return [k for v in e['changed'] for k in vs[v] if isinstance(k, tuple) and len(k) == 3 and k[2] == e['id'] and k[0] == v]


# 造场：共同运行规格、窗、方面、形成
def fn_sig(fn, _seen=None):
    """规则签名：代码、常量、名字、闭包、默认参数、关键字默认，以及所读模块全局量（函数递归取签名，其余取 repr）。"""
    _seen = set() if _seen is None else _seen
    if id(fn) in _seen: return ('rec',)
    _seen = _seen | {id(fn)}
    c = fn.__code__
    val = lambda x: fn_sig(x, _seen) if callable(x) and hasattr(x, '__code__') else repr(x)
    cl = tuple(val(x.cell_contents) for x in (fn.__closure__ or ()))
    dflt = tuple(val(x) for x in (fn.__defaults__ or ()))
    kw = tuple(sorted((k, val(v)) for k, v in (fn.__kwdefaults__ or {}).items()))
    gl = tuple(sorted((n, val(fn.__globals__[n])) for n in c.co_names if n in fn.__globals__ and not isinstance(fn.__globals__[n], type(sys)) and not isinstance(fn.__globals__[n], type)))
    return (c.co_code, repr(c.co_consts), c.co_names, cl, dflt, kw, gl)


def model_sig(M):
    dom = tuple(sorted((v, d[0], tuple(sorted(map(str, d[1]))) if d[0] == 'fin' else (fn_sig(d[1]), tuple(map(str, d[2])))) for v, d in M.dom.items()))
    return (M.fid, tuple(sorted(M.layer.items())), dom, tuple(sorted((k, tuple(sorted(v))) for k, v in M.ports.items())),
            tuple(sorted(M.support.items())), tuple(sorted((n, kd, tuple(sorted(ws))) for n, (kd, ws) in M.FM.items())),
            fn_sig(M.phi), tuple(sorted(M.phi_deg.items())))


def protocol(run, goal_var, W):
    """共同运行规格：模型（端口、分项、支撑、值域、读数）、场规则事件与输入事件（相位、途径、读写、函数代码、次数）、入口（目标变量除外）、环境、随机源、W。规程事件（申报方面者）不入。"""
    st0 = run.trace[0][1]
    rules = tuple((e['phase'], e['kind'], e['src'], tuple(e['reads']), tuple(sorted(e['out'])), e['fnsig'], tuple(sorted(e['deg'].items())))
                  for e in run.events if not is_procedure(e))
    inputs = tuple((e['phase'], tuple(sorted(e['out'].items()))) for e in run.events if e['kind'] == 'In' and in_W(e['phase'], W))
    return {'model': model_sig(run.M), 'rules_inputs': rules, 'inputs_in_W': inputs, 'entry': tuple(sorted((k, str(v)) for k, v in st0.items() if k != goal_var)),
            'env': getattr(run, 'env', None), 'seed': getattr(run, 'seed', None), 'W': W}


def is_procedure(e):
    """规程事件：申报了方面、且经端口以直写、门控或取得作用者。输入与场规则事件即使带方面标签也属共同背景。"""
    return bool(e.get('asp')) and e['src'] == 'port' and e['kind'] in ('Rw', 'Gt', 'Acq')


def covers(run, W):
    phs = [p for p, _, _ in run.trace]
    return tuple(W[0]) in phs and phs[-1] >= tuple(W[1])


def in_W(ph, W): return tuple(W[0]) <= ph <= tuple(W[1])


def first_form(run, crit, W):
    idx = [i for i, (p, _, _) in enumerate(run.trace) if in_W(p, W)]
    if not idx or crit(run.trace[idx[0]][1]): return None
    return next((i for i in idx if crit(run.trace[i][1])), None)


def form_intervals(run, crit, W):
    out, cur = [], None
    for ph, st, anc in run.trace:
        if not in_W(ph, W): continue
        if crit(st) and cur is None: cur = [ph, ph]
        elif crit(st): cur[1] = ph
        elif cur is not None: out.append(cur); cur = None
    if cur is not None: out.append(cur)
    return [list(map(list, iv)) for iv in out]


_ADMISSIONS, _TOKENS = {}, itertools.count(1)


def canon(x):
    """深度规范化为不可变值：映射按键排序为元组，序列与集合为元组，分数取字符串，其余取 repr。"""
    if isinstance(x, dict): return ('dict', tuple(sorted((canon(k), canon(v)) for k, v in x.items())))
    if isinstance(x, (list, tuple)): return ('seq', tuple(canon(v) for v in x))
    if isinstance(x, (set, frozenset)): return ('set', tuple(sorted(canon(v) for v in x)))
    if isinstance(x, Fr): return ('fr', str(x))
    if isinstance(x, bytes): return ('bytes', x.hex())
    return ('atom', repr(x))


def digest(x): return hashlib.sha256(repr(canon(x)).encode()).hexdigest()


def norm_W(W):
    """时窗规范化为两个相位元组组成的元组；不能规范化者定义域无效。"""
    try:
        a, b = W
        a, b = tuple(int(t) for t in a), tuple(int(t) for t in b)
        if any(int(t) != t for t in list(W[0]) + list(W[1])): raise ValueError
    except Exception:
        raise C.Invalid(f'window {W!r} is not a pair of phases')
    return (a, b)


class Comparison(dict):
    """经准入核对的比较对象：目标分支轨迹 + 绑定的目标族、判据、W 与准入结论。准入有效与 GoalResp 的真值分开表示。"""
    def __init__(self, runs, goals, crit, W, admitted, reason=None, goal_var=None, protocols=None, crit_vars=None, formed=None):
        super().__init__(runs); self.goals, self.crit, self.W, self.admitted, self.reason = tuple(goals), crit, W, admitted, reason
        self.goal_var, self.protocols = goal_var, protocols
        self.crit_vars = tuple(crit_vars) if crit_vars is not None else None
        self.crit_sig = fn_sig(crit) if admitted else None
        self.formed = dict(formed) if formed is not None else None
        self.prints = {g: fingerprint(r) for g, r in runs.items()} if admitted else None
        self.token = next(_TOKENS)
        if admitted:                                                     # 准入证据另存，独立于可编辑的对象属性
            _ADMISSIONS[self.token] = (self, {'goals': tuple(goals), 'W': W, 'crit': crit, 'crit_vars': self.crit_vars, 'crit_sig': self.crit_sig,
                                              'formed': dict(self.formed or {}), 'branches': frozenset(runs), 'goal_var': goal_var,
                                              'protocols': {g: digest(pr) for g, pr in (protocols or {}).items()}, 'prints': dict(self.prints)})


def fingerprint(run):
    """轨迹指纹：模型签名；每条事件记录的全部字段（含方面标识、读入与控制版本、逐输出依赖、规则签名）；
    状态与来源快照序列；版本来源快照序列；现行版本表；目标来源登记。D2 求值读取的证据全部在内。"""
    canon = lambda d: tuple(sorted((repr(k), repr(v)) for k, v in d.items()))
    evs = tuple(tuple(sorted((k, repr(canon(v)) if isinstance(v, dict) else repr(v)) for k, v in e.items())) for e in run.events)
    tr = tuple((ph, canon({k: str(v) for k, v in st.items()}), canon({k: canon(a) for k, a in anc.items()})) for ph, st, anc in run.trace)
    vt = tuple(canon({k: canon(a) for k, a in vs.items()}) for vs in run.vtrace)
    return hashlib.sha256(repr((model_sig(run.M), evs, tr, vt, canon(run.curv), canon(run.goal_src))).encode()).hexdigest()


def GoalResp(build_run, goals, crit, goal_var, W, crit_vars=None):
    try:
        W = norm_W(W)
    except C.Invalid as ex:
        return 'INVALID: ' + str(ex), None
    runs = {g: build_run(g) for g in goals}
    for r in runs.values():
        names = [e['asp'] for e in r.events if is_procedure(e)]
        if len(names) != len(set(names)):
            reason = 'INVALID: aspect names not unique within a branch'; return reason, Comparison(runs, goals, crit, W, False, reason)
    if not all(covers(r, W) for r in runs.values()):
        reason = 'INVALID: window not covered by trajectory'; return reason, Comparison(runs, goals, crit, W, False, reason)
    protos = {g: protocol(r, goal_var, W) for g, r in runs.items()}
    bad = [k for k in protos[goals[0]] if any(digest(protos[g][k]) != digest(protos[goals[0]][k]) for g in goals)]
    if bad:
        reason = f'INVALID: non-common comparison protocol {bad}'; return reason, Comparison(runs, goals, crit, W, False, reason)
    formed = {g: first_form(r, crit, W) for g, r in runs.items()}
    val = formed[goals[0]] is not None and any(formed[g] is None for g in goals[1:])
    return val, Comparison(runs, goals, crit, W, True, goal_var=goal_var, protocols=protos, crit_vars=crit_vars, formed=formed)


def A_G(runs, goals):
    """逐输出的方面差异：(方面, 输出变量) → 差异种类。强度只映射到实际取值不同的输出；操作、时刻、发生映射到该方面的全部输出；
    写集（位置）不同而无从对齐者记入 pending。"""
    ev = lambda r: {e['asp']: e for e in r.events if is_procedure(e)}
    a0 = ev(runs[goals[0]]); diff, pending = {}, set()
    for g in goals[1:]:
        a1 = ev(runs[g])
        for k in sorted(set(a0) | set(a1)):
            e0, e1 = a0.get(k), a1.get(k)
            if e0 is None: pending.add(k); continue                       # 主分支未发生：无主分支版本可绑定
            if e1 is None:
                for v in e0['out']: diff.setdefault((k, v), set()).add('发生')
                continue
            if set(e0['out']) != set(e1['out']): pending.add(k); continue
            for name, a, b in (('操作', e0['kind'], e1['kind']), ('时刻', e0['phase'], e1['phase'])):
                if a != b:
                    for v in e0['out']: diff.setdefault((k, v), set()).add(name)
            for v in e0['out']:
                if e0['out'][v] != e1['out'][v]: diff.setdefault((k, v), set()).add('强度')
    return {f'{k}:{v}': sorted(s) for (k, v), s in sorted(diff.items())}, sorted(pending), diff


def legal_alts(M, v, cur):
    d = M.dom[v]
    vals = sorted(d[1]) if d[0] == 'fin' else [Fr(y) for y in d[2]]
    return [y for y in dict.fromkeys(vals) if y != cur and M.in_dom(v, y)]


def bears_formation(run, crit, crit_vars, target_versions, W):
    """在窗 W 内首次形成的相位：单变量与成对联合探针，替代值一律取合法值。单变量依赖为 1 且版本来源确定地含目标版本 → 1（可分）；
    成对合法替代使判据翻转且两者都含目标版本 → 1（联合）；有未决或覆盖不足 → ⊥；其余 0（只报形式值）。"""
    i = first_form(run, crit, W)
    if i is None: return 'not formed in W', None
    ph, st, _ = run.trace[i]; vs = run.vtrace[i]
    hit = lambda v: any(vs.get(v, {}).get(t) is True for t in target_versions)
    pend = lambda v: any(t in vs.get(v, {}) for t in target_versions)
    res = False
    for v in crit_vars:
        d = C.depends(lambda ar: Fr(1 if crit({**st, **ar}) else 0), {v: st[v]}, v, run.M, None) if v in st else False
        if d is True and hit(v): return True, {'mode': '可分', 'phase': ph, 'var': v}
        if (d is None or d is True) and pend(v): res = None
    for u, v in itertools.combinations(crit_vars, 2):
        if u not in st or v not in st: continue
        for a, b in itertools.product(legal_alts(run.M, u, st[u]), legal_alts(run.M, v, st[v])):
            if crit({**st, u: a, v: b}) != crit(st):
                if hit(u) and hit(v): return True, {'mode': '联合', 'phase': ph, 'vars': [u, v], 'alt': [str(a), str(b)]}
                if pend(u) or pend(v): res = None
    return res, None


def GoalForm(runs, goals, crit, crit_vars, W):
    """通用 D2：只接收准入有效、且目标族、判据、W 与之绑定一致的比较对象；A_G（逐输出）→ 主分支对应输出版本 → bears_formation；
    无从对齐的方面存在而结果为 0 时给 ⊥。"""
    if not isinstance(runs, Comparison) or not runs.admitted:
        return {'D2': 'INVALID: comparison not admitted' + (f' ({runs.reason})' if isinstance(runs, Comparison) else '')}
    reg = _ADMISSIONS.get(getattr(runs, 'token', None))
    if reg is None or reg[0] is not runs:
        return {'D2': 'INVALID: no admission record for this comparison'}
    A = reg[1]
    if A['crit_vars'] is None:
        return {'D2': 'INVALID: comparison admitted for P15 only (criterion read set not bound)'}
    if frozenset(runs) != A['branches'] or set(goals) != set(A['branches']):
        return {'D2': 'INVALID: comparison branches differ from the admitted branch set'}
    try:
        W = norm_W(W)
    except C.Invalid as ex:
        return {'D2': 'INVALID: ' + str(ex)}
    if (tuple(goals) != A['goals'] or crit is not A['crit'] or W != A['W'] or tuple(crit_vars) != A['crit_vars']
            or (runs.goals, runs.crit, runs.W, runs.crit_vars, runs.crit_sig, runs.formed, runs.goal_var, runs.prints)
            != (A['goals'], A['crit'], A['W'], A['crit_vars'], A['crit_sig'], A['formed'], A['goal_var'], A['prints'])
            or {g: digest(pr) for g, pr in (runs.protocols or {}).items()} != A['protocols']):
        return {'D2': 'INVALID: bound items differ from the admission record'}
    if any(not covers(runs[g], W) or digest(protocol(runs[g], A['goal_var'], W)) != A['protocols'][g] or fingerprint(runs[g]) != A['prints'][g] for g in goals):
        return {'D2': 'INVALID: comparison branches differ from the admitted protocols/trajectories'}
    if tuple(goals) != runs.goals or crit is not runs.crit or W != runs.W:
        return {'D2': 'INVALID: goals/criterion/W differ from the admitted comparison'}
    if runs.crit_vars is None or tuple(crit_vars) != runs.crit_vars:
        return {'D2': 'INVALID: criterion read set not bound or differs from the admitted comparison'}
    if fn_sig(crit) != runs.crit_sig or any(first_form(runs[g], crit, W) != runs.formed[g] for g in goals):
        return {'D2': 'INVALID: criterion specification or formation differs from the admitted comparison'}
    if (runs.protocols is None or set(runs) != set(goals)
            or any(not covers(runs[g], W) or protocol(runs[g], runs.goal_var, W) != runs.protocols[g] or fingerprint(runs[g]) != runs.prints[g] for g in goals)):
        return {'D2': 'INVALID: comparison branches differ from the admitted protocols/trajectories'}
    shown, pending, diff = A_G(runs, goals)
    main = runs[goals[0]]
    ev = {e['asp']: e for e in main.events if is_procedure(e)}
    tv = [k for (a, v) in sorted(diff) for k in versions_of(main, ev[a]) if k[0] == v]
    val, wit = bears_formation(main, crit, crit_vars, tv, W)
    if val is False and pending: val = None
    return {'D2': val, 'witness': wit, 'A_G': shown, 'pending': pending, '目标版本': [vkey(k) for k in tv]}


def proc_versions(run, ids):
    return [k for e in run.events if e['id'] in ids for k in versions_of(run, e)]


def interval_ok(run, t1, t0):
    """保持区间：t1 ≤ t0，且两端都是轨迹中的快照相位。"""
    phs = {p for p, _, _ in run.trace}
    return tuple(t1) <= tuple(t0) and tuple(t1) in phs and tuple(t0) in phs


def held(run, vals, t1, t0):
    if not interval_ok(run, t1, t0): return False
    return all(all(st.get(v) == x for v, x in vals.items()) for ph, st, a in run.trace if tuple(t1) <= ph <= tuple(t0))


def bear_ids(run, ids, W=None):
    """以给定事件集为施作求全窗承担；W 给定时 τ 只取窗内快照（窗外事件可作上游来源）。"""
    res = False
    for ph, st, anc in run.trace:
        if W is not None and not in_W(ph, W): continue
        for v, d in run.dep_phi(st).items():
            if d is False: continue
            hit = [anc[v][i] for i in ids if i in anc.get(v, {})]
            if not hit: continue
            if d is True and any(h is True for h in hit): return True
            res = None
    return res


def goal_determined(run, e):
    """统一读法：版本来源确定地含持有锚，或含由已登记目标写入事件生成的版本。"""
    for v in e['changed']:
        for k, s in vanc_at(run, e)[v].items():
            if s is True and isinstance(k, tuple) and (k[0] == 'HOLD' or (len(k) == 3 and k[2] in run.goal_src)): return True
    return False


def BearW(run, part, kinds, W):
    """窗化承担：施作事件取 part、kind ∈ kinds、窗内相位、有实际改变者；τ 取窗内快照。"""
    ids = {e['id'] for e in run.events if e['part'] == part and e['kind'] in kinds and e['changed'] and in_W(e['phase'], W)}
    return bear_ids(run, ids, W) if ids else False


def EvAgg(tab):
    """逐事件聚合：每一事件只取其类适用的覆盖项，强 Kleene 合取。"""
    vals = []
    for row in tab.values():
        vals += [row[k] for k in row.get('适用项', [])]
    return K_and(vals) if vals else '不适用'          # 窗内无事件：聚合不适用，不作成立证据


def Ev(run, ids, t0, W=None):
    """各事件逐一接上其类的形式像（本原型覆盖的部分）；未覆盖项列明待核。"""
    tab = {}
    for e in run.events:
        if e['id'] not in ids: continue
        if W is not None and not in_W(e['phase'], W):
            tab[e['id']] = {'窗外': '不适用', '适用项': []}; continue
        if e['kind'] == 'Acq':
            tab[e['id']] = {'类': '起势事件', '实际改变': bool(e['changed']),
                            '逐相位保持到t0': bool(e['changed']) and held(run, {v: Fr(e['out'][v]) for v in e['changed']}, e['phase'], t0),
                            '适用项': ['实际改变', '逐相位保持到t0'], '待核': ['Pot、MechC、Init 按实例另核']}
        else:
            tab[e['id']] = {'类': '势引事件', '实际改变': bool(e['changed']), 'Rewrite': e['kind'] == 'Rw' and bool(e['changed']),
                            'Gate': e['kind'] == 'Gt' and bool(e['changed']), '单事件承担': bear_ids(run, {e['id']}, W) if e['changed'] else False,
                            '目标决定': goal_determined(run, e), '待核': ['六组其余职责按事件论元另核'],
                            '适用项': ['实际改变'] + (['Rewrite'] if e['kind'] == 'Rw' else []) + (['Gate'] if e['kind'] == 'Gt' else []) + ['单事件承担', '目标决定']}
    return tab


R = {}
# ================= 势引、调宿：H0 =================
H0lay = {'w': 'theta', 'th': 'theta', 'e': 'theta', 's': 'ext', 'g': 'ext', 'rec': 'ext'}
H0dom = {'w': Q(), 'th': Q(), 'e': ('fin', {Fr(1, 10), Fr(11, 10)}), 's': B01, 'g': Q(), 'rec': Q()}


def H0(goal=True, routes=('deepen',), exhaustive=True, fm=None, ports=None):
    return Mx(H0lay, H0dom, (lambda st: st['w'] - st['th'], {'w': 1, 'th': 1, 'e': 0}),
              ports=ports or {'Rw': {'w', 'th', 'e'}, 'In': {'s'}},
              FM=fm or {'deepen': ('Sed', {'w'}), 'ext_goal': ('Run', {'g'}), 'ext_sched': ('Run', set())},
              fid='H0', goal={'role': 'Xg', 'var': 'g', 'field': 'H0'} if goal else None, target={'g': {'field': 'H0', 'rel': 'w* = g'}},
              mech_routes=routes, mech_exhaustive=exhaustive)


def deepen(eid):
    return E(eid, 'Sed', 'deepen', 'F', ['w', 'e', 's'], ['w'], lambda v: {'w': v['w'] + v['e'] * v['s'] * v['w']}, {'w': 1, 'e': 1, 's': 1})


h0 = {'w': 1, 'th': 0, 'e': Fr(1, 10), 's': 0, 'g': 2, 'rec': 0}
r = RunX(H0(), h0, hold=True)
r.ev((0, 1), E('a', 'Rw', 'port', 'a', ['g'], ['w'], lambda v: {'w': v['g'] + 1}, {'g': 1}))
R['势引_目标导向直写w:=g+1'] = {'Org0': Org0(r), 'Eff': Eff(r, 'a'), 'ΔΦ（量）': str(DPhi(r)), 'ΔΦ≠0': DPhi_ne0(r), 'Bear': Bear(r, 'a'),
                           'GoalCond': GoalCond(r, 'a'), 'Rewrite': Rewrite(r, 'a'), 'Bear_R': Bear(r, 'a', {'Rw'}),
                           '携带条件': {'Φ': 'w − th（数学读出）', 'W': '[(0,0),(0,1)]'}}
r = RunX(H0(), h0, hold=True)
r.ev((0, 1), E('a', 'Rw', 'port', 'a', [], ['w'], lambda v: {'w': Fr(19, 10)}))
R['势引_固定日程直写（不读目标、未申报决定方式）'] = {'Bear': Bear(r, 'a'), 'GoalCond（形式值，只报不排除）': GoalCond(r, 'a')}
# 目标决定时刻（值恒定）：未申报 ctrl → 写值链不覆盖；申报 ctrl → 覆盖
for tag, ctrl in (('未申报决定方式', []), ('申报 ctrl=[g]', ['g'])):
    r = RunX(H0(), h0, hold=True)
    r.ev((0, 2), E('a', 'Rw', 'port', 'a', [], ['w'], lambda v: {'w': 3}, ctrl=ctrl))
    R[f'势引_目标决定时刻·{tag}'] = {'GoalCond': GoalCond(r, 'a')}
r = RunX(H0(goal=False), h0)
for k in range(1, 4):
    r.ev((k, 1), E(f's{k}', 'In', 'port', 'env', [], ['s'], lambda v: {'s': 1})); r.ev((k, 2), deepen(f'd{k}'))
R['势引_环境来流（无目标）'] = {'ΔΦ（量）': str(DPhi(r)), 'Bear(env)': Bear(r, 'env'), 'GoalCond(env)': GoalCond(r, 'env')}
r = RunX(H0(), h0, hold=True)
r.ev((0, 1), E('p', 'Rw', 'port', 'a', ['w'], ['w'], lambda v: {'w': v['w'] + 1}, {'w': 1}))
r.ev((0, 2), E('q', 'Rw', 'port', 'b', ['w'], ['w'], lambda v: {'w': v['w'] - 1}, {'w': 1}))
R['势引_相抵'] = {'Bear(a)': Bear(r, 'a'), 'ΔΦ≠0': DPhi_ne0(r)}

# ================= 同值重定与锚名 =================
def c501_collision():
    r = RunX(H0(), h0, hold=True)
    r.ev((0, 1), E('HOLD:Xg:g', 'Rw', 'port', 'a', [], ['w'], lambda v: {'w': 2})); return GoalCond(r, 'a')
R['锚名冲突（常数直写名为 HOLD:Xg:g）'] = attempt(c501_collision)
r = RunX(H0(fm={'deepen': ('Sed', {'w'}), 'ext_goal': ('Run', {'g'}), 'ext_pair': ('Run', {'s', 'rec'})}), h0)
r.ev((0, 1), E('pair', 'Run', 'ext_pair', 'x', ['s'], ['s', 'rec'], lambda v: {'s': 1, 'rec': v['s']}, {'s': 1}))
R['同值输出按写前来源重定'] = {'rec 同值（写 0）的来源': sorted(map(str, r.anc['rec'])), 's 的来源': sorted(map(str, r.anc['s'])),
                                 '不继承 pair': 'pair' not in r.anc['rec']}
r = RunX(H0(fm={'deepen': ('Sed', {'w'}), 'ext_goal': ('Run', {'g'}), 'ext_pair': ('Run', {'s', 'g'})}), h0, hold=True)
r.ev((0, 1), E('pairg', 'Run', 'ext_pair', 'x', ['s'], ['s', 'g'], lambda v: {'s': 1, 'g': 2 + v['s']}, {'s': 1}))
R['同值写目标变量（探针形）'] = {'g 的来源': sorted(map(str, r.anc['g'])), '不继承 pairg': 'pairg' not in r.anc['g']}
r = RunX(H0(fm={'deepen': ('Sed', {'w'}), 'ext_goal': ('Run', {'g'}), 'ext_split': ('Run', {'s', 'rec'}), 'ext_tail': ('Run', {'s'})}), h0)
r.ev((0, 1), E('e1', 'Rw', 'port', 'a', [], ['w'], lambda v: {'w': 2}))
r.ev((0, 2), E('e2', 'Run', 'ext_split', 'x', ['w'], ['s', 'rec'], lambda v: {'s': 1, 'rec': v['w'] + 1}, {'w': 1}))
r.ev((0, 3), E('e3', 'Run', 'ext_tail', 'x', ['s'], ['s'], lambda v: {'s': 1 - v['s']}, {'s': 1}))
ev_pairs = {(a, e['id']) for (ph, st, anc), e in zip(r.trace[1:], r.events) for v in e['changed'] for a in anc[v] if a != e['id']}
ver_rel = set()
for i, e in enumerate(r.events):
    for k in versions_of(r, e):
        for s, st in r.vtrace[i + 1][k[0]].items():
            if s != k and isinstance(s, tuple) and len(s) == 3: ver_rel.add((s, k))
R['多输出'] = {'事件对': sorted(ev_pairs), '(e1,e3)': ('e1', 'e3') in ev_pairs,
                   '事件投影传递': all((a, c) in ev_pairs for a, b in ev_pairs for b2, c in ev_pairs if b == b2),
                   '版本关系': sorted([vkey(a), vkey(b)] for a, b in ver_rel),
                   '版本关系传递': all((a, c) in ver_rel for a, b in ver_rel for b2, c in ver_rel if b == b2)}

# ================= 局部切片与 S 的三值 =================
lay = {'w1': 'theta', 'w2': 'theta', 'w3': 'theta'}
Ms = Mx(lay, {v: Q() for v in lay}, (lambda st: st['w1'] * st['w2'] + st['w3'], {'w1': 1, 'w2': 1, 'w3': 1}), ports={'Rw': set(lay)}, fid='S3')
r = RunX(Ms, {'w1': 1, 'w2': 0, 'w3': 0})
r.ev((0, 1), E('a1', 'Rw', 'port', 'a', [], ['w1'], lambda v: {'w1': 2}))
r.ev((0, 2), E('b1', 'Rw', 'port', 'b', [], ['w3'], lambda v: {'w3': 1}))
R['局部切片'] = {'Bear_R(a)（形式值）': Bear(r, 'a', {'Rw'}), 'ΔΦ≠0': DPhi_ne0(r), '另一背景 w2=1 上 Φ(w1=1,2)': ['1', '2'],
                     '读法': '形式 0 只报形式值，不排除结构依赖'}
def s_no_probe():
    m = H0(); m.phi_deg = {}; m.dom['w'] = ('pred', lambda x: True, []); m.dom['th'] = ('pred', lambda x: True, [])
    return S(RunX(m, h0))
R['S 缺试点'] = s_no_probe()
R['S 有完备探针'] = S(RunX(H0(), h0))

# ================= MechC 见证 =================
IN = lambda eid, v: E(eid, 'In', 'port', 'env', [], ['s'], lambda _: {'s': v})
proto_deepen = {'deepen×2': [((1, 1), IN('i1', 1)), ((1, 2), deepen('d1')), ((2, 1), IN('i2', 1)), ((2, 2), deepen('d2'))]}
mc = {}
mc['加深写回（读当前态）'] = MechC(lambda: RunX(H0(), h0), proto_deepen)
mc['机制未申报'] = MechC(lambda: RunX(H0(routes=None), h0), proto_deepen)
mc['空机制（申报穷尽）'] = MechC(lambda: RunX(H0(routes=()), h0), {})
mc['只读机制（申报穷尽）'] = MechC(lambda: RunX(H0(routes=('readonly',), fm={'readonly': ('Run', set()), 'ext_goal': ('Run', {'g'})}), h0),
                         {'ro': [((1, 1), IN('i1', 1)), ((1, 2), E('ro', 'Run', 'readonly', 'F', ['w'], [], lambda v: {}))]})
idfm = {'ident': ('Sed', {'w'}), 'ext_goal': ('Run', {'g'})}
mc['恒等写回 w←w'] = MechC(lambda: RunX(H0(routes=('ident',), fm=idfm), h0),
                       {'id': [((1, 1), IN('i1', 1)), ((1, 2), E('id1', 'Sed', 'ident', 'F', ['w', 's'], ['w'], lambda v: {'w': v['w']}, {'w': 1, 's': 1})),
                               ((2, 1), IN('i2', 1)), ((2, 2), E('id2', 'Sed', 'ident', 'F', ['w', 's'], ['w'], lambda v: {'w': v['w']}, {'w': 1, 's': 1}))]})
ndfm = {'tr': ('Sed', {'w'}), 'ext_goal': ('Run', {'g'})}
mc['读而不依赖（w := 1 + s，读 w）'] = MechC(lambda: RunX(H0(routes=('tr',), fm=ndfm), h0),
                                    {'nd': [((1, 1), IN('i1', 1)), ((1, 2), E('t1', 'Sed', 'tr', 'F', ['w', 's'], ['w'], lambda v: {'w': 1 + v['s']}, {'w': 1, 's': 1})),
                                            ((2, 1), IN('i2', 0)), ((2, 2), E('t2', 'Sed', 'tr', 'F', ['w', 's'], ['w'], lambda v: {'w': 1 + v['s']}, {'w': 1, 's': 1}))]})
xfm = {'tr': ('Sed', {'w'}), 'use': ('Sed', {'th'}), 'ext_goal': ('Run', {'g'})}
mc['跨分项历史途径（tr 留痕，use 读 w 写 th）'] = MechC(lambda: RunX(H0(routes=('tr', 'use'), fm=xfm), h0),
    {'x': [((1, 1), IN('i1', 1)), ((1, 2), E('t1', 'Sed', 'tr', 'F', ['s'], ['w'], lambda v: {'w': 1 + v['s']}, {'s': 1})),
           ((1, 3), E('u1', 'Sed', 'use', 'F', ['w'], ['th'], lambda v: {'th': v['w'] / 2}, {'w': 1}))]})
efm = {'tr': ('Sed', {'w'}), 'ext_record': ('Run', {'rec'}), 'ext_goal': ('Run', {'g'})}
mc['界外留痕（tr → ext_record → Rw 回写）'] = MechC(lambda: RunX(H0(routes=('tr', 'ext_record', 'port:Rw'), fm=efm), h0),
    {'ext': [((1, 1), IN('i1', 1)), ((1, 2), E('t1', 'Sed', 'tr', 'F', ['s'], ['w'], lambda v: {'w': 1 + v['s']}, {'s': 1})),
             ((1, 3), E('rec1', 'Run', 'ext_record', 'X', ['w'], ['rec'], lambda v: {'rec': v['w']}, {'w': 1})),
             ((2, 1), IN('i2', 0)), ((2, 2), E('t2', 'Sed', 'tr', 'F', ['s'], ['w'], lambda v: {'w': 1 + v['s']}, {'s': 1})),
             ((2, 3), E('rst', 'Rw', 'port', 'X', ['rec'], ['w'], lambda v: {'w': v['rec']}, {'rec': 1}))]})
mc['未申报路线 use 进入协议（申报只含 tr）'] = MechC(lambda: RunX(H0(routes=('tr',), fm=xfm), h0),
    {'outside': [((1, 1), IN('i1', 1)), ((1, 2), E('t1', 'Sed', 'tr', 'F', ['s'], ['w'], lambda v: {'w': 1 + v['s']}, {'s': 1})),
                 ((1, 3), E('u1', 'Sed', 'use', 'F', ['w'], ['th'], lambda v: {'th': v['w'] / 2}, {'w': 1}))]})
spfm = {'tr': ('Sed', {'w'}), 'ext_sp': ('Run', {'rec', 's'}), 'ext_goal': ('Run', {'g'})}
mc['跨输出拼接（同值输出依赖 w，另一输出改变而不依赖 w）'] = MechC(lambda: RunX(H0(routes=('tr', 'ext_sp', 'port:Rw'), fm=spfm), h0),
    {'splice': [((1, 1), IN('i1', 1)), ((1, 2), E('t1', 'Sed', 'tr', 'F', ['s'], ['w'], lambda v: {'w': 1 + v['s']}, {'s': 1})),
                ((1, 3), E('sp', 'Run', 'ext_sp', 'X', ['w', 'rec', 's'], ['rec', 's'], lambda v: {'rec': v['rec'] + v['w'] - 2, 's': 1 - v['s']}, {'w': 1, 'rec': 1, 's': 1})),
                ((1, 4), E('rst', 'Rw', 'port', 'X', ['rec'], ['w'], lambda v: {'w': v['rec'] + 1}, {'rec': 1}))]})
R['MechC 见证'] = {k: [v[0], v[1] if isinstance(v[1], str) else v[1]] for k, v in mc.items()}


# ================= 起势 Q =================
def Qm(sig=1, extra=False):
    names = ('a1', 'b1', 'a2', 'b2') + (('c',) if extra else ())
    lay = {k: 'theta' for k in names}; lay['s'] = 'ext'; lay['log'] = 'ext'
    dom = {k: Q(0) for k in names}; dom['s'] = B01; dom['log'] = Q()
    phi = lambda st: sum(st[a] * st[b] * sg for a, b, sg in (('a1', 'b1', 1), ('a2', 'b2', sig)) if a in st and b in st)   # 部分态：只计已接入的通路
    return Mx(lay, dom, (phi, {k: 1 for k in ('a1', 'b1', 'a2', 'b2')} | ({'c': 0} if extra else {})),
              ports={'Acq': {'a1', 'b1', 'a2', 'b2'}, 'Rw': set(names), 'In': {'s'}},
              FM={'flux': ('Sed', {'a1', 'b1', 'a2', 'b2'}), 'ext_log': ('Run', {'log'})}, fid='Q')


def acq(eid, vals, part='o'):
    return E(eid, 'Acq', 'port', part, [], list(vals), lambda v, vals=vals: dict(vals))


ALL1 = {'a1': 1, 'b1': 1, 'a2': 1, 'b2': 1}
r = RunX(Qm(), {'s': 0, 'log': 0})
R['起势_未接入（键缺）'] = {'接入的θ': attached_theta(r, (0, 0)), 'Pot(T=∅)': Pot(r, {}, (0, 0)), 'Org0': Org0(r)}
r = RunX(Qm(), {'s': 0, 'log': 0, 'a1': 0, 'b1': 0, 'a2': 0, 'b2': 0})
R['起势_已接入零权重'] = {'接入的θ': attached_theta(r, (0, 0)), 'Pot（形式值；局部背景）': Pot(r, set(ALL1), (0, 0)), 'Org0': Org0(r)}
r = RunX(Qm(-1), {'s': 0, 'log': 0})
r.ev((0, 1), acq('o1', ALL1))
T = T_star(r, 'o', (0, 1))
R['起势_相抵（入口未接入）'] = {'Φ': str(r.phi()), 'T*': sorted(T), 'Pot': Pot(r, T, (0, 1)), 'Init': Init(r, 'o', (0, 1), (0, 1), (0, 1)),
                         'Acq': Acq(r, 'o', (0, 1), (0, 1)), 'S': S(r)}
r = RunX(Qm(), {'s': 0, 'log': 0, **ALL1})
r.ev((2, 1), E('o2', 'Rw', 'port', 'o', [], ['b2'], lambda v: {'b2': 2}))
R['起势_中途写入'] = {'Init': Init(r, 'o', (2, 1), (0, 0), (0, 0)), 'Acq(o)': Acq(r, 'o', (2, 1), (2, 1))}
r = RunX(Qm(), {'s': 0, 'log': 0})
r.ev((0, 1), acq('P1', {**ALL1, 'b2': 2}, part='P'))
r.ev((0, 2), E('lg', 'Run', 'ext_log', 'o_prime', [], ['log'], lambda v: {'log': 1}))
R['起势_无关过程o′'] = {'Acq(P)': Acq(r, 'P', (0, 1), (0, 2)), 'Acq(o′)': Acq(r, 'o_prime', (0, 2), (0, 2))}
r = RunX(Qm(), {'s': 0, 'log': 0})
r.ev((0, 1), acq('o1', {**ALL1, 'b2': 2}))
r.ev((0, 2), E('cut', 'Rw', 'port', 'b', [], ['b2'], lambda v: {'b2': 3}))
r.ev((0, 3), E('back', 'Rw', 'port', 'b', [], ['b2'], lambda v: {'b2': 2}))
R['起势_端点恢复'] = {'Acq(o,(0,1),(0,3))': Acq(r, 'o', (0, 1), (0, 3))}
r = RunX(Qm(), {'s': 0, 'log': 0})
r.ev((0, 1), acq('o1', ALL1))
R['起势_未来事件'] = {'Acq(o,(0,0),(0,0))': Acq(r, 'o', (0, 0), (0, 0)), 'Init(o,(0,0),(0,0))': Init(r, 'o', (0, 0), (0, 0), (0, 0))}
r = RunX(Qm(extra=True), {'s': 0, 'log': 0, 'c': 1})
r.ev((0, 1), acq('o1', ALL1))
r.ev((0, 2), E('cb', 'Rw', 'port', 'b', [], ['c'], lambda v: {'c': 2}))
R['起势_无关θ变化（c 不属 T*）'] = {'T*': sorted(T_star(r, 'o', (0, 1))), 'Acq(o,(0,1),(0,2))': Acq(r, 'o', (0, 1), (0, 2))}


# ================= 调谐 G0 =================
def G0():
    ws = ('w1a', 'w1b', 'w2a', 'w2b', 'w3a', 'w3b')
    lay = {k: 'theta' for k in ws}; lay.update({'m1': 'm', 'm2': 'm', 'm3': 'm', 's': 'ext', 'g': 'ext'})
    dom = {k: Q(0) for k in ws}; dom.update({k: B01 for k in ('m1', 'm2', 'm3', 's')}); dom['g'] = Q()
    return Mx(lay, dom, (lambda st: st['w1a'] * st['w1b'] * st['m1'] + st['w2a'] * st['w2b'] * st['m2'], {k: 1 for k in ws}),
              support={'m1': 'w1a', 'm2': 'w2a', 'm3': 'w3a'}, ports={'Gt': {'m1', 'm2', 'm3'}, 'In': {'s'}},
              FM={'ext_goal': ('Run', {'g'})}, fid='G0', goal={'role': 'Xg', 'var': 'g', 'field': 'G0'}, target={'g': {'field': 'G0', 'rel': 'y1 加强'}})


g0s = {'w1a': 1, 'w1b': 1, 'w2a': 1, 'w2b': 2, 'w3a': Fr(11, 10), 'w3b': Fr(11, 10), 'm1': 1, 'm2': 0, 'm3': 1, 's': 0, 'g': 1}
r = RunX(G0(), g0s, hold=True)
r.ev((0, 1), E('aG1', 'Gt', 'port', 'a', ['g'], ['m1'], lambda v: {'m1': 1 - v['g']}, {'g': 1}))
r.ev((0, 2), E('aG2', 'Gt', 'port', 'a', ['g'], ['m2'], lambda v: {'m2': v['g']}, {'g': 1}))
R['调谐_S1（按目标门控）'] = {'Gate': Gate(r, 'a'), 'Bear_G': Bear(r, 'a', {'Gt'}), 'GoalCond': GoalCond(r, 'a'), 'ΔΦ≠0': DPhi_ne0(r)}

# ================= 造场 Z =================
ZW = ('xh1', 'h1y', 'xh2', 'h2y', 'xh3', 'h3z')
PA = (('xh1', 'h1y'), ('xh2', 'h2y'), ('xh3', 'h3z'))
ZALT = [Fr(j, 10) for j in range(0, 31)]


def Zm():
    lay = {k: 'theta' for k in ZW}; lay.update({'s': 'ext', 'g': 'ext'})
    dom = {k: ('pred', nonneg, ZALT) for k in ZW}; dom['s'] = B01; dom['g'] = ('fin', {Fr(1), Fr(2)})
    return Mx(lay, dom, (zphi, {k: (1 if k in ('xh1', 'h1y', 'xh2', 'h2y') else 0) for k in ZW}),
              ports={'Acq': set(ZW), 'Rw': set(ZW), 'In': {'s'}}, FM={'flux': ('Sed', set(ZW)), 'ext_goal': ('Run', {'g'})},
              fid='Z', goal={'role': 'Xg', 'var': 'g', 'field': 'Z'}, target={'g': {'field': 'Z', 'rel': 'τ_h(g)'}})


def nonneg(x): return x >= 0
def zphi(st): return sum(st[a] * st[b] for a, b in PA[:2] if a in st and b in st)


def flux_fn(v, rate=Fr(1, 10)):
    out = {k: v[k] for k in ZW}
    for a, b in PA:
        fl = v['s'] * v[a] * v[b]; out[a] = out[a] + fl * rate; out[b] = out[b] + fl * rate
    return out


def flux(eid, fn=flux_fn):
    return E(eid, 'Sed', 'flux', 'F', list(ZW) + ['s'], list(ZW), fn, {k: 1 for k in list(ZW) + ['s']})


def env_in(v): return {'s': 1}


crit = lambda st: all(k in st for k in ZW) and st['xh2'] * st['h2y'] >= 2 and st['xh2'] * st['h2y'] > st['xh1'] * st['h1y']
CV = ['xh1', 'h1y', 'xh2', 'h2y']
W_Z = ((0, 0), (6, 2))


def run_Z(entry, events, K=6, fx=flux_fn):
    r = RunX(Zm(), entry, hold=True); r.env = 'env: s=1 each tick'; r.seed = None
    for i, ev in enumerate(events, start=1): r.ev((0, i), ev)
    for k in range(1, K + 1):
        r.ev((k, 1), E(f'in{k}', 'In', 'port', 'env', [], ['s'], env_in)); r.ev((k, 2), flux(f'fl{k}', fx))
    return r


ones = {k: 1 for k in ZW}
r = run_Z({**ones, 'xh1': Fr(1, 2), 's': 0, 'g': 2}, [E('bp', 'Rw', 'port', 'P', [], ['h3z'], lambda v: {'h3z': Fr(1, 5)}, asp='b_h3z')])
R['造场_C-coin'] = {'D1': bears_formation(r, crit, CV, proc_versions(r, {'bp'}), W_Z)[0], '形成区间': form_intervals(r, crit, W_Z)}


def pmix_events():
    evs = [E(f'o_{k}', 'Acq', 'port', 'P', [], [k], lambda v, k=k: {k: 1}, asp=f'o_{k}') for k in ZW]
    evs += [E('b_xh1', 'Rw', 'port', 'P', ['g'], ['xh1'], lambda v: {'xh1': Fr(3, 10) if v['g'] == 2 else 1}, {'g': 1}, asp='b_xh1'),
            E('b_xh2', 'Rw', 'port', 'P', ['g'], ['xh2'], lambda v: {'xh2': Fr(3, 10) if v['g'] == 1 else 1}, {'g': 1}, asp='b_xh2')]
    return evs


def pmix(g): return run_Z({'s': 0, 'g': g}, pmix_events())


ok, runs = GoalResp(pmix, [2, 1], crit, 'g', W_Z, CV)
P_ids = {e['id'] for e in runs[2].events if e.get('asp')}
R['造场_P-mix'] = {'GoalResp': ok, **GoalForm(runs, [2, 1], crit, CV, W_Z),
                  'D1': bears_formation(runs[2], crit, CV, proc_versions(runs[2], P_ids), W_Z)[0], '形成区间': form_intervals(runs[2], crit, W_Z)}
pm_runs = runs


def diff_entry(g):
    return run_Z({'s': 0, 'g': g, **ones, **({'xh2': 2} if g == 2 else {})}, [E('same', 'Rw', 'port', 'P', ['xh2'], ['xh2'], lambda v: {'xh2': v['xh2'] + 1}, {'xh2': 1}, asp='same')])
R['造场_非共同入口'] = GoalResp(diff_entry, [2, 1], crit, 'g', W_Z)[0]
def diff_ports(g):
    r = pmix(g)
    if g == 1: r.M.ports['Rw'] = {'xh1'}
    return r
R['造场_替代分支端口不同'] = GoalResp(diff_ports, [2, 1], crit, 'g', W_Z)[0]
def diff_rule(g):
    return run_Z({'s': 0, 'g': g}, pmix_events(), fx=flux_fn if g == 2 else (lambda v: flux_fn(v, Fr(1, 5))))
R['造场_同名分项异规则'] = GoalResp(diff_rule, [2, 1], crit, 'g', W_Z)[0]


def timed(g):
    r = RunX(Zm(), {'s': 0, 'g': g}, hold=True); r.env = 'none'; r.seed = None
    for i, k in enumerate(ZW, 1): r.ev((0, i), E(f'o_{k}', 'Acq', 'port', 'P', [], [k], lambda v, k=k: {k: 1}, asp=f'o_{k}'))
    r.ev((0, 10 + g), E('t', 'Rw', 'port', 'P', [], ['h2y'], lambda v: {'h2y': 3}, asp='t_h2y'))
    r.ev((0, 13), E('in_end', 'In', 'port', 'env', [], ['s'], env_in))
    return r
W_T = ((0, 0), (0, 13))
ok, truns = GoalResp(timed, [2, 1], crit, 'g', W_T, CV)
R['造场_只改时刻'] = {'GoalResp': ok, **GoalForm(truns, [2, 1], crit, CV, W_T)}


def Jm():
    lay = {'ja': 'theta', 'jb': 'theta', 's': 'ext', 'g': 'ext'}
    return Mx(lay, {'ja': B01, 'jb': B01, 's': B01, 'g': ('fin', {Fr(1), Fr(2)})}, (jphi, {'ja': 1, 'jb': 1}),
              ports={'Acq': {'ja', 'jb'}, 'In': {'s'}}, FM={'ext_goal': ('Run', {'g'})}, fid='J',
              goal={'role': 'Xg', 'var': 'g', 'field': 'J'}, target={'g': {'field': 'J', 'rel': 'τ_or'}})


def jphi(st): return st.get('ja', 0) + st.get('jb', 0)


jcrit = lambda st: 'ja' in st and 'jb' in st and (st['ja'] >= 1 or st['jb'] >= 1)
def jrun(g):
    r = RunX(Jm(), {'s': 0, 'g': g}, hold=True); r.env = 'none'; r.seed = None
    r.ev((0, 1), E('pa', 'Acq', 'port', 'P', ['g'], ['ja'], lambda v: {'ja': 1 if v['g'] == 2 else 0}, {'g': 1}, asp='a'))
    r.ev((0, 2), E('pb', 'Acq', 'port', 'P', ['g'], ['jb'], lambda v: {'jb': 1 if v['g'] == 2 else 0}, {'g': 1}, asp='b'))
    return r
W_J = ((0, 0), (0, 2))
ok, jruns = GoalResp(jrun, [2, 1], jcrit, 'g', W_J, ['ja', 'jb'])
R['造场_联合方面'] = {'GoalResp': ok, **GoalForm(jruns, [2, 1], jcrit, ['ja', 'jb'], W_J)}
R['造场_入口单点窗'] = GoalResp(jrun, [2, 1], jcrit, 'g', ((0, 0), (0, 0)))[0]
def jports(g):
    r = jrun(g)
    if g == 1: r.M.ports['Rw'] = {'ja'}
    return r
R['造场_联合例替代分支端口不同'] = GoalResp(jports, [2, 1], jcrit, 'g', W_J, ['ja', 'jb'])[0]
nn_crit = lambda st: 'ja' in st and 'jb' in st and st['ja'] >= 0 and st['jb'] >= 0
R['造场_成对探针非法值'] = {'D1（替代值限合法域 {0,1}）': bears_formation(jruns[2], nn_crit, ['ja', 'jb'], proc_versions(jruns[2], {'pa', 'pb'}), W_J)[0]}

WR = {'xh1': Fr(3, 5), 'h1y': Fr(4, 5), 'xh2': Fr(1), 'h2y': Fr(6, 5), 'xh3': Fr(7, 10), 'h3z': Fr(9, 10)}
chance = lambda g: run_Z({'s': 0, 'g': g}, [E(f'o_{k}', 'Acq', 'port', 'P', [], [k], lambda v, k=k: {k: WR[k]}, asp=f'o_{k}') for k in ZW])
ok, runs = GoalResp(chance, [2, 1], crit, 'g', W_Z, CV)
R['造场_C-chance'] = {'GoalResp': ok, **GoalForm(runs, [2, 1], crit, CV, W_Z),
                     'D1': bears_formation(runs[2], crit, CV, proc_versions(runs[2], {f'o_{k}' for k in ZW}), W_Z)[0]}


def event_level_D2w(run, ids, W):
    """事件粒度的候选：∃ 事件 e ∈ ids，e 的某输出由目标决定且 e 的某输出承担形成（事件 id 在判据变量来源中）。作对照，不作 D2。"""
    i = first_form(run, crit, W); st, anc = run.trace[i][1], run.trace[i][2]
    out = False
    for e in run.events:
        if e['id'] not in ids: continue
        form = any(C.depends(lambda ar: Fr(1 if crit({**st, **ar}) else 0), {v: st[v]}, v, run.M, None) is True and anc[v].get(e['id']) is True for v in CV)
        out = out or (goal_determined(run, e) and form)
    return out


def sub_split(g):
    evs = [E(f'o_{k}', 'Acq', 'port', 'P', [], [k], lambda v, k=k: {k: WR[k]}, asp=f'o_{k}') for k in ZW if k != 'xh3']
    evs.append(E('o_xh3', 'Acq', 'port', 'P', ['g'], ['xh3'], lambda v: {'xh3': Fr(1, 3) if v['g'] == 2 else Fr(9, 10)}, {'g': 1}, asp='o_xh3'))
    return run_Z({'s': 0, 'g': g}, evs)


def sub_comp(g):
    return run_Z({'s': 0, 'g': g}, [E('o_all', 'Acq', 'port', 'P', ['g'], list(ZW),
                                      lambda v: {**{k: WR[k] for k in ZW}, 'xh3': Fr(1, 3) if v['g'] == 2 else Fr(9, 10)}, {'g': 1}, asp='o_all')])


for tag, fn in (('拆分', sub_split), ('复合取得事件', sub_comp)):
    ok, runs = GoalResp(fn, [2, 1], crit, 'g', W_Z, CV)
    ids = {e['id'] for e in runs[2].events if e.get('asp')}
    R[f'造场_C-sub（{tag}）'] = {'GoalResp': ok, **GoalForm(runs, [2, 1], crit, CV, W_Z), '事件粒度候选 D2w（对照）': event_level_D2w(runs[2], ids, W_Z)}
R['造场_S'] = S(runs[2])

R['造场_P-mix·Ev 逐事件'] = {'𝒫≠∅': bool(P_ids), '𝒯≠∅（申报 τ_h2）': True, '𝔊≠∅（{G_h2, G_h1}）': True,
                          '逐事件': Ev(pm_runs[2], {'b_xh1', 'b_xh2', 'o_xh2'}, (0, 8))}
r = RunX(Qm(), {'s': 0, 'log': 0, **ALL1})
r.ev((0, 1), acq('o', {'b2': 2}))
r.ev((0, 2), E('cut', 'Rw', 'port', 'b', [], ['b2'], lambda v: {'b2': 3}))
r.ev((0, 3), E('back', 'Rw', 'port', 'b', [], ['b2'], lambda v: {'b2': 2}))
R['造场_Ev·端点恢复'] = {'Ev': Ev(r, {'o'}, (0, 3)), 'Acq': Acq(r, 'o', (0, 1), (0, 3))}

# ================= SNN 最小例（LIF、STDP 时迹、不应期、抑制性中间神经元） =================
def SNN():
    lay = {'w': 'theta', 'm': 'm', 'u': 'r', 'z': 'r', 'ref': 'r', 'xpre': 'r', 'xpost': 'r', 'zpre': 'r', 'd': 'r', 'ctx': 'r', 'uI': 'r', 'zI': 'r', 'g': 'ext'}
    P = ('pred', lambda x: x >= 0, [Fr(j, 4) for j in range(0, 13)])
    dom = {'w': ('pred', lambda x: x >= 0, [Fr(j, 10) for j in range(0, 31)]), 'm': B01, 'u': P, 'z': B01, 'ref': ('fin', {Fr(0), Fr(1), Fr(2)}),
           'xpre': P, 'xpost': P, 'zpre': B01, 'd': B01, 'ctx': B01, 'uI': P, 'zI': B01, 'g': B01}
    return Mx(lay, dom, (lambda st: st['m'] * st['w'], {'w': 1, 'm': 1}), support={'m': 'w'},
              ports={'Gt': {'m'}, 'In': {'zpre', 'd', 'ctx'}},
              FM={'inter': ('Run', {'uI', 'zI'}), 'mem': ('Run', {'u'}), 'fire': ('Run', {'z', 'u', 'ref'}), 'tpre': ('Run', {'xpre'}),
                  'tpost': ('Run', {'xpost'}), 'stdp': ('Sed', {'w'}), 'ext_goal': ('Run', {'g'})},
              fid='SNN', goal={'role': 'Xg', 'var': 'g', 'field': 'SNN'}, target={'g': {'field': 'SNN', 'rel': '前→后突触传递'}})


A = Fr(1, 10)


def snn_run(pre, drive, g, w0=Fr(3, 2), K=4):
    r = RunX(SNN(), {'w': w0, 'm': 1, 'u': 0, 'z': 0, 'ref': 0, 'xpre': 0, 'xpost': 0, 'zpre': 0, 'd': 0, 'ctx': 0, 'uI': 0, 'zI': 0, 'g': g}, hold=True)
    log = []
    for k in range(1, K + 1):
        r.ev((k, 1), E(f'p{k}', 'In', 'port', 'env', [], ['zpre'], lambda v, s=pre[k - 1]: {'zpre': s}))
        r.ev((k, 2), E(f'd{k}', 'In', 'port', 'env', [], ['d'], lambda v, s=drive[k - 1]: {'d': s}))
        r.ev((k, 3), E(f'c{k}', 'In', 'port', 'a', ['g'], ['ctx'], lambda v: {'ctx': 1 - v['g']}, {'g': 1}))      # Xa 按目标给中间神经元送上下文
        r.ev((k, 4), E(f'I{k}', 'Run', 'inter', 'F', ['ctx', 'uI'], ['uI', 'zI'], lambda v: {'uI': v['ctx'], 'zI': 1 if v['ctx'] >= 1 else 0}, {'ctx': None, 'uI': 0}))
        r.ev((k, 5), E(f'inh{k}', 'Gt', 'port', 'I', ['zI'], ['m'], lambda v: {'m': 1 - v['zI']}, {'zI': 1}))   # 抑制性中间神经元的输出经抑制通道开合突触
        r.ev((k, 6), E(f'u{k}', 'Run', 'mem', 'F', ['u', 'm', 'w', 'zpre', 'd', 'ref'], ['u'],
                       lambda v: {'u': (v['u'] / 2 + v['m'] * v['w'] * v['zpre'] + v['d']) if v['ref'] == 0 else Fr(0)}))
        r.ev((k, 7), E(f'f{k}', 'Run', 'fire', 'F', ['u', 'ref'], ['z', 'u', 'ref'],
                       lambda v: {'z': 1, 'u': 0, 'ref': 2} if v['u'] >= 1 else {'z': 0, 'u': v['u'], 'ref': max(v['ref'] - 1, 0)}))
        r.ev((k, 8), E(f'tp{k}', 'Run', 'tpre', 'F', ['xpre', 'zpre'], ['xpre'], lambda v: {'xpre': v['xpre'] / 2 + v['zpre']}, {'xpre': 1, 'zpre': 1}))
        r.ev((k, 9), E(f'sd{k}', 'Sed', 'stdp', 'F', ['w', 'xpre', 'z', 'xpost', 'zpre'], ['w'],
                       lambda v: {'w': max(Fr(0), v['w'] + A * v['xpre'] * v['z'] - A * v['xpost'] * v['zpre'])}))
        r.ev((k, 10), E(f'tq{k}', 'Run', 'tpost', 'F', ['xpost', 'z'], ['xpost'], lambda v: {'xpost': v['xpost'] / 2 + v['z']}, {'xpost': 1, 'z': 1}))
        log.append({'k': k, 'zpre': str(pre[k - 1]), 'd': str(drive[k - 1]), 'm': str(r.s['m']), 'z': str(r.s['z']), 'ref': str(r.s['ref']), 'w': str(r.s['w'])})
    return r, log


snn = {}
r, lg = snn_run([1, 0, 0, 0], [0, 0, 0, 0], g=1); snn['前先于后（前放电驱动后放电，同拍）→ LTP'] = {'log': lg, 'w_end': str(r.s['w'])}
r, lg = snn_run([0, 1, 0, 0], [1, 0, 0, 0], g=1); snn['后先于前（第1拍驱动后放电，第2拍前放电）→ LTD'] = {'log': lg, 'w_end': str(r.s['w'])}
r, lg = snn_run([1, 1, 0, 1], [0, 0, 0, 0], g=1); snn['不应期（第2拍前放电落在不应期，第4拍再放电）'] = {'z': [x['z'] for x in lg], 'ref': [x['ref'] for x in lg]}
r, lg = snn_run([1, 0, 0, 0], [0, 0, 0, 0], g=0)
snn['按目标抑制（g=0 → 中间神经元放电 → m=0）'] = {'log': lg, 'Gate(I)': Gate(r, 'I'), 'Bear_G(I)': Bear(r, 'I', {'Gt'}), 'GoalCond(I)': GoalCond(r, 'I'),
                                           'ΔΦ（量）': str(DPhi(r)), '注': '中间神经元为场内控制部，其输出经已申报的抑制通道作用于突触；形式值不授事件资格'}
r1, _ = snn_run([1, 0, 0, 0], [0, 0, 0, 0], g=1)
snn['g=1 分支'] = {'Gate(I)（无实际改变）': Gate(r1, 'I'), 'Bear(F,沉积)': Bear(r1, 'F', {'Sed'})}
R['SNN 最小例'] = snn

json.dump(R, open(sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else 'shiyin_full.json', 'w'), ensure_ascii=False, indent=1, default=str)
print(json.dumps(R, ensure_ascii=False, indent=1, default=str))
~~~
