~~~python
"""误差驱动调宿数理式 · 核算（纯 Python，精确分数，确定性）
核心：shiyin_core.py。
- 目标来源登记有类型：入口持有锚（角色 Xg、变量 g）与窗内实际改变 g 的 ext_goal 事件；GoalCond 查登记，不查事件名。
  持有锚键保留 HOLD: 前缀，事件以该前缀命名即 INVALID；入口持有锚须与目标声明（角色、目标变量、被调场）绑定，
  目标声明的被调场字段须与 TargetDecl 及本模型的场标识一致，不合 INVALID。
- 实现类 𝒞_EDR：有限计算图（节点 op ∈ {in, mul, sq, add}）的前向、局部导数、伴随递推、梯度、动量均由图自动生成；
  子节点按（父, 子）去重登记一次，局部导数对所有出现位置求和；前向事件的次数按变量在运算中的出现次数生成。
  两层线性为其特例；另给一个含平方节点与加法汇合的三参数例。
- Target 申报项：目标规格指向被调场的输入—输出关系（申报），未申报为 ⊥；不涉内容资格。
- 运行入口的 TargetDecl 可由参数传入，以分别核验：查询缺登记 ⊥、查询场冲突 0、运行缺登记或冲突 INVALID、三方一致而无目标来源时 GoalCond ⊥。
- 学习率 η 申报为非负有理数（η < 0 INVALID）。
适配层：R1 界外过程表（ext_ 前缀，写集只落 ext 层）；R2 重定传播（In/Run 同值写入并入写前来源快照，不记新来源；写入目标变量者不论途径按同值规则）。
用例：锚名冲突；局部切片 0 与结构依赖；多输出事件下事件祖先不传递；动量下零梯度的实际改写；零动量下的零更新；
只写不读参数 u（初值 1，主更新为另一 part）；η = 0；多输出同值输出。
"""
import sys, json
sys.path.insert(0, '.')
import shiyin_core as C
from fractions import Fraction as Fr

Q = lambda: ('pred', lambda x: True, [Fr(k, 7) for k in range(-9, 10)])
DATA = {0: Fr(1), 1: Fr(2)}


class Graph:
    """nodes: [(name, op, args)]，拓扑序；op: 'in'（读输入 x）、'mul'（a·b）、'sq'（a²）、'add'（a+b）；参数为叶。输出节点 out。"""
    def __init__(self, params, nodes, out):
        self.params, self.nodes, self.out = list(params), list(nodes), out
        self.names = [n for n, _, _ in nodes]
        self.children = {v: [] for v in self.params + self.names}
        for n, op, args in nodes:
            for a in dict.fromkeys(args): self.children[a].append((n, op, args))   # 每个（父, 子）只登记一次

    def evalf(self, theta, x):
        val = dict(theta)
        for n, op, args in self.nodes:
            a = [val[t] for t in args]
            val[n] = {'in': lambda: x, 'mul': lambda: a[0] * a[1], 'sq': lambda: a[0] ** 2, 'add': lambda: a[0] + a[1]}[op]()
        return val[self.out]

    def degree(self, p):
        d = {q: (1 if q == p else 0) for q in self.params}
        for n, op, args in self.nodes:
            if op == 'in': d[n] = 0
            elif op == 'mul': d[n] = d[args[0]] + d[args[1]]
            elif op == 'sq': d[n] = 2 * d[args[0]]
            else: d[n] = max(d[args[0]], d[args[1]])
        return d[self.out]

    @staticmethod
    def local(op, args, wrt, val):
        """∂child/∂wrt。"""
        if op == 'mul': return sum(val[args[1 - i]] for i in range(2) if args[i] == wrt)
        if op == 'sq': return 2 * val[args[0]]
        if op == 'add': return sum(1 for a in args if a == wrt)
        return 0


def build(G):
    th = G.params; r = ['x'] + G.names
    dvars = ['d_' + v for v in G.names + G.params]
    ext = ['g', 'idx', 'ys', 'loss', 'stop'] + dvars + ['v_' + p for p in th]
    lay = {**{v: 'theta' for v in th}, **{v: 'r' for v in r}, **{v: 'ext' for v in ext}}
    dom = {v: Q() for v in lay}; dom['idx'] = ('fin', {Fr(0), Fr(1)}); dom['stop'] = ('fin', {Fr(0), Fr(1)})
    FM = {f'fwd_{n}': ('Run', {n}) for n in G.names}
    EXT = {'ext_goal': ('Run', {'g'}), 'ext_sample': ('Run', {'idx'}), 'ext_label': ('Run', {'ys'}),
           'ext_loss': ('Run', {'loss'}), 'ext_monitor': ('Run', {'stop'}),
           **{f'ext_adj_{v}': ('Run', {'d_' + v}) for v in G.names + G.params},
           **{f'ext_mom_{p}': ('Run', {'v_' + p}) for p in th}}
    for n, (_, ws) in EXT.items():
        assert n.startswith('ext_') and all(lay[v] == 'ext' for v in ws)
    phi = lambda st: G.evalf({p: st[p] for p in th}, Fr(1))           # 读数：x = 1 时的输入—输出值
    m = C.Model(lay, dom, (phi, {p: G.degree(p) for p in th}), ports={'Rw': set(th), 'In': {'x'}}, FM={**FM, **EXT})
    m.field_id = "B'"                       # 本模型的被调场标识（不可由调用者改写的注册字段）
    return m


def fwd_deg(op, rd):
    """前向事件对各读入变量的多项式次数：mul 按出现次数（a = b 时为 2），sq 为 2，add 为 1（重复亦 1），in 为 1。"""
    if op == 'mul': return {t: rd.count(t) for t in set(rd)}
    if op == 'sq': return {rd[0]: 2}
    return {t: 1 for t in set(rd)}


def E(eid, kind, src, part, reads, writes, fn, deg):
    return {'id': eid, 'kind': kind, 'src': src, 'part': part, 'reads': reads, 'writes': writes, 'fn': fn, 'deg': deg}


def step_events(G, k, eta, beta, seed, eps, part='a'):
    evs = [E(f'smp{k}', 'Run', 'ext_sample', 'Xa', [], ['idx'], lambda v: {'idx': Fr((k + seed) % 2)}, {}),
           E(f'x{k}', 'In', 'port', 'data', ['idx'], ['x'], lambda v: {'x': DATA[int(v['idx'])]}, {})]
    for n, op, args in G.nodes:                                         # 前向（F_M）
        rd = ['x'] if op == 'in' else list(args)
        f = {'in': lambda v: v['x'], 'mul': lambda v, a=args: v[a[0]] * v[a[1]], 'sq': lambda v, a=args: v[a[0]] ** 2,
             'add': lambda v, a=args: v[a[0]] + v[a[1]]}[op]
        evs.append(E(f'{n}{k}', 'Run', f'fwd_{n}', 'F', sorted(set(rd)), [n], lambda v, n=n, f=f: {n: f(v)}, fwd_deg(op, rd)))
    out = G.out
    evs += [E(f'ys{k}', 'Run', 'ext_label', 'Xa', ['g', 'x'], ['ys'], lambda v: {'ys': v['g'] * v['x']}, {'g': 1, 'x': 1}),
            E(f'l{k}', 'Run', 'ext_loss', 'Xa', [out, 'ys'], ['loss'], lambda v: {'loss': (v[out] - v['ys']) ** 2 / 2}, {out: 2, 'ys': 2}),
            E(f'adj_{out}{k}', 'Run', f'ext_adj_{out}', 'Xa', [out, 'ys'], ['d_' + out], lambda v: {'d_' + out: v[out] - v['ys']}, {out: 1, 'ys': 1})]
    for v in [n for n in reversed(G.names) if n != out] + G.params:   # 伴随递推：δ_v = Σ_c δ_c · ∂c/∂v
        ch = G.children[v]
        rd = sorted({'d_' + c for c, _, _ in ch} | {t for _, _, a in ch for t in a})
        def f(val, v=v, ch=ch):
            return {'d_' + v: sum(val['d_' + c] * Graph.local(op, a, v, val) for c, op, a in ch)}
        evs.append(E(f'adj_{v}{k}', 'Run', f'ext_adj_{v}', 'Xa', rd, ['d_' + v], f, {t: 1 for t in rd}))
    for p in G.params:
        evs.append(E(f'mom_{p}{k}', 'Run', f'ext_mom_{p}', 'Xa', ['v_' + p, 'd_' + p], ['v_' + p],
                     lambda v, p=p: {'v_' + p: beta * v['v_' + p] + v['d_' + p]}, {'v_' + p: 1, 'd_' + p: 1}))
    for p in G.params:
        evs.append(E(f'u_{p}{k}', 'Rw', 'port', part, [p, 'v_' + p], [p], lambda v, p=p: {p: v[p] - eta * v['v_' + p]}, {p: 1, 'v_' + p: 1}))
    evs.append(E(f'mon{k}', 'Run', 'ext_monitor', 'Xa', ['loss'], ['stop'], lambda v: {'stop': 1 if v['loss'] <= eps else 0}, {}))
    return evs


class RunR(C.Run):
    """适配层：R2 重定传播；有类型的目标来源登记；事件 src 记录。"""
    def __init__(self, model, state, hold=None, decl=None, target_decl=None):
        super().__init__(model, state)
        self.goal_src, self.src_of = {}, {}
        self.decl = dict(decl or GOAL_DECL)
        td = (TARGET_DECL if target_decl is None else target_decl).get(self.decl.get('var'))
        if td is None or self.decl.get('field') != td['field'] or td['field'] != getattr(model, 'field_id', None):
            raise C.Invalid(f"goal decl field {self.decl.get('field')} inconsistent with TargetDecl/model field")
        if hold:                                   # hold = (角色, 变量)，须与目标声明绑定
            if hold != (self.decl['role'], self.decl['var']):
                raise C.Invalid(f'hold {hold} not bound to declared goal ({self.decl["role"]}, {self.decl["var"]})')
            if model.layer.get(hold[1]) != 'ext' or hold[1] not in state:
                raise C.Invalid(f'hold var {hold[1]} not declared/available at entry')
            aid = f'HOLD:{hold[0]}:{hold[1]}'
            self.goal_src[aid] = {'type': 'hold', 'role': hold[0], 'var': hold[1]}
            self.anc[hold[1]] = {aid: True}; self.trace[0][2][hold[1]] = {aid: True}

    def ev(self, phase, e):
        # 来源域为事件标识与持有锚标识的不交并：HOLD: 前缀保留给锚，事件不得使用
        if isinstance(e.get('id'), str) and e['id'].startswith('HOLD:'):
            raise C.Invalid('event id uses reserved hold-anchor namespace')
        before ={v: self.s.get(v) for v in e['writes']}
        args = {v: self.s[v] for v in e['reads'] if v in self.s}
        read_anc = {v: dict(self.anc[v]) for v in args}          # 写前来源快照
        super().ev(phase, e)
        self.src_of[e['id']] = e['src']
        last = self.events[-1]
        if e['src'] == 'ext_goal' and e['part'] == self.decl['role'] and e['writes'] == [self.decl['var']] and last['changed'] == [self.decl['var']]:
            self.goal_src[e['id']] = {'type': 'event', 'role': 'Xg', 'var': 'g', 'phase': phase}
        if e['kind'] not in ('In', 'Run') or e['src'] == 'ext_goal':
            return
        out = e['fn'](args)
        redet = {}
        for x in e['writes']:
            if x == self.decl['var']: continue                  # 写入目标变量者（不论途径）不重定
            if before.get(x) is not None and Fr(out[x]) == before[x]:
                a = {}
                for v in e['reads']:
                    d = C.depends(lambda ar, x=x: Fr(e['fn'](ar)[x]), args, v, self.M, e.get('deg', {}).get(v))
                    if d is True: a = C.merge(a, read_anc[v])
                    elif d is None: a = C.merge(a, {k: None for k in read_anc[v]})
                redet[x] = a
        for x, a in redet.items():                                # 统一提交
            self.anc[x] = a; self.trace[-1][2][x] = dict(a)


def goalcond(run, part):
    if not run.goal_src: return None, None
    res = (False, None)
    for (ph, st, anc), e in zip(run.trace[1:], run.events):
        if e['part'] == part and e['kind'] == 'Rw' and e['changed']:
            for v in e['changed']:
                hits = {k: anc[v][k] for k in anc[v] if k in run.goal_src}
                if any(s is True for s in hits.values()):
                    return True, {'event': e['id'], 'phase': ph, 'var': v, 'sources': sorted(hits)}
                if hits: res = (None, None)
    return res


TARGET_DECL = {'g': {'field': "B'", 'relation': 'y = g·x on 𝓘'}}
GOAL_DECL = {'role': 'Xg', 'var': 'g', 'field': "B'"}       # GoalRep / TargetDecl 的同一论元


def target(decl, var, field):
    d = decl.get(var)
    if d is None: return None
    return d['field'] == field


def train(G, init, K, eta=Fr(1, 8), beta=Fr(0), seed=0, eps=Fr(1, 2), hold=('Xg', 'g'), set_goal=None, goal_id='G', rename=None,
          target_decl=None, part='a'):
    if Fr(eta) < 0: raise C.Invalid('learning rate η must be a non-negative rational')
    r = RunR(build(G), init, hold=hold, target_decl=target_decl)
    if set_goal is not None:
        r.ev((0, 1), E(goal_id, 'Run', 'ext_goal', 'Xg', [], ['g'], lambda v: {'g': set_goal}, {}))
    log = []
    for k in range(1, K + 1):
        for i, ev in enumerate(step_events(G, k, eta, beta, seed, eps, part=part), start=1):
            if rename and ev['id'] == rename[0]: ev = {**ev, 'id': rename[1]}
            r.ev((k, i), ev)
        log.append({'k': k, 'x': str(r.s['x']), **{p: str(r.s[p]) for p in G.params}, 'loss(更新前)': str(r.s['loss']),
                    'stop': str(r.s['stop']), 'Phi': str(r.phi())})
    return r, log


G2 = Graph(['w1', 'w2'], [('xin', 'in', ()), ('h', 'mul', ('w1', 'xin')), ('y', 'mul', ('w2', 'h'))], 'y')
G3 = Graph(['w1', 'w2', 'w3'], [('xin', 'in', ()), ('h', 'mul', ('w1', 'xin')), ('s', 'sq', ('h',)), ('m1', 'mul', ('w2', 's')),
                                 ('m2', 'mul', ('w3', 'xin')), ('y', 'add', ('m1', 'm2'))], 'y')


def init_for(G, **kw):
    st = {'x': 0, 'g': 2, 'idx': 0, 'ys': 0, 'loss': 0, 'stop': 0}
    for n in G.names: st[n] = 0
    for v in G.names + G.params: st['d_' + v] = 0
    for p in G.params: st['v_' + p] = 0
    st.update(kw); return st


R = {}
r, log = train(G2, init_for(G2, w1=1, w2=Fr(1, 2)), 4)
R['两层线性（对照）'] = {'log': log, 'Bear_R': C.bear(r, 'a', {'Rw'}), 'GoalCond': goalcond(r, 'a'),
                         '读数次数': {p: G2.degree(p) for p in G2.params}}
r3, log3 = train(G3, init_for(G3, w1=1, w2=Fr(1, 2), w3=Fr(1, 2)), 3, eta=Fr(1, 32))
R['三参数含平方节点'] = {'log': log3, 'Bear_R': C.bear(r3, 'a', {'Rw'}), 'GoalCond': goalcond(r3, 'a'),
                      '读数次数': {p: G3.degree(p) for p in G3.params}}
# 数值复核：梯度与有限差分无关的符号检查——对 G3 在一点上比较伴随结果与直接展开
def g3_grad(w1, w2, w3, x, ys):
    h = w1 * x; s = h * h; y = w2 * s + w3 * x; d = y - ys
    return {'w1': d * w2 * 2 * h * x, 'w2': d * s, 'w3': d * x}
rr = RunR(build(G3), init_for(G3, w1=1, w2=Fr(1, 2), w3=Fr(1, 2)), hold=('Xg', 'g'))
for i, ev in enumerate(step_events(G3, 1, Fr(1, 32), Fr(0), 0, Fr(1, 2)), start=1):
    rr.ev((1, i), ev)
    if ev['id'] == 'adj_w31': break
R['G3伴随与直接展开一致'] = all(rr.s['d_' + p] == v for p, v in g3_grad(Fr(1), Fr(1, 2), Fr(1, 2), rr.s['x'], rr.s['ys']).items())

# 目标来源登记
rA, lA = train(G2, init_for(G2, w1=1, w2=Fr(1, 2), g=0), 2, hold=None, set_goal=2)
rB, lB = train(G2, init_for(G2, w1=1, w2=Fr(1, 2)), 2, set_goal=2)
R['冷启动设目标与已持有'] = {'A': goalcond(rA, 'a')[0], 'B': goalcond(rB, 'a')[0], '轨迹相同': lA == lB}
rN, _ = train(G2, init_for(G2, w1=1, w2=Fr(1, 2)), 2, hold=None, rename=('h1', 'G'))
R['无持有+前向事件名为G'] = goalcond(rN, 'a')[0]
rG2, _ = train(G2, init_for(G2, w1=1, w2=Fr(1, 2), g=0), 2, hold=None, set_goal=2, goal_id='G2')
R['真实目标事件改名G2'] = goalcond(rG2, 'a')[0]
r0, _ = train(G2, init_for(G2, w1=1, w2=Fr(1, 2), g=0), 2)
R['目标为0（R2）'] = goalcond(r0, 'a')[0]
rz, _ = train(G2, init_for(G2, w1=1, w2=2), 2)
R['零更新'] = {'Rewrite': C.eff_label(rz, 'a', 'Rw'), 'Bear_R': C.bear(rz, 'a', {'Rw'})[0], 'GoalCond': goalcond(rz, 'a')[0]}
R['Target'] = {'已申报': target(TARGET_DECL, 'g', "B'"), '未申报': target({}, 'g', "B'")}

# AncEv 严格祖先的性质核验（事件对上）
pairs = set()
for (ph, st, anc), e in zip(r.trace[1:], r.events):
    for v in e['changed']:
        for a in anc[v]:
            if a != e['id'] and not a.startswith('HOLD:'): pairs.add((a, e['id']))
order = {e['id']: i for i, e in enumerate(r.events)}
R['AncEv严格'] = {'非自反': all(a != b for a, b in pairs), '先行（相位在前）': all(order[a] < order[b] for a, b in pairs if a in order),
                 '传递（抽查）': all((a, c) in pairs for a, b in pairs for b2, c in pairs if b == b2)}

# 错误持有变量
try:
    train(G2, init_for(G2, w1=1, w2=Fr(1, 2)), 1, hold=('Xg', 'w1')); R['持有锚绑定w1'] = 'accepted'
except C.Invalid as ex:
    R['持有锚绑定w1'] = 'INVALID: ' + str(ex)
# 重复位置（mul(w,w)、add(w,w)、sq(w)），x = 2，g = 2，w = 1
def grad_once(G, w=Fr(1)):
    rr = RunR(build(G), init_for(G, w=w), hold=('Xg', 'g'))
    for i, ev in enumerate(step_events(G, 1, Fr(1, 32), Fr(0), 0, Fr(1, 2)), start=1):
        rr.ev((1, i), ev)
        if ev['src'] == 'ext_adj_w': break
    return rr.s['d_w'], rr.s['y'], rr.s['ys']
Gmm = Graph(['w'], [('xin', 'in', ()), ('a', 'mul', ('w', 'xin')), ('y', 'mul', ('w', 'w'))], 'y')
Gaa = Graph(['w'], [('xin', 'in', ()), ('a', 'mul', ('w', 'xin')), ('y', 'add', ('w', 'w'))], 'y')
Gsq = Graph(['w'], [('xin', 'in', ()), ('a', 'mul', ('w', 'xin')), ('y', 'sq', ('w',))], 'y')
out = {}
for name, G, dlocal in (('mul(w,w)', Gmm, lambda w: 2 * w), ('add(w,w)', Gaa, lambda w: Fr(2)), ('sq(w)', Gsq, lambda w: 2 * w)):
    d, y, ys = grad_once(G)
    out[name] = {'d_w': str(d), '解析': str((y - ys) * dlocal(Fr(1))), '一致': d == (y - ys) * dlocal(Fr(1)),
                 '前向次数': fwd_deg(G.nodes[-1][1], list(G.nodes[-1][2])), '读数次数': G.degree('w')}
out['mul(w,w)与sq(w)梯度相同'] = out['mul(w,w)']['d_w'] == out['sq(w)']['d_w']
R['重复位置'] = out
# 生成次数与实际公式一致性抽查：对 G2、G3 每个前向事件，用多项式次数完备性检验（申报次数 + 1 个点）无矛盾
def deg_ok(G):
    ok = True
    for n, op, args in G.nodes:
        if op == 'in': continue
        rd = list(args); dg = fwd_deg(op, rd)
        f = {'mul': lambda v, a=args: v[a[0]] * v[a[1]], 'sq': lambda v, a=args: v[a[0]] ** 2, 'add': lambda v, a=args: v[a[0]] + v[a[1]]}[op]
        for t, d in dg.items():
            vals = [f({**{s: Fr(1) for s in rd}, t: Fr(j)}) for j in range(d + 2)]
            diffs = vals
            for _ in range(d + 1):
                diffs = [b - a for a, b in zip(diffs, diffs[1:])]
            ok = ok and all(x == 0 for x in diffs)          # 第 d+1 阶差分为 0：次数 ≤ d
            diffs = vals
            for _ in range(d):
                diffs = [b - a for a, b in zip(diffs, diffs[1:])]
            ok = ok and any(x != 0 for x in diffs)          # 第 d 阶差分非 0：次数恰为 d
    return ok
R['生成次数与公式一致'] = {'G2': deg_ok(G2), 'G3': deg_ok(G3), 'mul(w,w)': deg_ok(Gmm), 'add(w,w)': deg_ok(Gaa), 'sq(w)': deg_ok(Gsq)}

# 目标声明的被调场字段与 TargetDecl / 模型不一致
def try_decl(decl):
    try:
        rr = RunR(build(G2), init_for(G2, w1=1, w2=Fr(1, 2)), hold=('Xg', 'g'), decl=decl)
        for i, ev in enumerate(step_events(G2, 1, Fr(1, 8), Fr(0), 0, Fr(1, 2)), start=1): rr.ev((1, i), ev)
        return goalcond(rr, 'a')[0]
    except C.Invalid as ex:
        return 'INVALID: ' + str(ex)
R['目标声明场字段'] = {'OTHER': try_decl({'role': 'Xg', 'var': 'g', 'field': 'OTHER'}),
                    "B'（一致）": try_decl({'role': 'Xg', 'var': 'g', 'field': "B'"})}


# ---------------- 来源分域、局部切片、多输出、动量 ----------------
def safe_train(*a, **k):
    try:
        r, log = train(*a, **k); return r, log
    except C.Invalid as ex:
        return 'INVALID: ' + str(ex), None

# 来源分域：冲突输入（G2，w1 = w2 = g = 0，v_w1 = 2，β = 1/2，η = 1/8，一步）
col_init = init_for(G2, w1=0, w2=0, g=0, v_w1=2)
rn, ln = safe_train(G2, col_init, 1, beta=Fr(1, 2))
rc, _ = safe_train(G2, col_init, 1, beta=Fr(1, 2), rename=('u_w11', 'HOLD:Xg:g'))
R['锚名冲突'] = {'正常事件名 GoalCond': goalcond(rn, 'a')[0], 'w1': ln[0]['w1'],
                           '事件改名 HOLD:Xg:g': rc if isinstance(rc, str) else ('accepted', goalcond(rc, 'a')[0])}

# 局部切片 0 与结构依赖：Φ = w1·w2 + w3，入口 (1, 0, 0)；a 写 w1 := 2，b 写 w3 := 1
lay = {'w1': 'theta', 'w2': 'theta', 'w3': 'theta'}
phi3 = lambda st: st['w1'] * st['w2'] + st['w3']
Ms = C.Model(lay, {v: Q() for v in lay}, (phi3, {'w1': 1, 'w2': 1, 'w3': 1}), ports={'Rw': set(lay)})
rs = C.Run(Ms, {'w1': 1, 'w2': 0, 'w3': 0})
rs.ev((1, 1), E('a1', 'Rw', 'port', 'a', [], ['w1'], lambda v: {'w1': 2}, {}))
rs.ev((1, 2), E('b1', 'Rw', 'port', 'b', [], ['w3'], lambda v: {'w3': 1}, {}))
R['局部切片'] = {'Rewrite_a': C.eff_label(rs, 'a', 'Rw'), 'Bear_R_a（形式值）': C.bear(rs, 'a', {'Rw'})[0],
                         'ΔΦ_W': str(rs.phi() - phi3({'w1': 1, 'w2': 0, 'w3': 0})),
                         '另一合法切片 w2=1,w3=0 上 Φ(w1=1), Φ(w1=2)': [str(phi3({'w1': Fr(1), 'w2': Fr(1), 'w3': Fr(0)})),
                                                                   str(phi3({'w1': Fr(2), 'w2': Fr(1), 'w3': Fr(0)}))],
                         '读法': '形式 0 只报形式值；局部无差异不排除结构依赖'}

# 多输出事件：e1 直写 w1 := 2；e2（界外分项）同时写 d_w1 := w1、d_w2 := 1；e3 读 d_w2 写 v_w2 := 2·d_w2
lay = {'w1': 'theta', 'd_w1': 'ext', 'd_w2': 'ext', 'v_w2': 'ext'}
Mm = C.Model(lay, {v: Q() for v in lay}, (lambda st: st['w1'], {'w1': 1}), ports={'Rw': {'w1'}},
             FM={'ext_grad': ('Run', {'d_w1', 'd_w2'}), 'ext_mom': ('Run', {'v_w2'})})
rm = C.Run(Mm, {'w1': 1, 'd_w1': 0, 'd_w2': 0, 'v_w2': 0})
rm.ev((1, 1), E('e1', 'Rw', 'port', 'a', [], ['w1'], lambda v: {'w1': 2}, {}))
rm.ev((1, 2), E('e2', 'Run', 'ext_grad', 'Xa', ['w1'], ['d_w1', 'd_w2'], lambda v: {'d_w1': v['w1'], 'd_w2': 1}, {'w1': 1}))
rm.ev((1, 3), E('e3', 'Run', 'ext_mom', 'Xa', ['d_w2'], ['v_w2'], lambda v: {'v_w2': 2 * v['d_w2']}, {'d_w2': 1}))
def strict_pairs(run):
    P = set()
    for (ph, st, anc), e in zip(run.trace[1:], run.events):
        for v in e['changed']:
            for a in anc[v]:
                if a != e['id'] and not a.startswith('HOLD:'): P.add((a, e['id']))
    return P
pm = strict_pairs(rm)
R['多输出'] = {'严格祖先对': sorted(pm), '(e1,e2)': ('e1', 'e2') in pm, '(e2,e3)': ('e2', 'e3') in pm,
                         '(e1,e3)': ('e1', 'e3') in pm, '事件投影传递': all((a, c) in pm for a, b in pm for b2, c in pm if b == b2)}
single = all(len(e['writes']) == 1 for k in (1, 2) for e in step_events(G3, k, Fr(1, 32), Fr(0), 0, Fr(1, 2)))
R['§5.1 生成器单输出'] = {'G3 两步全部事件写集为单元素': single,
                           'G2 训练运行事件投影传递': R['AncEv严格']['传递（抽查）'],
                           'G3 训练运行事件投影传递': all((a, c) in strict_pairs(r3) for a, b in strict_pairs(r3) for b2, c in strict_pairs(r3) if b == b2)}

# 查询与运行入口分域
def run_with_target(td, hold=('Xg', 'g')):
    r, _ = safe_train(G2, init_for(G2, w1=1, w2=Fr(1, 2)), 1, target_decl=td, hold=hold)
    return r if isinstance(r, str) else goalcond(r, 'a')[0]
R['分域'] = {'查询·未登记': target({}, 'g', "B'"),
                        '查询·场冲突': target({'g': {'field': 'OTHER', 'relation': 'y = g·x on 𝓘'}}, 'g', "B'"),
                        '查询·一致': target(TARGET_DECL, 'g', "B'"),
                        '运行·缺 TargetDecl': run_with_target({}),
                        '运行·TargetDecl 场冲突': run_with_target({'g': {'field': 'OTHER', 'relation': 'y = g·x on 𝓘'}}),
                        '运行·三方一致、无持有无设定': run_with_target(None, hold=None),
                        '运行·三方一致、入口持有': run_with_target(None)}

# 动量：零梯度、非零旧动量 → 实际改写；零动量 → 零更新
rmo, lmo = safe_train(G2, init_for(G2, w1=1, w2=2, g=2, v_w1=2), 1, beta=Fr(1, 2))
R['零梯度非零动量'] = {'q_w1': str(rmo.s['d_w1']), 'q_w2': str(rmo.s['d_w2']), 'v_w1': str(rmo.s['v_w1']),
                               'w1': str(rmo.s['w1']), 'Rewrite': C.eff_label(rmo, 'a', 'Rw')}
rz0, _ = safe_train(G2, init_for(G2, w1=1, w2=2, g=2), 1, beta=Fr(0))
R['零更新（β=0，初动量 0，一步）'] = {'Rewrite': C.eff_label(rz0, 'a', 'Rw'), 'Bear_R': C.bear(rz0, 'a', {'Rw'})[0],
                                            'GoalCond': goalcond(rz0, 'a')[0]}

# 只写不读参数 u：u ∈ θ，不入前向与 Φ；入口 u = 1；主更新为 part b，a 只作 u ← (9/10)·u
G2u = Graph(['w1', 'w2', 'u'], G2.nodes, 'y')
ru = RunR(build(G2u), init_for(G2u, w1=1, w2=Fr(1, 2), u=1), hold=('Xg', 'g'))
for i, ev in enumerate(step_events(G2u, 1, Fr(1, 8), Fr(0), 0, Fr(1, 2), part='b'), start=1): ru.ev((1, i), ev)
ru.ev((1, 99), E('decay_u', 'Rw', 'port', 'a', ['u'], ['u'], lambda v: {'u': Fr(9, 10) * v['u']}, {'u': 1}))
R['只写不读参数 u'] = {'u': str(ru.s['u']), 'deg_Φ(u)': G2u.degree('u'), 'Rewrite_a': C.eff_label(ru, 'a', 'Rw'),
                        'Bear_R_a': C.bear(ru, 'a', {'Rw'})[0], 'Bear_R_b': C.bear(ru, 'b', {'Rw'})[0]}


# ---------------- 多输出同值输出与学习率域 ----------------
# 多输出事件中的同值输出：pair 按写前状态同时写 d_w1 := g（改变）、ys := d_w1（同值）；其后直写 w1 := ys
mp = build(G2); mp.FM['ext_pair'] = ('Run', {'d_w1', 'ys'})
rp = RunR(mp, init_for(G2, w1=1, w2=2), hold=('Xg', 'g'))
rp.ev((1, 1), E('pair', 'Run', 'ext_pair', 'Xa', ['d_w1', 'g'], ['d_w1', 'ys'], lambda v: {'d_w1': v['g'], 'ys': v['d_w1']}, {'g': 1, 'd_w1': 1}))
ys_anc = dict(rp.anc['ys'])
rp.ev((1, 2), E('write', 'Rw', 'port', 'a', ['ys'], ['w1'], lambda v: {'w1': v['ys']}, {'ys': 1}))
R['多输出同值输出'] = {'值': {k: str(rp.s[k]) for k in ('g', 'd_w1', 'ys', 'w1')}, 'ys 的来源': sorted(ys_anc),
                              'd_w1 的来源': sorted(rp.anc['d_w1']), 'GoalCond': goalcond(rp, 'a')[0]}
# 学习率域：η = 0、零梯度、非零动量 → 参数保持；η = 1/8 → 实际改写；η < 0 → INVALID
r0e, _ = safe_train(G2, init_for(G2, w1=1, w2=2, g=2, v_w1=2), 1, eta=Fr(0), beta=Fr(1, 2))
r8e, _ = safe_train(G2, init_for(G2, w1=1, w2=2, g=2, v_w1=2), 1, eta=Fr(1, 8), beta=Fr(1, 2))
rne, _ = safe_train(G2, init_for(G2, w1=1, w2=2, g=2, v_w1=2), 1, eta=Fr(-1, 8), beta=Fr(1, 2))
R['学习率域'] = {'η=0': {'q_w1': str(r0e.s['d_w1']), 'v_w1': str(r0e.s['v_w1']), 'w1': str(r0e.s['w1']), 'Rewrite': C.eff_label(r0e, 'a', 'Rw')},
                         'η=1/8': {'v_w1': str(r8e.s['v_w1']), 'w1': str(r8e.s['w1']), 'Rewrite': C.eff_label(r8e, 'a', 'Rw')},
                         'η=−1/8': rne}

json.dump(R, open(sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else 'edr_checks.json', 'w'), ensure_ascii=False, indent=1, default=str)
print(json.dumps(R, ensure_ascii=False, indent=1, default=str))
~~~

【资源 012 起】源码　ffn\_tune\_checks.py　SHA-256 89bcd878b35ec588acc98305e6a3cb5e5d57c60d392c5fc7e2a13f1f3c1a6fd9

~~~python
"""前馈网络调谐可能式 · 核算（纯 Python，精确分数，确定性）
- 抽象宿载运行规格 𝔖_ffn 以转移生成式实现（RunFFN）：推理段 k 内依次为 输入置值（相位 (k,1)）、逐层纯求值（(k,2)…，按拓扑序，
  只读本段已计算的前驱）；参数直写只在段前槽 (k,0,j)。其余转移一律 INVALID。推理段号须为正整数，0 保留作入口时间戳（执行前拒绝）。
- T3 事件须使用注册规则：节点名、读集、写集与注册函数对象逐项相符，否则执行前 INVALID。
- 相位引理按「每个节点的值 = 其最近一次计算所在段的 (x, θ) 经前向规则的值；未计算者为入口值」逐事件核验，含旧缓存例。
- 上下文例取成对读数 (1,0)/(1,1)；非法端口与非法写层分开诊断；扩展例的前向分项实际读入 m2；负输出对照（σ_y = 恒等）。
本程序不证明一般定理（证明在式文正文），不对真实硬件下结论，不签扩展例为一次调谐。
"""
import sys, json
sys.path.insert(0, '.')
import shiyin_core as C
from fractions import Fraction as Fr

Q = ('pred', lambda x: True, [Fr(k, 4) for k in range(-12, 13)])
TH = ['w11', 'w12', 'w21', 'w22', 'b1', 'b2', 'u1', 'u2']
XS = ['x1', 'x2']
NODES = ['h1', 'h2', 'y']          # 拓扑序
PRED = {'h1': XS, 'h2': XS, 'y': ['h1', 'h2']}


def relu(z): return z if z > 0 else Fr(0)


def forward(x, th):
    """前向规则 F：由 (x, θ) 算出全部节点值。"""
    h1 = relu(th['w11'] * x['x1'] + th['w12'] * x['x2'] + th['b1'])
    h2 = relu(th['w21'] * x['x1'] + th['w22'] * x['x2'] + th['b2'])
    return {'h1': h1, 'h2': h2, 'y': th['u1'] * h1 + th['u2'] * h2}


def Bffn(ports=None):
    lay = {**{v: 'theta' for v in TH}, **{v: 'r' for v in XS + NODES}}
    phi = lambda st: forward({'x1': Fr(1), 'x2': Fr(0)}, st)['y']     # 读出探针 x = (1, 0)，数学读出
    return C.Model(lay, {v: Q for v in lay}, (phi, {}), ports=ports or {'Rw': set(TH), 'In': set(XS)},
                   FM={'fwd_h1': ('Run', {'h1'}), 'fwd_h2': ('Run', {'h2'}), 'fwd_y': ('Run', {'y'})})


def E(eid, kind, src, part, reads, writes, fn, deg=None):
    return {'id': eid, 'kind': kind, 'src': src, 'part': part, 'reads': reads, 'writes': writes, 'fn': fn, 'deg': deg or {}}


FWD_FN = {'h1': lambda v: {'h1': relu(v['w11'] * v['x1'] + v['w12'] * v['x2'] + v['b1'])},
          'h2': lambda v: {'h2': relu(v['w21'] * v['x1'] + v['w22'] * v['x2'] + v['b2'])},
          'y': lambda v: {'y': v['u1'] * v['h1'] + v['u2'] * v['h2']}}
FWD_READS = {'h1': ['w11', 'w12', 'b1', 'x1', 'x2'], 'h2': ['w21', 'w22', 'b2', 'x1', 'x2'], 'y': ['u1', 'u2', 'h1', 'h2']}


def fwd(k):
    return [((k, 2 + i), E(f'{n}_{k}', 'Run', f'fwd_{n}', 'F', FWD_READS[n], [n], FWD_FN[n])) for i, n in enumerate(NODES)]


class RunFFN(C.Run):
    """𝔖_ffn 的转移生成式：
    T2 参数直写：kind Rw，相位 (k, 0, j)，只写 θ（段前槽；推理段内 θ 不变）；
    T1 输入置值：kind In，相位 (k, 1)，只写输入；开启第 k 段；
    T3 逐层纯求值：kind Run，src 为 fwd_n，相位 (k, i ≥ 2)，按拓扑序，所读前驱须已在本段计算（输入在本段置值）。
    另核相位引理：每个事件后，每个节点值 = 其最近计算段的 (x, θ) 经 F 的值；未计算者保持入口值。"""
    def __init__(self, model, state):
        super().__init__(model, state)
        self.seg = 0; self.stamp = {n: 0 for n in NODES}; self.inp_seg = 0
        self.seg_ctx = {0: None}; self.next_node = 0; self.lemma_ok = True; self.entry = {n: self.s[n] for n in NODES}

    def ev(self, phase, e):
        phase = tuple(phase); k = phase[0]
        if type(k) is not int or k < 1: raise C.Invalid(f'{e["id"]}: inference segment must be a positive integer')
        if e['kind'] == 'Rw':
            if len(phase) != 3 or phase[1] != 0: raise C.Invalid(f'{e["id"]}: θ write outside pre-segment slot (k,0,j)')
        elif e['kind'] == 'In':
            if phase[1:] != (1,): raise C.Invalid(f'{e["id"]}: input outside (k,1)')
        elif e['kind'] == 'Run' and e['src'].startswith('fwd_'):
            n = e['src'][4:]
            if k != self.seg or len(phase) != 2 or phase[1] < 2: raise C.Invalid(f'{e["id"]}: forward outside segment {self.seg}')
            if n not in NODES: raise C.Invalid(f'{e["id"]}: unknown forward node')
            if tuple(e['reads']) != tuple(FWD_READS[n]) or tuple(e['writes']) != (n,) or e['fn'] is not FWD_FN[n]:
                raise C.Invalid(f'{e["id"]}: forward event must use the registered rule')      # T3 绑定注册规则（B2）
            if NODES.index(n) != self.next_node: raise C.Invalid(f'{e["id"]}: not in topological order')
            for p in PRED[n]:
                if (p in XS and self.inp_seg != k) or (p in NODES and self.stamp[p] != k):
                    raise C.Invalid(f'{e["id"]}: reads {p} not computed in segment {k}')
        else:
            raise C.Invalid(f'{e["id"]}: transition not in generator of 𝔖_ffn')
        super().ev(phase, e)
        if e['kind'] == 'In':
            self.seg = k; self.inp_seg = k; self.next_node = 0
            self.seg_ctx[k] = ({v: self.s[v] for v in XS}, {v: self.s[v] for v in TH})
        elif e['kind'] == 'Run':
            self.stamp[e['src'][4:]] = k; self.next_node += 1
        self.check_lemma()

    def check_lemma(self):
        for n in NODES:
            s = self.stamp[n]
            want = self.entry[n] if s == 0 else forward(*self.seg_ctx[s])[n]
            if self.s[n] != want: self.lemma_ok = False


init = {'w11': 1, 'w12': 1, 'w21': 1, 'w22': -2, 'b1': 0, 'b2': 0, 'u1': 1, 'u2': 1, 'x1': 0, 'x2': 0, 'h1': 0, 'h2': 0, 'y': 0}
R = {}


def attempt(f):
    try:
        return f()
    except C.Invalid as ex:
        return 'INVALID: ' + str(ex)


def segment(r, k, x, part='a', pre=()):
    for j, ev in enumerate(pre, 1): r.ev((k, 0, j), ev)
    r.ev((k, 1), E(f'in{k}', 'In', 'port', part, [], list(x), lambda v, x=x: dict(x)))
    for ph, ev in fwd(k): r.ev(ph, ev)
    return r


# 相位引理：旧缓存例（第 1 段 x = (1,0)；第 2 段置 x2 = 1 而尚未前向）
r = RunFFN(Bffn(), init)
segment(r, 1, {'x1': 1, 'x2': 0})
h2_seg1 = r.s['h2']
r.ev((2, 1), E('in2', 'In', 'port', 'a', [], ['x2'], lambda v: {'x2': 1}))
stale = {'h2（置值后、前向前）': str(r.s['h2']), 'stamp(h2)': r.stamp['h2'], 'F(x 第2段, θ) 的 h2': str(forward({'x1': Fr(1), 'x2': Fr(1)}, r.s)['h2'])}
for ph, ev in fwd(2): r.ev(ph, ev)
stale['h2（第2段前向后）'] = str(r.s['h2']); stale['相位引理逐事件成立'] = r.lemma_ok
R['相位引理·旧缓存例'] = {'h2 第1段': str(h2_seg1), **stale}
# 段前槽的参数直写（如训练写回），之后的段按新 θ 计算；旧段节点在新段计算前仍对应旧 θ
r = RunFFN(Bffn(), init)
segment(r, 1, {'x1': 1, 'x2': 0})
r.ev((2, 0, 1), E('bias2', 'Rw', 'port', 'b', [], ['b2'], lambda v: {'b2': -2}))
ok_before = r.lemma_ok and r.stamp['h2'] == 1 and r.s['h2'] == 1
segment(r, 2, {'x1': 1, 'x2': 0})
R['段前直写'] = {'直写后旧值仍对应第1段 (x,θ)': ok_before, '第2段 h2': str(r.s['h2']), '相位引理逐事件成立': r.lemma_ok}
# 生成式之外的转移
def mid_write():
    r = RunFFN(Bffn(), init); r.ev((1, 1), E('in1', 'In', 'port', 'a', [], ['x1'], lambda v: {'x1': 1}))
    r.ev((1, 2), E('w_mid', 'Rw', 'port', 'a', [], ['w21'], lambda v: {'w21': 2})); return 'accepted'
def skip_order():
    r = RunFFN(Bffn(), init); r.ev((1, 1), E('in1', 'In', 'port', 'a', [], ['x1'], lambda v: {'x1': 1}))
    r.ev((1, 2), E('y1', 'Run', 'fwd_y', 'F', FWD_READS['y'], ['y'], FWD_FN['y'])); return 'accepted'
def no_input():
    r = RunFFN(Bffn(), init); segment(r, 1, {'x1': 1, 'x2': 0})
    r.ev((2, 2), E('h1_2', 'Run', 'fwd_h1', 'F', FWD_READS['h1'], ['h1'], FWD_FN['h1'])); return 'accepted'
def other_rule():
    r = RunFFN(Bffn(), init); r.M.FM['clamp'] = ('Run', {'h2'})
    r.ev((1, 1), E('in1', 'In', 'port', 'a', [], ['x1'], lambda v: {'x1': 1}))
    r.ev((1, 2), E('cl', 'Run', 'clamp', 'a', [], ['h2'], lambda v: {'h2': 0})); return 'accepted'
R['生成式之外'] = {'推理段内直写 θ': attempt(mid_write), '跳过拓扑序': attempt(skip_order), '未置输入即前向': attempt(no_input), '另立运行分项写激活': attempt(other_rule)}

# 非法端口与非法写层分开诊断
R['诊断'] = {
    '端口不许可：Gt 写 h2（基本模型无 Gt 端口）': attempt(lambda: (C.Run(Bffn(), init).ev((0, 1), E('g', 'Gt', 'port', 'a', [], ['h2'], lambda v: {'h2': 0})), 'accepted')[1]),
    '端口不许可：Rw 写 h2（基本模型直写端口只含 θ）': attempt(lambda: (C.Run(Bffn(), init).ev((0, 1), E('c', 'Rw', 'port', 'a', [], ['h2'], lambda v: {'h2': 0})), 'accepted')[1]),
    '写层不相容：诊断模型许可 Gt 端口写 h2': attempt(lambda: (C.Run(Bffn(ports={'Rw': set(TH), 'In': set(XS), 'Gt': {'h2'}}), init).ev(
        (0, 1), E('g', 'Gt', 'port', 'a', [], ['h2'], lambda v: {'h2': 0})), 'accepted')[1]),
    '写层不相容：诊断模型许可 Rw 端口写 h2': attempt(lambda: (C.Run(Bffn(ports={'Rw': set(TH) | {'h2'}, 'In': set(XS)}), init).ev(
        (0, 1), E('c', 'Rw', 'port', 'a', [], ['h2'], lambda v: {'h2': 0})), 'accepted')[1])}

# 成对读数：x = (1,0) 与 (1,1)
pair = {}
for name, x in (('(1,0)', {'x1': 1, 'x2': 0}), ('(1,1)', {'x1': 1, 'x2': 1})):
    r = segment(RunFFN(Bffn(), init), 1, x)
    pair[name] = {'h2': str(r.s['h2']), 'y': str(r.s['y']), 'a 的事件种类': sorted({e['kind'] for e in r.events if e['part'] == 'a'}),
                  'Rewrite': C.eff_label(r, 'a', 'Rw'), 'Gate': C.eff_label(r, 'a', 'Gt')}
R['上下文成对读数（对照：数值向量经同一 In 端口）'] = pair

# 直写标签（不授事件资格）
for name, var, val in (('剪枝 u2 := 0', 'u2', 0), ('偏置 b2 := −2', 'b2', -2)):
    r = RunFFN(Bffn(), init)
    r.ev((1, 0, 1), E('rw', 'Rw', 'port', 'a', [], [var], lambda v, var=var, val=val: {var: val}))
    segment(r, 1, {'x1': 1, 'x2': 0}, part='data')
    R[f'直写标签·{name}'] = {'Rewrite': C.eff_label(r, 'a', 'Rw'), 'Gate': C.eff_label(r, 'a', 'Gt'), 'Phi': str(r.phi()),
                          '注': '直写标签；是否成立为调宿另核，不授事件资格'}

# 生成式穷尽：上述合法运行中出现的转移种类
R['生成式穷尽（合法运行中的转移）'] = sorted({(e['kind']) for e in r.events})

# 扩展例：动态层 m2 支撑于连接 h2→y（u2），前向分项实际读入 m2
lay = {**{v: 'theta' for v in TH}, **{v: 'r' for v in XS + NODES}, 'm2': 'm'}
dom = {v: Q for v in lay}; dom['m2'] = ('fin', {Fr(0), Fr(1)})
fx = lambda st, x: st['u1'] * relu(st['w11'] * x[0] + st['w12'] * x[1] + st['b1']) + st['u2'] * st['m2'] * relu(st['w21'] * x[0] + st['w22'] * x[1] + st['b2'])
ext = C.Model(lay, dom, (lambda st: fx(st, (Fr(1), Fr(0))), {}), support={'m2': 'u2'},
              ports={'Rw': set(TH), 'In': set(XS), 'Gt': {'m2'}},
              FM={'fwd_h1': ('Run', {'h1'}), 'fwd_h2': ('Run', {'h2'}), 'fwd_y': ('Run', {'y'})})
def ext_run(gate_first):
    r = C.Run(ext, {**init, 'm2': 1})
    if gate_first is not None: r.ev((0, 1), E('g', 'Gt', 'port', 'a', [], ['m2'], lambda v: {'m2': gate_first}))
    r.ev((1, 1), E('in1', 'In', 'port', 'data', [], XS, lambda v: {'x1': 1, 'x2': 0}))
    r.ev((1, 2), E('h1_1', 'Run', 'fwd_h1', 'F', FWD_READS['h1'], ['h1'], FWD_FN['h1']))
    r.ev((1, 3), E('h2_1', 'Run', 'fwd_h2', 'F', FWD_READS['h2'], ['h2'], FWD_FN['h2']))
    r.ev((1, 4), E('y_1', 'Run', 'fwd_y', 'F', ['u1', 'u2', 'm2', 'h1', 'h2'], ['y'], lambda v: {'y': v['u1'] * v['h1'] + v['u2'] * v['m2'] * v['h2']}))
    return r
r1, r0 = ext_run(None), ext_run(Fr(0))
R['扩展例（前向实际读入 m2）'] = {'m2=1：y, Φ': [str(r1.s['y']), str(r1.phi())], 'm2=0：y, Φ': [str(r0.s['y']), str(r0.phi())],
                            'Gate': C.eff_label(r0, 'a', 'Gt'), 'Bear_G': C.bear(r0, 'a', {'Gt'})[0], 'y 的来源含门控事件': 'g' in r0.anc['y'],
                            'note': '形式条件可检；不签为一次调谐（六组职责、目标条件与使役另核）'}

# 同名换规则
def swap_fn():
    r = RunFFN(Bffn(), init); r.ev((1, 1), E('in1', 'In', 'port', 'a', [], ['x1'], lambda v: {'x1': 1}))
    try:
        r.ev((1, 2), E('constant', 'Run', 'fwd_h1', 'F', [], ['h1'], lambda v: {'h1': 99})); return 'accepted', str(r.s['h1'])
    except C.Invalid as ex:
        return 'INVALID: ' + str(ex), str(r.s['h1'])
def swap_reads():
    r = RunFFN(Bffn(), init); r.ev((1, 1), E('in1', 'In', 'port', 'a', [], ['x1'], lambda v: {'x1': 1}))
    try:
        r.ev((1, 2), E('h1_1', 'Run', 'fwd_h1', 'F', ['w11', 'b1', 'x1'], ['h1'], FWD_FN['h1'])); return 'accepted', str(r.s['h1'])
    except C.Invalid as ex:
        return 'INVALID: ' + str(ex), str(r.s['h1'])
R['T3 绑定注册规则'] = {'同名换函数（常数 99）': swap_fn(), '读集不符': swap_reads()}
# 段号与负输出
def seg0():
    r = RunFFN(Bffn(), init)
    try:
        segment(r, 0, {'x1': 1, 'x2': 0}); return 'accepted'
    except C.Invalid as ex:
        msg = 'INVALID: ' + str(ex)
    segment(r, 1, {'x1': 1, 'x2': 0})
    return msg, {'h1': str(r.s['h1']), 'lemma_ok': r.lemma_ok}
R['段号 0 拒绝、随后合法第 1 段'] = seg0()
r = RunFFN(Bffn(), {**init, 'u1': -1, 'u2': 0}); segment(r, 1, {'x1': 1, 'x2': 0})
R['负输出对照（σ_h = ReLU，σ_y = 恒等）'] = {'h1': str(r.s['h1']), 'h2': str(r.s['h2']), 'y': str(r.s['y']), '相位引理': r.lemma_ok}
json.dump(R, open(sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else 'ffn_tune_checks.json', 'w'), ensure_ascii=False, indent=1, default=str)
print(json.dumps(R, ensure_ascii=False, indent=1, default=str))
~~~

【资源 014 起】源码　ind\_effect\_checks.py　SHA-256 3dd174ea679b5ef60793da183f4a3efc32550450da0dbf7211b19ba0128ae77b

~~~python
"""势引作用必然式 · 核算（纯 Python，精确分数，确定性）
核心：shiyin_core.py。本程序：
- 在申报的读数结构性质（严格单调方向、斜率下界、余项 Lipschitz 上界——事前申报，不读 ΔΦ）与窗内事件、状态上核验条件组；
- 对满足条件组的例子验证 R = ΔΦ_W ≠ 0 ∧ Bear；对各边界例指出失效的条件项，并给出 R 的实际取值。
条件组：
  C1 严格位移：读数在 a 所写变量 v 上的申报严格单调（方向 sgn），对窗内一切相关背景成立；v 的值域有异于 W 末值的合法替代值。
     末态依赖检验至少提交一个异于末值的合法探针；门控／盒顶点核按「申报对 v 仿射 Φ = A(u)·v + B(u)」核总系数 A(u) 恒非零同号，
     A 由归一化端点差求得，并以入列探针核验仿射申报；顶点核限于 A 对背景多仿射。
  C2 实际改变：a 在 W 内有实际改变 v 的事件 e，且 e 的写入值异于 W 起点的 v 值。
  C3 隔离（三式择一）：C3a 读数读集中 v 以外变量 W 首末相同；C3b 余项界 m·|Δv| > L·‖Δu‖₁；C3c 同向：读数对各变量单调不减（或不增）且 Δv、Δu 各分量同号。
  C4 保持（二式择一）：C4a e 的版本为 W 末 v 的现行版本；C4b e 之后对 v 的写入都由对 v 依赖为 1 的场规则执行，且不减小 |v − v₀|、不改变 v − v₀ 的符号
     （逐个后继事件在其写前快照上核对 v 的依赖为 1）。
用例：空探针对照、两路相抵门控（单路系数非零而总系数为零）、推论反向例（a 只写读数无关变量、b 改读数变量）、非仿射门控例 Φ = (m − 1/2)²、
「承担不是反事实必要性」对照（同一场规则，含与不含 a 的直写，ΔΦ 相同）、离散域上导数同号而不单调的例；推论 5 各例以正文所写的完整读数与替代序列复核。
"""
import sys, json
sys.path.insert(0, '.')
import shiyin_core as C
from fractions import Fraction as Fr

Q = lambda: ('pred', lambda x: True, [Fr(k, 4) for k in range(-12, 13)])
B01 = ('fin', {Fr(0), Fr(1)})


def E(eid, kind, src, part, reads, writes, fn, deg=None):
    return {'id': eid, 'kind': kind, 'src': src, 'part': part, 'reads': list(reads), 'writes': list(writes), 'fn': fn, 'deg': deg or {}}


def run(model, init, steps):
    r = C.Run(model, init); r.orig = {}
    for ph, ev in steps: r.ev(ph, ev); r.orig[ev['id']] = ev
    return r


def snap(r, ph): return next(st for p, st, a in r.trace if p == tuple(ph))


def conds(r, a_id, v, W, decl):
    """decl：{'sgn': ±1 申报的 v 方向严格单调, 'slope': m, 'lip': L, 'mono_all': ±1 或 None, 'readset': [...]}（事前申报的读数结构性质）。"""
    s0, s1 = snap(r, W[0]), snap(r, W[1])
    ev = next(e for e in r.events if e['id'] == a_id)
    idx = r.events.index(ev)
    out = {}
    others0 = [u for u in decl['readset'] if u != v]
    out['C1·末态探针非空'] = len(r.M.probes(v, s1[v], 1)[0]) > 0
    if decl.get('vertex'):                                        # 申报 Φ 对 v 仿射：A(u) = (Φ(v_end, u) − Φ(v₀, u))/(v_end − v₀)，顶点核（A 对背景多仿射）
        import itertools
        As, aff = [], True
        pr = r.M.probes(v, s1[v], 1)[0]
        for corner in itertools.product(*[sorted({s0[u], s1[u]}) for u in others0]):
            st = dict(s1); st.update(dict(zip(others0, corner)))
            hi = r.phi({**st, v: s1[v]}); lo = r.phi({**st, v: s0[v]})
            if s1[v] == s0[v]: aff = False; break
            A = (hi - lo) / (s1[v] - s0[v]); As.append(A)
            aff = aff and all(r.phi({**st, v: y}) == hi + A * (y - s1[v]) for y in pr)   # 入列探针核仿射申报
        out['C1·仿射申报经探针核验'] = aff
        out['C1·盒顶点总系数恒非零同号'] = aff and (all(a > 0 for a in As) or all(a < 0 for a in As))
    else:
        out['C1·盒顶点总系数恒非零同号'] = None
    out['C1'] = decl.get('sgn') in (1, -1) and out['C1·末态探针非空'] and out['C1·盒顶点总系数恒非零同号'] is not False
    st_e = r.trace[idx + 1][1]
    out['C2'] = v in ev['changed'] and st_e[v] != s0[v]          # 实际改变，且写入值异于 W 起点值
    others = [u for u in decl['readset'] if u != v]
    du = {u: s1[u] - s0[u] for u in others}; dv = s1[v] - s0[v]
    out['C3a'] = all(x == 0 for x in du.values())
    out['C3b'] = decl.get('slope') is not None and decl.get('lip') is not None and decl['slope'] * abs(dv) > decl['lip'] * sum(abs(x) for x in du.values())
    ms = decl.get('mono_all')
    out['C3c'] = ms in (1, -1) and dv * ms > 0 and all(x * ms >= 0 for x in du.values())
    out['C3'] = out['C3a'] or out['C3b'] or out['C3c']
    later = [e for e in r.events[idx + 1:] if v in e['changed']]
    # C4a：e 之后无对 v 的实际改变
    out['C4a'] = not any(v in e['changed'] for e in later)
    v0 = s0[v]
    ok = True; cur = None
    for (ph, st, anc), e in zip(r.trace[1:], r.events):
        if e is ev: cur = st[v]
        elif cur is not None and v in e['changed']:
            pre = r.trace[r.events.index(e)][1]                     # 写前快照
            o = r.orig[e['id']]; args = {x: pre[x] for x in o['reads'] if x in pre}
            dep = v in args and C.depends(lambda a, o=o: Fr(o['fn'](a)[v]), args, v, r.M, o.get('deg', {}).get(v)) is True
            ok = ok and e['kind'] in ('Sed', 'Run') and dep and abs(st[v] - v0) >= abs(cur - v0) and (st[v] - v0) * (cur - v0) > 0
            cur = st[v]
    out['C4b'] = bool(later) and ok
    out['C4'] = out['C4a'] or out['C4b']
    out['全部'] = out['C1'] and out['C2'] and out['C3'] and out['C4']
    return out


def R(r, part, kinds, W):
    s0, s1 = snap(r, W[0]), snap(r, W[1])
    return {'ΔΦ': str(r.phi(s1) - r.phi(s0)), 'ΔΦ≠0': r.phi(s1) != r.phi(s0), 'Bear': C.bear(r, part, kinds)[0]}


OUT = {}
def case(name, model, init, steps, a_id, v, decl, part='a', kinds=('Rw',), W=None):
    r = run(model, init, steps)
    W = W or ((0, 0), r.trace[-1][0])
    c = conds(r, a_id, v, W, decl); res = R(r, part, set(kinds), W)
    OUT[name] = {'条件': c, '结果': res, '条件组成立 ⇒ R 成立': (not c['全部']) or (res['ΔΦ≠0'] and res['Bear'] is True)}


# 模型
def M(layer, phi, deg, fm=None, ports=None, dom=None, support=None):
    d = {v: Q() for v in layer}; d.update(dom or {})
    return C.Model(layer, d, (phi, deg), ports=ports or {'Rw': {v for v, L in layer.items() if L == 'theta'}}, FM=fm or {}, support=support)


H = M({'w': 'theta', 'th': 'theta'}, lambda s: s['w'] - s['th'], {'w': 1, 'th': 1})
case('T1 隔离直写：Φ = w − th，w 1 → 3', H, {'w': 1, 'th': 0}, [((0, 1), E('a', 'Rw', 'port', 'a', [], ['w'], lambda x: {'w': 3}))], 'a', 'w',
     {'sgn': 1, 'readset': ['w', 'th']})
SQ = M({'v': 'theta'}, lambda s: s['v'] ** 2, {'v': 2})
case('边界 1 平方读数：Φ = v²，v −1 → 1', SQ, {'v': -1}, [((0, 1), E('a', 'Rw', 'port', 'a', [], ['v'], lambda x: {'v': 1}))], 'a', 'v',
     {'sgn': None, 'readset': ['v']})
RL = M({'v': 'theta'}, lambda s: s['v'] if s['v'] > 0 else Fr(0), {'v': None})
case('边界 2 ReLU 平坦区：v −2 → −1', RL, {'v': -2}, [((0, 1), E('a', 'Rw', 'port', 'a', [], ['v'], lambda x: {'v': -1}))], 'a', 'v',
     {'sgn': None, 'readset': ['v']})
case('边界 3 相抵：a 写 w + 1，b 写 w − 1', H, {'w': 1, 'th': 0},
     [((0, 1), E('a', 'Rw', 'port', 'a', ['w'], ['w'], lambda x: {'w': x['w'] + 1}, {'w': 1})),
      ((0, 2), E('b', 'Rw', 'port', 'b', ['w'], ['w'], lambda x: {'w': x['w'] - 1}, {'w': 1}))], 'a', 'w', {'sgn': 1, 'readset': ['w', 'th']})
case('边界 4 覆写：a 写 w := 3，b 写 w := 1', H, {'w': 1, 'th': 0},
     [((0, 1), E('a', 'Rw', 'port', 'a', [], ['w'], lambda x: {'w': 3})), ((0, 2), E('b', 'Rw', 'port', 'b', [], ['w'], lambda x: {'w': 1}))], 'a', 'w',
     {'sgn': 1, 'readset': ['w', 'th']})
L2 = M({'w': 'theta', 'th': 'theta'}, lambda s: 2 * s['w'] + s['th'], {'w': 1, 'th': 1})
case('边界 8 窗内先移后还：b 写 w := 2，a 再写 w := 1（W 起点值）', H, {'w': 1, 'th': 0},
     [((0, 1), E('b', 'Rw', 'port', 'b', [], ['w'], lambda x: {'w': 2})), ((0, 2), E('a', 'Rw', 'port', 'a', [], ['w'], lambda x: {'w': 1}))], 'a', 'w',
     {'sgn': 1, 'readset': ['w', 'th']})
case('T2 余项界：Φ = 2w + th，a: w 0 → 1，b: th 0 → −3/2（m = 2, L = 1）', L2, {'w': 0, 'th': 0},
     [((0, 1), E('a', 'Rw', 'port', 'a', [], ['w'], lambda x: {'w': 1})), ((0, 2), E('b', 'Rw', 'port', 'b', [], ['th'], lambda x: {'th': Fr(-3, 2)}))],
     'a', 'w', {'sgn': 1, 'slope': 2, 'lip': 1, 'readset': ['w', 'th']})
case('边界 5 余项界不满足：同上而 th → −2', L2, {'w': 0, 'th': 0},
     [((0, 1), E('a', 'Rw', 'port', 'a', [], ['w'], lambda x: {'w': 1})), ((0, 2), E('b', 'Rw', 'port', 'b', [], ['th'], lambda x: {'th': -2}))],
     'a', 'w', {'sgn': 1, 'slope': 2, 'lip': 1, 'readset': ['w', 'th']})
PR = M({'w': 'theta', 'u': 'theta'}, lambda s: s['w'] * s['u'], {'w': 1, 'u': 1}, dom={'w': ('pred', lambda x: x > 0, [Fr(k, 4) for k in range(1, 13)]), 'u': ('pred', lambda x: x > 0, [Fr(k, 4) for k in range(1, 13)])})
case('T3 同向：Φ = w·u（正域），a: w 1 → 2，b: u 1 → 3', PR, {'w': 1, 'u': 1},
     [((0, 1), E('a', 'Rw', 'port', 'a', [], ['w'], lambda x: {'w': 2})), ((0, 2), E('b', 'Rw', 'port', 'b', [], ['u'], lambda x: {'u': 3}))],
     'a', 'w', {'sgn': 1, 'mono_all': 1, 'readset': ['w', 'u']})
DP = M({'w': 'theta', 'th': 'theta', 'e': 'theta', 's': 'ext'}, lambda s: s['w'] - s['th'], {'w': 1, 'th': 1, 'e': 0},
       fm={'deepen': ('Sed', {'w'})}, ports={'Rw': {'w', 'th'}, 'In': {'s'}}, dom={'e': ('fin', {Fr(1, 10)}), 's': B01})
deep = lambda i: E(i, 'Sed', 'deepen', 'F', ['w', 'e', 's'], ['w'], lambda x: {'w': x['w'] + x['e'] * x['s'] * x['w']}, {'w': 1, 'e': 1, 's': 1})
case('T4 后继沉积不回退：a: w 1 → 2，其后输入与加深', DP, {'w': 1, 'th': 0, 'e': Fr(1, 10), 's': 0},
     [((0, 1), E('a', 'Rw', 'port', 'a', [], ['w'], lambda x: {'w': 2})), ((1, 1), E('i', 'In', 'port', 'env', [], ['s'], lambda x: {'s': 1})), ((1, 2), deep('d'))],
     'a', 'w', {'sgn': 1, 'readset': ['w', 'th']})
case('T4′ 负向位移后接加深：a: w 1 → −1/2，加深使 w 远离 0 而不跨回 1', DP, {'w': 1, 'th': 0, 'e': Fr(1, 10), 's': 0},
     [((0, 1), E('a', 'Rw', 'port', 'a', [], ['w'], lambda x: {'w': Fr(-1, 2)})), ((1, 1), E('i', 'In', 'port', 'env', [], ['s'], lambda x: {'s': 1})), ((1, 2), deep('d'))],
     'a', 'w', {'sgn': 1, 'readset': ['w', 'th']})
GL = M({'w1a': 'theta', 'w1b': 'theta', 'w2a': 'theta', 'w2b': 'theta', 'w3a': 'theta', 'm1': 'm', 'm2': 'm', 'm3': 'm'},
       lambda s: s['w1a'] * s['w1b'] * s['m1'] + s['w2a'] * s['w2b'] * s['m2'], {k: 1 for k in ('w1a', 'w1b', 'w2a', 'w2b', 'm1', 'm2')},
       ports={'Gt': {'m1', 'm2', 'm3'}}, dom={'m1': B01, 'm2': B01, 'm3': B01}, support={'m1': 'w1a', 'm2': 'w2a', 'm3': 'w3a'})
g0 = {'w1a': 1, 'w1b': 1, 'w2a': 1, 'w2b': 2, 'w3a': 1, 'm1': 1, 'm2': 0, 'm3': 0}
case('T5 门控：去抑 m2 0 → 1（路径系数 w2a·w2b = 2 > 0）', GL, g0, [((0, 1), E('a', 'Gt', 'port', 'a', [], ['m2'], lambda x: {'m2': 1}))], 'a', 'm2',
     {'sgn': 1, 'vertex': True, 'readset': ['m1', 'm2', 'w1a', 'w1b', 'w2a', 'w2b']}, kinds=('Gt',))
case('边界 7 门控落在读数路径外：m3 0 → 1', GL, g0, [((0, 1), E('a', 'Gt', 'port', 'a', [], ['m3'], lambda x: {'m3': 1}))], 'a', 'm3',
     {'sgn': None, 'readset': ['m1', 'm2', 'w1a', 'w1b', 'w2a', 'w2b']}, kinds=('Gt',))

# 补充例
E0 = M({'w': 'theta'}, lambda s: s['w'], {'w': 1}, dom={'w': ('pred', lambda x: True, [])})
case('边界 9 空探针序列：Φ = w，w 0 → 1，替代序列为空', E0, {'w': 0}, [((0, 1), E('a', 'Rw', 'port', 'a', [], ['w'], lambda x: {'w': 1}))], 'a', 'w',
     {'sgn': 1, 'readset': ['w']})
E1 = M({'w': 'theta'}, lambda s: s['w'], {'w': 1}, dom={'w': ('pred', lambda x: True, [Fr(0)])})
case('T6 入列探针（入口值 0）：同上，替代序列 [0]', E1, {'w': 0}, [((0, 1), E('a', 'Rw', 'port', 'a', [], ['w'], lambda x: {'w': 1}))], 'a', 'w',
     {'sgn': 1, 'readset': ['w']})
GC = M({'pa': 'theta', 'pb': 'theta', 'm': 'm'}, lambda s: s['pa'] * s['m'] + s['pb'] * s['m'], {'pa': 1, 'pb': 1, 'm': 1},
       ports={'Gt': {'m'}}, dom={'m': B01}, support={'m': 'pa'})
case('边界 10 两路相抵门控：Φ = 2m − 2m（单路系数 2 非零，总系数 0）', GC, {'pa': 2, 'pb': -2, 'm': 0},
     [((0, 1), E('a', 'Gt', 'port', 'a', [], ['m'], lambda x: {'m': 1}))], 'a', 'm', {'sgn': 1, 'vertex': True, 'readset': ['m', 'pa', 'pb']}, kinds=('Gt',))
GN = M({'m': 'm', 'p': 'theta'}, lambda s: (s['m'] - Fr(1, 2)) ** 2, {'m': 2, 'p': 0}, ports={'Gt': {'m'}},
       dom={'m': ('pred', lambda x: 0 <= x <= 1, [Fr(0)])}, support={'m': 'p'})
case('边界 11 非仿射门控：Φ = (m − 1/2)²，m 1/2 → 1，入列探针 0（端点差 1/4 ≠ 0 而探针差 0）', GN, {'m': Fr(1, 2), 'p': 1},
     [((0, 1), E('a', 'Gt', 'port', 'a', [], ['m'], lambda x: {'m': 1}))], 'a', 'm', {'sgn': 1, 'vertex': True, 'readset': ['m']}, kinds=('Gt',))
HZ = M({'w': 'theta', 'th': 'theta', 'z': 'theta'}, lambda s: s['w'] - s['th'], {'w': 1, 'th': 1, 'z': 0})
r = run(HZ, {'w': 1, 'th': 0, 'z': 0}, [((0, 1), E('a', 'Rw', 'port', 'a', [], ['z'], lambda x: {'z': 5})), ((0, 2), E('b', 'Rw', 'port', 'b', [], ['w'], lambda x: {'w': 3}))])
OUT['推论反向：a 只写读数无关变量 z，b 写 w 1 → 3'] = {**R(r, 'a', {'Rw'}, ((0, 0), r.trace[-1][0])), '说明': '净差非零而 Bear(a) 非 1：净差不推出承担'}

# 承担不是反事实必要性。Φ = w，w ∈ {0, 1, 2, 3}，入口 1；场规则 Sed：w := 3（w ≥ 1）或 0
CF = M({'w': 'theta'}, lambda s: s['w'], {'w': 1}, fm={'rule': ('Sed', {'w'})}, dom={'w': ('fin', {Fr(0), Fr(1), Fr(2), Fr(3)})})
rule = lambda i: E(i, 'Sed', 'rule', 'F', ['w'], ['w'], lambda x: {'w': Fr(3) if x['w'] >= 1 else Fr(0)})
case('T7 C3a ∧ C4b（场规则阈写）：a 写 w := 2，其后场规则写 3', CF, {'w': 1},
     [((0, 1), E('a', 'Rw', 'port', 'a', [], ['w'], lambda x: {'w': 2})), ((1, 1), rule('r'))], 'a', 'w', {'sgn': 1, 'readset': ['w']})
r0 = run(CF, {'w': 1}, [((1, 1), rule('r'))])
OUT['反事实对照：去掉 a 的直写，同一场规则与窗'] = {**R(r0, 'a', {'Rw'}, ((0, 0), r0.trace[-1][0])), '说明': '含 a 时 ΔΦ = 2、Bear_R(a) = 1；去掉 a 时 ΔΦ 仍为 2'}
# 离散域上导数同号不足以推出严格单调：合法域 {0, 1}，Φ(v) = v(v − 1)(2v − 1)
ph = lambda v: v * (v - 1) * (2 * v - 1); dph = lambda v: 6 * v * v - 6 * v + 1
OUT['离散域导数例 Φ = v(v − 1)(2v − 1)，域 {0, 1}'] = {'Φ(0)、Φ(1)': [str(ph(Fr(0))), str(ph(Fr(1)))], 'Φ′(0)、Φ′(1)': [str(dph(Fr(0))), str(dph(Fr(1)))]}
# 推论 5 前两例按正文的完整读数与替代序列
SQ2 = M({'v': 'theta'}, lambda s: s['v'] ** 2, {'v': 2}, dom={'v': ('pred', lambda x: True, [Fr(0), Fr(2)])})
r = run(SQ2, {'v': -1}, [((0, 1), E('a', 'Rw', 'port', 'a', [], ['v'], lambda x: {'v': 1}))])
OUT['推论 5·平方：Φ = v²，替代序列 {0, 2}，v −1 → 1'] = R(r, 'a', {'Rw'}, ((0, 0), r.trace[-1][0]))
RL2 = M({'v': 'theta'}, lambda s: max(Fr(0), s['v']), {'v': None}, dom={'v': ('pred', lambda x: True, [Fr(1)])})
r = run(RL2, {'v': -2}, [((0, 1), E('a', 'Rw', 'port', 'a', [], ['v'], lambda x: {'v': -1}))])
OUT['推论 5·ReLU：Φ = max(0, v)，替代序列 {1}，v −2 → −1'] = R(r, 'a', {'Rw'}, ((0, 0), r.trace[-1][0]))

OUT['总核'] = all(v['条件组成立 ⇒ R 成立'] for k, v in OUT.items() if isinstance(v, dict) and '条件组成立 ⇒ R 成立' in v)
json.dump(OUT, open(sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else 'ind_effect_checks.json', 'w'), ensure_ascii=False, indent=1, default=str)
print(json.dumps(OUT, ensure_ascii=False, indent=1, default=str))
~~~

【资源 016 起】源码　converge\_checks.py　SHA-256 4ff2b3ef16bdc021aa29a7182e9d0407752a3bba9d1d3b78d50553a864923eef

~~~python
"""调宿收束必然式 · 核算（纯 Python，精确分数，确定性）
- 直接递推：参数线性模型 y = θ·φ(x)、β = 0 的逐样本写回 θ ← θ − η(θ·φ(x) − g·x)φ(x)、周期采样；与 M_EDR 事件运行（edr_checks 的图与生成器）逐步对照。
- 五项区分（外部停机、一次零更新、协议止调、达标、对齐）的蕴含图与反例；「一次零更新而非协议止调」取 M_EDR 合法标签（y* = g·x）的例，并以事件运行核对。
- 首证子类的收束（场具化第二十定理 20-2 的前提）：插值极限、未触子空间留存、有限步到达与渐近收束之分。
- β = 0 时动量 v_k = q_k（非恒为 0）；完整运行状态 (θ_k, v_k) 收敛到 (θ∞, 0)。
- 非参数线性图上「残差非零而梯度为零」的例（y = w²·x，w = 0，g = 1）：「零写回 ⇒ 对齐」须参数线性与 φ(x) ≠ 0 等前件。
- 对齐而 ηβv ≠ 0 时写回而对齐保持；有限步到达的动量索引差；缺秩不可实现目标的周期截面。
- 边界：步长越界（带初始误差条件）、初始已对齐而 η 越界仍固定、不可实现目标的周期截面、η = 0、动量。
"""
import sys, json, os, runpy, tempfile, contextlib, io
from fractions import Fraction as Fr
HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)); sys.path.insert(0, HERE); ARGV = list(sys.argv)
with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp, contextlib.redirect_stdout(io.StringIO()):
    cwd = os.getcwd(); os.chdir(tmp); sys.argv = ['edr', os.path.join(tmp, 'o.json')]
    EDR = runpy.run_path(os.path.join(HERE, 'edr_checks.py')); os.chdir(cwd); sys.argv = ARGV
Graph, RunR, build, step_events, init_for, DATA = EDR['Graph'], EDR['RunR'], EDR['build'], EDR['step_events'], EDR['init_for'], EDR['DATA']


def dot(a, b): return sum(x * y for x, y in zip(a, b))


def rec(theta, feats, targets, order, eta, beta=Fr(0), K=40, v=None):
    """直接递推（M_EDR 第四节，β 可非零）：返回各步 (θ, 动量, 写回量是否为零)。"""
    th = list(theta); v = list(v or [Fr(0)] * len(th)); out = []
    for k in range(K):
        i = order(k); s = feats[i]; r = dot(th, s) - targets[i]
        q = [r * x for x in s]; v = [beta * a + b for a, b in zip(v, q)]
        new = [a - eta * b for a, b in zip(th, v)]
        out.append({'k': k + 1, 'i': i, 'theta': [str(x) for x in new], 'zero_write': new == th, 'loss': str(r * r / 2)})
        th = new
    return th, v, out


R = {}
cyc = lambda k: k % 2                        # M_EDR 采样表 π(k, 0) = k mod 2：x = 1, 2, 1, 2, ...
X = [DATA[0], DATA[1]]

# 一、与 M_EDR 事件运行逐步对照（G2lin：y = w1·x + w2·x²）
Glin = Graph(['w1', 'w2'], [('xin', 'in', ()), ('m1', 'mul', ('w1', 'xin')), ('s', 'sq', ('xin',)), ('m2', 'mul', ('w2', 's')), ('y', 'add', ('m1', 'm2'))], 'y')
g, eta = Fr(2), Fr(1, 16)
r = RunR(build(Glin), init_for(Glin, w1=0, w2=0, g=g), hold=('Xg', 'g'))
mEDR = []
for k in range(1, 7):
    for i, ev in enumerate(step_events(Glin, k, eta, Fr(0), 0, Fr(1, 2)), 1): r.ev((k, i), ev)
    mEDR.append([str(r.s['w1']), str(r.s['w2'])])
feats = [[x, x * x] for x in X]; tg = [g * x for x in X]
_, _, tr = rec([Fr(0), Fr(0)], feats, tg, lambda k: (k + 1) % 2, eta, K=6)     # M_EDR 第 k 步取 idx = k mod 2，k 自 1 起
R['一 递推与 M_EDR 事件运行一致（前 6 步）'] = [t['theta'] for t in tr] == mEDR

# 二、首证子类的收束：特征张满（唯一插值点 θ* = (g, 0)）
th, _, tr = rec([Fr(0), Fr(0)], feats, tg, cyc, eta, K=60)
# 一周期（两相位）的仿射映射 θ ← Aθ + c：A = (I − η s₂s₂ᵀ)(I − η s₁s₁ᵀ)
def P(s): return [[(1 if i == j else 0) - eta * s[i] * s[j] for j in range(2)] for i in range(2)]
def mm(A, B): return [[sum(A[i][k] * B[k][j] for k in range(2)) for j in range(2)] for i in range(2)]
A = mm(P(feats[1]), P(feats[0])); tr_A = A[0][0] + A[1][1]; det_A = A[0][0] * A[1][1] - A[0][1] * A[1][0]
dist = [max(abs(Fr(t['theta'][0]) - g), abs(Fr(t['theta'][1]))) for t in tr]
R['二 张满特征'] = {'步长条件 η‖φ‖²': [str(eta * dot(s, s)) for s in feats], '周期映射 A 的迹、行列式': [str(tr_A), str(det_A)],
                  '谱半径 < 1（|det| < 1 且 |tr| < 1 + det）': abs(det_A) < 1 and abs(tr_A) < 1 + det_A,
                  '插值点 (g, 0) 是周期映射的不动点': rec([g, Fr(0)], feats, tg, cyc, eta, K=2)[0] == [g, Fr(0)],
                  '与插值点的距离（第 10、30、60 步）': [float(dist[9]), float(dist[29]), float(dist[59])],
                  '每一步都有写回（原轨迹不精确到达）': all(not t['zero_write'] for t in tr)}
# 极限态继续执行：零写回（协议止调）
_, _, tr2 = rec([g, Fr(0)], feats, tg, cyc, eta, K=6)
R['二 极限态（对齐）继续执行'] = all(t['zero_write'] for t in tr2)

# 三、未触子空间留存：y = w1·x + w2·x（特征 (x, x)，V = span(1,1)）
f2 = [[x, x] for x in X]
th, _, _ = rec([Fr(1), Fr(-1)], f2, tg, cyc, Fr(1, 16), K=400)
R['三 未触子空间'] = {'w1 − w2（V⊥ 分量，初值 2）': str(th[0] - th[1]), 'w1 + w2 → g': abs(th[0] + th[1] - g) < Fr(1, 10 ** 6),
                    '极限由初值决定（两初值对照）': str(rec([Fr(3), Fr(-3)], f2, tg, cyc, Fr(1, 16), K=400)[0][0] - rec([Fr(3), Fr(-3)], f2, tg, cyc, Fr(1, 16), K=400)[0][1])}

# 四、渐近收束与有限步到达
_, _, tr = rec([Fr(0)], [[Fr(1)]], [Fr(1)], lambda k: 0, Fr(1, 2), K=8)
R['四 θ₀ = 0、目标 1、η = 1/2'] = {'θ_k': [t['theta'][0] for t in tr], '无一步零写回': all(not t['zero_write'] for t in tr)}
_, _, tr = rec([Fr(0)], [[Fr(1)]], [Fr(1)], lambda k: 0, Fr(1), K=3)
R['四 η‖φ‖² = 1 单模式：一步到达'] = [(t['theta'][0], t['zero_write']) for t in tr]

# 五、步长越界
_, _, tr = rec([Fr(0)], [[x] for x in X], tg, cyc, Fr(3, 2), K=8)
R['五 η = 3/2（η·x²：3/2 与 6；周期因子 (1 − 3/2)(1 − 6) = 5/2）、初始误差非零：发散'] = [t['theta'][0] for t in tr]
_, _, tr = rec([Fr(0)], [[x] for x in X], tg, cyc, Fr(3, 4), K=12)
R['五 η = 3/4（x = 2 的模式越界，周期因子 −1/2）：仍收束——越界不推出不收束'] = [t['theta'][0] for t in tr][-3:]
_, _, tr = rec([g], [[x] for x in X], tg, cyc, Fr(3), K=4)
R['五 初始已对齐、η = 3'] = [(t['theta'][0], t['zero_write']) for t in tr]

# 六、不可实现目标：y = w·x²，标签 g·x（1 处要 w = g，2 处要 w = g/2）
fn = [[x * x] for x in X]
th, _, tr = rec([Fr(0)], fn, tg, cyc, Fr(1, 16), K=200)
# 两相位周期截面的精确不动点：w ← w − η(w − g)；w ← w − η·4(4w − 2g)
a1, b1 = 1 - Fr(1, 16), Fr(1, 16) * g; a2, b2 = 1 - Fr(1, 16) * 16, Fr(1, 16) * 4 * 2 * g
p_after2 = (a2 * b1 + b2) / (1 - a2 * a1); p_after1 = a1 * p_after2 + b1
R['六 不可实现目标'] = {'周期截面（第 1 相位后，第 2 相位后）': [str(p_after1), str(p_after2)], '轨迹逼近截面': abs(th[0] - p_after2) < Fr(1, 10 ** 6),
                     '截面上仍逐步写回（不止调）': p_after1 != p_after2}

# 七、五项区分的反例
_, _, tr = rec([Fr(0)], [[x] for x in X], tg, cyc, Fr(0), K=3)
R['七 η = 0：协议止调而不对齐'] = {'零写回': all(t['zero_write'] for t in tr), '对齐': False}
th, _, tr = rec([Fr(1), Fr(0)], [[Fr(1), Fr(1)], [Fr(2), Fr(4)]], [Fr(1), Fr(4)], cyc, Fr(1, 32), K=2)
R['七 一次零更新而非协议止调'] = {'第 1 步零写回': tr[0]['zero_write'], '第 2 步零写回': tr[1]['zero_write']}
_, _, tr = rec([Fr(3, 2)], [[x] for x in X], tg, cyc, Fr(1, 8), K=1)
R['七 达标而不对齐（ε = 1/2）'] = {'第 1 步损失': tr[0]['loss'], '≤ ε': Fr(tr[0]['loss']) <= Fr(1, 2), '对齐': False}
th, v, tr = rec([g], [[x] for x in X], tg, cyc, Fr(1, 8), beta=Fr(1, 2), K=2, v=[Fr(2)])
R['七 对齐而有动量：仍写回（对齐 ⇏ 协议止调，β > 0）'] = [(t['theta'][0], t['zero_write']) for t in tr]

# 八、基线探针：同特征交替标签（不一致外标）的周期截面；η = 3 下初始已对齐固定
_, _, tr = rec([Fr(2, 3)], [[Fr(1)], [Fr(1)]], [Fr(0), Fr(1)], cyc, Fr(1, 2), K=4)
R['八 不一致外标（标签 0、1 交替，η = 1/2，入口 2/3）'] = {'各步 θ': [t['theta'][0] for t in tr], '周期截面 {1/3, 2/3}，不止调': all(not t['zero_write'] for t in tr)}

# 九、零写回 ⇏ 对齐（非参数线性）：y = w²·x，w = 0，g = 1；M_EDR 事件运行一步
Gsq = Graph(['w'], [('xin', 'in', ()), ('s', 'sq', ('w',)), ('y', 'mul', ('s', 'xin'))], 'y')
rq = RunR(build(Gsq), init_for(Gsq, w=0, g=1), hold=('Xg', 'g'))
for i, ev in enumerate(step_events(Gsq, 1, Fr(1, 8), Fr(0), 0, Fr(1, 2)), 1): rq.ev((1, i), ev)
R['九 y = w²·x，w = 0，g = 1：残差非零而零写回'] = {'x': str(rq.s['x']), '残差 y − g·x': str(rq.s['d_y']), '梯度 q_w': str(rq.s['d_w']), 'w（写回后）': str(rq.s['w'])}

# 十、β = 0 时动量 v_k = q_k；完整运行状态收敛到 (θ∞, 0)
_, v1, tr1 = rec([Fr(1)], [[Fr(1)]], [Fr(2)], lambda k: 0, Fr(1), K=1)
vs = []
for k in range(1, 9):
    _, vk, _ = rec([Fr(0)], [[Fr(1)]], [Fr(1)], lambda k: 0, Fr(1, 2), K=k); vs.append(str(vk[0]))
R['十 β = 0 的动量'] = {'w₀ = 1、g = 2、x = 1、η = 1：首步 v_w（≠ 0）': str(v1[0]), '首步 w': tr1[0]['theta'][0],
                     'θ₀ = 0、目标 1、η = 1/2：v_k = q_k（k = 1…8）→ 0': vs}

# 十一、一次零更新而非协议止调（M_EDR 合法标签 y* = g·x）：y = w1·x + w2·x²，g = 1，入口 (2, −1)，η = 1/32，种子 s = 1（首样本 x = 1）
r11 = RunR(build(Glin), init_for(Glin, w1=2, w2=-1, g=1), hold=('Xg', 'g')); rows = []
for k in (1, 2):
    before = (r11.s['w1'], r11.s['w2'])
    for i, ev in enumerate(step_events(Glin, k, Fr(1, 32), Fr(0), 1, Fr(1, 2)), 1): r11.ev((k, i), ev)
    rows.append({'k': k, 'x': str(r11.s['x']), '残差': str(r11.s['d_y']), '零写回': (r11.s['w1'], r11.s['w2']) == before})
R['十一 一次零更新而非协议止调（M_EDR 事件运行）'] = rows

# 十二、对齐、写回、离开对齐三分：y = (w1 + w2)·x，g = 2，θ = (1, 1)，v = (1, −1)，η = 1/2
f12 = [[Fr(1), Fr(1)], [Fr(2), Fr(2)]]; t12 = [Fr(2), Fr(4)]
thb, vb, trb = rec([Fr(1), Fr(1)], f12, t12, cyc, Fr(1, 2), beta=Fr(1, 2), K=1, v=[Fr(1), Fr(-1)])
th0, v0, tr0 = rec([Fr(1), Fr(1)], f12, t12, cyc, Fr(1, 2), beta=Fr(0), K=1, v=[Fr(1), Fr(-1)])
R['十二 对齐而有动量'] = {'β = 1/2：下一步 θ': [str(x) for x in thb], 'w1 + w2（对齐保持）': str(sum(thb)), 'β = 0：下一步 θ、v': [[str(x) for x in th0], [str(x) for x in v0]]}
# 十三、有限步到达的动量索引差：y = w·x，x = 1，g = 2，w₀ = 1，η = 1，β = 0
s1 = rec([Fr(1)], [[Fr(1)]], [Fr(2)], lambda k: 0, Fr(1), K=1); s2 = rec([Fr(1)], [[Fr(1)]], [Fr(2)], lambda k: 0, Fr(1), K=2)
R['十三 首次到达与动量归零'] = {'第 1 步后 (w, v)': [str(s1[0][0]), str(s1[1][0])], '第 2 步后 (w, v)': [str(s2[0][0]), str(s2[1][0])], '第 2 步零写回': s2[2][1]['zero_write']}
# 十四、缺秩不可实现：y = (w1 + w2)·x²，标签 2x，D = {1, 2}，η = 1/32，入口 (1, 0)
f14 = [[Fr(1), Fr(1)], [Fr(4), Fr(4)]]; th14, _, tr14 = rec([Fr(1), Fr(0)], f14, [Fr(2), Fr(4)], cyc, Fr(1, 32), K=4)
R['十四 缺秩不可实现'] = {'各步 θ': [t['theta'] for t in tr14], '各步 w1 + w2': [str(sum(Fr(x) for x in t['theta'])) for t in tr14],
                      'w1 − w2 保持': all(Fr(t['theta'][0]) - Fr(t['theta'][1]) == 1 for t in tr14), '每步写回': all(not t['zero_write'] for t in tr14)}

json.dump(R, open(sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else os.path.join(HERE, 'converge_checks.json'), 'w'), ensure_ascii=False, indent=1, default=str)
print(json.dumps(R, ensure_ascii=False, indent=1, default=str))
~~~

【资源 018 起】源码　obs\_inverse\_checks.py　SHA-256 85b96a3e15c9801438500092b6c7a4a003a4fdd9ca16d0919770e70ba0ab634f

~~~python
"""调宿观测反演式 · 核算（纯 Python，精确分数，确定性）
- 在严格 M_EDR 事件运行（edr_checks 的图、生成器、RunR 与 goalcond）上实现四个观测协议，分别读出：
  O1：每步 (y_k, ℓ_k)；O2-梯度：O1 + 每步梯度 q_k；O2-更新：O1 + 每步写回量 η·v_k；O3：O1 + 申报时刻（每步末）的参数坐标。
- 同纤维检验：在申报的有限候选格上逐一运行同一前向域，收集与真值观测相同者（纤维 ∩ 格），与解析纤维对照。
- 目标量：目标数值 g（单参数 y = w·x）；参数 θ（两层 y = w2·w1·x）；来源结构（持有锚／窗内设定／未登记）。
- O1 对称轨道的 1、2、4 点纤维与乘积零集；O3 奇异写回 c = ±1 的重根单点与非重根两点；纯末态协议 O末 与含全程的 O3 分列；
  目标设定事件在运行窗 (0, 1) 处、首个样本观测之前。
- 固定探针域上持有锚变体 GoalCond 恒为确定值（0 或 1）的逐运行核验；空目标探针例（域外，两支均 ⊥）；O末 见证取 η = 1 已知、入口隐藏。
- 有限目标域上反射根不合法、纤维取交；列表在而合法探针为零（g ≥ 2）的域外例；η = 1/2 时纯末态唯一反演入口而 Bear_R = 1；
  η = 1/2 配置下 O末 对 Bear_R 全域可辨（w₁ = 1 + w₀/2，Bear_R = 1 ⟺ w₀ ≠ 2）的格上逐点核对。
- 纯末态两配置固定 w 的读数探针（19 点、deg_Φ(w) = 1）；w 替代序列为空的对照（Bear_R 为 ⊥）。
- 基线探针（g = ±1、η = 0）。
"""
import sys, json, os, runpy, tempfile, contextlib, io, math
from fractions import Fraction as Fr
HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)); sys.path.insert(0, HERE); ARGV = list(sys.argv)
with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp, contextlib.redirect_stdout(io.StringIO()):
    cwd = os.getcwd(); os.chdir(tmp); sys.argv = ['edr', os.path.join(tmp, 'o.json')]
    EDR = runpy.run_path(os.path.join(HERE, 'edr_checks.py')); os.chdir(cwd); sys.argv = ARGV
Graph, RunR, build, step_events, init_for, E, goalcond, G2 = (EDR[k] for k in ('Graph', 'RunR', 'build', 'step_events', 'init_for', 'E', 'goalcond', 'G2'))
C = EDR['C']

G1 = Graph(['w'], [('xin', 'in', ()), ('y', 'mul', ('w', 'xin'))], 'y')       # y = w·x


def run(G, theta, g, K, eta, beta=Fr(0), seed=0, mode='hold', v0=None, empty_g=False, gdom=None, empty_w=False):
    """严格 M_EDR 运行；mode：hold（入口持有锚）、set（入口 g = 0，运行窗 (0, 1) 处 ext_goal 设定，在首个样本观测之前）、none（未登记）。返回 (run, 逐步观测)。"""
    init = init_for(G, **{p: Fr(t) for p, t in zip(G.params, theta)}, g=(Fr(0) if mode == 'set' else Fr(g)),
                    **{'v_' + p: Fr(t) for p, t in zip(G.params, v0 or [])})
    model = build(G)
    if empty_g: model.dom['g'] = ('pred', lambda x: True, [])          # 域外：目标 g 的替代序列置空
    if gdom is not None: model.dom['g'] = gdom
    if empty_w: model.dom['w'] = ('pred', lambda x: True, [])
    r = RunR(model, init, hold=(('Xg', 'g') if mode == 'hold' else None))
    if mode == 'set':
        r.ev((0, 1), E('G', 'Run', 'ext_goal', 'Xg', [], ['g'], lambda v: {'g': Fr(g)}, {}))
    obs = []
    for k in range(1, K + 1):
        for i, ev in enumerate(step_events(G, k, Fr(eta), Fr(beta), seed, Fr(1, 2)), 1): r.ev((k, i), ev)
        obs.append({'x': r.s['x'], 'y': r.s[G.out], 'loss': r.s['loss'], 'q': tuple(r.s['d_' + p] for p in G.params),
                    'upd': tuple(Fr(eta) * r.s['v_' + p] for p in G.params), 'theta': tuple(r.s[p] for p in G.params)})
    return r, obs


PROTO = {'O1': ('y', 'loss'), 'O2-梯度': ('y', 'loss', 'q'), 'O2-更新': ('y', 'loss', 'upd'), 'O3': ('y', 'loss', 'theta')}


def view(obs, proto): return tuple(tuple(o[f] for f in PROTO[proto]) for o in obs)


def fibre(G, true_kw, cand_kw_list, proto, **kw):
    """同纤维检验：候选格中与真值观测完全相同者。"""
    ref = view(run(G, **true_kw, **kw)[1], proto)
    return [c for c in cand_kw_list if view(run(G, **c, **kw)[1], proto) == ref]


R = {}
S = lambda xs: [str(x) if not isinstance(x, (tuple, list)) else [str(y) for y in x] for x in xs]

# 〇、基线探针：x = 1、θ = 0、η = 0，g = ±1
p = run(G1, [0], 1, 1, 0, seed=1)[1]; n = run(G1, [0], -1, 1, 0, seed=1)[1]
R['〇 g = ±1 探针（x = 1、θ = 0、η = 0）'] = {'x': str(p[0]['x']),
    **{pr: view(p, pr) == view(n, pr) for pr in PROTO}, 'q（g = 1，g = −1）': [str(p[0]['q'][0]), str(n[0]['q'][0])]}

# 一、目标数值 g（单参数 y = w·x；已知：图、θ₀、采样表、η、β、初动量；隐藏：g）
GRID = [Fr(k, 2) for k in range(-8, 9)]
cand = lambda: [{'theta': [Fr(1, 2)], 'g': c} for c in GRID]
true = {'theta': [Fr(1, 2)], 'g': Fr(2)}
gs = lambda L: [str(c['g']) for c in L]
def sqrt_fr(q):
    a, b = q.numerator, q.denominator; ra, rb = math.isqrt(a), math.isqrt(b)
    return Fr(ra, rb) if ra * ra == a and rb * rb == b else None
# 解析候选：每步 |y − g·x| = √(2ℓ)，g ∈ {(y ± √(2ℓ))/x}
o = run(G1, **true, K=2, eta=Fr(1, 8))[1]
per = [sorted({(t['y'] + s * sqrt_fr(2 * t['loss'])) / t['x'] for s in (1, -1)}) for t in o]
R['一 g 的逐步候选（θ₀ = 1/2、η = 1/8、真值 g = 2）'] = {'第 1 步': S(per[0]), '第 2 步': S(per[1]), '交集': S(sorted(set(per[0]) & set(per[1])))}
tab = {}
for K, eta in ((1, Fr(1, 8)), (2, Fr(1, 8)), (3, Fr(0))):
    tab[f'K = {K}、η = {eta}'] = {pr: gs(fibre(G1, true, cand(), pr, K=K, eta=eta)) for pr in PROTO}
R['一 g 的纤维 ∩ 候选格 {−4, −7/2, …, 4}'] = tab
# η = 0 时 O1 纤维 = {g, 2θ₀ − g}（关于 θ₀ 的反射）
R['一 η = 0、O1 纤维为关于 θ₀ 的反射对'] = all(set(gs(fibre(G1, {'theta': [Fr(1, 2)], 'g': c}, cand(), 'O1', K=3, eta=Fr(0)))) ==
                                        {str(c), str(1 - c)} & set(gs(cand())) for c in GRID)
# 已对齐（g = θ₀）：q = 0、写回为 0，纤维由损失决定（ℓ = 0 ⇒ g·x = θ₀·x）
R['一 已对齐时各协议的纤维'] = {pr: gs(fibre(G1, {'theta': [Fr(1, 2)], 'g': Fr(1, 2)}, cand(), pr, K=2, eta=Fr(1, 8))) for pr in PROTO}

# 二、参数 θ（两层 y = w2·(w1·x)；已知：g、采样表、η、β、初动量；隐藏：θ₀）
PG = sorted({s * Fr(a) for a in ('0', '1/4', '1/2', '1', '2', '4') for s in (1, -1)})
pc = [{'theta': [a, b], 'g': Fr(2)} for a in PG for b in PG]
tt = {'theta': [Fr(1), Fr(1, 2)], 'g': Fr(2)}
th = lambda L: sorted('(%s, %s)' % (str(c['theta'][0]), str(c['theta'][1])) for c in L)
tab = {}
for K, eta in ((1, Fr(1, 8)), (2, Fr(1, 8)), (2, Fr(0))):
    tab[f'K = {K}、η = {eta}'] = {pr: th(fibre(G2, tt, pc, pr, K=K, eta=eta)) for pr in PROTO}
R['二 θ₀ 的纤维 ∩ 候选格（真值 (1, 1/2)）'] = tab
# 解析：K ≥ 2、η > 0、δ₁ ≠ 0、x ≠ 0 时 O1 读出 P = w1·w2 与 S = w1² + w2²，纤维 = {±(w1, w2), ±(w2, w1)}
orbit = sorted({'(%s, %s)' % (str(s * a), str(s * b)) for a, b in ((Fr(1), Fr(1, 2)), (Fr(1, 2), Fr(1))) for s in (1, -1)})
R['二 解析纤维（交换 × 联合变号）'] = {'轨道': orbit, '与格上 O1 纤维相同': orbit == tab['K = 2、η = 1/8']['O1']}
# O3 只读每步末坐标：θ₁ = [[1, −c], [−c, 1]]θ₀，c = η·δ₁·x₁（δ₁ 由 O1 的 P = y₁/x₁ 已知）；c² = 1 时退化
# x₁ = 2、P = 1/2、g = 2：δ₁ = −3，c = −6η；η = 1/6 给 c = −1
R['二 O3（每步末坐标）在 c = −1 时的纤维（η = 1/6）'] = th(fibre(G2, tt, pc, 'O3', K=2, eta=Fr(1, 6)))
# 重标度 (c·w1, w2/c) 保持前向函数，不保持多步轨迹
r1 = run(G2, [Fr(1), Fr(1, 2)], 2, 2, Fr(1, 8))[1]; r2 = run(G2, [Fr(2), Fr(1, 4)], 2, 2, Fr(1, 8))[1]
R['二 重标度 (1, 1/2) → (2, 1/4)'] = {'第 1 步 y 相同': r1[0]['y'] == r2[0]['y'], '第 2 步 y': S([r1[1]['y'], r2[1]['y']]),
                                    '第 1 步梯度': [S(r1[0]['q']), S(r2[0]['q'])]}
# 已对齐（δ₁ = 0）：O1、O2 不能越过双曲线
ta = {'theta': [Fr(2), Fr(1)], 'g': Fr(2)}
R['二 已对齐入口 (2, 1)：纤维'] = {pr: th(fibre(G2, ta, pc, pr, K=2, eta=Fr(1, 8))) for pr in PROTO}

# 三、来源结构：持有锚／窗内设定／未登记，数值同为 g = 2
runs = {m: run(G2, [Fr(1), Fr(1, 2)], 2, 2, Fr(1, 8), mode=m) for m in ('hold', 'set', 'none')}
R['三 来源结构对照'] = {
    '四协议观测相同': {pr: len({view(o, pr) for _, o in runs.values()}) == 1 for pr in PROTO},
    '目标来源登记': {m: sorted(v['type'] for v in r.goal_src.values()) for m, (r, _) in runs.items()},
    'GoalCond': {m: goalcond(r, 'a')[0] for m, (r, _) in runs.items()},
    'Bear_R(a)': {m: C.bear(r, 'a', {'Rw'})[0] for m, (r, _) in runs.items()}}

# 四、残余：低损失 ⇏ 对齐；末态 ⇏ 承担（末态相同、承担不同）；梯度存在 ⇏ 目标决定
lo = run(G1, [Fr(3, 2)], 2, 1, Fr(0), seed=1)[1]
R['四 低损失不对齐'] = {'ℓ': str(lo[0]['loss']), '≤ 1/2': lo[0]['loss'] <= Fr(1, 2), 'w·1 = g·1': lo[0]['y'] == 2}
ra, oa = run(G1, [Fr(2)], 2, 1, Fr(1, 8), seed=1)        # 已对齐：零更新
rb, ob = run(G1, [Fr(1)], 2, 1, Fr(1), seed=1)           # η‖φ‖² = 1：一步到达 w = 2
R['四 末态相同、承担不同'] = {'末态': [S(oa[-1]['theta']), S(ob[-1]['theta'])], 'Rewrite(a)': [C.eff_label(ra, 'a', 'Rw'), C.eff_label(rb, 'a', 'Rw')],
                          'Bear_R(a)': [C.bear(ra, 'a', {'Rw'})[0], C.bear(rb, 'a', {'Rw'})[0]]}

# O1 轨道纤维的点数（K = 2、η = 1/8、x₁ = 2、g = 2）与乘积零集
cand_g = lambda g: [{'theta': [a, b], 'g': g} for a in PG for b in PG]
R['二 O1 轨道纤维点数（K = 2、η = 1/8）'] = {'(%s, %s)' % tuple(map(str, t)): th(fibre(G2, {'theta': list(t), 'g': Fr(2)}, cand_g(Fr(2)), 'O1', K=2, eta=Fr(1, 8)))
                                         for t in ((Fr(0), Fr(0)), (Fr(1), Fr(1)), (Fr(1), Fr(1, 2)))}
z = th(fibre(G2, {'theta': [Fr(0), Fr(1)], 'g': Fr(2)}, cand_g(Fr(2)), 'O1', K=1, eta=Fr(1, 8)))
R['二 P = 0、K = 1：O1 纤维为坐标轴之并 ∩ 格'] = {'纤维点数': len(z), '等于 {w₁w₂ = 0} ∩ 格': z == th([c for c in cand_g(Fr(2)) if c['theta'][0] * c['theta'][1] == 0])}
# O3（每步末）奇异写回：c = −1 取和 A，判别式 (w₁ − w₂)²；c = +1 取差 B，判别式 (w₁ + w₂)²
R['二 O3 奇异写回'] = {
 'c = −1（g = 2、x₁ = 2、η = 1/4）入口 (1, 1)：重根': th(fibre(G2, {'theta': [Fr(1), Fr(1)], 'g': Fr(2)}, cand_g(Fr(2)), 'O3', K=2, eta=Fr(1, 4))),
 'c = −1（η = 1/6）入口 (1, 1/2)：两点': th(fibre(G2, tt, pc, 'O3', K=2, eta=Fr(1, 6))),
 'c = +1（g = −2、x₁ = 1、η = 1）入口 (1, −1)：重根': th(fibre(G2, {'theta': [Fr(1), Fr(-1)], 'g': Fr(-2)}, cand_g(Fr(-2)), 'O3', K=2, eta=Fr(1), seed=1)),
 'c = +1（同上）入口 (2, −1/2)：两点': th(fibre(G2, {'theta': [Fr(2), Fr(-1, 2)], 'g': Fr(-2)}, cand_g(Fr(-2)), 'O3', K=2, eta=Fr(1), seed=1))}
# 纯末态协议 O末：只读窗末参数坐标（与含全程 O1 的 O3 分列）
R['四 O末 与 O1 对照（末态相同、承担不同）'] = {'O末 相同': oa[-1]['theta'] == ob[-1]['theta'], 'O1 相同': view(oa, 'O1') == view(ob, 'O1'),
                                         '前像条件': 'y = w·x，g = 2，x = 1（seed = 1），K = 1；入口 2、η = 1/8 与入口 1、η = 1'}

# 固定探针域 𝒟（build 给定的替代序列与各事件申报次数）上，持有锚变体的 GoalCond 逐运行为确定值
det = []
for c in pc:
    for K, eta in ((1, Fr(1, 8)), (2, Fr(1, 8)), (2, Fr(0))):
        det.append(goalcond(run(G2, **c, K=K, eta=eta)[0], 'a')[0])
for c in cand():
    for K, eta in ((1, Fr(1, 8)), (2, Fr(1, 8)), (3, Fr(0))):
        det.append(goalcond(run(G1, **c, K=K, eta=eta)[0], 'a')[0])
R['三 冻结探针域：持有锚 GoalCond 逐运行确定'] = {'运行数': len(det), '取值分布': {str(k): det.count(k) for k in (True, False, None)},
    '未登记变体（同一组运行）恒 ⊥': all(goalcond(run(G2, **c, K=2, eta=Fr(1, 8), mode='none')[0], 'a')[0] is None for c in pc)}
rh = run(G1, [Fr(1)], 2, 1, Fr(1), seed=1, empty_g=True); rn = run(G1, [Fr(1)], 2, 1, Fr(1), seed=1, mode='none', empty_g=True)
R['三 域外：目标 g 替代序列为空'] = {'GoalCond（持有锚，未登记）': [goalcond(rh[0], 'a')[0], goalcond(rn[0], 'a')[0]], '观测相同': view(rh[1], 'O3') == view(rn[1], 'O3')}
# O末 见证（η = 1 已知、入口隐藏）：y = w·x，g = 2，x = 1，K = 1
w1r, w1o = run(G1, [Fr(1)], 2, 1, Fr(1), seed=1); w2r, w2o = run(G1, [Fr(2)], 2, 1, Fr(1), seed=1)
R['四 O末 见证（η = 1，入口 1 与 2）'] = {'末态': [S(w1o[-1]['theta']), S(w2o[-1]['theta'])], 'Bear_R(a)': [C.bear(w1r, 'a', {'Rw'})[0], C.bear(w2r, 'a', {'Rw'})[0]]}

# g 的合法域 {0, 2}（窄域）：y = w·x，x = 1，η = 0，w₀ = 1/2，K = 1；反射根 −1 不合法，合法值 0 观测不同
fin = ('fin', {Fr(0), Fr(2)})
o_t = run(G1, [Fr(1, 2)], 2, 1, Fr(0), seed=1, gdom=fin)[1]; o_0 = run(G1, [Fr(1, 2)], 0, 1, Fr(0), seed=1, gdom=fin)[1]
o_r = run(G1, [Fr(1, 2)], -1, 1, Fr(0), seed=1)[1]
R['五 窄目标域：纤维取交'] = {'g = 2 的 O1': S(view(o_t, 'O1')[0]), 'g = 0 的 O1 相同': view(o_0, 'O1') == view(o_t, 'O1'),
                         '反射根 −1 在开放域中 O1 相同': view(o_r, 'O1') == view(o_t, 'O1'), '−1 在窄域合法': Fr(-1) in fin[1]}
# 同一 19 点列表，g 的谓词域 g ≥ 2：合法探针为零，两支 ⊥
grid = [Fr(k, 7) for k in range(-9, 10)]; ge2 = ('pred', lambda x: x >= 2, grid)
rh2 = run(G1, [Fr(1)], 2, 1, Fr(1), seed=1, gdom=ge2); rn2 = run(G1, [Fr(1)], 2, 1, Fr(1), seed=1, mode='none', gdom=ge2)
R['五 列表在而合法探针为零（g ≥ 2）'] = {'合法替代值数': len(rh2[0].M.probes('g', Fr(2), 1)[0]), 'GoalCond（持有锚，未登记）': [goalcond(rh2[0], 'a')[0], goalcond(rn2[0], 'a')[0]],
                                    '观测相同': view(rh2[1], 'O3') == view(rn2[1], 'O3')}
# η = 1/2，g = 2，x = 1，K = 1：窗末 w₁ = 1 唯一反演 w₀ = 0，Bear_R = 1
cands = [c for c in GRID if run(G1, [c], 2, 1, Fr(1, 2), seed=1)[1][-1]['theta'] == (Fr(1),)]
rb = run(G1, [Fr(0)], 2, 1, Fr(1, 2), seed=1)[0]
R['五 O末 可辨的观测值（η = 1/2）'] = {'格上入口纤维': [str(c) for c in cands], 'Bear_R(a)': C.bear(rb, 'a', {'Rw'})[0]}

# η = 1/2 配置（y = w·x，x = 1，g = 2，K = 1）：O末 唯一定 w₀，且 Bear_R = 1 ⟺ w₀ ≠ 2
rows, ok = [], True
for c in GRID:
    rr, oo = run(G1, [c], 2, 1, Fr(1, 2), seed=1)
    b = C.bear(rr, 'a', {'Rw'})[0]
    ok = ok and oo[-1]['theta'][0] == 1 + c / 2 and b is (c != 2)
R['五 η = 1/2 配置：O末 全域可辨'] = {'格上逐点 w₁ = 1 + w₀/2 且 Bear_R = 1 ⟺ w₀ ≠ 2': ok, '格点数': len(GRID)}

# 纯末态两配置的读数探针。冻结：w 的替代序列为 19 点 {k/7}，deg_Φ(w) = 1；对照：替代序列为空
pr = {}
for eta in (Fr(1), Fr(1, 2)):
    a, oa_ = run(G1, [Fr(1)], 2, 1, eta, seed=1); b, ob_ = run(G1, [Fr(1)], 2, 1, eta, seed=1, empty_w=True)
    pr[str(eta)] = {'末态': [S(oa_[-1]['theta']), S(ob_[-1]['theta'])], 'Bear_R（19 点，空探针）': [C.bear(a, 'a', {'Rw'})[0], C.bear(b, 'a', {'Rw'})[0]],
                    'w 的读数次数': build(G1).phi_deg['w'], '19 点规范化后对末态 w 的合法替代值数': len(build(G1).probes('w', oa_[-1]['theta'][0], 19)[0])}
R['五 纯末态配置的读数探针'] = pr

json.dump(R, open(sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else os.path.join(HERE, 'obs_inverse_checks.json'), 'w'), ensure_ascii=False, indent=1, default=str)
print(json.dumps(R, ensure_ascii=False, indent=1, default=str))
~~~

~~~json
  "观测相同": true
 },
 "四 O末 见证（η = 1，入口 1 与 2）": {

    "2"

 },
 "五 窄目标域：纤维取交": {

 },
 "五 列表在而合法探针为零（g ≥ 2）": {

 },

 },
 "五 η = 1/2 配置：O末 全域可辨": {

 },
~~~
