【资源 020 起】源码　mode\_judge\_checks.py　SHA-256 baff29b12a7aa155048f3f872c7eca0e466f0cb602c3b1ea5163d2c8108c15b6

~~~python
"""势引作用方式判定式 · 核算（纯 Python，精确分数，确定性）
- 观测函数显式化：obs(r, V) 只输出该权限级可见的字段（含事件标识与来源标识的同一关联）；全部判词只读观测与判定实例 χ，不读运行对象。
- 判定实例 χ = (施作 part, W, W 入口相位)；各判词统一按 χ 窗化。
- 场自身规则分项的归属由模型显式申报（M.field_rules，F_M 场自身分项集合），观测从申报读取，不以分项名的前缀或字面名判归属。
- L1a 分列：Target 登记查询（接收明确的目标变量，只读登记表）、P6 形式值（GoalCond^f）、结构判词（1 读回；否定只按条件化规则 H18-1）；V2 只在持有锚子域可算 P6。
  P6 的缺省层集为 {θ, m, r}；显式层过滤只接受其子集，含 ext 等未在观测内的层时拒绝（未申报投影），不给形式值；过滤区分「未提供」与「申报空集」（种类与层两维）。
- L1b 分列：P1、P3 的可排除（V0，带接入完整与 B2 条件）与 Target 查询排除。
- L2 只对可见层（θ、m、r）作实际改变或同值的判断，写 ext 的落点报「界外落点，观测外」，不作同值推断。
- L3 分项：L3a 边界（施作者对 F 的申报归属，归属表从判定实例传入）、L3b 先后（入口取得以运行入口段为准／窗内在途）、
  L3c 写后直接读入（从写入事件取其生成的唯一版本，按申报的读入窗与严格先后查读者；读现行版本且依赖为 1；依赖未决给 ⊥）。
- L4 为「控制边参与」：逐边追踪门控事件 ctrl 变量的写前现行版本，看其生成事件是否属维护过程；写值参与另列为 L4w（不入判词）。
- 末节以同一判词重跑正文各例。
- 另有：跨窗排除对照、Acq／同值写入分类、L1b′／L1b″ 的可靠否定、探针两例。
"""
import sys, json, os, runpy, tempfile, contextlib, io
from fractions import Fraction as Fr
HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)); sys.path.insert(0, HERE); ARGV = list(sys.argv)
with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp, contextlib.redirect_stdout(io.StringIO()):
    cwd = os.getcwd(); os.chdir(tmp); sys.argv = ['full', os.path.join(tmp, 'o.json')]
    X = runpy.run_path(os.path.join(HERE, 'shiyin_full.py')); os.chdir(cwd); sys.argv = ARGV
C, Mx, RunX, E, GoalCond = X['C'], X['Mx'], X['RunX'], X['E'], X['GoalCond']

Q = lambda: ('pred', lambda x: True, [Fr(k, 4) for k in range(-12, 13)])
B01 = ('fin', {Fr(0), Fr(1)})
GOAL = {'role': 'Xg', 'var': 'g', 'field': 'F'}; TGT = {'g': {'field': 'F'}}
INSIDE = {'F': '场内', 'I': '场内', 'a': '场外', 'b': '场外', 'M': '场外（维护过程）', 'Xg': '场外', 'env': '场外'}   # 申报的归属表（H18-3）


def model(goal=True, in_ext=False):
    layer = {'w': 'theta', 'u': 'theta', 'q': 'theta', 'm': 'm', 'r': 'r', 'g': 'ext', 'mt': 'ext'}
    dom = {v: Q() for v in layer}; dom['m'] = B01
    FM = {'sed': ('Sed', {'w'}), 'run': ('Run', {'r'}), 'ext_goal': ('Run', {'g'}), 'ext_mt': ('Run', {'mt'})}
    M = Mx(layer, dom, (lambda s: s['w'] * s['m'] + s['u'] + s.get('q', 0), {'w': 1, 'm': 1, 'u': 1, 'q': 1}),
              support={'m': 'w'}, ports={'Rw': {'w', 'u'}, 'Gt': {'m'}, 'In': {'r', 'mt'} if in_ext else {'r'}, 'Acq': {'q'}}, FM=FM, fid='F',
              goal=GOAL if goal else None, target=TGT if goal else {})
    M.field_rules = {'sed', 'run'}                     # 申报：F_M 中的场自身规则分项（X_ext 为 ext_goal、ext_mt）
    return M


def go(steps, init=None, hold=True, goal=True, in_ext=False):
    r = RunX(model(goal, in_ext), {'w': 1, 'u': 0, 'm': 1, 'r': 0, 'g': 2, 'mt': 0, **(init or {})}, hold=hold and goal)
    for ph, e in steps: r.ev(ph, e)
    return r


# ---------------- 观测函数 ----------------
def obs(r, V):
    o = {'V': V, 'snaps': [(ph, {k: v for k, v in st.items() if r.M.layer[k] in ('theta', 'm', 'r')}) for ph, st, _ in r.trace],
         'layer': dict(r.M.layer), 'phi': r.M.phi, 'fid': r.M.fid}
    if V >= 1:
        o['FM_field'] = sorted(r.M.field_rules)                                    # 申报的场自身规则分项（显式成员关系）
        o['events'] = [{'id': e['id'], 'phase': e['phase'], 'kind': e['kind'], 'src': e['src'], 'part': e['part'],
                        'reads': tuple(e['reads']), 'writes': tuple(sorted(e['out']))} for e in r.events]
    if V >= 2:
        for oe, e in zip(o['events'], r.events):
            oe.update({'ctrl': tuple(e['ctrl']), 'ctrl_versions': dict(e['ctrl_versions']), 'read_versions': dict(e['read_versions']),
                       'deps': {k: v for k, v in e['deps'].items()}})
        o['anc'] = [{k: dict(v) for k, v in anc.items()} for _, _, anc in r.trace]
        o['versions'] = [dict(r.vtrace[i]) for i in range(len(r.vtrace))]
    if V >= 3:
        o['goal_decl'] = r.M.goal; o['target_decl'] = dict(r.M.target); o['goal_src'] = dict(r.goal_src)
    return o


def canon(x):
    if isinstance(x, dict): return sorted((repr(k), canon(v)) for k, v in x.items())
    if isinstance(x, (list, tuple)): return [canon(v) for v in x]
    return repr(x)
def same(o1, o2):
    return canon({a: b for a, b in o1.items() if a != 'phi'}) == canon({a: b for a, b in o2.items() if a != 'phi'})


in_W = lambda ph, W: tuple(W[0]) <= tuple(ph) <= tuple(W[1])
def changed(o, i):
    a, b = o['snaps'][i][1], o['snaps'][i + 1][1]
    return [v for v in o['events'][i]['writes'] if a.get(v) != b.get(v)]


# ---------------- 判词（只读 χ 与观测） ----------------
VISIBLE = {'theta', 'm', 'r'}
def vis_changed(o, i):
    """可见层上的实际改变；写 ext 者另列为观测外落点。"""
    a, b = o['snaps'][i][1], o['snaps'][i + 1][1]
    ws = o['events'][i]['writes']
    return [v for v in ws if o['layer'][v] in VISIBLE and a.get(v) != b.get(v)], [v for v in ws if o['layer'][v] not in VISIBLE]
def is_field(o, e): return e['kind'] in ('Sed', 'Run') and e['src'] in o['FM_field']
def L2(o, chi):
    if o['V'] < 1: return ('⊥', '证据不足：V0 无事件')
    out = []
    for i, e in enumerate(o['events']):
        if not in_W(e['phase'], chi['W']): continue
        ch, hid = vis_changed(o, i)
        if e['part'] == chi['part']:
            if ch: out.append((e['id'], e['kind'], tuple(ch)))
            if hid: out.append((e['id'], e['kind'] + '（界外落点，观测外）', tuple(hid)))
            if not ch and not hid and e['kind'] in ('Rw', 'Gt', 'Acq', 'In'): out.append((e['id'], e['kind'] + '（同值，不是实际改变）', ()))
        elif is_field(o, e) and ch:
            out.append((e['id'], f'场自身 {e["kind"]}（另列）', tuple(ch)))
    return out
def L3c(o, chi, var):
    if o['V'] < 2: return ('⊥', '证据不足')
    RW = chi.get('RW', chi['W']); res = 0; found = False
    for i, e in enumerate(o['events']):
        if e['part'] != chi['part'] or not in_W(e['phase'], chi['W']) or var not in vis_changed(o, i)[0]: continue
        ver = next(k for k in o['versions'][i + 1][var] if isinstance(k, tuple) and len(k) == 3 and k[0] == var and k[2] == e['id'])
        found = True
        for j in range(i + 1, len(o['events'])):
            f = o['events'][j]
            if not is_field(o, f) or not in_W(f['phase'], RW) or f['read_versions'].get(var) != ver: continue
            for y in vis_changed(o, j)[0]:
                d = f['deps'].get((y, var))
                if d is True: return 1
                if d is None: res = None
    if not found: return 0
    return res if res is not None else ('⊥', '上游未决：依赖未决')



def P6(o, chi):
    """GoalCond^f（Π 可给）：V3 全算；V2 只在持有锚子域（来源标识带锚前缀）可签 1，否则 ⊥。"""
    if o['V'] < 2: return ('⊥', '证据不足：V1 无来源')
    OBS_LAYERS = {'theta', 'm', 'r'}                       # 观测投影所含的层（缺省层集）
    if chi.get('layers') is not None and not set(chi['layers']) <= OBS_LAYERS:
        return ('未申报投影', '层过滤含观测之外的层：' + ','.join(sorted(set(chi['layers']) - OBS_LAYERS)))
    src = (lambda k: k in o['goal_src']) if o['V'] >= 3 else (lambda k: isinstance(k, str) and k.startswith('HOLD:'))
    if o['V'] >= 3 and not o['goal_src']: return ('⊥', '上游未决：无目标来源登记')
    res = 0
    for i, e in enumerate(o['events']):
        if e['part'] != chi['part'] or not in_W(e['phase'], chi['W']): continue
        if chi.get('kinds') is not None and e['kind'] not in chi['kinds']: continue
        for v in changed(o, i):
            if chi.get('layers') is not None and o['layer'][v] not in chi['layers']: continue
            hits = [s for k, s in o['anc'][i + 1][v].items() if src(k)]
            if any(h is True for h in hits): return 1
            if hits: res = None
    if o['V'] == 2 and res == 0: return ('⊥', '证据不足：V2 不见事件型目标登记')
    return res if res is not None else ('⊥', '上游未决：来源未决')


def Target_query(reg, var, fid):
    """登记层查询（不是运行）：reg 为目标指向登记表，var 为明确的目标变量，fid 为被引场标识。"""
    t = reg.get(var)
    return ('⊥', '未登记') if t is None else (1 if t['field'] == fid else 0)


def L1a_struct(o, chi, H18_1=False):
    p = P6(o, chi)
    if p == 1: return 1                                   # B1—B4 下读回
    if H18_1 and o['V'] >= 3 and not o['goal_src']: return 0   # 条件化规则：登记穷尽且无目标来源
    return ('⊥', '形式值 0 只报形式值' if p == 0 else (p[1] if isinstance(p, tuple) else '未决'))


def L1b_P1P3(o, chi):
    """V0：W 入口接入的 θ 为空（接入表示完整，H）或 ΔΦ_W = 0（B2）→ 0；否则 ⊥。"""
    s0 = next(st for ph, st in o['snaps'] if tuple(ph) == tuple(chi['W0'])); s1 = [st for ph, st in o['snaps'] if in_W(ph, chi['W'])][-1]
    if not any(o['layer'][v] == 'theta' for v in s0): return 0
    return 0 if o['phi'](s1) - o['phi'](s0) == 0 else ('⊥', '肯定向未证')


def L1b_branch(o, chi, kind):
    """L1b′（kind = Rw）、L1b″（kind = Gt）：V1，登记穷尽（H18-2）下 W 内无该类实际改变 → 0；否则 ⊥。"""
    if o['V'] < 1: return ('⊥', '证据不足')
    has = any(e['part'] == chi['part'] and e['kind'] == kind and in_W(e['phase'], chi['W']) and changed(o, i) for i, e in enumerate(o['events']))
    return ('⊥', '必要项成立，肯定向未证') if has else 0


def L3a(o, chi): return chi.get('membership', {}).get(chi['part'], '未申报') if o['V'] >= 1 else ('⊥', '证据不足')
def L3b(o, chi):
    if o['V'] < 1: return ('⊥', '证据不足')
    return [(e['id'], '入口取得' if e['kind'] == 'Acq' and e['phase'][0] == 0 else ('窗内在途' if in_W(e['phase'], chi['W']) else '窗外'))
            for i, e in enumerate(o['events']) if e['part'] == chi['part'] and changed(o, i)]


def L4(o, chi, maint='M'):
    """控制边参与：W 内施作的门控事件，其 ctrl 变量的写前现行版本由维护过程的事件生成 → 1；否则 0（ctrl 受信任，5.2）。"""
    if o['V'] < 2: return ('⊥', '证据不足')
    m_ids = {e['id'] for e in o['events'] if e['part'] == maint}
    for i, e in enumerate(o['events']):
        if e['part'] == chi['part'] and e['kind'] == 'Gt' and in_W(e['phase'], chi['W']) and changed(o, i):
            if any(isinstance(v, tuple) and v[2] in m_ids for v in e['ctrl_versions'].values()): return 1
    return 0
def L4w(o, chi, maint='M'):
    """候选（不入判词）：写值参与——门控事件读入的现行版本由维护过程生成且依赖为 1。"""
    if o['V'] < 2: return ('⊥', '证据不足')
    m_ids = {e['id'] for e in o['events'] if e['part'] == maint}
    for i, e in enumerate(o['events']):
        if e['part'] == chi['part'] and e['kind'] == 'Gt' and changed(o, i):
            for v, ver in e['read_versions'].items():
                if isinstance(ver, tuple) and ver[2] in m_ids and any(e['deps'].get((y, v)) is True for y in changed(o, i)): return 1
    return 0


R = {}
CHI = lambda part, W=((1, 1), (9, 9)), W0=(0, 0), **kw: {'part': part, 'W': W, 'W0': W0, 'membership': INSIDE, **kw}
fix = lambda x: x if not isinstance(x, tuple) else list(x)

# 一、L2 在 V0 不足；V1 足够
A = go([((1, 1), E('a1', 'Rw', 'port', 'a', [], ['w'], lambda x: {'w': 2}))])
B = go([((1, 1), E('s1', 'Sed', 'sed', 'F', [], ['w'], lambda x: {'w': 2}))])
R['一 L2'] = {'V0 相等': same(obs(A, 0), obs(B, 0)), 'L2(a)（V1）': [L2(obs(A, 1), CHI('a')), L2(obs(B, 1), CHI('a'))], 'L2（V0）': fix(L2(obs(A, 0), CHI('a')))}

# 二、L1a：V1 不足（依赖不同）；V2 对事件型目标来源不足（探针）；V2 持有锚子域足够
A = go([((1, 1), E('a1', 'Rw', 'port', 'a', ['w', 'g'], ['w'], lambda x: {'w': x['w'] + 1}, {'w': 1, 'g': 1}))])
B = go([((1, 1), E('a1', 'Rw', 'port', 'a', ['w', 'g'], ['w'], lambda x: {'w': x['w'] + x['g'] - 1}, {'w': 1, 'g': 1}))])
R['二 L1a·V1 不足'] = {'V1 相等': same(obs(A, 1), obs(B, 1)), 'P6（V2）': [fix(P6(obs(A, 2), CHI('a'))), fix(P6(obs(B, 2), CHI('a')))], 'P6（V3）': [fix(P6(obs(A, 3), CHI('a'))), fix(P6(obs(B, 3), CHI('a')))]}
def tgt_run(declared):
    r = go([], hold=False, goal=declared)
    r.ev((1, 1), E('G', 'Run', 'ext_goal', 'Xg', [], ['g'], lambda s: {'g': 3}))
    r.ev((1, 2), E('A', 'Rw', 'port', 'a', ['g'], ['w'], lambda s: {'w': s['g']}, {'g': 1}))
    return r
Y, U = tgt_run(True), tgt_run(False)
R['二 L1a·V2 对事件型目标来源不足（探针）'] = {'V2 相等': same(obs(Y, 2), obs(U, 2)), 'V3 相等': same(obs(Y, 3), obs(U, 3)),
    'P6（V2）': [fix(P6(obs(Y, 2), CHI('a'))), fix(P6(obs(U, 2), CHI('a')))], 'P6（V3）': [fix(P6(obs(Y, 3), CHI('a'))), fix(P6(obs(U, 3), CHI('a')))],
    '形式 GoalCond': [GoalCond(Y, 'a'), GoalCond(U, 'a')]}
R['二 L1a·V2 持有锚子域'] = {'P6（V2）': fix(P6(obs(B, 2), CHI('a'))), 'P6（V3）': fix(P6(obs(B, 3), CHI('a'))), '形式 GoalCond': GoalCond(B, 'a')}
R['二 L1a 结构判词与 Target 查询'] = {'B（P6 = 1）': fix(L1a_struct(obs(B, 3), CHI('a'))), 'A（P6 = 0）': fix(L1a_struct(obs(A, 3), CHI('a'))),
    '未登记、无 H18-1': fix(L1a_struct(obs(U, 3), CHI('a'))), '未登记、H18-1': fix(L1a_struct(obs(U, 3), CHI('a'), H18_1=True)),
    'Target 查询（有登记）': Target_query(obs(Y, 3)['target_decl'], 'g', 'F'), 'Target 查询（未登记）': fix(Target_query(obs(U, 3)['target_decl'], 'g', 'F'))}

# 三、L1b：P1、P3（V0）；L1b′、L1b″（V1）；跨窗排除
A = go([((1, 1), E('a1', 'Rw', 'port', 'a', [], ['u'], lambda x: {'u': 1})), ((1, 2), E('b1', 'Rw', 'port', 'b', [], ['u'], lambda x: {'u': 0}))])
Bp = go([((1, 1), E('a1', 'Rw', 'port', 'a', [], ['u'], lambda x: {'u': 1}))])
Gt_only = go([((1, 1), E('i1', 'Gt', 'port', 'a', [], ['m'], lambda x: {'m': 0}))])
R['三 L1b'] = {'ΔΦ = 0：L1b(a)（V0）': fix(L1b_P1P3(obs(A, 0), CHI('a'))), 'ΔΦ ≠ 0：L1b(a)': fix(L1b_P1P3(obs(Bp, 0), CHI('a'))),
    '只有门控：L1b′（调宿）': fix(L1b_branch(obs(Gt_only, 1), CHI('a'), 'Rw')), '只有门控：L1b″（调谐）': fix(L1b_branch(obs(Gt_only, 1), CHI('a'), 'Gt'))}
X2 = go([((1, 1), E('a1', 'Rw', 'port', 'a', [], ['u'], lambda x: {'u': 1})), ((2, 1), E('f1', 'Run', 'run', 'F', [], ['r'], lambda x: {'r': 1}))])
R['三 跨窗排除'] = {'W = 第 2 拍：L2(a)': L2(obs(X2, 1), CHI('a', ((2, 1), (2, 9)), (1, 1))), 'W = 第 1—2 拍：L2(a)': L2(obs(X2, 1), CHI('a', ((1, 1), (2, 9))))}

# 四、L3：边界、先后（Acq、同值）、写后直接读入
A = go([((0, 1), E('q0', 'Acq', 'port', 'a', [], ['q'], lambda x: {'q': 1})), ((1, 1), E('a1', 'Rw', 'port', 'a', [], ['w'], lambda x: {'w': 1})),
        ((1, 2), E('a2', 'Rw', 'port', 'a', [], ['w'], lambda x: {'w': 2})), ((1, 3), E('f1', 'Run', 'run', 'F', ['w'], ['r'], lambda x: {'r': x['w'] - 1}, {'w': 1}))])
B = go([((0, 1), E('q0', 'Acq', 'port', 'a', [], ['q'], lambda x: {'q': 1})), ((1, 1), E('a1', 'Rw', 'port', 'a', [], ['w'], lambda x: {'w': 1})),
        ((1, 2), E('a2', 'Rw', 'port', 'a', [], ['w'], lambda x: {'w': 2})), ((1, 3), E('f1', 'Run', 'run', 'F', ['w'], ['r'], lambda x: {'r': Fr(1)}, {'w': 1}))])
R['四 L3'] = {'L3a 边界（a）': L3a(obs(A, 1), CHI('a')), 'L3b 先后': L3b(obs(A, 1), CHI('a')), 'L2（含同值写入）': L2(obs(A, 1), CHI('a')),
    'V1 相等': same(obs(A, 1), obs(B, 1)), 'L3c 写后直接读入（V2）': [L3c(obs(A, 2), CHI('a'), 'w'), L3c(obs(B, 2), CHI('a'), 'w')]}

# 五、L4：控制边参与；V1 不足；探针（只经写值链、无 ctrl）
A = go([((1, 1), E('mt1', 'Run', 'ext_mt', 'M', [], ['mt'], lambda x: {'mt': 1})), ((1, 2), E('i1', 'Gt', 'port', 'I', [], ['m'], lambda x: {'m': 0}, ctrl=['mt']))])
B = go([((1, 1), E('mt1', 'Run', 'ext_mt', 'M', [], ['mt'], lambda x: {'mt': 1})), ((1, 2), E('i1', 'Gt', 'port', 'I', [], ['m'], lambda x: {'m': 0}))])
Pv = go([((1, 1), E('mt1', 'Run', 'ext_mt', 'M', [], ['mt'], lambda x: {'mt': 1})), ((1, 2), E('i1', 'Gt', 'port', 'I', ['mt'], ['m'], lambda x: {'m': 1 - x['mt']}, {'mt': 1}))])
R['五 L4'] = {'V1 相等': same(obs(A, 1), obs(B, 1)), 'L4（V2）': [L4(obs(A, 2), CHI('I')), L4(obs(B, 2), CHI('I'))],
    '探针（写值链、无 ctrl）：L4 / L4w（候选）': [L4(obs(Pv, 2), CHI('I')), L4w(obs(Pv, 2), CHI('I'))]}

# 六、判词独立
A = go([((1, 1), E('a1', 'Rw', 'port', 'a', ['w', 'g'], ['w'], lambda x: {'w': x['w'] + x['g'] - 1}, {'w': 1, 'g': 1}))])
R['六 判词独立'] = {'L2(a)': L2(obs(A, 1), CHI('a')), 'P6': P6(obs(A, 3), CHI('a')), 'L1b(a)': fix(L1b_P1P3(obs(A, 0), CHI('a')))}

# ---------------- 七、Target 查询、P6 过滤与 L3c 读入窗 ----------------
R['七 Target 查询'] = {'目标变量 mt 登记指向 F': Target_query({'mt': {'field': 'F'}}, 'mt', 'F'),
                        '登记指向另一场 F2（登记层准入排除；相应带目标声明的运行定义域无效）': Target_query({'g': {'field': 'F2'}}, 'g', 'F'),
                        '运行内建立带不一致目标声明的运行': (lambda: (lambda m: X['attempt'](lambda: RunX(m, {'w': 1, 'u': 0, 'm': 1, 'r': 0, 'g': 2, 'mt': 0})))(
                            Mx({'w': 'theta', 'u': 'theta', 'q': 'theta', 'm': 'm', 'r': 'r', 'g': 'ext', 'mt': 'ext'},
                               {**{v: Q() for v in ('w', 'u', 'q', 'r', 'g', 'mt')}, 'm': B01}, (lambda s: s['w'], {'w': 1}), support={'m': 'w'},
                               ports={'Rw': {'w'}}, FM={}, fid='F', goal=GOAL, target={'g': {'field': 'F2'}})))()}
# P6 的空过滤：Π 的种类集或层集申报为空
Bh = go([((1, 1), E('a1', 'Rw', 'port', 'a', ['w', 'g'], ['w'], lambda x: {'w': x['w'] + x['g'] - 1}, {'w': 1, 'g': 1}))])
R['七 P6 过滤'] = {'未提供': P6(obs(Bh, 3), CHI('a')), '种类集为空': fix(P6(obs(Bh, 3), CHI('a', kinds=set()))), '层集为空': fix(P6(obs(Bh, 3), CHI('a', layers=set()))),
                    '势引数理式实现 GoalCond(kinds=∅)': GoalCond(Bh, 'a', kinds=set()), '势引数理式实现 GoalCond(layers=∅)': GoalCond(Bh, 'a', layers=())}
# L3c 读入窗两例与正例
c1 = go([((1, 1), E('a1', 'Rw', 'port', 'a', [], ['w'], lambda x: {'w': 2})),
         ((2, 1), E('f1', 'Run', 'run', 'F', ['w'], ['r'], lambda x: {'r': x['w'] - 1}, {'w': 1})),
         ((2, 2), E('a2', 'Rw', 'port', 'a', ['w'], ['w'], lambda x: {'w': x['w'] + 1}, {'w': 1}))])
R['七 L3c 读入窗'] = {
    '(a) W = 第 2 拍：窗内新版本无人读入': fix(L3c(obs(c1, 2), CHI('a', ((2, 1), (2, 9))), 'w')),
    '(b) W = 第 1 拍：读者在第 2 拍（读入窗 = W）': fix(L3c(obs(c1, 2), CHI('a', ((1, 1), (1, 9))), 'w')),
    '(b′) W = 第 1 拍、申报读入窗第 1—2 拍': fix(L3c(obs(c1, 2), CHI('a', ((1, 1), (1, 9)), RW=((1, 1), (2, 9))), 'w')),
    '正例：W = 第 1—2 拍': fix(L3c(obs(c1, 2), CHI('a', ((1, 1), (2, 9))), 'w'))}

# ---------------- 八、P6 的 ext 投影 ----------------
Mext = go([((1, 1), E('a1', 'Run', 'ext_mt', 'a', ['g'], ['mt'], lambda x: {'mt': x['g']}, {'g': 1}))])
R['八 P6 的 ext 投影'] = {'P6（V3；层集 {ext}）': fix(P6(obs(Mext, 3), CHI('a', layers={'ext'}))),
    'P6（V3；缺省层集）': fix(P6(obs(Mext, 3), CHI('a'))), '势引数理式实现 GoalCond(layers = (ext,))': GoalCond(Mext, 'a', layers=('ext',)),
    '势引数理式实现 GoalCond（缺省层集）': GoalCond(Mext, 'a'), 'P6（层集 {θ, m}）': fix(P6(obs(Bh, 3), CHI('a', layers={'theta', 'm'})))}

# 九、界外落点：In 端口写 ext 变量 mt，初态 0，分别写 0 与 1
P0 = go([((1, 1), E('i1', 'In', 'port', 'a', [], ['mt'], lambda x: {'mt': 0}))], in_ext=True)
P1_ = go([((1, 1), E('i1', 'In', 'port', 'a', [], ['mt'], lambda x: {'mt': 1}))], in_ext=True)
R['九 界外落点'] = {'V1 相等': same(obs(P0, 1), obs(P1_, 1)), 'L2': [L2(obs(P0, 1), CHI('a')), L2(obs(P1_, 1), CHI('a'))]}
Pm = go([((1, 1), E('i1', 'In', 'port', 'a', [], ['r', 'mt'], lambda x: {'r': 1, 'mt': 1}))], in_ext=True)
R['九 同一事件写 r 与 ext'] = L2(obs(Pm, 1), CHI('a'))
# 十、场自身事件的识别：场自身分项以 part = local 执行；界外 Run 读 w 为负例
Lc = go([((1, 1), E('a1', 'Rw', 'port', 'a', [], ['w'], lambda x: {'w': 2})), ((1, 2), E('f1', 'Run', 'run', 'local', ['w'], ['r'], lambda x: {'r': x['w']}, {'w': 1}))])
Ex = go([((1, 1), E('a1', 'Rw', 'port', 'a', [], ['w'], lambda x: {'w': 2})), ((1, 2), E('x1', 'Run', 'ext_mt', 'M', ['w'], ['mt'], lambda x: {'mt': x['w']}, {'w': 1}))])
R['十 场自身事件的识别'] = {'part = local 的场自身分项：L3c': fix(L3c(obs(Lc, 2), CHI('a'), 'w')),
    '界外 Run 读 w：L3c': fix(L3c(obs(Ex, 2), CHI('a'), 'w')), 'L2（local）': L2(obs(Lc, 1), CHI('a'))}

def go_named(rule_name, as_field=True):
    """同一场自身读规则以任意合法名登记；归属以申报成员关系为准。"""
    M = model(); M.FM[rule_name] = ('Run', {'r'})
    M.field_rules = {'sed', 'run'} | ({rule_name} if as_field else set())
    r = RunX(M, {'w': 1, 'u': 0, 'm': 1, 'r': 0, 'g': 2, 'mt': 0}, hold=True)
    r.ev((1, 1), E('a1', 'Rw', 'port', 'a', [], ['w'], lambda x: {'w': 2}))
    r.ev((1, 2), E('f1', 'Run', rule_name, 'F', ['w'], ['r'], lambda x: {'r': x['w']}, {'w': 1}))
    return r
Ra, Rb = go_named('reader'), go_named('ext_reader')
R['十 改名对照（同一场自身规则）'] = {'名 reader：L3c': fix(L3c(obs(Ra, 2), CHI('a'), 'w')), '名 ext_reader：L3c': fix(L3c(obs(Rb, 2), CHI('a'), 'w')),
    '名 reader：L2': L2(obs(Ra, 1), CHI('a')), '名 ext_reader：L2': L2(obs(Rb, 1), CHI('a'))}
Rx = go_named('ext_obs', as_field=False)
R['十 真正界外 Run 负例（未申报为场自身）'] = {'L3c': fix(L3c(obs(Rx, 2), CHI('a'), 'w')), 'L2': L2(obs(Rx, 1), CHI('a'))}

# 十一、正文各例
def ex(steps, **kw): return go(steps, **kw)
fin = {}
A = ex([((1, 1), E('a1', 'Rw', 'port', 'a', [], ['w'], lambda x: {'w': 2}))]); B = ex([((1, 1), E('s1', 'Sed', 'sed', 'F', [], ['w'], lambda x: {'w': 2}))])
fin['定理 5(a) L2，V0'] = {'V0 相等': same(obs(A, 0), obs(B, 0)), 'L2（V1）': [L2(obs(A, 1), CHI('a')), L2(obs(B, 1), CHI('a'))]}
A = ex([((1, 1), E('a1', 'Rw', 'port', 'a', [], ['w'], lambda x: {'w': 2})), ((1, 2), E('f1', 'Run', 'run', 'F', ['w'], ['r'], lambda x: {'r': x['w'] - 1}, {'w': 1}))])
B = ex([((1, 1), E('a1', 'Rw', 'port', 'a', [], ['w'], lambda x: {'w': 2})), ((1, 2), E('f1', 'Run', 'run', 'F', ['w'], ['r'], lambda x: {'r': Fr(1)}, {'w': 1}))])
fin['定理 5(d) L3c，V1'] = {'V1 相等': same(obs(A, 1), obs(B, 1)), 'L3c（V2）': [fix(L3c(obs(A, 2), CHI('a'), 'w')), fix(L3c(obs(B, 2), CHI('a'), 'w'))]}
A = ex([((1, 1), E('a1', 'Rw', 'port', 'a', ['w', 'g'], ['w'], lambda x: {'w': x['w'] + x['g'] - 1}, {'w': 1, 'g': 1}))])
fin['例一'] = {'L2': L2(obs(A, 1), CHI('a')), 'P6': P6(obs(A, 3), CHI('a')), 'L1b': fix(L1b_P1P3(obs(A, 0), CHI('a')))}
A = ex([((1, 1), E('a1', 'Rw', 'port', 'a', [], ['u'], lambda x: {'u': 1})), ((1, 2), E('b1', 'Rw', 'port', 'b', [], ['u'], lambda x: {'u': 0}))])
fin['例二'] = {'L1b': fix(L1b_P1P3(obs(A, 0), CHI('a'))), 'L2': L2(obs(A, 1), CHI('a'))}
X2 = ex([((1, 1), E('a1', 'Rw', 'port', 'a', [], ['u'], lambda x: {'u': 1})), ((2, 1), E('f1', 'Run', 'run', 'F', [], ['r'], lambda x: {'r': 1}))])
fin['例四 跨窗'] = {'W = 第 2 拍': L2(obs(X2, 1), CHI('a', ((2, 1), (2, 9)), (1, 1))), 'W = 第 1—2 拍': L2(obs(X2, 1), CHI('a', ((1, 1), (2, 9))))}
fin['例五 读入窗'] = {'W = 第 2 拍': fix(L3c(obs(c1, 2), CHI('a', ((2, 1), (2, 9))), 'w')), 'W = 第 1 拍、RW = W': fix(L3c(obs(c1, 2), CHI('a', ((1, 1), (1, 9))), 'w')),
                   'W = 第 1 拍、RW = 第 1—2 拍': fix(L3c(obs(c1, 2), CHI('a', ((1, 1), (1, 9)), RW=((1, 1), (2, 9))), 'w'))}
fin['定理 4 后例（part = local）'] = fix(L3c(obs(Lc, 2), CHI('a'), 'w'))
fin['定理 1 后 ext 例'] = [L2(obs(P0, 1), CHI('a')), L2(obs(P1_, 1), CHI('a'))]
fin['混合 In（同一事件写 r 与 ext 变量 mt）'] = L2(obs(Pm, 1), CHI('a'))
R['十一 正文各例'] = fin

json.dump(R, open(sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else os.path.join(HERE, 'mode_judge_checks.json'), 'w'), ensure_ascii=False, indent=1, default=str)
print(json.dumps(R, ensure_ascii=False, indent=1, default=str))
~~~

【资源 022 起】源码　balance\_judge\_checks.py　SHA-256 77b03e7ae7821ad199cf4db19f769682b42ac69422b82dc11eb1ecd0e2e06273

~~~python
"""调宿平衡判定式 · 核算（纯 Python，精确分数，确定性）
- 前向关系：M_EDR 第四节（采样、前向、标签、损失、伴随、梯度、动量、写回）；三种计算图的直接递推，并与 edr_checks 的事件运行逐值对照。
- 被判对象：时点 t* 的状态 (θ*, v*, 相位)，此后协议（周期采样 D = {1, 2}、η、β、g）固定。
- 判据（周期 P = |D| = 2）：
  J_bal（被动，O1 + O2-更新，窗 P）：窗内各步损失为 0 且写回量为 0 → 1；否则 → 0。
  J_c （被动，O2-更新，窗 P + 1）：窗内各步写回量为 0 → 1；否则 → 0。窗 P 的版本只给 {0, ⊥}（动量抵消例）。
  J_e （主动探针：固定 θ* 上对 D 各输入读出残差）：全 0 → 1；否则 → 0。
  J_e′（被动，O1 + O2-更新，窗 P）：先写回为 0 的前缀内出现非零损失 → 0；窗内全 0 → 1；其余 → ⊥。
- 真值：对齐 = θ* 在 D 上各输入残差为 0；协议止调 = 此后长视界（16 步）每步写回为 0（与解析判别式 η = 0 ∨ (β·v* = 0 ∧ ∀x q(θ*, x) = 0) 对照）。
- 定理 4 的不可辨对在完整 O2-更新（含 O1）下可分；已知 β = 0 子域上纯写回投影窗 P 的完全判据；
  g 未知时 J_e^lin 的读法（ℓ₁ ≠ 0 判 0；ℓ₁ = 0 由 g = y₁/x₁ 还原）；J_bal(t*) = 0 只排除被判时刻的例。
"""
import sys, json, os, runpy, tempfile, contextlib, io, itertools
from fractions import Fraction as Fr
HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)); sys.path.insert(0, HERE); ARGV = list(sys.argv)
with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp, contextlib.redirect_stdout(io.StringIO()):
    cwd = os.getcwd(); os.chdir(tmp); sys.argv = ['edr', os.path.join(tmp, 'o.json')]
    EDR = runpy.run_path(os.path.join(HERE, 'edr_checks.py')); os.chdir(cwd); sys.argv = ARGV
Graph, RunR, build, step_events, init_for = (EDR[k] for k in ('Graph', 'RunR', 'build', 'step_events', 'init_for'))

D = [Fr(1), Fr(2)]; P = len(D)
# 三种图：(名, 输出, 梯度, M_EDR 图)
GRAPHS = {
    '参数线性 y = w1·x + w2·x²': (lambda w, x: w[0] * x + w[1] * x * x, lambda w, x, r: (r * x, r * x * x),
                                Graph(['w1', 'w2'], [('xin', 'in', ()), ('m1', 'mul', ('w1', 'xin')), ('s', 'sq', ('xin',)), ('m2', 'mul', ('w2', 's')), ('y', 'add', ('m1', 'm2'))], 'y')),
    '两层 y = w2·(w1·x)': (lambda w, x: w[1] * w[0] * x, lambda w, x, r: (r * w[1] * x, r * w[0] * x),
                          Graph(['w1', 'w2'], [('xin', 'in', ()), ('h', 'mul', ('w1', 'xin')), ('y', 'mul', ('w2', 'h'))], 'y')),
    '平方 y = w1²·x（w2 不入前向）': (lambda w, x: w[0] * w[0] * x, lambda w, x, r: (r * 2 * w[0] * x, Fr(0)),
                                 Graph(['w1', 'w2'], [('xin', 'in', ()), ('s', 'sq', ('w1',)), ('y', 'mul', ('s', 'xin'))], 'y')),
}


def run(name, theta, v, g, eta, beta, K, phase=0):
    """直接递推：自状态 (θ, v, 相位) 起执行 K 步。返回每步 (x, y, ℓ, 写回量向量, q) 与末态。第 k 步取 D[(phase + k − 1) mod P]。"""
    f, grad, _ = GRAPHS[name]; th = list(theta); vv = list(v); out = []
    for k in range(K):
        x = D[(phase + k) % P]; y = f(th, x); r = y - g * x; q = grad(th, x, r)
        vv = [beta * a + b for a, b in zip(vv, q)]; upd = [eta * a for a in vv]
        th = [a - u for a, u in zip(th, upd)]
        out.append({'x': x, 'y': y, 'loss': r * r / 2, 'upd': tuple(upd), 'q': tuple(q)})
    return out, th, vv


def aligned(name, theta, g): return all(GRAPHS[name][0](theta, x) == g * x for x in D)
def stop_true(name, theta, v, g, eta, beta, H=16):
    """长视界逐步执行，遇首个非零写回即止（避免发散轨迹的分数膨胀）。"""
    th, vv = list(theta), list(v); ph = 0
    for _ in range(H):
        o, th, vv = run(name, th, vv, g, eta, beta, 1, phase=ph); ph += 1
        if not all(u == 0 for u in o[0]['upd']): return False
    return True
def stop_formula(name, theta, v, g, eta, beta):
    if eta == 0: return True
    f, grad, _ = GRAPHS[name]
    return all(beta * a == 0 for a in v) and all(all(c == 0 for c in grad(theta, x, f(theta, x) - g * x)) for x in D)

def zero(s): return all(u == 0 for u in s['upd'])
def J_bal(obs): return 1 if all(s['loss'] == 0 and zero(s) for s in obs[:P]) else 0
def J_c(obs, W): return 1 if all(zero(s) for s in obs[:W]) else 0
def J_c_short(obs): return 0 if not all(zero(s) for s in obs[:P]) else None
def J_e_active(name, theta, g): return 1 if aligned(name, theta, g) else 0
def J_e_passive(obs):
    for s in obs[:P]:
        if s['loss'] != 0: return 0
        if not zero(s): return None
    return 1


R = {}
# 一、直接递推与 M_EDR 事件运行逐值对照（含非零初动量）
chk = []
for name, (f, grad, G) in GRAPHS.items():
    th0, v0, g, eta, beta = [Fr(1), Fr(-1, 2)], [Fr(1, 2), Fr(-1)], Fr(1), Fr(1, 8), Fr(1, 2)
    r = RunR(build(G), init_for(G, w1=th0[0], w2=th0[1], v_w1=v0[0], v_w2=v0[1], g=g), hold=('Xg', 'g'))
    obs, _, _ = run(name, th0, v0, g, eta, beta, 4); ok = True
    for k in range(1, 5):
        for i, ev in enumerate(step_events(G, k, eta, beta, 1, Fr(1, 2)), 1): r.ev((k, i), ev)   # 种子 1：首样本 x = 1
        ok = ok and (r.s['loss'] == obs[k - 1]['loss']) and (r.s['y'] == obs[k - 1]['y']) and \
            (eta * r.s['v_w1'], eta * r.s['v_w2']) == obs[k - 1]['upd']
    chk.append(ok)
R['一 直接递推与 M_EDR 事件运行一致（三图、β = 1/2、非零初动量、4 步）'] = all(chk)

# 二、格上逐状态检验判据的两向有效性
vals = [Fr(-1), Fr(0), Fr(1, 2), Fr(1), Fr(2)]
stats = {}
for name in GRAPHS:
    for g, eta, beta in itertools.product([Fr(0), Fr(1)], [Fr(0), Fr(1, 4), Fr(1)], [Fr(0), Fr(1, 2)]):
        for th in itertools.product(vals, repeat=2):
            for v in itertools.product([Fr(0), Fr(-1), Fr(1)], repeat=2):
                th, v = list(th), list(v)
                obs, _, _ = run(name, th, v, g, eta, beta, P + 1)
                al = aligned(name, th, g); st = stop_true(name, th, v, g, eta, beta); bal = al and st
                s = stats.setdefault(name, {'状态数': 0, 'J_bal 错判': 0, 'J_c(P+1) 错判': 0, 'J_c(P) 判 0 错': 0, 'J_c(P) ⊥': 0,
                                            'J_e 主动 错判': 0, 'J_e′ 被动 错判': 0, 'J_e′ 被动 ⊥': 0, '止调真值与解析判别式不符': 0, '平衡态数': 0})
                s['状态数'] += 1; s['平衡态数'] += bal
                s['J_bal 错判'] += J_bal(obs) != bal
                s['J_c(P+1) 错判'] += J_c(obs, P + 1) != st
                jc = J_c_short(obs); s['J_c(P) 判 0 错'] += (jc == 0 and st); s['J_c(P) ⊥'] += jc is None
                s['J_e 主动 错判'] += J_e_active(name, th, g) != al
                je = J_e_passive(obs); s['J_e′ 被动 错判'] += (je is not None and je != al); s['J_e′ 被动 ⊥'] += je is None
                s['止调真值与解析判别式不符'] += st != stop_formula(name, th, v, g, eta, beta)
R['二 格上两向有效性（每图 2 × 3 × 2 × 25 × 9 = 2700 个状态）'] = stats

# 三、两例与 ⊥ 的必要性
o1, _, _ = run('参数线性 y = w1·x + w2·x²', [Fr(1), Fr(-1)], [Fr(-2), Fr(0)], Fr(0), Fr(1), Fr(1, 2), 2)
R['三 例一：两步损失为 0 而不对齐'] = {'各步损失': [str(s['loss']) for s in o1], '各步写回量': [[str(u) for u in s['upd']] for s in o1],
                                 '入口对齐': aligned('参数线性 y = w1·x + w2·x²', [Fr(1), Fr(-1)], Fr(0)),
                                 'J_bal': J_bal(o1), 'J_e′ 被动': J_e_passive(o1)}
# 例二（单样本周期 P = 1 的动量抵消）：y = w·x，x = 1，g = 0，η = 1，β = 1/2，w₀ = 1，v₀ = −2
def run1(w, v, g, eta, beta, K):
    out = []
    for _ in range(K):
        r = w - g; v = beta * v + r; u = eta * v; w = w - u; out.append({'loss': r * r / 2, 'upd': u})
    return out
o2 = run1(Fr(1), Fr(-2), Fr(0), Fr(1), Fr(1, 2), 3)
R['三 例二：动量抵消（单样本周期）'] = {'各步写回量': [str(s['upd']) for s in o2], '窗 P = 1 全零': o2[0]['upd'] == 0, '窗 P + 1 = 2 有非零': o2[1]['upd'] != 0}
# ⊥ 的必要性（同观测而真值不同的对）
def search(key_fn, truth, graphs, gs=(Fr(0), Fr(1)), etas=(Fr(1),), betas=(Fr(1, 2),), vs=(Fr(0), Fr(-1), Fr(1), Fr(-2), Fr(2))):
    by = {}
    for name in graphs:
        for g, eta, beta in itertools.product(gs, etas, betas):
            for th in itertools.product(vals, repeat=2):
                for v in itertools.product(vs, repeat=2):
                    obs, _, _ = run(name, list(th), list(v), g, eta, beta, P)
                    k = (name, g, eta, beta, key_fn(obs)); t = truth(name, list(th), list(v), g, eta, beta)
                    by.setdefault(k, {}).setdefault(t, (list(th), list(v)))
    for k, d in by.items():
        if len(d) > 1:
            (t1, a), (t2, b) = list(d.items())[:2]
            return {'图': k[0], 'g、η、β': [str(k[1]), str(k[2]), str(k[3])], f'真值 {t1}': [[str(x) for x in a[0]], [str(x) for x in a[1]]], f'真值 {t2}': [[str(x) for x in b[0]], [str(x) for x in b[1]]]}
    return None
upd_key = lambda obs: tuple(s['upd'] for s in obs)
full_key = lambda obs: tuple((s['y'], s['loss'], s['upd']) for s in obs)
R['三 ⊥ 的必要性'] = {
  'J_c 窗 P 只用 O2-更新：止调真值不同的同观测对': search(upd_key, lambda n, th, v, g, e, b: stop_true(n, th, v, g, e, b), ['参数线性 y = w1·x + w2·x²']),
  'J_c 窗 P 用 O1 + O2-更新（参数线性图）：同观测对': search(full_key, lambda n, th, v, g, e, b: stop_true(n, th, v, g, e, b), ['参数线性 y = w1·x + w2·x²']),
  'J_c 窗 P 用 O1 + O2-更新（平方图）：同观测对': search(full_key, lambda n, th, v, g, e, b: stop_true(n, th, v, g, e, b), ['平方 y = w1²·x（w2 不入前向）']),
  'J_e′ 被动（O1 + O2-更新，窗 P）：对齐真值不同的同观测对': search(full_key, lambda n, th, v, g, e, b: aligned(n, th, g), list(GRAPHS))}
# J_c 窗 P 的改进判据（O2-梯度 + 已知 η）：窗内写回全 0 时，η = 0 → 1；η > 0 → 第 1 步梯度为 0 则 1，否则 0
bad = 0; n = 0
for name in GRAPHS:
    for g, eta, beta in itertools.product([Fr(0), Fr(1)], [Fr(0), Fr(1, 4), Fr(1)], [Fr(0), Fr(1, 2)]):
        for th in itertools.product(vals, repeat=2):
            for v in itertools.product([Fr(0), Fr(-1), Fr(1), Fr(-2)], repeat=2):
                obs, _, _ = run(name, list(th), list(v), g, eta, beta, P); n += 1
                j = 0 if not all(zero(s) for s in obs) else (1 if eta == 0 or all(c == 0 for c in obs[0]['q']) else 0)
                bad += j != stop_true(name, list(th), list(v), g, eta, beta)
R['三 J_c 窗 P（O2-更新 + O2-梯度，η 已知）错判数'] = {'状态数': n, '错判': bad}

# 四、达标与监视标志
o4, _, _ = run('参数线性 y = w1·x + w2·x²', [Fr(3, 2), Fr(0)], [Fr(0), Fr(0)], Fr(2), Fr(0), Fr(0), P)
R['四 达标不判对齐（η = 0，ε = 1/2）'] = {'各步损失': [str(s['loss']) for s in o4], '各步 ℓ ≤ ε': [s['loss'] <= Fr(1, 2) for s in o4],
                                   '对齐': aligned('参数线性 y = w1·x + w2·x²', [Fr(3, 2), Fr(0)], Fr(2)), 'J_bal': J_bal(o4)}

# 五、参数线性图上的被动对齐判据（O1 + O2-更新，φ 已知）：由观测还原 θ* 处各输入的残差
def J_e_lin(obs, g):
    acc = [Fr(0), Fr(0)]
    for s in obs[:P]:
        x = s['x']; phi = (x, x * x)
        if s['y'] + sum(a * b for a, b in zip(acc, phi)) != g * x: return 0
        acc = [a + u for a, u in zip(acc, s['upd'])]
    return 1
bad = 0; n = 0
for g, eta, beta in itertools.product([Fr(0), Fr(1)], [Fr(0), Fr(1, 4), Fr(1)], [Fr(0), Fr(1, 2)]):
    for th in itertools.product(vals, repeat=2):
        for v in itertools.product([Fr(0), Fr(-1), Fr(1), Fr(-2)], repeat=2):
            obs, _, _ = run('参数线性 y = w1·x + w2·x²', list(th), list(v), g, eta, beta, P); n += 1
            bad += J_e_lin(obs, g) != aligned('参数线性 y = w1·x + w2·x²', list(th), g)
R['五 参数线性图：被动对齐判据（由写回量还原 θ* 处残差）'] = {'状态数': n, '错判': bad, '例一判值': J_e_lin(o1, Fr(0))}

# ---------------- 五、不可辨对与完全判据 ----------------
nm = '参数线性 y = w1·x + w2·x²'
o_a, _, _ = run(nm, [Fr(-1), Fr(1, 2)], [Fr(1), Fr(1)], Fr(0), Fr(1), Fr(1, 2), P)
o_b, _, _ = run(nm, [Fr(0), Fr(0)], [Fr(0), Fr(0)], Fr(0), Fr(1), Fr(1, 2), P)
R['五 定理 4 的对：纯写回投影相同，完整 O2-更新可分'] = {
    '纯写回投影相同': [s['upd'] for s in o_a] == [s['upd'] for s in o_b],
    '首步 (y, ℓ)': [[str(o_a[0]['y']), str(o_a[0]['loss'])], [str(o_b[0]['y']), str(o_b[0]['loss'])]]}
# 已知 β = 0：纯写回投影窗 P 全零 ⇒ 止调
bad = 0; n = 0
for name in GRAPHS:
    for g, eta in itertools.product([Fr(0), Fr(1)], [Fr(0), Fr(1, 4), Fr(1)]):
        for th in itertools.product(vals, repeat=2):
            for v in itertools.product([Fr(0), Fr(-1), Fr(1)], repeat=2):
                obs, _, _ = run(name, list(th), list(v), g, eta, Fr(0), P); n += 1
                j = 1 if all(zero(s) for s in obs) else 0
                bad += j != stop_true(name, list(th), list(v), g, eta, Fr(0))
R['五 已知 β = 0：纯写回投影窗 P'] = {'状态数': n, '错判': bad}
# g 未知时的 J_e^lin
def J_e_lin_gfree(obs):
    if obs[0]['loss'] != 0: return 0
    g = obs[0]['y'] / obs[0]['x']
    return J_e_lin(obs, g)
bad = 0; n = 0
for g, eta, beta in itertools.product([Fr(0), Fr(1), Fr(2)], [Fr(0), Fr(1, 4), Fr(1)], [Fr(0), Fr(1, 2)]):
    for th in itertools.product(vals, repeat=2):
        for v in itertools.product([Fr(0), Fr(-1), Fr(1)], repeat=2):
            obs, _, _ = run(nm, list(th), list(v), g, eta, beta, P); n += 1
            bad += J_e_lin_gfree(obs) != aligned(nm, list(th), g)
R['五 J_e^lin（g 未知）'] = {'状态数': n, '错判': bad}
# J_bal(t*) = 0 只排除被判时刻：y = w·x，x = 1，g = 0，w = 1，η = 1，β = 0
o = run1(Fr(1), Fr(0), Fr(0), Fr(1), Fr(0), 2)
R['五 J_bal 只判被判时刻'] = {'t* 处写回量': str(o[0]['upd']), '下一时刻写回量与损失': [str(o[1]['upd']), str(o[1]['loss'])]}

json.dump(R, open(sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else os.path.join(HERE, 'balance_judge_checks.json'), 'w'), ensure_ascii=False, indent=1, default=str)
print(json.dumps(R, ensure_ascii=False, indent=1, default=str))
~~~

~~~json
 },
 "三 例一：两步损失为 0 而不对齐": {
~~~

【资源 024 起】源码　tune\_compat\_checks.py　SHA-256 665d2bf3864b0cecb64c536c267f6ad8f2e6d087267a87942ce24560199e7d5f

~~~python
"""调谐条件相容式 · 核算（纯 Python，精确分数，确定性）
共存模型：M_Ind（shiyin_full）中的最小脉冲网络 F（LIF、不应期、STDP 时迹、抑制性中间神经元、突触前抑制门 m）。
同一运行分两窗：
  W′（第 1—4 拍，g = 1，不门控）：前突触输入驱动放电，STDP 两次实际改写 w——迹应、组织参与（膜电位读 w）、历史动力（后一版本依赖前一版本），作实际窗的场具化证据；
  W （第 5—8 拍）：目标设定事件在 (4, 11) 把 g 写为 0；施作者 c 的上下文输入经场内中间神经元规则（P3-规）决定其放电，
     中间神经元的输出经已申报的抑制通道（Gt，part I）把 m 由 1 写为 0；W 内无事件实际改变 θ（STDP 事件照常执行而同值）。
  STDP 的 LTD 项取释放量 m·zpre（突触前抑制同时阻断释放所驱动的可塑性），W 内前突触输入第 5、7 拍到达，传输实际读取 m。
在同一 F、施作 I、窗 W、读数 Φ = m·w 上逐项求 P4 的形式值；另给 K1 对照（抑制作为膜电流，不经门）。
模型申报机制路线与许可输入，重放规则级协议求 P10（MechC）；P4 各项按 W（入口快照 (4, 11)、第 5—8 拍事件）窗化求值
（Org₀ 补承担与正依赖；Eff、Gate、Bear、Bear_G 窗化；GoalCond 取 Π = {Gt} × {m}），并给 W′ 与整次运行的对照。
输出申报值域、替代序列与承重依赖见证（所检替代值与其余背景）；第 7 拍背景驱动改为 1 的对照（规格须固定 d）。
"""
import sys, json, os, runpy, tempfile, contextlib, io
from fractions import Fraction as Fr
HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)); sys.path.insert(0, HERE); ARGV = list(sys.argv)
with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp, contextlib.redirect_stdout(io.StringIO()):
    cwd = os.getcwd(); os.chdir(tmp); sys.argv = ['full', os.path.join(tmp, 'o.json')]
    X = runpy.run_path(os.path.join(HERE, 'shiyin_full.py')); os.chdir(cwd); sys.argv = ARGV
C, RunX, E, SNN, A = X['C'], X['RunX'], X['E'], X['SNN'], X['A']
BearW, MechC, Pot, in_W_x, dep_phi = X['BearW'], X['MechC'], X['Pot'], X['in_W'], X['dep_phi']
GoalCond, Gate, Bear, Eff, Org0, vanc_at, versions_of, version_path, snap = (X[k] for k in ('GoalCond', 'Gate', 'Bear', 'Eff', 'Org0', 'vanc_at', 'versions_of', 'version_path', 'snap'))


def step(r, k, pre, drive, gated=True, inh_current=False):
    r.ev((k, 1), E(f'p{k}', 'In', 'port', 'env', [], ['zpre'], lambda v, s=pre: {'zpre': s}))
    r.ev((k, 2), E(f'd{k}', 'In', 'port', 'env', [], ['d'], lambda v, s=drive: {'d': s}))
    r.ev((k, 3), E(f'c{k}', 'In', 'port', 'c', ['g'], ['ctx'], lambda v: {'ctx': 1 - v['g']}, {'g': 1}))
    r.ev((k, 4), E(f'I{k}', 'Run', 'inter', 'F', ['ctx', 'uI'], ['uI', 'zI'], lambda v: {'uI': v['ctx'], 'zI': 1 if v['ctx'] >= 1 else 0}, {'ctx': None, 'uI': 0}))
    if gated:
        r.ev((k, 5), E(f'inh{k}', 'Gt', 'port', 'I', ['zI'], ['m'], lambda v: {'m': 1 - v['zI']}, {'zI': 1}))
    sub = (lambda v: v['zI']) if inh_current else (lambda v: 0)
    r.ev((k, 6), E(f'u{k}', 'Run', 'mem', 'F', ['u', 'm', 'w', 'zpre', 'd', 'ref', 'zI'], ['u'],
                   lambda v: {'u': max(Fr(0), v['u'] / 2 + v['m'] * v['w'] * v['zpre'] + v['d'] - sub(v)) if v['ref'] == 0 else Fr(0)}))
    r.ev((k, 7), E(f'f{k}', 'Run', 'fire', 'F', ['u', 'ref'], ['z', 'u', 'ref'],
                   lambda v: {'z': 1, 'u': 0, 'ref': 2} if v['u'] >= 1 else {'z': 0, 'u': v['u'], 'ref': max(v['ref'] - 1, 0)}))
    r.ev((k, 8), E(f'tp{k}', 'Run', 'tpre', 'F', ['xpre', 'zpre'], ['xpre'], lambda v: {'xpre': v['xpre'] / 2 + v['zpre']}, {'xpre': 1, 'zpre': 1}))
    r.ev((k, 9), E(f'sd{k}', 'Sed', 'stdp', 'F', ['w', 'xpre', 'z', 'xpost', 'zpre', 'm'], ['w'],          # 突触前抑制同时阻断释放所驱动的 LTD 项
                   lambda v: {'w': max(Fr(0), v['w'] + A * v['xpre'] * v['z'] - A * v['xpost'] * v['m'] * v['zpre'])}))
    r.ev((k, 10), E(f'tq{k}', 'Run', 'tpost', 'F', ['xpost', 'z'], ['xpost'], lambda v: {'xpost': v['xpost'] / 2 + v['z']}, {'xpost': 1, 'z': 1}))


def build_run(gated=True, inh_current=False):
    M = SNN()
    r = RunX(M, {'w': Fr(3, 2), 'm': 1, 'u': 0, 'z': 0, 'ref': 0, 'xpre': 0, 'xpost': 0, 'zpre': 0, 'd': 0, 'ctx': 0, 'uI': 0, 'zI': 0, 'g': 1})
    for k, p in zip(range(1, 5), [1, 0, 0, 1]): step(r, k, p, 0, gated, inh_current)
    r.ev((4, 11), E('G', 'Run', 'ext_goal', 'Xg', [], ['g'], lambda v: {'g': 0}))         # 目标设定（登记为目标来源）
    for k, p in zip(range(5, 9), [1, 0, 1, 0]): step(r, k, p, 0, gated, inh_current)
    return r


def phase_of(r, eid): return next(e['phase'] for e in r.events if e['id'] == eid)
W = ((5, 1), (8, 10)); Wp = ((1, 1), (4, 10))
in_W = lambda ph, w: tuple(w[0]) <= tuple(ph) <= tuple(w[1])

r = build_run()
R = {}
w_hist = [(str(ph), str(st['w']), str(st['m'])) for ph, st, _ in r.trace if ph[1] in (9, 5) or ph == (0, 0)]
# P1：W 内无事件实际改变 θ
theta_changes_W = [e['id'] for e in r.events if in_W(e['phase'], W) and any(r.M.layer[v] == 'theta' for v in e['changed'])]
sed_in_W = [e['id'] for e in r.events if in_W(e['phase'], W) and e['kind'] == 'Sed']
R['P1 物理层保持（W 内无 θ 实际改变）'] = {'W 内 θ 实际改变的事件': theta_changes_W, 'W 内照常执行而同值的 STDP 事件': sed_in_W}
# P2：m 在既有连接上，传输实际读取 m
m_changes = [(e['id'], e['kind'], e['part']) for e in r.events if 'm' in e['changed']]
mem_dep_m = [e['id'] for e in r.events if e['id'].startswith('u') and in_W(e['phase'], W) and e['deps'].get(('u', 'm')) is True]
R['P2 通透性可变'] = {'m 的支撑连接': r.M.support['m'], '实际改变 m 的事件': m_changes, 'W 内膜电位对 m 的依赖为 1 的事件': mem_dep_m}
# P3（规）：门控由场内中间神经元规则决定；读值链见证（不靠 ctrl 标签）
gt = next(e for e in r.events if 'm' in e['changed'])
R['P3 场内使役（申报的场内控制规则）'] = {'门控事件': gt['id'], '其 ctrl 申报': gt['ctrl'], '读入': gt['reads'], '对 zI 的依赖': str(gt['deps'].get(('m', 'zI'))),
    'zI 的写入者': next((e['id'], e['src'], e['part']) for e in reversed(r.events[:r.events.index(gt)]) if 'zI' in e['changed'])}
# P4：同一 F、施作 I、W、Φ 上的必要结构
s0 = snap(r, (4, 11))[0]; s1 = r.trace[-1][1]
dphi = r.M.phi(s1) - r.M.phi(s0)
R['P4 必要结构（同一实例）'] = {
    'Org₀': Org0(r), 'GoalRep／Target 声明': [r.M.goal, r.M.target], '目标来源登记': sorted(r.goal_src.items()),
    'ΔΦ_W（W 入口到末）': str(dphi), 'Eff(I)': Eff(r, 'I'), 'Gate(I)': Gate(r, 'I'), 'Bear_G(I)': Bear(r, 'I', {'Gt'}),
    'Bear(I)': Bear(r, 'I'), 'GoalCond(I；窗 W)': GoalCond(r, 'I', W=W), 'GoalCond 命中的落点': [v for v in gt['changed']]}
# 目标链绑定该门控落点：版本路径 G → c5 → I5 → inh5（m）
mv = next(k for k in versions_of(r, gt) if k[0] == 'm')
gv = ('g', 1, 'G')
R['P4 目标链（版本路径）'] = version_path(r, gv, mv)
# P5：W′ 中的实际窗场具化证据：STDP 版本（依赖输入）→ 被膜电位读入 → 后一 STDP 版本依赖之
sd = [e for e in r.events if e['id'].startswith('sd') and in_W(e['phase'], Wp) and 'w' in e['changed']]
v1 = next(k for k in versions_of(r, sd[0]) if k[0] == 'w'); v2 = next(k for k in versions_of(r, sd[-1]) if k[0] == 'w')
ins = {e['id'] for e in r.events if e['kind'] == 'In'}
trace_ok = any(isinstance(k, tuple) and len(k) == 3 and k[2] in ins and s is True for k, s in vanc_at(r, sd[0])['w'].items())
reader = next(((e['id']) for e in r.events if e['read_versions'].get('w') == v1 and 'u' in e['changed'] and e['deps'].get(('u', 'w')) is True), None)
R['P5 实际窗 W′ 的场具化证据'] = {'W′ 内 STDP 实际改写': [(e['id'], e['out']['w']) for e in sd], '迹应（版本来源含输入事件）': trace_ok,
    '组织参与（读入该版本且实际改变的膜电位事件）': reader, '历史动力（后一版本到前一版本的路径）': version_path(r, v1, v2)}
R['w、m 轨迹（入口、各拍 m 写与 STDP 后）'] = w_hist

# K1 对照：抑制作为膜电流（不经门控端口）
r2 = build_run(gated=False, inh_current=True)
s0b = snap(r2, (4, 11))[0]
R['K1 对照：抑制作为膜电流'] = {'Gate(I)': Gate(r2, 'I'), 'I 的事件数': sum(1 for e in r2.events if e['part'] == 'I'),
    'ΔΦ_W': str(r2.M.phi(r2.trace[-1][1]) - r2.M.phi(s0b)), '注': '抑制由场自身膜电位规则（Run）承担，不是门控落点'}

# ---------------- 规则级 P10 与窗化求值 ----------------
ROUTES = {'inter', 'mem', 'fire', 'tpre', 'tpost', 'stdp', 'port:Gt', 'port:In', 'ext_goal'}
def SNN_decl():
    M = SNN(); M.mech_routes = set(ROUTES); M.mech_exhaustive = True; return M
INIT = {'w': Fr(3, 2), 'm': 1, 'u': 0, 'z': 0, 'ref': 0, 'xpre': 0, 'xpost': 0, 'zpre': 0, 'd': 0, 'ctx': 0, 'uI': 0, 'zI': 0, 'g': 1}
class Rec:
    """收集 step() 生成的事件，供规则级协议重放。"""
    def __init__(self): self.steps = []
    def ev(self, ph, e): self.steps.append((ph, e))
rec = Rec()
for k, p in zip(range(1, 5), [1, 0, 0, 1]): step(rec, k, p, 0)
mc = MechC(lambda: RunX(SNN_decl(), dict(INIT)), {'W′ 协议（第 1—4 拍）': rec.steps})
R['P5 规则级：P10（申报机制路线、许可输入 port:In）'] = {'申报路线': sorted(ROUTES), 'MechC': mc}
R['P5 原模型未申报机制时 P10'] = MechC(lambda: RunX(SNN(), dict(INIT)), {'W′ 协议（第 1—4 拍）': rec.steps})

# 实际运行改用申报机制路线的模型（同一 F）；P4 窗化
def build_run2():
    r = RunX(SNN_decl(), dict(INIT))
    for k, p in zip(range(1, 5), [1, 0, 0, 1]): step(r, k, p, 0)
    r.ev((4, 11), E('G', 'Run', 'ext_goal', 'Xg', [], ['g'], lambda v: {'g': 0}))
    for k, p in zip(range(5, 9), [1, 0, 1, 0]): step(r, k, p, 0)
    return r
r2 = build_run2()
W0 = (4, 11)
s_in = snap(r2, W0)[0]; s_end = r2.trace[-1][1]
evW = lambda part, kinds=None: [e for e in r2.events if e['part'] == part and in_W(e['phase'], W) and e['changed'] and (kinds is None or e['kind'] in kinds)]
R['P4 窗化求值（W 入口 (4, 11)，W 内事件第 5—8 拍）'] = {
    'Org₀：入口接入的 θ 非空': Org0(r2, W0), 'Org₀：入口 w 对 Φ 的依赖（承担与正依赖，Pot(c)）': Pot(r2, ['w'], W0),
    'ΔΦ_W': str(r2.M.phi(s_end) - r2.M.phi(s_in)),
    'Eff_W(I)（W 内 I 有实际改变 θ/m/r 的事件）': [e['id'] for e in evW('I') if any(r2.M.layer[v] in ('theta', 'm', 'r') for v in e['changed'])],
    'Gate_W(I)': [e['id'] for e in evW('I', {'Gt'})],
    'Bear_W(I)': BearW(r2, 'I', C.KINDS, W), 'Bear_G,W(I)': BearW(r2, 'I', {'Gt'}, W),
    'GoalCond(I；Π = {Gt} × {m}；W)': GoalCond(r2, 'I', kinds={'Gt'}, layers=('m',), W=W)}
R['P4 对照：窗的取法'] = {'Bear_G 取 W′': BearW(r2, 'I', {'Gt'}, Wp), 'Bear_G 取整次运行（不窗化）': Bear(r2, 'I', {'Gt'})}
R['P1、P2 复核（申报机制路线的模型）'] = {
    'W 内 θ 实际改变': [e['id'] for e in r2.events if in_W(e['phase'], W) and any(r2.M.layer[v] == 'theta' for v in e['changed'])],
    'W 内传输对 m 依赖为 1': [e['id'] for e in r2.events if e['id'].startswith('u') and in_W(e['phase'], W) and e['deps'].get(('u', 'm')) is True]}

# ---------------- 申报值域、承重依赖与施作标识 ----------------
def dom_desc(M, v):
    d = M.dom[v]
    return ('有限值域', sorted(str(x) for x in d[1])) if d[0] == 'fin' else ('有理谓词值域', '替代序列', [str(x) for x in d[2]])
M0 = SNN_decl()
R['值域与替代序列（申报）'] = {v: dom_desc(M0, v) for v in sorted(M0.layer)}
R['读数次数'] = {k: v for k, v in M0.phi_deg.items()}
def dep_detail(run, eid, out, v):
    e = next(x for x in run.events if x['id'] == eid)
    ph = e['phase']; i = run.events.index(e); pre = run.trace[i][1]
    alts, complete = run.M.probes(v, pre[v], e['deg'].get(v))
    return {'事件': eid, '输出': out, '读入': v, '写前值': str(pre[v]), '所检替代值': [str(a) for a in alts][:3], '完备': complete, '依赖': str(e['deps'].get((out, v)))}
def phi_dep(run, ph, v):
    st = snap(run, ph)[0]; alts, complete = run.M.probes(v, st[v], run.M.phi_deg.get(v))
    return {'快照': str(ph), '变量': v, '当前值': str(st[v]), '所检替代值': [str(a) for a in alts][:3], '其余背景': {k: str(st[k]) for k in ('w', 'm')}, '依赖': str(dep_phi(run, st, v))}
R['承重依赖见证'] = {
    'zI → m（第 5 拍门控）': dep_detail(r2, 'inh5', 'm', 'zI'),
    'm → u（第 7 拍传输）': dep_detail(r2, 'u7', 'u', 'm'),
    'W 入口 w → Φ': phi_dep(r2, (4, 11), 'w'),
    '门控后 m → Φ': phi_dep(r2, (5, 5), 'm'),
    'W′：第 4 拍膜电位读第 1 拍 w 版本': dep_detail(r2, 'u4', 'u', 'w'),
    'W′：第 4 拍 STDP 读 w': dep_detail(r2, 'sd4', 'w', 'w')}
R['施作标识'] = {'门控施作': sorted({e['part'] for e in r2.events if e['kind'] == 'Gt'}), '上下文输入': sorted({e['part'] for e in r2.events if e['id'].startswith('c')})}
# 背景驱动须固定：第 7 拍 d = 1 的对照
def build_run_d7():
    r = RunX(SNN_decl(), dict(INIT))
    for k, p in zip(range(1, 5), [1, 0, 0, 1]): step(r, k, p, 0)
    r.ev((4, 11), E('G', 'Run', 'ext_goal', 'Xg', [], ['g'], lambda v: {'g': 0}))
    for k, p in zip(range(5, 9), [1, 0, 1, 0]): step(r, k, p, 1 if k == 7 else 0)
    return r
r3 = build_run_d7()
R['对照：第 7 拍 d = 1'] = {'W 内 θ 实际改变': [e['id'] for e in r3.events if in_W(e['phase'], W) and any(r3.M.layer[v] == 'theta' for v in e['changed'])], '末 w': str(r3.s['w'])}

json.dump(R, open(sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else os.path.join(HERE, 'tune_compat_checks.json'), 'w'), ensure_ascii=False, indent=1, default=str)
print(json.dumps(R, ensure_ascii=False, indent=1, default=str))
~~~

【资源 026 起】源码　lawworker.py　SHA-256 42db612f0a5ddd7b7e6e359d3213c501d7e12e1df957453e0f86c040d6267add

~~~python
"""势引律工构造式 · 核算（纯 Python，精确有理，确定性）
- 同一联合执行：一张全局事件表依相位次序执行，每个事件同时在 F 模型（fid F）与 G 模型（fid G）中执行；在对方模型中以界外分项
  （ext_G、ext_F、ext_act）出现。跨场传输是实际事件：G 的 In 端口读 F 的读数 y 与目标 g；G 的 plan 读 C 生成命令 cmd；
  F 的 Rw 端口事件（part G）读 cmd 写 w1。版本与来源在两个模型中各自连续，不以入口赋值代替来源。
- 不用探针写入：G 的形成数据来自它自己第一次调控（W1）的实际后果（读数差 obs 与自身命令的副本 dlt）。
- 各 W 入口相位的 Org₀；形成时刻（首个有效观测）与其后无变化更新分写。
- 形成史检验在同一 F 上：H1（W1 后 G 读入观测并沉积）与 H0（W1 后 G 的观测输入缺席）；跨场移植（p = 3 所形成的 C 换入 p = 2）单列。
- 固定日程负例：复演参考史 H1 的命令序列，在 H0 下不变；固定日程在保留各自形成史（观测与沉积照常）的前提下只在施作端执行。
- 每个 W 的完整必要项账；比较甲逐次（改前、改后两臂），从实际改前、改后快照分叉，保持实际残留。
"""
import sys, os, json, runpy, tempfile, contextlib, io
from fractions import Fraction as Fr
HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)); sys.path.insert(0, HERE); ARGV = list(sys.argv)
with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp, contextlib.redirect_stdout(io.StringIO()):
    cwd = os.getcwd(); os.chdir(tmp); sys.argv = ['full', os.path.join(tmp, 'o.json')]
    X = runpy.run_path(os.path.join(HERE, 'shiyin_full.py')); os.chdir(cwd); sys.argv = ARGV
C, Mx, RunX, E, GoalCond, MechC = X['C'], X['Mx'], X['RunX'], X['E'], X['GoalCond'], X['MechC']
Org0, BearW, in_W, version_path, versions_of, vanc_at, dep_phi = X['Org0'], X['BearW'], X['in_W'], X['version_path'], X['versions_of'], X['vanc_at'], X['dep_phi']

Q = ('pred', lambda x: True, [Fr(k, 4) for k in range(-12, 13)])
QNZ = ('pred', lambda x: x != 0, [Fr(k, 4) for k in range(-12, 13) if k != 0])
B01 = ('fin', {Fr(0), Fr(1)})
FV = ('w0', 'w1', 'x0', 'x1', 'u', 'y', 'g'); GV = ('c', 'Cc', 'dlt', 'obs', 'yG', 'gG', 'cmd')
R = {}
def S(x): return str(x)

def F_model(p, decl=True):
    layer = {'w0': 'theta', 'w1': 'theta', 'x0': 'r', 'x1': 'r', 'u': 'r', 'y': 'r', 'g': 'ext', **{v: 'ext' for v in GV}}
    dom = {v: Q for v in layer}; dom.update({'x0': B01, 'x1': B01, 'c': QNZ, 'Cc': QNZ, 'dlt': QNZ})
    FM = {'mem': ('Run', {'u'}), 'stdp': ('Sed', {'w1'}), 'readout': ('Run', {'y'}), 'ext_goal': ('Run', {'g'}), 'ext_G': ('Run', set(GV))}
    M = Mx(layer, dom, (lambda s, p=p: s['w0'] + p * s['w1'], {'w0': 1, 'w1': 1}), ports={'Rw': {'w0', 'w1'}, 'In': {'x0', 'x1'}},
           FM=FM, fid='F', goal={'role': 'Xg', 'var': 'g', 'field': 'F'}, target={'g': {'field': 'F'}})
    if decl: M.mech_routes = {'mem', 'stdp', 'readout', 'ext_goal', 'ext_G', 'port:In', 'port:Rw'}; M.mech_exhaustive = True
    return M
def G_model(decl=True):
    layer = {'c': 'theta', 'Cc': 'theta', 'dlt': 'r', 'obs': 'r', 'yG': 'r', 'gG': 'r', 'cmd': 'r', **{v: 'ext' for v in FV}}
    dom = {v: Q for v in layer}; dom.update({'x0': B01, 'x1': B01, 'c': QNZ, 'Cc': QNZ, 'dlt': QNZ})
    FM = {'eff': ('Run', {'dlt'}), 'lms': ('Sed', {'c'}), 'consol': ('Sed', {'Cc'}), 'plan': ('Run', {'cmd'}),
          'ext_F': ('Run', set(FV) - {'g'}), 'ext_act': ('Run', {'w1'})}
    M = Mx(layer, dom, (lambda s: s['Cc'], {'Cc': 1}), ports={'In': {'obs', 'yG', 'gG'}}, FM=FM, fid='G')
    if decl: M.mech_routes = {'eff', 'lms', 'consol', 'plan', 'ext_F', 'ext_act', 'port:In'}; M.mech_exhaustive = True
    return M
INIT = {'w0': Fr(1, 2), 'w1': Fr(1, 2), 'x0': Fr(0), 'x1': Fr(0), 'u': Fr(0), 'y': Fr(0), 'g': Fr(2),
        'c': Fr(1), 'Cc': Fr(1), 'dlt': Fr(1), 'obs': Fr(0), 'yG': Fr(0), 'gG': Fr(0), 'cmd': Fr(0)}

class Joint:
    """全局事件表的联合执行：每个事件在两个模型中各执行一次（本方模型按其种类与分项，对方模型以界外分项出现）。"""
    def __init__(self, p, init=None):
        st = {**INIT, **(init or {})}
        self.F = RunX(F_model(p), dict(st), hold=True); self.G = RunX(G_model(), dict(st), hold=False); self.log = []
    def ev(self, ph, owner, eid, kind, src, part, reads, writes, fn, deg=None):
        if owner == 'F':
            fe = E(eid, kind, src, part, reads, writes, fn, deg); ge = E(eid, 'Run', 'ext_act' if part == 'G' else 'ext_F', part, reads, writes, fn, deg)
        else:
            ge = E(eid, kind, src, part, reads, writes, fn, deg); fe = E(eid, 'Run', 'ext_G', part, reads, writes, fn, deg)
        self.F.ev(ph, fe); self.G.ev(ph, ge); self.log.append((ph, owner, eid))
        assert self.F.s == self.G.s, eid            # 两模型数值同步

def F_beat(J, k, x0, x1):
    J.ev((k, 1), 'F', f'in{k}', 'In', 'port', 'env', [], ['x0', 'x1'], lambda a, v=(x0, x1): {'x0': Fr(v[0]), 'x1': Fr(v[1])})
    J.ev((k, 2), 'F', f'mem{k}', 'Run', 'mem', 'F', ['w0', 'w1', 'x0', 'x1'], ['u'], lambda a: {'u': a['w0'] * a['x0'] + a['w1'] * a['x1']}, {'w0': 1, 'w1': 1})
    J.ev((k, 3), 'F', f'stdp{k}', 'Sed', 'stdp', 'F', ['w1', 'x1', 'u'], ['w1'], lambda a: {'w1': a['w1'] + (Fr(1, 8) if a['x1'] == 1 and a['u'] >= 1 else 0)}, {'w1': 1})
def W_stage(J, k, p, observe=True, script=None, reset_C=False):
    """一个调控窗：F 读出 → G 经 In 读 y、g → G 的 plan 读 C 出令 → F 的 Rw 端口事件（part G）执行 → F 读出 → G 读观测、副本、沉积。"""
    J.ev((k, 1), 'F', f'ro{k}', 'Run', 'readout', 'F', ['w0', 'w1'], ['y'], lambda a, p=p: {'y': a['w0'] + p * a['w1']}, {'w0': 1, 'w1': 1})
    if reset_C:
        J.ev((k, 2), 'G', f'reset{k}', 'Sed', 'consol', 'test', [], ['Cc'], lambda a: {'Cc': INIT['Cc']})          # 检验干预
    if script is None:
        J.ev((k, 3), 'G', f'iny{k}', 'In', 'port', 'G', ['y'], ['yG'], lambda a: {'yG': a['y']}, {'y': 1})
        J.ev((k, 4), 'G', f'ing{k}', 'In', 'port', 'G', ['g'], ['gG'], lambda a: {'gG': a['g']}, {'g': 1})
        J.ev((k, 5), 'G', f'plan{k}', 'Run', 'plan', 'G', ['gG', 'yG', 'Cc'], ['cmd'], lambda a: {'cmd': (a['gG'] - a['yG']) / a['Cc']}, {'gG': 1, 'yG': 1})
        J.ev((k, 6), 'F', f'act{k}', 'Rw', 'port', 'G', ['w1', 'cmd'], ['w1'], lambda a: {'w1': a['w1'] + a['cmd']}, {'w1': 1, 'cmd': 1})
    else:
        J.ev((k, 6), 'F', f'act{k}', 'Rw', 'port', 'S', ['w1'], ['w1'], lambda a, d=script: {'w1': a['w1'] + d}, {'w1': 1})
    J.ev((k, 7), 'F', f'ro2_{k}', 'Run', 'readout', 'F', ['w0', 'w1'], ['y'], lambda a, p=p: {'y': a['w0'] + p * a['w1']}, {'w0': 1, 'w1': 1})
    if script is None and observe and (not isinstance(J, Joint) or J.F.s['cmd'] != 0):     # G 的观测与沉积以本窗有非零命令为触发
        J.ev((k, 8), 'G', f'obs{k}', 'In', 'port', 'G', ['y', 'yG'], ['obs'], lambda a: {'obs': a['y'] - a['yG']}, {'y': 1, 'yG': 1})
        J.ev((k, 9), 'G', f'eff{k}', 'Run', 'eff', 'G', ['cmd'], ['dlt'], lambda a: {'dlt': a['cmd']}, {'cmd': 1})
        J.ev((k, 10), 'G', f'lms{k}', 'Sed', 'lms', 'G', ['c', 'obs', 'dlt'], ['c'], lambda a: {'c': a['c'] + (a['obs'] - a['c'] * a['dlt']) / a['dlt']}, {'c': 1, 'obs': 1})
        J.ev((k, 11), 'G', f'con{k}', 'Sed', 'consol', 'G', ['Cc', 'c'], ['Cc'], lambda a: {'Cc': a['c']}, {'c': 1, 'Cc': 1})

def episode(p, hist='H1', script=None, reset_at=None):
    """W1（第 20 拍，形成调控）→ 两拍正常运行 → W2（第 30 拍）→ 两拍 → W3（第 40 拍）。
    H1：W1 后 G 读入观测并沉积；H0：W1 后 G 的观测输入缺席（不形成）。W2、W3 中 G 照常读观测（若出令）。"""
    J = Joint(p)
    sc = script or {}
    W_stage(J, 20, p, observe=(hist == 'H1'), script=sc.get(20))
    for k in (25, 26): F_beat(J, k, 1, 1)
    W_stage(J, 30, p, script=sc.get(30), reset_C=(reset_at == 30))
    for k in (35, 36): F_beat(J, k, 1, 1)
    W_stage(J, 40, p, script=sc.get(40))
    return J
def acts(J): return [(e['id'], S(e['out']['w1']), S(J.F.trace[i + 2][1]['y'])) for i, e in enumerate(J.F.events) if e['id'].startswith('act')]

# ---------------- 主构造：p = 2，H1 ----------------
J1 = episode(2)
R['主构造（p=2，H1）：各次施作 (事件, 写后 w1, 写后读数)'] = acts(J1)
R['主构造：G 的 C 的版本与值'] = [(str(k), S(J1.G.trace[i + 1][1]['Cc'])) for i, e in enumerate(J1.G.events) if 'Cc' in e['changed'] for k in [e['id']]]
# 跨场来源证书：act30、act40 写出的 w1 版本来源中含 G 的 C 版本（F 模型内）与 G 模型中 cmd 的来源含 Cc 版本
def src_cert(J, aid):
    i = next(i for i, e in enumerate(J.F.events) if e['id'] == aid)
    v = J.F.vtrace[i + 1]['w1']
    keys = sorted(str(k) for k, s in v.items() if s is True and isinstance(k, tuple) and k[0] in ('Cc', 'c', 'HOLD'))
    return keys
R['跨场来源（F 模型）：W2、W3 施作所写 w1 版本的来源中 C、c 的版本与目标持有锚'] = {a: src_cert(J1, a) for a in ('act30', 'act40')}
gi = next(i for i, e in enumerate(J1.G.events) if e['id'] == 'plan30')
R['跨场来源（G 模型）：plan30 读入的 Cc 版本、cmd 来源中的 Cc 版本'] = (str(J1.G.events[gi]['read_versions'].get('Cc')),
    sorted(str(k) for k, s in J1.G.vtrace[gi + 1]['cmd'].items() if s is True and isinstance(k, tuple) and k[0] == 'Cc'))

# ---------------- C1、C2 资格 ----------------
class Rec:
    def __init__(self): self.steps = []
    def ev(self, ph, e): self.steps.append((ph, e))
class RecJ:
    def __init__(self): self.F, self.G = Rec(), Rec()
    def ev(self, ph, owner, eid, kind, src, part, reads, writes, fn, deg=None):
        if owner == 'F': self.F.ev(ph, E(eid, kind, src, part, reads, writes, fn, deg)); self.G.ev(ph, E(eid, 'Run', 'ext_act' if part == 'G' else 'ext_F', part, reads, writes, fn, deg))
        else: self.G.ev(ph, E(eid, kind, src, part, reads, writes, fn, deg)); self.F.ev(ph, E(eid, 'Run', 'ext_G', part, reads, writes, fn, deg))
rj = RecJ(); W_stage(rj, 20, 2)
for k in (25, 26): F_beat(rj, k, 1, 1)
R['C1 F 规则级 P10（同一 F：p = 2）'] = MechC(lambda: RunX(F_model(2), dict(INIT), hold=True), {'联合协议（W1 + 两拍）': rj.F.steps})
R['C1 F 机制未申报时'] = MechC(lambda: RunX(F_model(2, False), dict(INIT), hold=True), {'联合协议（W1 + 两拍）': rj.F.steps})
R['C2 G 规则级 P10'] = MechC(lambda: RunX(G_model(), dict(INIT)), {'联合协议（W1 + 两拍）': rj.G.steps})
R['C2 G 机制未申报时'] = MechC(lambda: RunX(G_model(False), dict(INIT)), {'联合协议（W1 + 两拍）': rj.G.steps})
# 实际窗：F 两次正常拍的版本路径；G 的形成（W1）
sd = [e for e in J1.F.events if e['id'] in ('stdp25', 'stdp26')]
fv1 = next(k for k in versions_of(J1.F, sd[0]) if k[0] == 'w1'); fv2 = next(k for k in versions_of(J1.F, sd[1]) if k[0] == 'w1')
R['C1 F 实际窗（第 25、26 拍）：版本路径'] = version_path(J1.F, fv1, fv2)
lm = next(e for e in J1.G.events if e['id'] == 'lms20'); cs = next(e for e in J1.G.events if e['id'] == 'con20')
gv1 = next(k for k in versions_of(J1.G, lm) if k[0] == 'c'); gv2 = next(k for k in versions_of(J1.G, cs) if k[0] == 'Cc')
ins = {e['id'] for e in J1.G.events if e['kind'] == 'In'}
R['C2 G 实际窗（W1 形成）'] = {'c 版本来源含 G 的输入事件': sorted(str(k) for k, s in vanc_at(J1.G, lm)['c'].items() if isinstance(k, tuple) and len(k) == 3 and k[2] in ins and s is True),
                            '版本路径 c → C': version_path(J1.G, gv1, gv2),
                            '基线（入口 C）、形成值、分辨 ε_L、留存窗': (S(INIT['Cc']), S(J1.G.s['Cc']), '0', 'W1 末至 W3 末 C 不变')}

# ---------------- C3 截取：隔离案例与纤维 ----------------
HELD = [Fr(3, 2), Fr(-2), Fr(1, 3)]
seen = {}
for i, e in enumerate(J1.G.events):
    if e['id'].startswith('lms'): st = J1.G.trace[i][1]; seen[st['dlt']] = st['obs']
R['C3 形成期所见 (δ, ΔΦ)'] = [(S(d), S(o)) for d, o in seen.items()]
R['C3 隔离案例：真值 p·δ、G 预言 C·δ、查表有无'] = [(S(d), S(2 * d), S(J1.G.s['Cc'] * d), d in seen) for d in HELD]
J3 = episode(3)
R['C3 另一被引场 p = 3：C 与隔离案例预言'] = (S(J3.G.s['Cc']), [(S(d), S(3 * d), S(J3.G.s['Cc'] * d)) for d in HELD])

# ---------------- C5 必要项账（每个 W） ----------------
def forms(J, W):
    r = J.F; sub = [e for e in r.events if e['part'] == 'G' and in_W(e['phase'], W)]
    pre = [st for ph, st, _ in r.trace if ph < W[0]][-1]; post = [st for ph, st, _ in r.trace if in_W(ph, W)][-1]
    return {'Eff_W': any(e['changed'] and any(r.M.layer[v] != 'ext' for v in e['changed']) for e in sub), 'L2': sorted({e['kind'] for e in sub if e['changed'] and e['kind'] != 'Run'}),
            'Org0': Org0(r), '入口组织承担（Φ 对 w1）': str(dep_phi(r, pre, 'w1')), 'ΔΦ_W': S(r.M.phi(post) - r.M.phi(pre)),
            'Rewrite_W': any(e['kind'] == 'Rw' and e['changed'] for e in sub), 'Bear_R,W': BearW(r, 'G', {'Rw'}, W), 'GoalCond_W': GoalCond(r, 'G', kinds={'Rw'}, W=W),
            'GoalRep/Target': (r.M.goal['field'], r.M.target['g']['field'], r.M.fid), '边界': 'G 在 F 外（变量不交，经端口相接）', '事件资格': '不签'}
R['C5 必要项账'] = {'W1（形成调控，C = 入口值）': forms(J1, ((20, 0), (20, 99))), 'W2（据 L）': forms(J1, ((30, 0), (30, 99))), 'W3（据 L）': forms(J1, ((40, 0), (40, 99)))}

# ---------------- C6 历史承担 ----------------
J0 = episode(2, 'H0')
R['C6(a) 同一 F(p=2)：H1 与 H0 的各次施作、W2 前 C'] = {'H1': (acts(J1), S([st for ph, st, _ in J1.G.trace if ph < (30, 0)][-1]['Cc'])),
                                               'H0': (acts(J0), S([st for ph, st, _ in J0.G.trace if ph < (30, 0)][-1]['Cc']))}
JT = Joint(2, init={'c': Fr(3), 'Cc': Fr(3)})
W_stage(JT, 20, 2, observe=False)
R['C6 跨场移植对照（p = 3 所形成的 C = 3 换入 p = 2 的 G，不再形成）：W1 施作'] = acts(JT)
JR = episode(2, reset_at=30)
R['C6(b) 检验干预：W2 前把 C 复位为入口值 1（H1 下）'] = acts(JR)
ref_cmds = [Fr(J1.F.events[i]['out']['w1']) - Fr(J1.F.trace[i][1]['w1']) for i, e in enumerate(J1.F.events) if e['id'].startswith('act')]
sched = {20: ref_cmds[0], 30: ref_cmds[1], 40: ref_cmds[2]}
JS1 = episode(2, 'H1', script=sched); JS0 = episode(2, 'H0', script=sched)
R['C6(e) 固定日程（复演 H1 的写入量）'] = {'写入量': [S(x) for x in ref_cmds], 'H1 下': acts(JS1), 'H0 下': acts(JS0), '在 H1、H0 下写入相同': acts(JS1) == acts(JS0)}
R['C6(c) 形成证据与在用证据'] = {'形成（G）': ['obs20', 'eff20', 'lms20', 'con20'], '在用（G→F）': ['plan30 读 Cc', 'act30 读 cmd', 'plan40 读 Cc', 'act40 读 cmd']}

# ---------------- C7 比较甲（逐次） ----------------
def prot(w0, w1, n=4):
    return [(S(w0 * 1 + w1 * 0), int(w0 >= 1)) for _ in range(n)]
cmp7 = {}
for i, e in enumerate(J1.F.events):
    if e['id'].startswith('act'):
        pre = J1.F.trace[i][1]; post = J1.F.trace[i + 1][1]
        cmp7[e['id']] = prot(pre['w0'], pre['w1']) == prot(post['w0'], post['w1'])
R['C7 比较甲：各次施作改前、改后两臂在 𝓘_prot = {(1,0)} 上 4 拍活动相同（残留 u = 0、输入史相同）'] = cmp7
R['C7 各调控窗内 F 无输入事件'] = all(not (e['kind'] == 'In' and e['part'] == 'env') for e in J1.F.events if any(in_W(e['phase'], ((k, 0), (k, 99))) for k in (20, 30, 40)))

# ======================= 入口相位、形成时刻、比较甲与固定日程 =======================
def forms3(J, W):
    r = J.F; ph0 = [ph for ph, _, _ in r.trace if ph < W[0]][-1]
    d = forms(J, W); d.pop('Org0', None); d[f'Org0（W 入口相位 {ph0}）'] = Org0(r, ph0); return d
R['C5 必要项账（Org₀ 按各 W 入口相位）'] = {'W1': forms3(J1, ((20, 0), (20, 99))), 'W2': forms3(J1, ((30, 0), (30, 99))), 'W3': forms3(J1, ((40, 0), (40, 99)))}
R['C3 形成时刻：各 lms 事件是否实际改变 c（首个有效观测形成斜率，其后为用后验证）'] = [(e['id'], bool(e['changed'])) for e in J1.G.events if e['src'] == 'lms']
# 比较甲——从实际改前、改后快照分叉，保持实际残留 u，各臂执行四拍输入 (1, 0)
def fork_F(st, p=2, n=4):
    r = RunX(F_model(p), {k: st[k] for k in INIT}, hold=True)
    out = []
    for j in range(n):
        k = 100 + j
        r.ev((k, 1), E(f'pi{k}', 'In', 'port', 'env', [], ['x0', 'x1'], lambda a: {'x0': Fr(1), 'x1': Fr(0)}))
        r.ev((k, 2), E(f'pm{k}', 'Run', 'mem', 'F', ['w0', 'w1', 'x0', 'x1'], ['u'], lambda a: {'u': a['w0'] * a['x0'] + a['w1'] * a['x1']}, {'w0': 1, 'w1': 1}))
        r.ev((k, 3), E(f'ps{k}', 'Sed', 'stdp', 'F', ['w1', 'x1', 'u'], ['w1'], lambda a: {'w1': a['w1'] + (Fr(1, 8) if a['x1'] == 1 and a['u'] >= 1 else 0)}, {'w1': 1}))
        out.append(S(r.s['u']))
    return out, S(r.s['w1'])
c7 = {}
for i, e in enumerate(J1.F.events):
    if e['id'].startswith('act'):
        pre, post = J1.F.trace[i][1], J1.F.trace[i + 1][1]
        a_pre, a_post = fork_F(pre), fork_F(post)
        c7[e['id']] = {'实际残留 u（两臂共同）': S(pre['u']), '改前臂 4 拍膜驱动': a_pre[0], '改后臂 4 拍膜驱动': a_post[0], '相同': a_pre[0] == a_post[0]}
R['C7 比较甲（实际残留、协议：四拍输入 (1, 0)；保护读数为膜驱动 u）'] = c7
# 固定日程——各史照常观测与沉积，只在施作端以同一日程执行
def W_stage_sched(J, k, p, observe, d):
    J.ev((k, 1), 'F', f'ro{k}', 'Run', 'readout', 'F', ['w0', 'w1'], ['y'], lambda a, p=p: {'y': a['w0'] + p * a['w1']}, {'w0': 1, 'w1': 1})
    J.ev((k, 3), 'G', f'iny{k}', 'In', 'port', 'G', ['y'], ['yG'], lambda a: {'yG': a['y']}, {'y': 1})
    J.ev((k, 4), 'G', f'ing{k}', 'In', 'port', 'G', ['g'], ['gG'], lambda a: {'gG': a['g']}, {'g': 1})
    J.ev((k, 5), 'G', f'plan{k}', 'Run', 'plan', 'G', ['gG', 'yG', 'Cc'], ['cmd'], lambda a: {'cmd': (a['gG'] - a['yG']) / a['Cc']}, {'gG': 1, 'yG': 1})
    J.ev((k, 6), 'F', f'act{k}', 'Rw', 'port', 'S', ['w1'], ['w1'], lambda a, d=d: {'w1': a['w1'] + d}, {'w1': 1})
    J.ev((k, 7), 'F', f'ro2_{k}', 'Run', 'readout', 'F', ['w0', 'w1'], ['y'], lambda a, p=p: {'y': a['w0'] + p * a['w1']}, {'w0': 1, 'w1': 1})
    if observe and J.F.s['cmd'] != 0:
        J.ev((k, 8), 'G', f'obs{k}', 'In', 'port', 'G', ['y', 'yG'], ['obs'], lambda a: {'obs': a['y'] - a['yG']}, {'y': 1, 'yG': 1})
        J.ev((k, 9), 'G', f'eff{k}', 'Run', 'eff', 'G', ['cmd'], ['dlt'], lambda a: {'dlt': a['cmd']}, {'cmd': 1})
        J.ev((k, 10), 'G', f'lms{k}', 'Sed', 'lms', 'G', ['c', 'obs', 'dlt'], ['c'], lambda a: {'c': a['c'] + (a['obs'] - a['c'] * a['dlt']) / a['dlt']}, {'c': 1, 'obs': 1})
        J.ev((k, 11), 'G', f'con{k}', 'Sed', 'consol', 'G', ['Cc', 'c'], ['Cc'], lambda a: {'Cc': a['c']}, {'c': 1, 'Cc': 1})
def episode_sched(p, hist):
    J = Joint(p)
    W_stage_sched(J, 20, p, hist == 'H1', sched[20])
    for k in (25, 26): F_beat(J, k, 1, 1)
    c_w2 = S(J.G.s['Cc'])
    W_stage_sched(J, 30, p, True, sched[30])
    for k in (35, 36): F_beat(J, k, 1, 1)
    W_stage_sched(J, 40, p, True, sched[40])
    return J, c_w2
JSs1, cS1 = episode_sched(2, 'H1'); JSs0, cS0 = episode_sched(2, 'H0')
R['C6(e) 固定日程（施作端；各史观测与沉积照常）'] = {'W2 入口 C（H1、H0）': (cS1, cS0), '写入量': [S(x) for x in ref_cmds],
    'H1 下施作': acts(JSs1), 'H0 下施作': acts(JSs0), '两史施作相同': acts(JSs1) == acts(JSs0),
    '对照：律工在 H1、H0 下的 W2 施作': (acts(J1)[1], acts(J0)[1])}

def fix(o):
    if isinstance(o, dict): return {str(k): fix(v) for k, v in o.items()}
    if isinstance(o, (list, tuple)): return [fix(x) for x in o]
    if isinstance(o, Fr): return str(o)
    return o
json.dump(fix(R), open('lawworker.json', 'w'), ensure_ascii=False, indent=1, sort_keys=True, default=str)
print(json.dumps(fix(R), ensure_ascii=False, indent=1, default=str))
~~~

~~~python
"""调宿收束极限式 · 核算（纯 Python，精确有理）
M_EDR 参数线性子类：y = θ·φ(x)，标签 g·x，q = (θ·φ − g·x)·φ，v ← β·v + q，θ ← θ − η·v（第七篇第四节；第十篇 4.1）。
- λ = 1 时各相位截面极限与全序列不收敛；带动量单模式的边界（根 −1、−β）与越界（激发／未激发）。
- 边界上 −β 纯模态入口收敛；β = 0 越界两根；不可实现仿射漂移（φ ≡ 1、标签 (1,2)、η = 2）。
- β = 0、v0⊥ ≠ 0 无 V⊥ 位移；β = 0、ηs = 1 一步对齐；不可实现越界发散例。
各项对应式文正文的命题编号。
"""
import json
from fractions import Fraction as Fr
from itertools import product

def dot(a, b): return sum(x * y for x, y in zip(a, b))
def S(x): return str(x)

def run(phis, xs, g, eta, beta, th, v, K):
    """返回逐步 (θ_k, v_k)，k = 0..K；周期样本 (φ_j, x_j)。"""
    out = [(tuple(th), tuple(v))]
    P = len(phis)
    for k in range(K):
        ph, x = phis[k % P], xs[k % P]
        r = dot(th, ph) - g * x
        v = [beta * vi + r * pi for vi, pi in zip(v, ph)]
        th = [ti - eta * vi for ti, vi in zip(th, v)]
        out.append((tuple(th), tuple(v)))
    return out

def aligned(th, phis, xs, g): return all(dot(th, p) == g * x for p, x in zip(phis, xs))
def bal(th, v, phis, xs, g, eta, beta): return aligned(th, phis, xs, g) and all(eta * beta * vi == 0 for vi in v)
def first(seq, pred):
    for k, s in enumerate(seq):
        if pred(*s): return k
    return None

R = {}

# ---------- 命题 1：三种首次时刻（例） ----------
tr = run([(Fr(1),)], [Fr(1)], Fr(0), Fr(1, 2), Fr(1, 2), [Fr(1)], [Fr(2)], 6)
R['命题1 例 y=wx, g=0, η=β=1/2, (w0,v0)=(1,2)'] = {
    '轨迹': [(S(t[0][0]), S(t[1][0])) for t in tr[:4]],
    'k_e': first(tr, lambda th, v: aligned(th, [(Fr(1),)], [Fr(1)], Fr(0))),
    'k_bal（至 6 步）': first(tr, lambda th, v: bal(th, v, [(Fr(1),)], [Fr(1)], Fr(0), Fr(1, 2), Fr(1, 2)))}

# ---------- 命题 2：β = 0 前缀零空间（嵌套）与有限到达 ----------
def matmul(A, B): return [[sum(A[i][k] * B[k][j] for k in range(len(B))) for j in range(len(B[0]))] for i in range(len(A))]
def Aj(phi, eta):
    n = len(phi); return [[(1 if i == j else 0) - eta * phi[i] * phi[j] for j in range(n)] for i in range(n)]
def rank(M):
    M = [row[:] for row in M]; r = 0; n = len(M[0])
    for c in range(n):
        piv = next((i for i in range(r, len(M)) if M[i][c] != 0), None)
        if piv is None: continue
        M[r], M[piv] = M[piv], M[r]
        for i in range(len(M)):
            if i != r and M[i][c] != 0:
                f = M[i][c] / M[r][c]; M[i] = [a - f * b for a, b in zip(M[i], M[r])]
        r += 1
    return r
def prefix_kernel_dims(phis, eta, steps):
    n = len(phis[0]); B = [[1 if i == j else 0 for j in range(n)] for i in range(n)]; dims = []
    for k in range(steps):
        B = matmul(Aj(phis[k % len(phis)], eta), B); dims.append(n - rank(B))
    return dims
# 正交特征 e1、3e2，η 使 η‖φ1‖² = 1：第一步零化 e1 分量
ph2 = [(Fr(1), Fr(0)), (Fr(0), Fr(3))]
R['命题2 前缀零空间维数（嵌套不减）'] = {
    'φ = e1, 3e2；η = 1（η‖φ1‖²=1）': prefix_kernel_dims(ph2, Fr(1), 6),
    'φ = e1, 3e2；η = 1/9（η‖φ2‖²=1）': prefix_kernel_dims(ph2, Fr(1, 9), 6),
    'φ = e1, 3e2；η = 1/10（无奇异步）': prefix_kernel_dims(ph2, Fr(1, 10), 6),
    'φ = (1,1),(1,−1)；η = 1/2（两步皆奇异，正交）': prefix_kernel_dims([(Fr(1), Fr(1)), (Fr(1), Fr(-1))], Fr(1, 2), 4)}
# 首次到达相位由前缀决定：η = 1/9 时 e1 分量非零者永不到达，e1 分量为零者在第 2 步（相位 2）到达
for th0, name in (([Fr(0), Fr(5)], 'θ0 = (0,5)'), ([Fr(1), Fr(5)], 'θ0 = (1,5)')):
    tr = run(ph2, [Fr(1), Fr(1)], Fr(0), Fr(1, 9), Fr(0), th0, [Fr(0), Fr(0)], 8)
    R.setdefault('命题2 首次到达（g=0，目标 θ*=0）', {})[name] = first(tr, lambda th, v: aligned(th, ph2, [Fr(1), Fr(1)], Fr(0)))

# ---------- 命题 3：谱边界，收束参数域可不连通 ----------
def f_scalar(eta, lams):
    p = Fr(1)
    for l in lams: p *= (1 - eta * l)
    return p
lams = [Fr(1), Fr(9)]   # y = w·x，D = {1, 3}，周期 (1, 3)
R['命题3 标量周期因子 f(η) = (1−η)(1−9η)'] = {S(e): S(f_scalar(e, lams)) for e in (Fr(1, 10), Fr(1, 5), Fr(1, 2), Fr(1), Fr(10, 9), Fr(6, 5))}
def scalar_run(eta, w0, K, g=Fr(2)):
    tr = run([(Fr(1),), (Fr(3),)], [Fr(1), Fr(3)], g, eta, Fr(0), [w0], [Fr(0)], K)
    return [S(t[0][0] - g) for t in tr[::2][:6]]
R['命题3 周期截面误差 w−g（g=2, w0=3）'] = {'η=1/10（收束）': scalar_run(Fr(1, 10), Fr(3), 10), 'η=1/2（发散）': scalar_run(Fr(1, 2), Fr(3), 10),
                                    'η=1（第 1 步到达）': scalar_run(Fr(1), Fr(3), 10), 'η=10/9（截面不动、不到达）': scalar_run(Fr(10, 9), Fr(3), 10)}
tr = run([(Fr(1),), (Fr(3),)], [Fr(1), Fr(3)], Fr(2), Fr(10, 9), Fr(0), [Fr(3)], [Fr(0)], 6)
R['命题3 η=10/9 周期内轨迹（目标可实现而不收束）'] = [S(t[0][0]) for t in tr]
# ρ>1 而未激发：正交 e1、3e2，η = 1/2：e2 方向因子 −7/2
R['命题3 ρ>1 未激发分量'] = {'θ0=(3,0)，g=0': [tuple(S(c) for c in t[0]) for t in run(ph2, [Fr(1), Fr(1)], Fr(0), Fr(1, 2), Fr(0), [Fr(3), Fr(0)], [Fr(0), Fr(0)], 6)[::2]],
                         'θ0=(3,1)，g=0': [tuple(S(c) for c in t[0]) for t in run(ph2, [Fr(1), Fr(1)], Fr(0), Fr(1, 2), Fr(0), [Fr(3), Fr(1)], [Fr(0), Fr(0)], 6)[::2]]}
# 格点上 |f|<1 区域（η = i/90）——区间不连通的有理见证
grid = [Fr(i, 90) for i in range(1, 106)]
inside = [abs(f_scalar(e, lams)) < 1 for e in grid]
segs = []; cur = None
for e, b in zip(grid, inside):
    if b and cur is None: cur = [e, e]
    elif b: cur[1] = e
    elif cur is not None: segs.append((S(cur[0]), S(cur[1]))); cur = None
if cur: segs.append((S(cur[0]), S(cur[1])))
R['命题3 η=i/90 格点上收束段（|f|<1）'] = segs

# ---------- 命题 4：单模式带动量 ----------
def hb_stable(es, beta):
    """(e,v) 映射 N = [[1−es, −ηβ],[s, β]]（坐标取 φ 方向且 s=‖φ‖²=1 时 es = η）；以 Jury 条件判 |z|<1。"""
    tr_ = 1 + beta - es; det = beta
    return abs(det) < 1 and abs(tr_) < 1 + det
R['命题4 单模式带动量收束条件 0<ηs<2(1+β) 的格点核对'] = all(
    hb_stable(es, b) == (0 < es < 2 * (1 + b)) for es in [Fr(i, 8) for i in range(0, 40)] for b in [Fr(0), Fr(1, 4), Fr(1, 2), Fr(3, 4)])
# V⊥ 动量位移：θ ∈ R²，φ = e1（V = span e1），v0⊥ = 2，η = 1，β = 1/2
tr = run([(Fr(1), Fr(0))], [Fr(1)], Fr(0), Fr(1), Fr(1, 2), [Fr(1, 2), Fr(0)], [Fr(0), Fr(2)], 40)
R['命题4 V⊥ 动量位移（η=1, β=1/2, v0⊥=2）'] = {'θ⊥ 第 1—6 步': [S(t[0][1]) for t in tr[1:7]], '第 40 步与极限 −2 之差': S(tr[40][0][1] + 2),
                                           '公式 −ηβv0⊥/(1−β)': S(-Fr(1) * Fr(1, 2) * 2 / (1 - Fr(1, 2))),
                                           'k_bal（至 40 步）': first(tr, lambda th, v: bal(th, v, [(Fr(1), Fr(0))], [Fr(1)], Fr(0), Fr(1), Fr(1, 2)))}
# β>0 时 k_e 的例外集为可数条直线之并：枚举 (e0, v0) 小格点，记首次 e_k = 0 的 k
def ke(e0, v0, eta, beta, K=12):
    tr = run([(Fr(1),)], [Fr(1)], Fr(0), eta, beta, [e0], [v0], K)
    return first(tr, lambda th, v: th[0] == 0)
cnt = {}
for e0, v0 in product([Fr(i, 2) for i in range(-4, 5)], repeat=2):
    k = ke(e0, v0, Fr(1, 2), Fr(1, 2)); cnt[str(k)] = cnt.get(str(k), 0) + 1
R['命题4 β=1/2、η=1/2 格点 (e0,v0) 上 k_e 分布'] = dict(sorted(cnt.items()))
R['命题4 k_bal 有限 ⇔ 入口已平衡（N 可逆，det = β）：格点上 k_bal≠0 而有限者的个数'] = sum(
    1 for e0, v0 in product([Fr(i, 2) for i in range(-4, 5)], repeat=2)
    if (lambda k: k not in (None, 0))(first(run([(Fr(1),)], [Fr(1)], Fr(0), Fr(1, 2), Fr(1, 2), [e0], [v0], 12),
                                             lambda th, v: bal(th, v, [(Fr(1),)], [Fr(1)], Fr(0), Fr(1, 2), Fr(1, 2)))))

# ---------- 命题 5：不可达 ----------
# 常值特征 φ(x)=1、目标 g·x、D={1,2}：不可实现；周期 (1,2) 与 (1,1,2)
def section_fix(order, g, eta):
    """周期截面不动点（标量仿射映射 w ↦ a w + b）。"""
    a, b = Fr(1), Fr(0)
    for x in order: a, b = a * (1 - eta), b * (1 - eta) + eta * g * x
    return b / (1 - a)
R['命题5 常值特征截面不动点（g=1）'] = {f'周期 {o}，η={S(e)}': S(section_fix(o, Fr(1), e)) for o in ((1, 2), (1, 1, 2)) for e in (Fr(1, 2), Fr(1, 10), Fr(1, 100), Fr(1, 1000))}
R['命题5 η→0⁺ 极限（加权最小二乘：周期内样本均值）'] = {'(1,2)': '3/2', '(1,1,2)': '4/3'}
# 全程不对齐、写回非零（η>0，K6）
tr = run([(Fr(1),), (Fr(1),)], [Fr(1), Fr(2)], Fr(1), Fr(1, 2), Fr(0), [Fr(0)], [Fr(0)], 40)
R['命题5 不可达：40 步内对齐次数、零写回次数'] = (sum(aligned(t[0], [(Fr(1),), (Fr(1),)], [Fr(1), Fr(2)], Fr(1)) for t in tr),
                                       sum(1 for a, b in zip(tr, tr[1:]) if a[0] == b[0]))
# 缺 K6 的止调而不对齐（例）：y = w(x + x²)，D = {−1, 1}，目标 x，w = 1/2
tr = run([(Fr(0),), (Fr(2),)], [Fr(-1), Fr(1)], Fr(1), Fr(1), Fr(0), [Fr(1, 2)], [Fr(0)], 8)
R['命题5 缺 K6 例：w 轨迹、对齐否'] = ([S(t[0][0]) for t in tr], aligned(tr[-1][0], [(Fr(0),), (Fr(2),)], [Fr(-1), Fr(1)], Fr(1)))

# ---------------- 截面极限与动量边界 ----------------
# λ = 1（η = 10/9，周期 (1,3)，g = 2，w0 = 3）：各相位截面极限 B_j e*，全序列不收敛
tr = run([(Fr(1),), (Fr(3),)], [Fr(1), Fr(3)], Fr(2), Fr(10, 9), Fr(0), [Fr(3)], [Fr(0)], 12)
ph0 = [t[0][0] - 2 for t in tr[0::2]]; ph1 = [t[0][0] - 2 for t in tr[1::2]]
R['命题3′ λ=1：相位 0 截面误差、相位 1 截面误差、B_1 e* = (1 − η·1)·e*'] = ([S(x) for x in ph0[:4]], [S(x) for x in ph1[:4]], S((1 - Fr(10, 9)) * ph0[0]))
R['命题3′ λ=1：两相位极限不同 ⇒ 全序列不收敛'] = ph0[-1] != ph1[-1]
# 正交两模式、λ=−1 与收束模态叠加：φ = e1、e2，η 使 e1 因子 −1（η=2）而 e2 用 3e2 时因子 1−18 发散——改取 φ = e1、(1/2)e2：η=2 时因子 −1 与 1/2
phm = [(Fr(1), Fr(0)), (Fr(0), Fr(1, 2))]
tr = run(phm, [Fr(1), Fr(1)], Fr(0), Fr(2), Fr(0), [Fr(1), Fr(1)], [Fr(0), Fr(0)], 8)
R['命题3′ 模态叠加（e1 因子 −1、e2 因子 1/2）：周期截面'] = [tuple(S(c) for c in t[0]) for t in tr[0::2]]
# 带动量单模式边界 ηs = 2(1+β)：根 −1、−β
def hb(e0, v0, eta, beta, K):
    tr = run([(Fr(1),)], [Fr(1)], Fr(0), eta, beta, [e0], [v0], K)
    return [(S(t[0][0]), S(t[1][0])) for t in tr]
R['命题4′ 边界 η=3、β=1/2、s=1：(e0,v0)=(1,−2/3)（根 −1 的特征向量）'] = hb(Fr(1), Fr(-2, 3), Fr(3), Fr(1, 2), 4)
R['命题4′ 边界：(e0,v0)=(1,0)，第 20、21 步（趋于二周期）'] = hb(Fr(1), Fr(0), Fr(3), Fr(1, 2), 21)[20:]
# 越界：β=1/2、ηs=15/4，根 −1/4 与 −2（有理）；稳定特征向量 (1, −4/3)
R['命题4′ 越界 η=15/4：稳定特征向量入口 (1,−4/3) 前 4 步'] = hb(Fr(1), Fr(-4, 3), Fr(15, 4), Fr(1, 2), 4)
R['命题4′ 越界 η=15/4：入口 (1,0) 前 4 步'] = hb(Fr(1), Fr(0), Fr(15, 4), Fr(1, 2), 4)

# ---------------- 纯模态入口与仿射漂移 ----------------
R['命题4′ 边界 η=3、β=1/2：−β 纯模态入口 (1,−1) 前 5 步'] = hb(Fr(1), Fr(-1), Fr(3), Fr(1, 2), 5)
R['命题4′ β=0、ηs=3 越界：入口 (1,0) 前 4 步（根 0 与 1−ηs = −2）'] = hb(Fr(1), Fr(0), Fr(3), Fr(0), 4)
# 仿射漂移：φ ≡ 1，周期标签 (1, 2)（y = w·1，目标 1 与 2，不可实现），η = 2：两步因子 −1，M = 1，截面 w ↦ w + 2
def drift(eta, w0, K):
    w = w0; out = [w]
    for k in range(K):
        t = (Fr(1), Fr(2))[k % 2]; w = w - eta * (w - t); out.append(w)
    return out
dd = drift(Fr(2), Fr(0), 10)
R['命题5′ 仿射漂移（φ≡1、标签 1、2、η=2）：周期截面、周期内轨迹'] = ([S(x) for x in dd[0::2]], [S(x) for x in dd[:6]])

# ---------------- V⊥、一步对齐与发散 ----------------
tr = run([(Fr(1), Fr(0))], [Fr(1)], Fr(0), Fr(1), Fr(0), [Fr(1, 2), Fr(0)], [Fr(0), Fr(2)], 5)
R['β=0、v0⊥=2：θ⊥ 前 5 步'] = [S(t[0][1]) for t in tr]
R['β=0、ηs=1：入口 (e0,v0) 格点上第 1 步对齐的比例'] = all(run([(Fr(1),)], [Fr(1)], Fr(0), Fr(1), Fr(0), [e], [v], 1)[1][0][0] == 0 for e in [Fr(i, 2) for i in range(-4, 5)] for v in [Fr(i, 2) for i in range(-4, 5)])
dd = drift(Fr(3), Fr(1), 8); dd0 = drift(Fr(3), Fr(0), 8)
R['不可实现越界（φ≡1、标签 1、2、η=3）：w0=1 截面、w0=0 截面'] = ([S(x) for x in dd[0::2]], [S(x) for x in dd0[0::2]])

def fix(o):
    if isinstance(o, dict): return {str(k): fix(v) for k, v in o.items()}
    if isinstance(o, (list, tuple)): return [fix(x) for x in o]
    if isinstance(o, Fr): return str(o)
    return o
json.dump(fix(R), open('limit_checks.json', 'w'), ensure_ascii=False, indent=1, sort_keys=True)
print(json.dumps(fix(R), ensure_ascii=False, indent=1))
~~~
