【资源 036 起】源码　reach\_checks.py　SHA-256 6c1bb9e4539a63bc0d7da18809b6c52d97a578385562126f62525723f39f6b8a

~~~python
"""维护律工可达式 · 核算（纯 Python，精确有理，确定性）
承第十五（lawworker 的联合执行：F、G 两模型逐事件同步）。本程序：
- 续行三支：K0（空窗：窗间无输入事件）、Kp（保护输入：窗间两拍输入 (1, 0)）、Km（主史续行：窗间两拍输入 (1, 1)）。
- 主路径：第十五主史到 W2（或 W3）末，再接 K0／Kp 续行 8 窗；Km 续行 10 窗。逐窗记录 Φ 入、cmd、施作是否改变 w1、Φ 出、C；
  逐拍记录宿主自身沉积是否改变 w1 与 Φ。
- 一般定理的网格核算（轻量模拟器，与联合执行在主路径上逐窗比对）：F_p（p ≠ 0）、任意入口 w、g、C ≠ 0，K0／Kp 自入口起。
- 常斜率支（形成被阻断：读数差观测不到达）、学习支、扰动支；不可达例：A 与 B 分判；初态变体单列申报。
"""
import sys, os, json, runpy, tempfile, contextlib, io, itertools
from fractions import Fraction as Fr
HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)); sys.path.insert(0, HERE); ARGV = list(sys.argv)
with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp, contextlib.redirect_stdout(io.StringIO()):
    cwd = os.getcwd(); os.chdir(tmp); sys.argv = ['lw']
    L = runpy.run_path(os.path.join(HERE, 'lawworker.py')); os.chdir(cwd); sys.argv = ARGV
Joint, W_stage, F_beat, INIT = L['Joint'], L['W_stage'], L['F_beat'], L['INIT']
R = {}
def S(x): return str(x)
Kin = {'K0': None, 'Kp': (1, 0), 'Km': (1, 1)}

def phi(J, p): return J.F.s['w0'] + p * J.F.s['w1']
def window(J, k, p, observe=True):
    pin = phi(J, p); W_stage(J, k, p, observe=observe)
    a = next(e for e in J.F.events if e['id'] == f'act{k}')
    return {'窗': k, 'Φ入': S(pin), 'cmd': S(J.F.s['cmd']), '施作改变 w1': bool(a['changed']), 'Φ出': S(phi(J, p)), 'C': S(J.G.s['Cc']),
            'G 沉积执行': any(e['id'] == f'lms{k}' for e in J.G.events)}
def beats(J, k, p, K):
    out = []
    if Kin[K] is None: return out
    for b in (k + 5, k + 6):
        pin = phi(J, p); F_beat(J, b, *Kin[K])
        sd = next(e for e in J.F.events if e['id'] == f'stdp{b}')
        out.append({'拍': b, '输入': Kin[K], 'u': S(J.F.s['u']), '沉积改变 w1': bool(sd['changed']), 'ΔΦ': S(phi(J, p) - pin)})
    return out
def run(p, prefix, K, n, init=None, observe=True):
    """prefix：主史前缀窗数（1、2、3）；之后按续行 K 执行 n 窗。主史窗间为两拍 (1, 1)。"""
    J = Joint(p, init); log = []
    ks = [20, 30, 40][:prefix]
    for i, k in enumerate(ks):
        log.append(window(J, k, p, observe))
        if i < prefix - 1:
            for b in (k + 5, k + 6): F_beat(J, b, 1, 1)
    k = ks[-1]
    for j in range(n):
        bl = beats(J, k, p, K); k += 10
        log.append({'续行拍': bl, **window(J, k, p, observe)})
    return J, log

# ---------------- 主路径 ----------------
P = {}
for pre, K in [(2, 'K0'), (2, 'Kp'), (3, 'K0'), (3, 'Kp'), (1, 'Km')]:
    J, log = run(2, pre, K, 8 if K != 'Km' else 10)
    P[f'主史前缀 {pre} 窗 + 续行 {K}'] = log
R['主路径（p = 2，第十五入口）'] = P
def first_align(log):
    for i, w in enumerate(log):
        if Fr(w['Φ出']) == 2: return i + 1, w['窗']
def bal_from(log):
    """自哪一窗末起：此后各窗 cmd = 0、施作不改 w1、Φ 入 = Φ 出 = 2（模型内判据 J_K，逐窗）。"""
    for i in range(len(log) - 3):          # 其后至少三窗作逐窗核对（末窗不作空真）
        if all(Fr(w['cmd']) == 0 and not w['施作改变 w1'] and Fr(w['Φ入']) == 2 for w in log[i + 1:]) and Fr(log[i]['Φ出']) == 2:
            return i + 1, log[i]['窗']
    return None
R['主路径：续行内 G 的沉积是否执行（W2 之后）'] = {k: [w['G 沉积执行'] for w in v[2:]] for k, v in P.items()}
R['主路径：首次对齐（窗序号, 窗）与自某窗末起的 J_K（续行内逐窗零写回且对齐）'] = {
    k: {'首次对齐': first_align(v), '平衡自': bal_from(v), '续行内宿主沉积改变 w1 的拍': [b['拍'] for w in v for b in w.get('续行拍', []) if b['沉积改变 w1']]}
    for k, v in P.items()}

# ---------------- W2 末：属 A；对 B 的归属依续行 ----------------
J2, _ = run(2, 2, 'K0', 0); st = dict(J2.F.s)
wit = {'W2 末状态 (w0, w1, C)': (S(st['w0']), S(st['w1']), S(st['Cc'])), 'Φ': S(st['w0'] + 2 * st['w1'])}
for K in ('K0', 'Kp', 'Km'):
    J, log = run(2, 2, K, 6)
    wit[f'续行 {K}：W2 之后各窗 cmd'] = [w['cmd'] for w in log[2:]]
R['区别见证：W2 末 ∈ A；∈ B_K0、B_Kp；∉ B_Km'] = wit

# ---------------- 轻量模拟器（与联合执行比对） ----------------
def sim(p, w0, w1, g, C, K, n, block=False, prefix_main=0):
    c = C; out = []; halt = None
    sched = ['W'] + (['Bm', 'Bm', 'W'] * (prefix_main - 1) if prefix_main else []) + ([] if Kin[K] is None else ['B', 'B']) * 0
    seq = ['W']
    for _ in range(max(prefix_main - 1, 0)): seq += ['Bm', 'Bm', 'W']
    for _ in range(n): seq += ([] if Kin[K] is None else ['B', 'B']) + ['W']
    for op in seq:
        if op in ('B', 'Bm'):
            x0, x1 = (1, 1) if op == 'Bm' else Kin[K]; u = w0 * x0 + w1 * x1
            if x1 == 1 and u >= 1: w1 += Fr(1, 8)
            continue
        y = w0 + p * w1
        if C == 0: halt = f'第 {len(out) + 1} 窗计划无定义（C = 0）'; break
        cmd = (g - y) / C; w1 += cmd; y2 = w0 + p * w1
        if cmd != 0 and not block:
            obs, dlt = y2 - y, cmd; c = c + (obs - c * dlt) / dlt; C = c
        out.append((y, cmd, y2, C))
    return out, halt
cmp = {}
for pre, K in [(2, 'K0'), (2, 'Kp'), (3, 'Kp'), (1, 'Km')]:
    a = [(Fr(w['Φ入']), Fr(w['cmd']), Fr(w['Φ出']), Fr(w['C'])) for w in P[f'主史前缀 {pre} 窗 + 续行 {K}']]
    b, _ = sim(2, Fr(1, 2), Fr(1, 2), Fr(2), Fr(1), K, 8 if K != 'Km' else 10, prefix_main=pre)
    cmp[f'{pre}+{K}'] = a == b
R['模拟器与联合执行逐窗一致'] = cmp

# ---------------- 一般定理网格 ----------------
ps = [Fr(x) for x in ('-2', '-1', '-1/2', '1/3', '1', '2', '3', '5/2')]
ws = [Fr(x) for x in ('-1', '0', '1/2', '3/2')]; gs = [Fr(x) for x in ('-1', '0', '2')]; Cs = [Fr(x) for x in ('-1', '1/2', '1', '3')]
cnt = {'总数': 0, 'B 至迟于第 2 窗末取得（其后 8 窗零写回且对齐）': 0, '首次对齐窗 ∈ {0, 1, 2}': 0, '入口已对齐 ⇒ 全程零写回': 0, '形成后 C = p': 0}
bad = []
for p, w0, w1, g, C, K in itertools.product(ps, ws, ws, gs, Cs, ('K0', 'Kp')):
    cnt['总数'] += 1; out, h = sim(p, w0, w1, g, C, K, 9)
    e0 = w0 + p * w1 - g
    fa = 0 if e0 == 0 else next((i + 1 for i, o in enumerate(out) if o[2] == g), None)
    ok = h is None and all(o[1] == 0 and o[2] == g for o in out[2:])
    cnt['B 至迟于第 2 窗末取得（其后 8 窗零写回且对齐）'] += ok; cnt['首次对齐窗 ∈ {0, 1, 2}'] += fa in (0, 1, 2)
    if e0 == 0: cnt['入口已对齐 ⇒ 全程零写回'] += all(o[1] == 0 for o in out)
    else: cnt['形成后 C = p'] += out[0][3] == p
    if not ok: bad.append((S(p), S(w0), S(w1), S(g), S(C), K))
cnt['入口已对齐者数'] = sum(1 for p, w0, w1, g in itertools.product(ps, ws, ws, gs) if w0 + p * w1 == g) * len(Cs) * 2
cnt['反例'] = bad
R['一般定理网格（F_p，p ≠ 0；任意入口 w、g、C ≠ 0；K0／Kp 自入口起）'] = cnt
ex1 = {(S(p), S(C)): [(S(o[0]), S(o[1]), S(o[2])) for o in sim(p, Fr(1, 2), Fr(1, 2), Fr(2), C, 'K0', 3)[0]] for p, C in [(Fr(2), Fr(1)), (Fr(1), Fr(1)), (Fr(3), Fr(1)), (Fr(-1), Fr(3))]}
R['学习支示例（入口 w = (1/2, 1/2)、g = 2，K0）：(p, C入口) → 各窗 (Φ入, cmd, Φ出)'] = ex1

# ---------------- 常斜率支（形成被阻断） ----------------
def blocked(p, w0, w1, g, C, n=8):
    out, h = sim(p, w0, w1, g, C, 'K0', n - 1, block=True)
    return {'各窗 Φ出': [S(o[2]) for o in out], '误差 e_n': [S(o[2] - g) for o in out], '因子 1 − p/C': S(1 - p / C), '停机': h}
R['常斜率支（受限协议：读数差观测不到达，C 固定为入口值；K0）'] = {
    '第十五入口 p = 2、C = 1（因子 −1，e₀ = −1/2）：振荡': blocked(Fr(2), Fr(1, 2), Fr(1, 2), Fr(2), Fr(1)),
    '第十五入口 p = 3、C = 1（因子 −2，e₀ = 0）：恒对齐': blocked(Fr(3), Fr(1, 2), Fr(1, 2), Fr(2), Fr(1)),
    '〔初态变体〕p = 2、C = 3/2（因子 −1/3，e₀ = −1/2）：极限收束、有限窗不到达': blocked(Fr(2), Fr(1, 2), Fr(1, 2), Fr(2), Fr(3, 2), 10),
    '〔初态变体〕p = 3、g = 3、C = 1（因子 −2，e₀ = −1）：发散': blocked(Fr(3), Fr(1, 2), Fr(1, 2), Fr(3), Fr(1)),
    '〔初态变体〕p = 2、C = 2（因子 0，e₀ = −1/2）：一窗到达': blocked(Fr(2), Fr(1, 2), Fr(1, 2), Fr(2), Fr(2)),
}
Jb, logb = run(2, 1, 'K0', 7, observe=False)
R['受限协议在联合执行中复核（p = 2、K0、观测不到达）：各窗 (Φ出, C, G 沉积执行)'] = [(w['Φ出'], w['C'], w['G 沉积执行']) for w in logb]

# ---------------- 不可达 ----------------
p0, h0 = sim(Fr(0), Fr(1, 2), Fr(1, 2), Fr(2), Fr(1), 'K0', 5)
p0b, _ = sim(Fr(0), Fr(1, 2), Fr(1, 2), Fr(2), Fr(1), 'K0', 5, block=True)
km, _ = sim(Fr(2), Fr(1, 2), Fr(1, 2), Fr(2), Fr(1), 'Km', 30)
R['不可达例'] = {
    'U1（对 A）受限协议·形成阻断，p = 2，第十五入口：可达读数像': sorted({S(o[2]) for o in sim(Fr(2), Fr(1, 2), Fr(1, 2), Fr(2), Fr(1), 'K0', 30, block=True)[0]}),
    'U2（对 A）读数不依赖被写位 p = 0：形成照常时': {'各窗 Φ出': [S(o[2]) for o in p0], '停机': h0},
    'U2（对 A）p = 0、形成阻断：各窗 (Φ出, cmd)': [(S(o[2]), S(o[1])) for o in p0b],
    'U3（对 B，A 可达）主史续行 Km：31 窗中 Φ出 = 2 的窗数、cmd = 0 的窗数（W1 之后）': (sum(o[2] == 2 for o in km), sum(o[1] == 0 for o in km[1:])),
    'U3 Km：对齐态 w1 = 3/4 时下一拍膜驱动与沉积': (S(Fr(1, 2) + Fr(3, 4)), '≥ 1 且 x1 = 1 ⇒ w1 += 1/8'),
}

def fix(o):
    if isinstance(o, dict): return {str(k): fix(v) for k, v in o.items()}
    if isinstance(o, (list, tuple)): return [fix(x) for x in o]
    if isinstance(o, Fr): return str(o)
    return o
json.dump(fix(R), open('reach_checks.json', 'w'), ensure_ascii=False, indent=1, sort_keys=True, default=str)
print(json.dumps(fix(R), ensure_ascii=False, indent=1, default=str))
~~~

~~~python
"""第十八、第十九、第二十正文所列各例的核算（纯 Python，精确有理，确定性）
第十八：入口截面（e₀ = 0）与后继第一窗；主路径前缀（W1 后先执行第 25—26 拍，再于 W2 后分支）；W1 后即取 K0 的对照；Km 自 W2 后的同态分支。
第十八：宿主 F_0 在同一联合模型上——观测到达时第一窗沉积 c := 0 越出非零类型即被拒；g = w0 支入口对齐且零写回；R_obs 下命令非零而读数不变。
第十九：正文规则（分相位 u_pre、z、u_post）的独立实现与构造程序逐拍逐变量一致（含放电复位、直接门态、纯抑、纯去抑、非整数目标标签）。
第十九：单目标实例只有抑（无去抑窗）。
第二十：身份判据的量词——合法空入口上 id 见证 (II) 而 (I) 不成立；合格实例经 swap 运输（连同任务）后仍合格且同型。
"""
import sys, os, json, contextlib, io
from fractions import Fraction as Fr
HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)); sys.path.insert(0, HERE)
def prefix(script, stop):
    p = os.path.join(HERE, script); src = open(p, encoding='utf-8').read().split(stop)[0]
    scope = {'__file__': p}
    with contextlib.redirect_stdout(io.StringIO()):
        exec(compile(src, p, 'exec'), scope)
    return scope
R = {}
def S(x): return str(x)

# ======================= 第十八 =======================
rc = prefix('reach_checks.py', 'R = {}')
Joint, W_stage, F_beat = rc['Joint'], rc['W_stage'], rc['F_beat']
def phi(J, p): return J.F.s['w0'] + p * J.F.s['w1']
def win(J, k, p, observe=True):
    pin = phi(J, p); W_stage(J, k, p, observe=observe); return (S(pin), S(J.F.s['cmd']), S(phi(J, p)), S(J.G.s['Cc']))
def beats(J, k, x):
    for b in (k + 5, k + 6): F_beat(J, b, *x)
f18 = {}
# 入口截面：p = 3、第十五入口（e₀ = 0），K0 与 Kp，后继第一窗为 W1
for K, x in (('K0', None), ('Kp', (1, 0))):
    J = Joint(Fr(3)); log = []
    for k in (20, 30, 40, 50):
        log.append(win(J, k, Fr(3)))
        if x: beats(J, k, x)
    f18[f'入口截面 e₀ = 0（p = 3），续行 {K}：各窗 (Φ入, cmd, Φ出, C)'] = log
# 主路径前缀：W1 → 第 25、26 拍 (1, 1) → W2 → 分支
for K, x in (('K0', None), ('Kp', (1, 0)), ('Km', (1, 1))):
    J = Joint(Fr(2)); log = [win(J, 20, Fr(2))]; beats(J, 20, (1, 1)); log.append(win(J, 30, Fr(2)))
    st = (S(J.F.s['w0']), S(J.F.s['w1']), S(J.G.s['Cc']))
    for k in (40, 50, 60):
        if x: beats(J, k - 10, x)
        log.append(win(J, k, Fr(2)))
    f18[f'主路径：W1、第 25—26 拍、W2，其后 {K}'] = {'W2 末 (w0, w1, C)': st, '各窗': log}
# 对照：W1 后即取 K0（无第 25、26 拍）
J = Joint(Fr(2)); f18['对照：W1 后即取 K0（定理 2 的情形）'] = [win(J, k, Fr(2)) for k in (20, 30, 40, 50)]
# F_0
try:
    J = Joint(Fr(0)); W_stage(J, 20, Fr(0)); f18['F_0，g = 2，观测到达'] = '未拒（错误）'
except Exception as ex:
    f18['F_0，g = 2，观测到达'] = f'第一窗被拒：{ex}'
J = Joint(Fr(0), {'g': Fr(1, 2)}); f18['F_0，g = w0 = 1/2，K0：各窗'] = [win(J, k, Fr(0)) for k in (20, 30, 40)]
J = Joint(Fr(0)); f18['F_0，g = 2，R_obs，K0：各窗'] = [win(J, k, Fr(0), observe=False) for k in (20, 30, 40)]
R['第十八'] = f18

# ======================= 第十九 =======================
tc = prefix('tune_construct.py', '# ======================= 主示范')
Inst, build, forms, search_target, in_W = tc['Inst'], tc['build'], tc['forms'], tc['search_target'], tc['in_W']
def formal_sim(I):
    """按正文 2.2、2.3 的规则独立执行；返回逐拍记录（每拍末的各变量）。"""
    n, G = I.n, I.goals
    st = {'u': Fr(0), 'z': Fr(0), **{f'm{i}': I.m0[i] for i in range(n)}, **{f'q{g}': Fr(0) for g in G},
          **{f'uK{g}': Fr(0) for g in G}, **{f'zK{g}': Fr(0) for g in G}, **{f'uT{i}': I.tonic[i] for i in I.gsel}, **{f'zT{i}': I.tonic[i] for i in I.gsel}}
    rec = []; gv = Fr(0)
    def beat(zp, teach):
        ctx = {g: Fr(1 if gv == g else 0) for g in G}; d = {g: Fr(1 if g == teach else 0) for g in G}
        for g in G:
            uK = st[f'q{g}'] * ctx[g] + d[g]; st[f'uK{g}'] = uK; st[f'zK{g}'] = Fr(1 if uK >= 1 else 0)
            st[f'q{g}'] = min(Fr(1), st[f'q{g}'] + Fr(1, 2) * ctx[g] * (1 if uK >= 1 else 0))
        for i in I.gsel:
            uT = I.tonic[i] * (1 - sum(st[f'zK{g}'] for g in G if i in I.Og[g])) + sum(st[f'zK{g}'] for g in G if i in I.Cg[g])
            st[f'uT{i}'] = Fr(uT); st[f'zT{i}'] = Fr(1 if uT >= 1 else 0)
        for i in I.gsel: st[f'm{i}'] = 1 - st[f'zT{i}']
        u_pre = st['u'] / 2 + sum(st[f'm{i}'] * I.w[i] * zp[i] for i in range(n))
        z = Fr(1 if u_pre >= 1 else 0); u_post = Fr(0) if z == 1 else u_pre
        st['z'], st['u'] = z, u_post
        rec.append({'u_pre': u_pre, **{k: v for k, v in st.items()}})
    zero, one = [0] * n, [1] * n
    for g in G:
        gv = g; beat(zero, g); beat(zero, g); gv = Fr(0); beat(zero, None)
    for g in G:
        gv = g; beat(one, None); beat(one, None); gv = Fr(0); beat(one, None)
    return rec
def prog_beats(r):
    """构造程序运行的逐拍记录：膜驱动事件后的 u 为 u_pre，放电事件后的状态为拍末。"""
    out = []; pre = None
    for i, e in enumerate(r.events):
        st = r.trace[i + 1][1]
        if e['src'] == 'mem': pre = st['u']
        if e['src'] == 'fire': out.append({'u_pre': pre, **{k: v for k, v in st.items()}})
    return out
def compare(I):
    r, Ws = build(I); a = formal_sim(I); b = prog_beats(r)
    keys = [k for k in a[0]]
    return len(a) == len(b) and all(x[k] == y[k] for x, y in zip(a, b) for k in keys), len(a), sum(1 for x in a if x['z'] == 1 and x['u_pre'] >= 1 and x['u'] == 0)
f19 = {}
cases = {
    '主实例（两目标：抑、去抑）': Inst([1, Fr(1, 2), 1], [1, 1, 1], [0, 1, 2], [1, 1, 0], {1: search_target([1, Fr(1, 2), 1], [1, 1, 1], [0, 1, 2], [1, 1, 0], Fr(1, 2)), 2: search_target([1, Fr(1, 2), 1], [1, 1, 1], [0, 1, 2], [1, 1, 0], Fr(5, 2))}),
    '单门 w = 1、纯抑、目标标签 1/3（直接门态）': Inst([1], [1], [0], [1], {Fr(1, 3): [0]}),
    '纯去抑（直接门态）': Inst([1, 1], [1, 1], [0, 1], [0, 1], {2: [1, 1]}),
    '直接门态、负标签与正标签': Inst([1, Fr(1, 2)], [1, -1], [0, 1], [1, 0], {Fr(-2): [0, 0], Fr(5): [1, 1]}),
}
f19['正文规则与构造程序逐拍逐变量一致（一致, 拍数, 放电复位拍数）'] = {k: compare(I) for k, I in cases.items()}
I = cases['单门 w = 1、纯抑、目标标签 1/3（直接门态）']; r, Ws = build(I); f = forms(r, I, Ws['W1/3'], Ws['W1/3入口'])
f19['单目标实例：调谐窗内门态改变、方向、复位窗'] = {'W 内门态': f['W 内门态'], 'Φ 入、Φ 末': f['Φ 入、Φ 末'], '去抑集': I.Og[Fr(1, 3)], '抑集': I.Cg[Fr(1, 3)],
    '复位窗门态改变': [e['id'] for e in r.events if e['kind'] == 'Gt' and e['changed'] and in_W(e['phase'], Ws['R1/3'])]}
I = cases['直接门态、负标签与正标签']; r, Ws = build(I)
f19['直接门态的诱导目标读数 Φ(m^g) 与窗末读数'] = {S(g): (S(I.phi_of(I.mt[g])), forms(r, I, Ws[f'W{g}'], Ws[f'W{g}入口'])['Φ 入、Φ 末'][1]) for g in I.goals}
R['第十九'] = f19

# ======================= 第二十 =======================
sc = prefix('source_checks.py', '# ---- 起点与默认来源的排除')
ent, run, inSigma, aligned, TAU, Hist = sc['ent'], sc['run'], sc['inSigma'], sc['aligned'], sc['TAU'], sc['Hist']
SW = {'A': 'B', 'B': 'A', 'AB': 'AB'}; PI = {'wA': 'wB', 'wB': 'wA', 'aA': 'aB', 'aB': 'aA', 'u': 'u', 'kap': 'kap'}
def pi_st(st): return {PI[k]: v for k, v in st.items()}
def pi_op(o): return ('b', SW[o[1]], o[2]) if o[0] == 'b' else o
def qual(h, tau): return aligned(h.st, tau) and inSigma(h.st, tau) and h.mem('u')
f20 = {}
for name, e in (('合法空入口 w = (1, 1)', ent(Fr(0))), ('交换入口 w = (1/2, 1)', sc['ent_B'](Fr(0)))):
    h = run(e, [])
    f20[f'{name}，κ = 0，空记录：id 见证 (II)；(I) 是否成立'] = {'(II)': True, '(I)：Σ_mem 资格': qual(h, TAU) or qual(h, {'A': 0, 'B': 1, 'AB': 0})}
# 合格实例（τ = (1, 0, 0)）经 swap 运输（入口、操作、任务同运）
tauA = {'A': 1, 'B': 0, 'AB': 0}; tauB = {'A': 0, 'B': 1, 'AB': 0}
ops = [('b', 'AB', 0)] * 3 + [('b', 'A', 0), ('b', 'B', 0)]
h1 = run(sc['ent_A'](Fr(1, 2)), ops)
h2 = Hist(pi_st(sc['ent_A'](Fr(1, 2))))
for o in ops: h2.op(pi_op(o))
f20['合格实例经 swap 运输'] = {'原实例 Σ_mem((1,0,0))': qual(h1, tauA), '像实例 Σ_mem((0,1,0))': qual(h2, tauB), '像实例对未运输任务 (1,0,0)': qual(h2, tauA),
    '末态互为 π 像': pi_st(h1.st) == h2.st}
R['第二十'] = f20

def fix(o):
    if isinstance(o, dict): return {str(k): fix(v) for k, v in o.items()}
    if isinstance(o, (list, tuple)): return [fix(x) for x in o]
    if isinstance(o, Fr): return str(o)
    return o
json.dump(fix(R), open('text_checks_18_20.json', 'w'), ensure_ascii=False, indent=1, sort_keys=True, default=str)
print(json.dumps(fix(R), ensure_ascii=False, indent=1, default=str))
~~~

【资源 040 起】源码　tune\_source\_checks.py　SHA-256 da254ad1762a0068f63ca61366839156a6cd4412a57f24ca2c2dc10dae308612

~~~python
"""脉冲网络调谐构造式 ·「q_g 候选结构的来源」一节的核算（纯 Python，精确有理，确定性）
H_obs 由构造程序 tune_construct.build 直接生成并逐事件核对；评价点含入口；编辑语义为可执行的历史削减；
背景干预单列；真正的空历史控制；在固定首次评价点上检验保留历史的充分性与极小性（穷举可删项子集）；
异史例补目标恢复事件；背景阻断与检验运行的两种组合分报。
来源协议 Π_src：
  Dyn^Π：第十九第二节的场规则（控制、赫布沉积、紧张性、门控、膜驱动、放电）；目标设定事件属外部历史。
  H_obs：build(I0) 的运行——T1（目标 1，两拍教学，输入 0）、复位拍（目标 0，输入 0）、T2、复位拍、W1（两拍输入 1）、R1（目标 0，输入 1）、W2、R2。
  历史项（可删）：每拍的 目标设定事件 G_k（若有）、上下文输入 ctx_k（该拍输入端口事件中的上下文分量）、教学驱动 d_k、前突触输入 zp_k（非零者）。
  削减语义：删 G_k = 该拍不发生目标设定事件（目标沿前值）；删 ctx_k = 该拍上下文输入分量取 0（只此一拍，其余分量不变）；删 d_k = 该拍教学驱动取 0；删 zp_k = 该拍前突触输入取 0。
  背景干预（不属历史削减）：全程阻断控制神经元对紧张性抑制元的输出。
  Judge^Π = Γ_cand（(a) 场内形成、(b) 冻结快照探针门态改变、(c) q_g := 0 探针无改变）；T_Π = 入口与每拍末；Gran^Π = 上述历史项。
"""
import os, json, contextlib, io, itertools, inspect, textwrap
from fractions import Fraction as Fr
HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
def prefix(script, stop):
    p = os.path.join(HERE, script); src = open(p, encoding='utf-8').read().split(stop)[0]; sc = {'__file__': p}
    with contextlib.redirect_stdout(io.StringIO()): exec(compile(src, p, 'exec'), sc)
    return sc, src
T, TSRC = prefix('tune_construct.py', '# ======================= 主示范实例')
Inst, search_target, build, goal_ev, model, init_state, RunX, in_W = (T[k] for k in ('Inst', 'search_target', 'build', 'goal_ev', 'model', 'init_state', 'RunX', 'in_W'))
# 带上下文削减的拍：由构造程序 beat 源码作唯一改动生成（上下文分量乘 (1 − mask)），其余逐字相同
bsrc = TSRC[TSRC.index('def beat('):TSRC.index('def goal_ev(')]
old = "lambda v: {f'ctx{g}': Fr(1 if v['g'] == g else 0) for g in I.goals}"
assert bsrc.count(old) == 1
bsrc = bsrc.replace('def beat(r, I, k, zp, teach=None, lesion=False):', 'def beat_m(r, I, k, zp, teach=None, lesion=False, mask_ctx=False):').replace(
    old, "lambda v, mc=mask_ctx: {f'ctx{g}': Fr(1 if (v['g'] == g and not mc) else 0) for g in I.goals}")
exec(bsrc, T); beat_m = T['beat_m']
S = str; R = {}
w, a, gsel, m0 = [1, Fr(1, 2), 1], [1, 1, 1], [0, 1, 2], [1, 1, 0]
I0 = Inst(w, a, gsel, m0, {1: search_target(w, a, gsel, m0, Fr(1, 2)), 2: search_target(w, a, gsel, m0, Fr(5, 2))})
Z, O = [0] * I0.n, [1] * I0.n
H_OBS = [(1, Z, 1), (None, Z, 1), (0, Z, None), (2, Z, 2), (None, Z, 2), (0, Z, None),
         (1, O, None), (None, O, None), (0, O, None), (2, O, None), (None, O, None), (0, O, None)]
NAMES = ['入口', 'T1-1', 'T1-2', '复位1', 'T2-1', 'T2-2', '复位2', 'W1-1', 'W1-2', 'R1', 'W2-1', 'W2-2', 'R2']
ENTRY = init_state(I0); RUNTIME = [v for v in ENTRY if not (v.startswith('w') or v.startswith('q'))]

def run(hist, dele=frozenset(), lesion=False, q0=Fr(0), upto=None):
    r = RunX(model(I0), init_state(I0, q0)); ends = [0]
    for k, (gs, zp, te) in enumerate(hist[:upto] if upto else hist, 1):
        if gs is not None and ('G', k) not in dele: goal_ev(r, k, gs)
        zp2 = Z if ('zp', k) in dele else zp; te2 = None if ('d', k) in dele else te
        beat_m(r, I0, k, zp2, teach=te2, lesion=lesion, mask_ctx=('ctx', k) in dele); ends.append(len(r.events))
    return r, ends
# 〇 H_obs 与构造程序 build 逐事件核对
rb, Wb = build(I0); ro, _ = run(H_OBS)
sig = lambda r: [(e['id'], e['kind'], tuple(e['changed']), tuple(sorted(e['out'].items()))) for e in r.events]
R['〇 H_obs 与构造程序 build 逐事件核对'] = {'事件数（build, 本程序）': (len(rb.events), len(ro.events)), '逐事件相同': sig(rb) == sig(ro),
    'build 各窗相位': {k: [list(x) if isinstance(x, tuple) else x for x in v] if isinstance(v, tuple) and isinstance(v[0], tuple) else list(v) for k, v in Wb.items()}}

def probe(state, g, lesion=False):
    s = dict(state)
    for v in RUNTIME: s[v] = ENTRY[v]
    rp = RunX(model(I0), s); goal_ev(rp, 1, g); beat_m(rp, I0, 1, O, lesion=lesion); beat_m(rp, I0, 2, O, lesion=lesion)
    gates = {f'm{i}' for i in I0.Cg[g] + I0.Og[g]}
    return any(e['kind'] == 'Gt' and set(e['changed']) & gates for e in rp.events)
def judge(r, n, g, lesion=False):
    st = r.trace[n][1]; vq = r.vtrace[n][f'q{g}']; ins = {e['id'] for e in r.events if e['kind'] == 'In'}
    a_ = st[f'q{g}'] != 0 and any(isinstance(k, tuple) and len(k) == 3 and k[2] in ins and s is True for k, s in vq.items())
    return int(a_ and probe(st, g, lesion) and not probe({**st, f'q{g}': Fr(0)}, g, lesion)), int(a_)
def seq(hist, g, **kw):
    r, ends = run(hist, **kw); return [judge(r, n, g, kw.get('lesion', False))[0] for n in ends]
def first(js): return next((i for i, j in enumerate(js) if j == 1), None)
J = {g: seq(H_OBS, g) for g in I0.goals}; TAU = {g: first(J[g]) for g in I0.goals}
R['一 H_obs 上的 Judge 序列与首次取得（评价点含入口）'] = {g: {'序列': list(zip(NAMES, J[g])), '首次': NAMES[TAU[g]], '其后留存': all(J[g][TAU[g]:])} for g in I0.goals}

# 一′ 实际作用链：W_g 门控所写 m 版本的来源含 q_g 版本（C3(ii) 依赖、C4 链在原运行上的对应）
def m_src(r, W, g):
    out = {}
    for i, e in enumerate(r.events):
        if e['kind'] == 'Gt' and e['changed'] and in_W(e['phase'], W):
            v = r.vtrace[i + 1][e['changed'][0]]
            out[e['id']] = sorted(str(k) for k, s in v.items() if s is True and isinstance(k, tuple) and k[0] == f'q{g}')
    return out
R['一′ 原运行调谐窗门控写入的 q_g 来源'] = {f'W{g}': m_src(rb, Wb[f'W{g}'], g) for g in I0.goals}

# 二 首次评价点前的历史项与保留历史的充分性、极小性
def items_upto(hist, k_max):
    it = []
    for k, (gs, zp, te) in enumerate(hist[:k_max], 1):
        if gs is not None: it.append(('G', k))
        it.append(('ctx', k))
        if te is not None: it.append(('d', k))
        if any(zp): it.append(('zp', k))
    return it
SRC = {}
for g in I0.goals:
    tau = TAU[g]; items = items_upto(H_OBS, tau)
    def ok(keep):
        dele = frozenset(x for x in items if x not in keep)
        js = seq(H_OBS, g, dele=dele, upto=tau)
        return js[tau] == 1 and all(j == 0 for j in js[:tau])
    suff = [set(c) for n in range(len(items) + 1) for c in itertools.combinations(items, n) if ok(set(c))]
    mins = [c for c in suff if not any(d < c for d in suff)]
    single = {f'{x[0]}_{x[1]}': (lambda js: NAMES[first(js)] if first(js) is not None else None)(seq(H_OBS, g, dele=frozenset([x]))) for x in items}
    SRC[f'g = {g}（τ = {NAMES[tau]}）'] = {'τ 前历史项': [f'{x[0]}_{x[1]}' for x in items], '充分保留集数': len(suff),
        '极小充分保留集': [sorted(f'{x[0]}_{x[1]}' for x in c) for c in mins], '单删后首次取得（全程）': single}
R['二 固定首次评价点的保留充分性与极小性（穷举 τ 前历史项子集；Judge 二值，无 ⊥）'] = SRC
# 三 真正的空历史控制：删去全部历史项（目标设定、上下文、教学驱动、前突触输入）
ALL = frozenset(items_upto(H_OBS, len(H_OBS)))
R['三 空历史控制 H_∅（全部历史项删去，Dyn^Π 照常）'] = {g: {'Judge 序列': seq(H_OBS, g, dele=ALL), 'q 末值': S(run(H_OBS, dele=ALL)[0].s[f'q{g}'])} for g in I0.goals}
# 四 背景干预（单列）
R['四 背景干预：全程阻断控制输出'] = {g: {'Judge 序列': seq(H_OBS, g, lesion=True), 'q 末值': S(run(H_OBS, lesion=True)[0].s[f'q{g}']),
                                    'q 形成写入与原运行相同': [(e['id'], e['out']) for e in run(H_OBS, lesion=True)[0].events if e['src'] == 'heb' and e['changed']] ==
                                                            [(e['id'], e['out']) for e in ro.events if e['src'] == 'heb' and e['changed']]} for g in I0.goals}
# 五 默认来源对照：q_g 预置 1，删去全部历史项
R['五 预置对照（q_g 入口 1，全部历史项删去）'] = {g: {'(a) 序列': [judge(rr, n, g)[1] for n in ee], 'Judge 序列': [judge(rr, n, g)[0] for n in ee]}
                                         for g in I0.goals for rr, ee in [run(H_OBS, dele=ALL, q0=Fr(1))]}

# 六 异史例（与 H_obs 不同的历史；不作同史多解）
def hist_report(name, hist, g):
    r, ends = run(hist); js = [judge(r, n, g)[0] for n in ends]; f = first(js)
    return {'历史（目标设定, 输入, 教学）': [(gs, 'Z' if zp == Z else 'O', te) for gs, zp, te in hist[:6]], '实际目标设定事件': [e['id'] + ':' + e['out']['g'] for e in r.events if e['src'] == 'ext_goal'][:6],
            f'q{g} 写入': [(e['id'], e['out']) for e in r.events if e['src'] == 'heb' and e['changed'] and f'q{g}' in e['changed']],
            f'q{g} 末值': S(r.s[f'q{g}']), '首次取得评价点（拍号，0 为入口）': f, 'Judge 前 5 点': js[:5]}
ALT = {}
for g, rest in ((1, H_OBS[2:]),):
    ALT['插一拍目标 0、不恢复目标'] = hist_report('gap', [(1, Z, 1), (0, Z, None), (None, Z, 1)] + rest, 1)
    ALT['插一拍目标 0、第二次教学前恢复目标 1'] = hist_report('gap_restore', [(1, Z, 1), (0, Z, None), (1, Z, 1)] + rest, 1)
    ALT['教学三拍（第三拍同值）'] = hist_report('three', [(1, Z, 1), (None, Z, 1), (None, Z, 1)] + rest, 1)
H2_gap = H_OBS[:3] + [(2, Z, 2), (0, Z, None), (2, Z, 2)] + H_OBS[5:]
ALT['g = 2：插一拍目标 0、第二次教学前恢复目标 2'] = hist_report('gap2', H2_gap, 2)
R['六 异史例（另外的历史，不作同史多解）'] = ALT
# 七 背景阻断与检验运行的组合
R['七 背景阻断与检验运行的组合'] = {g: {'历史阻断、检验运行同阻断（协议所取）': seq(H_OBS, g, lesion=True),
                                    '历史阻断、检验运行不阻断': (lambda rr: [judge(rr[0], n, g, False)[0] for n in rr[1]])(run(H_OBS, lesion=True))} for g in I0.goals}
R['七′ 削减单位（按实际执行）'] = {'ctx_k': '该拍上下文组全体（各 g 的 ctx 分量）取 0', 'd_k': '该拍教学驱动向量取 0（各 g 的 d 分量）', 'zp_k': '该拍前突触输入向量取全 0',
                         'G_k': '该拍不发生目标设定事件；目标为持续寄存量，沿前值', 'R1、R2 前突触输入': '全 1（教学窗后两复位拍为全 0）'}

def fix(o):
    if isinstance(o, dict): return {str(k): fix(v) for k, v in o.items()}
    if isinstance(o, (list, tuple, set, frozenset)): return [fix(x) for x in o]
    if isinstance(o, Fr): return str(o)
    return o
json.dump(fix(R), open(os.path.join(HERE, 'tune_source_checks.json'), 'w'), ensure_ascii=False, indent=1, sort_keys=True, default=str)
print(json.dumps(fix(R), ensure_ascii=False, indent=1, default=str))
~~~

【资源 042 起】源码　tunelaw\_admit.py　SHA-256 24bb6cb487885c8b55479e26fa0715e8e2065e15383879489c7005f0096061d6

~~~python
"""调谐律来源对象的准入核算（纯 Python，精确有理，确定性）
沿用《脉冲网络调谐构造式》的构造程序（tune_construct 至「主示范实例」之前的部分，前缀执行）。
核查：q_g 形成链的层位与事件种类；空历史控制；单拍教学；阻断控制输出（全程）下的形成与作用；门控在形成链之外。
"""
import os, json, contextlib, io
from fractions import Fraction as Fr
HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
def prefix(script, stop):
    p = os.path.join(HERE, script); src = open(p, encoding='utf-8').read().split(stop)[0]; sc = {'__file__': p}
    with contextlib.redirect_stdout(io.StringIO()): exec(compile(src, p, 'exec'), sc)
    return sc
T = prefix('tune_construct.py', '# ======================= 主示范实例')
Inst, search_target, build, forms, beat, goal_ev, model, init_state, RunX = (T[k] for k in ('Inst', 'search_target', 'build', 'forms', 'beat', 'goal_ev', 'model', 'init_state', 'RunX'))
vanc_at, versions_of, in_W = T['vanc_at'], T['versions_of'], T['in_W']
S = str; R = {}
w, a, gsel, m0 = [1, Fr(1, 2), 1], [1, 1, 1], [0, 1, 2], [1, 1, 0]
I0 = Inst(w, a, gsel, m0, {1: search_target(w, a, gsel, m0, Fr(1, 2)), 2: search_target(w, a, gsel, m0, Fr(5, 2))})

def ancestry(r, ev, var):
    """事件 ev 所写 var 版本的来源（版本祖先）中的事件：(事件, 种类, 来源, 所写层)。"""
    anc = vanc_at(r, ev)[var]; byid = {e['id']: e for e in r.events}; out = set()
    for k, s in anc.items():
        if s is True and isinstance(k, tuple) and len(k) == 3 and k[2] in byid:
            e = byid[k[2]]; out.add((e['id'], e['kind'], e['src'], r.M.layer.get(k[0], '?')))
    return sorted(out)
def q_final_writer(r, g):
    ws = [e for e in r.events if e['src'] == 'heb' and e['changed'] and f'q{g}' in e['changed']]
    return ws[-1] if ws else None
def gate_summary(r, Ws):
    return {f'W{g}': forms(r, I0, Ws[f'W{g}'], Ws[f'W{g}入口'])['W 内门态'] for g in I0.goals}

# A 主运行：q_g 末版本的来源链
r0, W0 = build(I0)
A = {}
for g in I0.goals:
    e = q_final_writer(r0, g); anc = ancestry(r0, e, f'q{g}')
    A[f'q{g}'] = {'末版本写入事件': e['id'], '来源链事件（事件, 种类, 来源, 层）': anc,
                  '来源链含门控事件或 m 层': any(k == 'Gt' or lay == 'm' for _, k, _, lay in anc),
                  '来源链层集合': sorted({lay for *_, lay in anc}), '来源链事件种类': sorted({k for _, k, _, _ in anc})}
R['A 主运行：q_g 末版本来源链（宿载层形成）'] = A
R['A′ 教学窗内门控也实际改变（形成链之外的旁支）'] = [(e['id'], e['changed']) for e in r0.events if e['kind'] == 'Gt' and e['changed'] and (in_W(e['phase'], W0['T1']) or in_W(e['phase'], W0['T2']))]

# B 空历史控制（无教学窗，其余如故）
rB, WB = build(I0, teach=False)
R['B 空历史控制 H_∅'] = {'q 末值': {g: S(rB.trace[-1][1][f'q{g}']) for g in I0.goals}, '调谐窗门态改变': gate_summary(rB, WB)}

# C 单拍教学（每目标只一拍 d = 1）
def build_partial(I, beats_per_goal=1, lesion=False):
    r = RunX(model(I), init_state(I)); k = 1; Ws = {}; zero = [0] * I.n; one = [1] * I.n
    for g in I.goals:
        goal_ev(r, k, g)
        for j in range(beats_per_goal): beat(r, I, k + j, zero, teach=g, lesion=lesion)
        k += beats_per_goal; goal_ev(r, k, 0); beat(r, I, k, zero, lesion=lesion); k += 1
    for g in I.goals:
        goal_ev(r, k, g); Ws[f'W{g}入口'] = (k, 0); beat(r, I, k, one, lesion=lesion); e2 = beat(r, I, k + 1, one, lesion=lesion); Ws[f'W{g}'] = ((k, 1), e2); k += 2
        goal_ev(r, k, 0); beat(r, I, k, one, lesion=lesion); k += 1
    return r, Ws
rC, WC = build_partial(I0, 1)
R['C 单拍教学'] = {'q 末值': {g: S(rC.trace[-1][1][f'q{g}']) for g in I0.goals}, '调谐窗门态改变': gate_summary(rC, WC)}

# D 全程阻断控制神经元对紧张性抑制元的输出（教学与调谐同阻断）
rD, WD = build(I0, lesion=True)
R['D 全程阻断控制输出'] = {'q 末值': {g: S(rD.trace[-1][1][f'q{g}']) for g in I0.goals},
    'q 形成事件序列与主运行相同': [(e['id'], e['changed']) for e in rD.events if e['src'] == 'heb' and e['changed']] == [(e['id'], e['changed']) for e in r0.events if e['src'] == 'heb' and e['changed']],
    '教学窗门态改变': [(e['id'], e['changed']) for e in rD.events if e['kind'] == 'Gt' and e['changed'] and (in_W(e['phase'], WD['T1']) or in_W(e['phase'], WD['T2']))],
    '调谐窗门态改变': gate_summary(rD, WD)}

# E 主运行的作用对照
R['E 主运行调谐窗门态改变'] = gate_summary(r0, W0)

def fix(o):
    if isinstance(o, dict): return {str(k): fix(v) for k, v in o.items()}
    if isinstance(o, (list, tuple)): return [fix(x) for x in o]
    if isinstance(o, Fr): return str(o)
    return o
json.dump(fix(R), open(os.path.join(HERE, 'tunelaw_admit.json'), 'w'), ensure_ascii=False, indent=1, sort_keys=True, default=str)
print(json.dumps(fix(R), ensure_ascii=False, indent=1, default=str))
~~~

【资源 044 起】源码　struct\_admit.py　SHA-256 c08650f2ac7d6ac4c3fe91afd5f4512f3147e441b307dad696f88690b8d0bf60

~~~python
"""一般化结构源式 · 对象准入核算（纯 Python，精确有理，确定性）
宿主：第十七的脉冲宿主 𝔐_I（输入 A、B、AB；中间神经元募集 z_I；抑制 u；门 m 支撑在 w_A、w_B、u 上）。
事前申报：限二输入任务及明确的重标记族；申报子群 G = {id, swap}（π 交换 A、B 位标签，σ_g 交换输入 A、B，ψ 取恒等）。
主论元为宿载成员层对象（工作域 D_mem：门恒开、无门控事件）。
(a) 同宿主身份：结构型 Σ(τ) 以标签角色（正单输入集 P、负合取 AB）、组织关系、目标与容许映射定义；核 D18-5 的等变条件（读数、
    活动、写入、过程配对 PC_g）与形成记录、成员的等变；核 Σ 的 G 不变。
    模板同型与整态改名同一分立：探针 u = 3/2 与 2；G 不变推出模板同型，反之不成立；任务轨道。
(b) 跨任务可复用：任务族 T2 = 二输入非单调任务全体 {(1,1,0), (1,0,0), (0,1,0)}（z(A), z(B), z(AB)）；对齐 ⇒ Σ(τ)，逐关系承担；
    单调任务上存在不具 Σ 的对齐态（可复用域恰为 T2）；冗余扩展（两个中间神经元）的合计式；三输入分布式抑制反例复核。
    成员层复用：D_F（场自身沉积史、u 入口在基线、留存条款）中 τ ∈ T2 对齐 ⇒ Σ(τ) ∧ mem(u)，枚举全部长度 ≤ 6 的输入—教学史；
    纯直写边界单列；三项任务各交一条全部承担位合格、含 u 形成与留存的历史。
(c) 等变：写入在全部门态 × κ ∈ {0, 1/4, 1/2, 1} 上核；历史等变含直写事件与 u 非零形成，逐史比对末态、ℛ 条目、形成版本、资格区间、
    成员与留存计时（全部长度 ≤ 4 的事件序列，三个入口）。
"""
import json, itertools
from fractions import Fraction as Fr
R = {}
def S(x): return str(x)
SIG = {'A': (1, 0), 'B': (0, 1), 'AB': (1, 1)}
SWAP = {'A': 'B', 'B': 'A', 'AB': 'AB'}

# ---------------- 宿主读出 ----------------
def zI(st, s): return 1 if st['aA'] * SIG[s][0] + st['aB'] * SIG[s][1] >= 1 else 0
def v(st, s): return st['mA'] * st['wA'] * SIG[s][0] + st['mB'] * st['wB'] * SIG[s][1] - st['mu'] * st['u'] * zI(st, s)
def z(st, s): return 1 if v(st, s) >= 1 else 0
def aligned(st, tau): return all(z(st, s) == tau[s] for s in SIG)
def pi(st):
    return {'wA': st['wB'], 'wB': st['wA'], 'aA': st['aB'], 'aB': st['aA'], 'mA': st['mB'], 'mB': st['mA'], 'u': st['u'], 'mu': st['mu'], 'kap': st['kap']}
def tau_swap(tau): return {SWAP[s]: tau[s] for s in tau}

# ---------------- 结构型 Σ(τ) ----------------
def P_of(tau): return [x for x in ('A', 'B') if tau[x] == 1]
def Sigma(st, tau):
    P = P_of(tau); wt = {'A': st['mA'] * st['wA'], 'B': st['mB'] * st['wB']}; ut = st['mu'] * st['u']
    rel = {'R1 合取募集 z_I(AB) = 1': zI(st, 'AB') == 1,
           'R2 正输入不募集 ∀X∈P：z_I(X) = 0': all(zI(st, x) == 0 for x in P),
           'R3 抑制有效 ũ > 0': ut > 0,
           'R4 正输入兴奋有效 ∀X∈P：w̃_X > 0': all(wt[x] > 0 for x in P),
           'D 深浅关系 ũ > w̃_A + w̃_B − 1': ut > wt['A'] + wt['B'] - 1}
    return rel
def inSigma(st, tau): return all(Sigma(st, tau).values())

TAUS = {tuple(t): {'A': t[0], 'B': t[1], 'AB': t[2]} for t in itertools.product((0, 1), repeat=3)}
def monotone(t): return t['AB'] >= t['A'] and t['AB'] >= t['B']
T2 = {k: t for k, t in TAUS.items() if not monotone(t)}
R['任务分类（z(A), z(B), z(AB)）'] = {'非单调 T2': sorted(T2), '单调': sorted(k for k, t in TAUS.items() if monotone(t))}

GW = [Fr(x) for x in ('0', '1/2', '1', '3/2', '2')]; GA = [Fr(x) for x in ('0', '1/4', '1/2', '3/4', '1', '5/4')]; GU = [Fr(x) for x in ('0', '1/2', '1', '3/2', '2', '5/2', '3')]
def states(gates=True):
    for wA, wB, aA, aB, u in itertools.product(GW, GW, GA, GA, GU):
        for mA, mB, mu in (itertools.product((0, 1), repeat=3) if gates else [(1, 1, 1)]):
            yield {'wA': wA, 'wB': wB, 'aA': aA, 'aB': aB, 'u': u, 'mA': Fr(mA), 'mB': Fr(mB), 'mu': Fr(mu), 'kap': Fr(0)}

# ---------------- (b) 对齐 ⇒ Σ(τ)；承担；可复用域 ----------------
nec, bear, cnt = {}, {}, {}
for st in states():
    for k, t in TAUS.items():
        if not aligned(st, t): continue
        cnt[k] = cnt.get(k, 0) + 1
        if k in T2:
            nec.setdefault(k, [0, 0]); nec[k][0] += 1; nec[k][1] += inSigma(st, t)
            P = P_of(t); b = bear.setdefault(k, {'u := 0（AB 失配）': 0, 'a := 0（AB 失配）': 0, 'a_X := 1，X∈P（X 失配）': 0, 'w_X := 0，X∈P（X 失配）': 0})
            b['u := 0（AB 失配）'] += not aligned({**st, 'u': Fr(0)}, t)
            b['a := 0（AB 失配）'] += not aligned({**st, 'aA': Fr(0), 'aB': Fr(0)}, t)
            b['a_X := 1，X∈P（X 失配）'] += all(z({**st, 'a' + x: Fr(1)}, x) == 0 for x in P)
            b['w_X := 0，X∈P（X 失配）'] += all(z({**st, 'w' + x: Fr(0)}, x) == 0 for x in P)
R['(b) 对齐 ⇒ Σ(τ)：(对齐态数, 其中属 Σ(τ) 者)'] = {str(k): v for k, v in nec.items()}
R['(b) 逐关系承担：破坏后失配的对齐态数（应等于对齐态数）'] = {str(k): v for k, v in bear.items()}
mono_wit = {}
for k, t in TAUS.items():
    if k in T2: continue
    w = next((st for st in states(False) if aligned(st, t) and not inSigma(st, t) and st['u'] == 0), None)
    mono_wit[str(k)] = None if w is None else {x: S(w[x]) for x in ('wA', 'wB', 'aA', 'aB', 'u')}
R['(b) 单调任务：存在 ũ = 0 的对齐态（Σ 不必要）'] = mono_wit
non_triv = {}
for k, t in T2.items():
    ent = {'wA': Fr(1), 'wB': Fr(1), 'aA': Fr(1, 2), 'aB': Fr(1, 2), 'u': Fr(0), 'mA': Fr(1), 'mB': Fr(1), 'mu': Fr(1), 'kap': Fr(0)}
    nm = next((st for st in states(False) if inSigma(st, t) and not aligned(st, t)), None)
    non_triv[str(k)] = {'入口 (1, 1, 1/2, 1/2, 0) 属 Σ(τ)': inSigma(ent, t), 'Σ(τ) 中有不对齐者': None if nm is None else {x: S(nm[x]) for x in ('wA', 'wB', 'aA', 'aB', 'u')}}
R['(b) 非平凡'] = non_triv

# 冗余扩展：两个中间神经元 j = 1, 2；合计式 Σ_{J₀(P)} ũ_j > 1（τ = (1,1,0)）、> w̃_Y（τ 只有 X 为正，Y 为另一输入）
def red_states():
    G2 = [Fr(x) for x in ('0', '1/2', '1', '3/2', '2')]; GA2 = [Fr(x) for x in ('0', '1/2', '1')]
    for wA, wB in itertools.product(G2, repeat=2):
        for a1A, a1B, a2A, a2B in itertools.product(GA2, repeat=4):
            for u1, u2 in itertools.product([Fr(x) for x in ('0', '1', '2', '3')], repeat=2):
                yield (wA, wB, (a1A, a1B, u1), (a2A, a2B, u2))
def red_out(st, s):
    wA, wB, *us = st; xa, xb = SIG[s]
    return 1 if wA * xa + wB * xb - sum(u * (1 if aA * xa + aB * xb >= 1 else 0) for aA, aB, u in us) >= 1 else 0
red = {}
for st in red_states():
    for k, t in T2.items():
        if not all(red_out(st, s) == t[s] for s in SIG): continue
        P = P_of(t); J0 = [u for aA, aB, u in st[2:] if aA + aB >= 1 and all((aA if x == 'A' else aB) < 1 for x in P)]
        bound = 1 if len(P) == 2 else (st[1] if P == ['A'] else st[0])
        rr = red.setdefault(str(k), [0, 0]); rr[0] += 1; rr[1] += sum(J0) > bound
R['(b) 冗余扩展（两单元）：(对齐态数, 满足合计式者)；J₀(P) = 在 AB 上募集、在一切正输入上不募集的单元'] = red

# 三输入反例（处置①的理由）
def tri(sub):
    exc = len(sub); inh = sum(Fr(4, 5) for pair in itertools.combinations((0, 1, 2), 2) if set(pair) <= set(sub))
    return exc - inh
R['三输入分布式抑制反例：各子集膜驱动与放电'] = {str(sub): (S(tri(sub)), tri(sub) >= 1) for n in (1, 2, 3) for sub in itertools.combinations((0, 1, 2), n)}
R['三输入反例：只在全合取上募集的单元数'] = 0

# ---------------- (a) 同宿主身份：D18-5 等变与 Σ 的 G 不变 ----------------
eq = {'读数 Φ_z、Φ_v 不变': 0, '各输入的放电等变 z(π·σ, σ_g s) = z(σ, s)': 0, 'Σ 的 G 不变：σ ∈ Σ(τ) ⇔ π·σ ∈ Σ(σ_g τ)': 0, '对齐等变': 0, '总数': 0}
for st in states():
    p = pi(st); eq['总数'] += 1
    eq['读数 Φ_z、Φ_v 不变'] += z(p, 'AB') == z(st, 'AB') and v(p, 'AB') == v(st, 'AB')
    eq['各输入的放电等变 z(π·σ, σ_g s) = z(σ, s)'] += all(z(p, SWAP[s]) == z(st, s) for s in SIG)
    eq['Σ 的 G 不变：σ ∈ Σ(τ) ⇔ π·σ ∈ Σ(σ_g τ)'] += all(inSigma(st, t) == inSigma(p, tau_swap(t)) for t in TAUS.values())
    eq['对齐等变'] += all(aligned(st, t) == aligned(p, tau_swap(t)) for t in TAUS.values())
R['(a) 等变核验（全网格含门态）'] = eq

# 一拍规则（第十七 4.1）与过程配对 PC_g：兴奋沉积 w_X ↔ w_{πX}，募集沉积 a_X ↔ a_{πX}，抑制沉积 u ↔ u
def beat(st, s, t):
    """返回 (新状态, 各过程对各位的写入贡献 {(过程, 位): 增量})。"""
    xa, xb = SIG[s]; zi = zI(st, s); zz = z(st, s); c = {}
    c[('exc', 'wA')] = t * xa * Fr(1, 2); c[('exc', 'wB')] = t * xb * Fr(1, 2)
    c[('rec', 'aA')] = t * xa * Fr(1, 4); c[('rec', 'aB')] = t * xb * Fr(1, 4)
    c[('inh', 'u')] = st['kap'] * zi * zz
    new = dict(st)
    for (pr, site), d in c.items(): new[site] = st[site] + d
    return new, c
PIS = {'wA': 'wB', 'wB': 'wA', 'aA': 'aB', 'aB': 'aA', 'u': 'u'}
pc = {'写入等变 W(π·σ, σ_g s) = π·W(σ, s)': 0, 'PC_g：过程贡献逐对相同（κ_g 取同名过程）': 0, '总数': 0}
for st in states(False):
    for kap in (Fr(0), Fr(1, 2)):
        st2 = {**st, 'kap': kap}
        for s in SIG:
            for t in (0, 1):
                n1, c1 = beat(st2, s, t); n2, c2 = beat(pi(st2), SWAP[s], t); pc['总数'] += 1
                pc['写入等变 W(π·σ, σ_g s) = π·W(σ, s)'] += n2 == pi(n1)
                pc['PC_g：过程贡献逐对相同（κ_g 取同名过程）'] += all(c2[(pr, PIS[site])] == d for (pr, site), d in c1.items())
R['(a) 规则等变与过程配对（门全开；κ ∈ {0, 1/2}；全网格 × 输入 × 教学信号）'] = pc

# 形成记录与成员的等变：第十七形成史 A、A、B、B（t = 1）+ 留存 A、B、A、B（t = 0），及其改名史
def history(entry, seq):
    """按 D18-3：资格区间由场自身写入律在输入下偏离基线的版本开始；成员 = 区间延续且当前深度版本之后执行拍数 ≥ L = 2。"""
    st = dict(entry); base = {x: entry[x] for x in PIS}; form, last, recs = {}, {}, []
    for b, (s, t) in enumerate(seq, 1):
        new, c = beat(st, s, t)
        for (pr, site), d in c.items():
            if d != 0:
                recs.append((b, site, pr))
                if site not in form and new[site] != base[site]: form[site] = (b, pr)
                last[site] = b
            if new[site] == base[site] and site in form: form.pop(site); last.pop(site, None)
        st = new
    nb = len(seq); mem = sorted(x for x in form if nb - last[x] >= 2)
    return st, form, mem, recs
seq = [('A', 1), ('A', 1), ('B', 1), ('B', 1), ('A', 0), ('B', 0), ('A', 0), ('B', 0)]
ent = {'wA': Fr(1), 'wB': Fr(1), 'aA': Fr(0), 'aB': Fr(0), 'u': Fr(0), 'mA': Fr(1), 'mB': Fr(1), 'mu': Fr(1), 'kap': Fr(0)}
h1 = history(ent, seq); h2 = history(pi(ent), [(SWAP[s], t) for s, t in seq])
R['(a) 形成记录与成员等变（第十七形成史与其改名史）'] = {
    '原史：末态、形成版本、成员': ({k: S(h1[0][k]) for k in PIS}, {k: v for k, v in sorted(h1[1].items())}, h1[2]),
    '改名史：末态、形成版本、成员': ({k: S(h2[0][k]) for k in PIS}, {k: v for k, v in sorted(h2[1].items())}, h2[2]),
    '末态 π 对应': pi(h1[0]) == h2[0], '成员 π 对应': sorted(PIS[x] for x in h1[2]) == h2[2],
    '形成记录条目逐条改名对应': sorted((b, PIS[x], pr) for b, x, pr in h1[3]) == sorted(h2[3])}

# ======================= 探针、轨道、等变与成员层复用 =======================
SITES = ('wA', 'wB', 'aA', 'aB', 'u')
class Hist:
    """历史引擎：拍（场自身三项沉积）与直写事件；资格区间按第十七 2.2(a)：
    形成 = 场自身沉积在输入下写出偏离基线的版本且该位不在区间内；区间内的改写（沉积或直写）只改深度；回到基线终止区间；
    不在区间内的直写升起不开区间。成员 = 区间延续且当前深度版本之后执行拍数 ≥ 2。"""
    def __init__(self, entry):
        self.st = dict(entry); self.base = {x: entry[x] for x in SITES}; self.form = {}; self.last = {}; self.recs = []; self.nb = 0
    def _upd(self, site, old, new, kind, label):
        if new == old: return
        self.recs.append((self.nb, site, label))
        if site in self.form:
            if new == self.base[site]: self.form.pop(site); self.last.pop(site, None)
            else: self.last[site] = self.nb
        elif kind == 'Sed' and new != self.base[site]:
            self.form[site] = (self.nb, label); self.last[site] = self.nb
    def beat(self, s, t):
        self.nb += 1; new, c = beat(self.st, s, t)
        for site in SITES: self._upd(site, self.st[site], new[site], 'Sed', {'wA': 'exc', 'wB': 'exc', 'aA': 'rec', 'aB': 'rec', 'u': 'inh'}[site])
        self.st = new
    def rw(self, site, val):
        old = self.st[site]; self.st = {**self.st, site: Fr(val)}; self._upd(site, old, Fr(val), 'Rw', 'rw')
    def mem(self): return sorted(x for x in self.form if self.nb - self.last[x] >= 2)
    def clock(self): return {x: self.nb - self.last[x] for x in self.form}
def run_ops(entry, ops):
    h = Hist(entry)
    for op in ops:
        if op[0] == 'b': h.beat(op[1], op[2])
        else: h.rw(op[1], op[2])
    return h
def pi_op(op): return ('b', SWAP[op[1]], op[2]) if op[0] == 'b' else ('r', PIS[op[1]], op[2])

# ---- 09-02：模板同型与整态改名同一 ----
tau_ab = {'A': 1, 'B': 1, 'AB': 0}
e1 = {'wA': Fr(1), 'wB': Fr(1), 'aA': Fr(1, 2), 'aB': Fr(1, 2), 'u': Fr(3, 2), 'mA': Fr(1), 'mB': Fr(1), 'mu': Fr(1), 'kap': Fr(0)}; e2 = {**e1, 'u': Fr(2)}
R['探针：u = 3/2 与 u = 2'] = {'两态对 (1,1,0) 对齐': (aligned(e1, tau_ab), aligned(e2, tau_ab)), '模板关系逐项同值': Sigma(e1, tau_ab) == Sigma(e2, tau_ab),
    '模板同型（同属 Σ(τ)）': inSigma(e1, tau_ab) and inSigma(e2, tau_ab), '整态改名同一（经 id 或 swap 互换）': e2 in (e1, pi(e1))}
orb = {}
for k, t in TAUS.items():
    orb.setdefault(tuple(sorted({k, tuple(tau_swap(t)[x] for x in ('A', 'B', 'AB'))})), None)
R['任务在 G 下的轨道'] = [list(o) for o in orb]
cnt_gi = sum(1 for st in states() for t in T2.values() if inSigma(st, t) and inSigma(pi(st), tau_swap(t)))
cnt_s = sum(1 for st in states() for t in T2.values() if inSigma(st, t))
R['改名同一 ⇒ 模板同型（σ ∈ Σ(τ) 时 π·σ ∈ Σ(σ_g τ)）：(满足数, Σ 内态数)'] = (cnt_gi, cnt_s)

# ---- 09-03：写入等变（全部门态 × 四个 κ） ----
pc2 = {'写入等变': 0, 'PC_g 逐对': 0, '总数': 0}
for st in states():
    for kap in (Fr(0), Fr(1, 4), Fr(1, 2), Fr(1)):
        st2 = {**st, 'kap': kap}
        for s_ in SIG:
            for t in (0, 1):
                n1, c1 = beat(st2, s_, t); n2, c2 = beat(pi(st2), SWAP[s_], t); pc2['总数'] += 1
                pc2['写入等变'] += n2 == pi(n1); pc2['PC_g 逐对'] += all(c2[(pr, PIS[site])] == d for (pr, site), d in c1.items())
R['写入等变（门态 8 种 × κ 4 值 × 全网格 × 输入 × 教学信号）'] = pc2
# 历史等变：拍与直写事件的全部长度 ≤ 4 序列
OPS = [('b', s_, t) for s_ in ('A', 'B', 'AB') for t in (0, 1)] + [('r', 'u', 2), ('r', 'u', 0), ('r', 'wA', 0), ('r', 'wB', 0), ('r', 'aA', 1), ('r', 'aB', 1)]
assert all(pi_op(o) in OPS for o in OPS)
ENTS = {'第十七例一入口（κ = 1/2）': {'wA': Fr(1), 'wB': Fr(1), 'aA': Fr(1, 2), 'aB': Fr(1, 2), 'u': Fr(0), 'mA': Fr(1), 'mB': Fr(1), 'mu': Fr(1), 'kap': Fr(1, 2)},
        '非对称入口、门 m_A = 0（κ = 1/2）': {'wA': Fr(1, 2), 'wB': Fr(1), 'aA': Fr(1, 4), 'aB': Fr(1, 2), 'u': Fr(1, 2), 'mA': Fr(0), 'mB': Fr(1), 'mu': Fr(1), 'kap': Fr(1, 2)},
        '低入口（κ = 1）': {'wA': Fr(1, 2), 'wB': Fr(1, 2), 'aA': Fr(1, 4), 'aB': Fr(1, 4), 'u': Fr(0), 'mA': Fr(1), 'mB': Fr(1), 'mu': Fr(1), 'kap': Fr(1)}}
heq = {}
for name, ent in ENTS.items():
    c = {'史数': 0, '末态 π 对应': 0, 'ℛ 条目逐条对应': 0, '形成版本对应': 0, '成员对应': 0, '留存计时对应': 0, '含 u 形成的史数': 0}
    for L in range(1, 5):
        for ops in itertools.product(OPS, repeat=L):
            h1 = run_ops(ent, ops); h2 = run_ops(pi(ent), [pi_op(o) for o in ops]); c['史数'] += 1
            c['末态 π 对应'] += pi(h1.st) == h2.st
            c['ℛ 条目逐条对应'] += sorted((b, PIS[x], l) for b, x, l in h1.recs) == sorted(h2.recs)
            c['形成版本对应'] += {PIS[x]: v for x, v in h1.form.items()} == h2.form
            c['成员对应'] += sorted(PIS[x] for x in h1.mem()) == h2.mem()
            c['留存计时对应'] += {PIS[x]: v for x, v in h1.clock().items()} == h2.clock()
            c['含 u 形成的史数'] += 'u' in h1.form and h1.form['u'][1] == 'inh'
    heq[name] = c
R['历史等变（拍与直写，全部长度 ≤ 4 序列）'] = heq

# ---- 09-01：成员层复用（D_F：门恒开，只有场自身沉积，u 入口在基线） ----
def dfs(ent, L, visit):
    def rec(h, depth):
        visit(h)
        if depth == L: return
        for s_ in ('A', 'B', 'AB'):
            for t in (0, 1):
                h2 = Hist(ent); h2.st, h2.form, h2.last, h2.recs, h2.nb = dict(h.st), dict(h.form), dict(h.last), list(h.recs), h.nb
                h2.beat(s_, t); rec(h2, depth + 1)
    rec(Hist(ent), 0)
memc = {str(k): {'对齐且 u 已留存': 0, '其中 Σ(τ) ∧ mem(u)': 0, '对齐而 u 未满留存（条款所排除）': 0} for k in T2}
ex_ret = {}
for wA, wB, aA, aB, kap in itertools.product((Fr(1, 2), Fr(1)), (Fr(1, 2), Fr(1)), (Fr(1, 4), Fr(1, 2)), (Fr(1, 4), Fr(1, 2)), (Fr(1, 2), Fr(1))):
    ent = {'wA': wA, 'wB': wB, 'aA': aA, 'aB': aB, 'u': Fr(0), 'mA': Fr(1), 'mB': Fr(1), 'mu': Fr(1), 'kap': kap}
    def visit(h):
        for k, t in T2.items():
            if not aligned(h.st, t): continue
            ret = 'u' in h.form and h.nb - h.last['u'] >= 2
            d = memc[str(k)]
            if ret:
                d['对齐且 u 已留存'] += 1; d['其中 Σ(τ) ∧ mem(u)'] += inSigma(h.st, t) and 'u' in h.mem()
            else:
                d['对齐而 u 未满留存（条款所排除）'] += 1; ex_ret.setdefault(str(k), (str({x: S(ent[x]) for x in SITES + ('kap',)}), h.nb, {x: S(h.st[x]) for x in SITES}, h.clock()))
    dfs(ent, 6, visit)
R['成员层复用（D_F，入口 w ∈ {1/2, 1}²、a ∈ {1/4, 1/2}²、u = 0、κ ∈ {1/2, 1}；全部长度 ≤ 6 输入—教学史）'] = memc
R['留存条款的必要（对齐而 u 未满留存之例：入口、拍数、状态、留存计时）'] = ex_ret
# 纯直写边界（D_F 之外）
h = run_ops(ENTS['第十七例一入口（κ = 1/2）'] | {'kap': Fr(0)}, [('b', 'A', 0), ('b', 'B', 0), ('r', 'u', 2), ('b', 'A', 0), ('b', 'B', 0), ('b', 'AB', 0)])
R['纯直写边界（κ = 0，u 由基线直写到 2；第十七例五型）'] = {'对齐 (1,1,0)': aligned(h.st, tau_ab), 'Σ(τ)': inSigma(h.st, tau_ab), 'u 有资格区间': 'u' in h.form, '成员': h.mem()}
# 三项任务各一条全部承担位合格的历史
def full_hist(ent, ops, t):
    h = run_ops(ent, ops); P = P_of(t); bearing = [f'w{x}' for x in P] + ['aA', 'aB', 'u']
    return {'入口': {x: S(ent[x]) for x in SITES + ('kap',)}, '事件序列': [f'{o[1]}{"(t=1)" if o[2] else ""}' for o in ops], '末态': {x: S(h.st[x]) for x in SITES},
            '对齐': aligned(h.st, t), 'Σ(τ)': inSigma(h.st, t), '承担位': bearing, '成员': h.mem(), '承担位全为成员': all(x in h.mem() for x in bearing),
            '形成版本': {x: v for x, v in sorted(h.form.items())}, '留存计时': h.clock()}
base_e = {'mA': Fr(1), 'mB': Fr(1), 'mu': Fr(1), 'kap': Fr(1, 2)}
teachAB = [('b', 'A', 1), ('b', 'B', 1)] + [('b', 'AB', 0)] * 4 + [('b', 'A', 0), ('b', 'B', 0), ('b', 'A', 0), ('b', 'B', 0)]
R['全部承担位合格的历史'] = {
    '(1,1,0)': full_hist({'wA': Fr(1, 2), 'wB': Fr(1, 2), 'aA': Fr(1, 4), 'aB': Fr(1, 4), 'u': Fr(0), **base_e}, teachAB, tau_ab),
    '(1,0,0)': full_hist({'wA': Fr(1, 2), 'wB': Fr(0), 'aA': Fr(1, 4), 'aB': Fr(1, 4), 'u': Fr(0), **base_e}, teachAB, {'A': 1, 'B': 0, 'AB': 0}),
    '(0,1,0)': full_hist({'wA': Fr(0), 'wB': Fr(1, 2), 'aA': Fr(1, 4), 'aB': Fr(1, 4), 'u': Fr(0), **base_e}, [pi_op(o) for o in teachAB], {'A': 0, 'B': 1, 'AB': 0})}

def fix(o):
    if isinstance(o, dict): return {str(k): fix(v) for k, v in o.items()}
    if isinstance(o, (list, tuple)): return [fix(x) for x in o]
    if isinstance(o, Fr): return str(o)
    return o
json.dump(fix(R), open('struct_admit.json', 'w'), ensure_ascii=False, indent=1, sort_keys=True, default=str)
print(json.dumps(fix(R), ensure_ascii=False, indent=1, default=str))
~~~

【资源 046 起】源码　stab\_checks.py　SHA-256 50af07a012f9b054b6a3c8db83d3c389b4f82f7842b46491ce999bc67e2d3beb

~~~python
"""一般化结构稳定式 · 核算（纯 Python，精确有理，确定性）
宿主与资格规则逐字沿用 source_checks（第二十的 𝔐_I、D_mem、资格区间、Σ、mem）；本程序只加稳定检验。
首证域：τ = (1,1,0)，初态 = 第二十 S1 终态（入口 κ = 1/2，AB 三拍，A、B 留存）；第二域：τ = (1,0,0)，初态 = 第二十 1.5 的承担位全合格史终态。
Δ1：t = 0 的输入续行；Δ2：t ∈ {0,1}；Δ3：一次直写 u := c，c ∈ D_c = {k/8：k = 0..24}；Δ4：一次直写 κ := κ′；Δ43：κ′ 后 c。
判定截面：每次直写后与每拍末。固定对象层：u 的当前资格区间的形成版本与初态相同；存在层：u 有资格区间且为成员。
"""
import sys, os, json, itertools, contextlib, io
from fractions import Fraction as Fr
HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
def prefix(script, stop):
    p = os.path.join(HERE, script); src = open(p, encoding='utf-8').read().split(stop)[0]; sc = {'__file__': p}
    with contextlib.redirect_stdout(io.StringIO()): exec(compile(src, p, 'exec'), sc)
    return sc
sc = prefix('source_checks.py', '# ---- 起点与默认来源的排除')
Hist, aligned, inSigma, z = sc['Hist'], sc['aligned'], sc['inSigma'], sc['z']
R = {}
def S(x): return str(x)
TAU = {'A': 1, 'B': 1, 'AB': 0}; TAUA = {'A': 1, 'B': 0, 'AB': 0}
def ent(w, a, u, k): return {'wA': Fr(w[0]), 'wB': Fr(w[1]), 'aA': Fr(a[0]), 'aB': Fr(a[1]), 'u': Fr(u), 'kap': Fr(k)}
def build(entry, ops):
    h = Hist(entry)
    for o in ops: h.op(o)
    return h
RET = [('b', 'A', 0), ('b', 'B', 0)]
H0 = build(ent((1, 1), (Fr(1, 2), Fr(1, 2)), 0, Fr(1, 2)), [('b', 'AB', 0)] * 3 + RET)                       # 双正 S1
HA = build(ent((Fr(1, 2), 0), (Fr(1, 4), Fr(1, 4)), 0, Fr(1, 2)), [('b', 'A', 1), ('b', 'B', 1)] + [('b', 'AB', 0)] * 4 + [('b', 'A', 0), ('b', 'B', 0)] * 2)  # 单正
DOM = {'双正 (1,1,0)': (H0, TAU), '单正 (1,0,0)': (HA, TAUA)}
FORM0 = {k: v[0].form['u'] for k, v in DOM.items()}
def thr(h): return h.st['wA'] + h.st['wB'] - 1
def sect(h, tau, f0):
    q = inSigma(h.st, tau) and h.mem('u')
    return {'Q': q, 'Q∧对齐': q and aligned(h.st, tau), '固定对象': q and h.form.get('u') == f0, 'D': h.st['u'] > thr(h), 'mem': h.mem('u'),
            '区间': 'u' in h.form, '原区间': h.form.get('u') == f0}
R['初态'] = {k: {'状态': {x: S(v[0].st[x]) for x in ('wA', 'wB', 'aA', 'aB', 'u', 'kap')}, '阈 w_A + w_B − 1': S(thr(v[0])), **sect(v[0], v[1], FORM0[k])} for k, v in DOM.items()}

# ---- Δ1：全部 t = 0 序列（长度 ≤ 8），逐截面 ----
d1 = {}
for k, (h0, tau) in DOM.items():
    tot = ok = 0
    for L in range(1, 9):
        for seq in itertools.product(('A', 'B', 'AB'), repeat=L):
            h = h0.clone(); good = True
            for s_ in seq:
                h.op(('b', s_, 0)); x = sect(h, tau, FORM0[k]); good &= x['固定对象'] and x['Q∧对齐']
            tot += 1; ok += good
    d1[k] = (ok, tot)
R['Δ1 全部 t = 0 序列（长度 ≤ 8）逐截面固定对象层 Q ∧ 对齐保持：(保持数, 序列数)'] = d1

# ---- Δ2：t ∈ {0,1}（长度 ≤ 5），首次失效与恢复分类 ----
d2 = {}
for k, (h0, tau) in DOM.items():
    first = {}; c = {'全程保持': 0, '失效后固定对象恢复（末截面）': 0, '失效未恢复（末截面）': 0, '序列数': 0}; ex = {}
    for L in range(1, 6):
        for seq in itertools.product([(s_, t) for s_ in ('A', 'B', 'AB') for t in (0, 1)], repeat=L):
            h = h0.clone(); failed = False; fi = None
            ft = next((i + 1 for i, (s_, t) in enumerate(seq) if t == 1), None)
            for i, (s_, t) in enumerate(seq):
                h.op(('b', s_, t)); x = sect(h, tau, FORM0[k])
                if not (x['Q∧对齐'] and x['固定对象']) and not failed: failed, fi = True, i + 1
            c['序列数'] += 1; x = sect(h, tau, FORM0[k])
            if not failed: c['全程保持'] += 1
            elif x['Q∧对齐'] and x['固定对象']: c['失效后固定对象恢复（末截面）'] += 1; ex.setdefault('恢复例', [f'{s_}(t={t})' for s_, t in seq])
            else: c['失效未恢复（末截面）'] += 1
            if failed: first[fi] = first.get(fi, 0) + 1
            c['首次失效恰在首个教学拍'] = c.get('首次失效恰在首个教学拍', 0) + (failed and fi == ft); c['含教学拍的序列'] = c.get('含教学拍的序列', 0) + (ft is not None)
    h = h0.clone(); h.op(('b', 'A', 1))
    d2[k] = {**c, '首次失效拍号分布': first, **ex, '一个 A 教学拍后': {x: S(h.st[x]) for x in ('wA', 'aA', 'u')} | {'阈': S(thr(h)), **{kk: vv for kk, vv in sect(h, tau, FORM0[k]).items() if kk in ('D', 'Q')}}}
R['Δ2 t ∈ {0,1} 序列（长度 ≤ 5）'] = d2

# ---- Δ3 / Δ43：u := c，再 Δ1 续行（长度 ≤ 7），按拍序充要条件核验 ----
DC = [Fr(k, 8) for k in range(25)]
def predict(h_after, c, seq, kap, th, f0_same):
    """按拍序的复归判据：返回首个复归截面（拍号）或 None。
    c > th：任两拍后（无后续写入）；0 < c ≤ th 且 κ > 0：第 m* 个 AB 拍（c + m*·κ > th 的最小 m*）之后再执行两拍；c = 0：存在层同 0 < c ≤ th 的计数（新区间）；κ = 0 且 c ≤ th：不复归。"""
    if c > th: need_ab = 0
    elif kap == 0: return None
    else:
        need_ab = 0; u = c
        while u <= th: u += kap; need_ab += 1
    cnt = 0; last = 0 if need_ab == 0 else None
    for i, s_ in enumerate(seq, 1):
        if need_ab and s_ == 'AB' and cnt < need_ab:
            cnt += 1
            if cnt == need_ab: last = i
    if last is None: return None
    return last + 2 if last + 2 <= len(seq) else None
d3 = {}
for k, (h0, tau) in DOM.items():
    th = thr(h0); rows = {}
    for kap in (None, Fr(0), Fr(1, 4), Fr(1)):
        for c in DC:
            if c == h0.st['u']: continue
            agree = tot = 0; rec_any = False; fixed_rec = exist_rec = 0
            for L in range(0, 8):
                for seq in itertools.product(('A', 'B', 'AB'), repeat=L):
                    h = h0.clone()
                    if kap is not None: h.op(('r', 'kap', kap))
                    h.op(('r', 'u', c)); imm = sect(h, tau, FORM0[k])
                    rec = None
                    for i, s_ in enumerate(seq, 1):
                        h.op(('b', s_, 0)); x = sect(h, tau, FORM0[k])
                        layer = '固定对象' if c > 0 else 'Q'
                        if rec is None and x['Q∧对齐'] and (x['固定对象'] if c > 0 else x['Q']): rec = i
                    kk = h0.st['kap'] if kap is None else kap
                    pr = predict(h, c, seq, kk, th, True)
                    tot += 1; agree += (pr == rec); rec_any |= rec is not None
                    if rec is not None and c > 0: fixed_rec += 1
                    if rec is not None and c == 0: exist_rec += 1; assert h.form.get('u') != FORM0[k]
            key = f'κ′ = {"不写" if kap is None else S(kap)}，c = {S(c)}'
            rows[key] = {'即时 D': imm['D'], '即时 mem': imm['mem'], '即时 Q': imm['Q'], '原区间延续': imm['原区间'],
                         '按拍序判据与逐截面复归一致': f'{agree}/{tot}', '存在复归轨迹': rec_any,
                         '复归层': ('固定对象层' if c > 0 else '存在层（新区间）') if rec_any else '不复归'}
    d3[k] = {'阈': S(th), '各值': rows}
R['Δ3、Δ43：u := c 后按 Δ1 续行（长度 ≤ 7 的全部序列）'] = d3
def bounds(k):
    rows = d3[k]['各值']; out = {}
    for kap in ('不写', '0', '1/4', '1'):
        fD = [Fr(r.split('c = ')[1]) for r, v in rows.items() if r.startswith(f'κ′ = {kap}，') and not v['即时 D']]
        fQ = [Fr(r.split('c = ')[1]) for r, v in rows.items() if r.startswith(f'κ′ = {kap}，') and not v['即时 Q']]
        nr = [Fr(r.split('c = ')[1]) for r, v in rows.items() if r.startswith(f'κ′ = {kap}，') and not v['存在复归轨迹']]
        out[f'κ′ {kap}'] = {'D 失效集最大元': S(max(fD)) if fD else None, '即时 Q 失效的 c 数': len(fQ), '不复归的 c': [S(x) for x in nr]}
    return out
R['边界（D_c 上）'] = {k: bounds(k) for k in DOM}
# 持续验证：复归后再 2 拍 Δ1，Q ∧ 对齐保持（例：c = 1/2，序列 AB、AB、A、B、A、B）
h = H0.clone(); h.op(('r', 'u', Fr(1, 2))); tr = []
for s_ in ('AB', 'AB', 'A', 'B', 'A', 'B'):
    h.op(('b', s_, 0)); x = sect(h, TAU, FORM0['双正 (1,1,0)']); tr.append((s_, S(h.st['u']), x['D'], x['mem'], x['固定对象'] and x['Q∧对齐']))
R['恢复与持续验证示例（双正，c = 1/2；每拍：输入, u, D, mem, 固定对象层 Q∧对齐）'] = tr
h = H0.clone(); h.op(('r', 'kap', Fr(0))); h.op(('r', 'u', Fr(5, 4))); tr = [('直写后', sect(h, TAU, FORM0['双正 (1,1,0)'])['Q'])]
for s_ in ('A', 'A', 'A'):
    h.op(('b', s_, 0)); tr.append((s_, sect(h, TAU, FORM0['双正 (1,1,0)'])['固定对象']))
R['例：κ′ = 0、c = 5/4（截面：直写后、各拍末的固定对象层 Q）'] = tr

def fix(o):
    if isinstance(o, dict): return {str(k): fix(v) for k, v in o.items()}
    if isinstance(o, (list, tuple)): return [fix(x) for x in o]
    if isinstance(o, Fr): return str(o)
    return o
json.dump(fix(R), open('stab_checks.json', 'w'), ensure_ascii=False, indent=1, sort_keys=True, default=str)
print(json.dumps(fix(R), ensure_ascii=False, indent=1, default=str))
~~~

【资源 048 起】源码　wsprog\_checks.py　SHA-256 79365742d4f5d9ab55be14b0bf94bfbd7c9d528772185d1ade8f6a63f8ecf007

~~~python
"""权重空间编程区分式 · 核算（纯 Python，精确有理，确定性）
共同协议：宿主 F_p（入口 w = (1/2, 1/2)），目标 g，联合协议窗内次序与触发（cmd ≠ 0 且读数差观测到达），窗间 K0，观察至第 2 窗末。
H_L：第十五的 G（lawworker 联合执行；c = C = 1 入口）。
H_P⁰：规则计算过程 R⁰，写入量 (g − y)/p̂，p̂ = 2 固定；无观测读入。
H_P¹：规则计算过程 R¹，p̂ 初值 1，触发时 p̂ := obs/cmd（运行层寄存器，计算分项 Run 写入）。
R 与 F 的联合执行沿用 lawworker 的 Joint 机制（两模型逐事件同步）；R 的模型申报：变量全为运行层（p̂ 存于寄存器 Cc，层 r），分项 plan、upd、eff 均为 Run，无宿载层沉积，机制路线穷尽申报。
首证域：p = 3、g = 3（入口误差 −1）。观测族：𝒪_end（第 2 窗末 (w0, w1, Φ)）⊂ 𝒪_beh（逐窗 F 读数与对 F 的写入投影：拍、相位、位置、数值）⊂ 𝒪_mech。
"""
import sys, os, json, itertools, contextlib, io
from fractions import Fraction as Fr
HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
def prefix(script, stop):
    p = os.path.join(HERE, script); src = open(p, encoding='utf-8').read().split(stop)[0]; sc = {'__file__': p}
    with contextlib.redirect_stdout(io.StringIO()): exec(compile(src, p, 'exec'), sc)
    return sc
L = prefix('lawworker.py', '# ---------------- 主构造')
Joint, W_stage, Mx, RunX, E, MechC, Q, QNZ, B01, FV, F_model, INIT = (L[k] for k in ('Joint', 'W_stage', 'Mx', 'RunX', 'E', 'MechC', 'Q', 'QNZ', 'B01', 'FV', 'F_model', 'INIT'))
R = {}
def S(x): return str(x)
def R_model(adaptive, decl=True):
    layer = {'c': 'r', 'Cc': 'r', 'dlt': 'r', 'obs': 'r', 'yG': 'r', 'gG': 'r', 'cmd': 'r', **{v: 'ext' for v in FV}}
    dom = {v: Q for v in layer}; dom.update({'x0': B01, 'x1': B01, 'Cc': QNZ, 'dlt': QNZ, 'c': QNZ})
    FM = {'plan': ('Run', {'cmd'}), 'eff': ('Run', {'dlt'}), 'upd': ('Run', {'Cc'}), 'ext_F': ('Run', set(FV) - {'g'}), 'ext_act': ('Run', {'w1'})}
    M = Mx(layer, dom, (lambda s: s['Cc'], {'Cc': 1}), ports={'In': {'obs', 'yG', 'gG'}}, FM=FM, fid='R')
    if decl: M.mech_routes = {'plan', 'eff', 'upd', 'ext_F', 'ext_act', 'port:In'}; M.mech_exhaustive = True
    return M
class JointR(Joint):
    def __init__(self, p, adaptive, init=None):
        st = {**INIT, **(init or {})}
        self.F = RunX(F_model(p), dict(st), hold=True); self.G = RunX(R_model(adaptive), dict(st), hold=False); self.log = []; self.adaptive = adaptive
def R_stage(J, k, p):
    J.ev((k, 1), 'F', f'ro{k}', 'Run', 'readout', 'F', ['w0', 'w1'], ['y'], lambda a, p=p: {'y': a['w0'] + p * a['w1']}, {'w0': 1, 'w1': 1})
    J.ev((k, 3), 'G', f'iny{k}', 'In', 'port', 'R', ['y'], ['yG'], lambda a: {'yG': a['y']}, {'y': 1})
    J.ev((k, 4), 'G', f'ing{k}', 'In', 'port', 'R', ['g'], ['gG'], lambda a: {'gG': a['g']}, {'g': 1})
    J.ev((k, 5), 'G', f'plan{k}', 'Run', 'plan', 'R', ['gG', 'yG', 'Cc'], ['cmd'], lambda a: {'cmd': (a['gG'] - a['yG']) / a['Cc']}, {'gG': 1, 'yG': 1})
    J.ev((k, 6), 'F', f'act{k}', 'Rw', 'port', 'R', ['w1', 'cmd'], ['w1'], lambda a: {'w1': a['w1'] + a['cmd']}, {'w1': 1, 'cmd': 1})
    J.ev((k, 7), 'F', f'ro2_{k}', 'Run', 'readout', 'F', ['w0', 'w1'], ['y'], lambda a, p=p: {'y': a['w0'] + p * a['w1']}, {'w0': 1, 'w1': 1})
    if J.adaptive and J.F.s['cmd'] != 0:
        J.ev((k, 8), 'G', f'obs{k}', 'In', 'port', 'R', ['y', 'yG'], ['obs'], lambda a: {'obs': a['y'] - a['yG']}, {'y': 1, 'yG': 1})
        J.ev((k, 9), 'G', f'eff{k}', 'Run', 'eff', 'R', ['cmd'], ['dlt'], lambda a: {'dlt': a['cmd']}, {'cmd': 1})
        J.ev((k, 10), 'G', f'upd{k}', 'Run', 'upd', 'R', ['obs', 'dlt'], ['Cc'], lambda a: {'Cc': a['obs'] / a['dlt']})
def run(cand, p, g, n=2, w=(Fr(1, 2), Fr(1, 2))):
    init = {'g': Fr(g), 'w0': Fr(w[0]), 'w1': Fr(w[1])}
    if cand == 'H_L': J = Joint(Fr(p), init); stage = lambda k: W_stage(J, k, Fr(p))
    else:
        J = JointR(Fr(p), cand == 'H_P1', {**init, 'Cc': Fr(2) if cand == 'H_P0' else Fr(1), 'c': Fr(1)}); stage = lambda k: R_stage(J, k, Fr(p))
    for i in range(n): stage(20 + 10 * i)
    return J
def proj_end(J, p): s = J.F.s; return (S(s['w0']), S(s['w1']), S(s['w0'] + Fr(p) * s['w1']))
def proj_beh(J):
    """逐窗读数与对 F 的写入投影（拍、相位、位置、数值）；不含写入者内部状态与来源标签。"""
    out = []
    for i, e in enumerate(J.F.events):
        if e['src'] == 'readout': out.append(('读数', e['phase'], S(J.F.trace[i + 1][1]['y'])))
        if e['kind'] == 'Rw' and e['changed']: out.append(('写入', e['phase'], 'w1', S(J.F.trace[i + 1][1]['w1'] - J.F.trace[i][1]['w1'])))
    return out
C3 = ('H_P0', 'H_P1', 'H_L')
runs = {c: run(c, 3, 3) for c in C3}
R['首证域（p = 3，g = 3）：𝒪_end'] = {c: proj_end(runs[c], 3) for c in C3}
R['首证域：𝒪_beh 投影'] = {c: proj_beh(runs[c]) for c in C3}
R['首证域：逐对比较（𝒪_end 相同, 𝒪_beh 相同）'] = {f'{a} 对 {b}': (proj_end(runs[a], 3) == proj_end(runs[b], 3), proj_beh(runs[a]) == proj_beh(runs[b])) for a, b in (('H_P0', 'H_L'), ('H_P0', 'H_P1'), ('H_P1', 'H_L'))}
R['首证域：第 2 窗末误差'] = {c: S(runs[c].F.s['w0'] + 3 * runs[c].F.s['w1'] - 3) for c in C3}
R['首证域：p̂ 与 C 的逐窗值'] = {'H_P1 的 p̂（Cc）': [S(st['Cc']) for ph, st, _ in runs['H_P1'].G.trace if ph[1] in (5,)], 'H_L 的 C': [S(st['Cc']) for ph, st, _ in runs['H_L'].G.trace if ph[1] in (5,)]}

# ---- H_P¹ 与 H_L 的逐窗耦合：族上核验（入口误差、宿主、目标变化；入口已对齐列未判） ----
cpl = {'例数': 0, '𝒪_beh 投影逐窗相同': 0, '入口已对齐（两臂全程零写，未判）': 0}
for p, g, w1 in itertools.product((Fr(-1), Fr(1, 2), Fr(1), Fr(2), Fr(3), Fr(5, 2)), (Fr(0), Fr(2), Fr(3), Fr(-1)), (Fr(0), Fr(1, 2), Fr(1))):
    a, b = run('H_P1', p, g, 3, (Fr(1, 2), w1)), run('H_L', p, g, 3, (Fr(1, 2), w1))
    cpl['例数'] += 1; cpl['𝒪_beh 投影逐窗相同'] += proj_beh(a) == proj_beh(b)
    cpl['入口已对齐（两臂全程零写，未判）'] += (Fr(1, 2) + p * w1 == g)
R['H_P¹ 与 H_L 逐窗耦合（p 六值 × g 四值 × w1 三值，三窗）'] = cpl
# H_P⁰ 与 H_L：首证域外的末态交叠（p = 2）
o = {c: run(c, 2, 2) for c in ('H_P0', 'H_L')}
R['首证域外交叠：p = 2、g = 2 的第 2 窗末 (w0, w1, Φ)'] = {c: proj_end(o[c], 2) for c in o}

# ---- 𝒪_mech：机制申报下的规则级 P10 与实际窗 ----
class Rec:
    def __init__(self): self.F, self.G = Rec1(), Rec1()
    def ev(self, ph, owner, eid, kind, src, part, reads, writes, fn, deg=None):
        if owner == 'F': self.F.add(ph, E(eid, kind, src, part, reads, writes, fn, deg)); self.G.add(ph, E(eid, 'Run', 'ext_act' if part in ('G', 'R') else 'ext_F', part, reads, writes, fn, deg))
        else: self.G.add(ph, E(eid, kind, src, part, reads, writes, fn, deg)); self.F.add(ph, E(eid, 'Run', 'ext_G', part, reads, writes, fn, deg))
class Rec1:
    def __init__(self): self.steps = []
    def add(self, ph, e): self.steps.append((ph, e))
rec = Rec(); rec.F.s = {'cmd': Fr(1)}; rec.adaptive = True
R_stage(rec, 20, Fr(3))
st0 = {**INIT, 'g': Fr(3), 'Cc': Fr(1)}
R['𝒪_mech：R¹ 的规则级 P10（机制申报穷尽；一窗协议重放）'] = MechC(lambda: RunX(R_model(True), dict(st0)), {'R¹ 一窗': rec.G.steps})
R['𝒪_mech：R¹ 机制未申报时'] = MechC(lambda: RunX(R_model(True, False), dict(st0)), {'R¹ 一窗': rec.G.steps})
G_model = L['G_model']; recG = Rec(); recG.F.s = {'cmd': Fr(1)}
class JJ:  # 供 W_stage 记录 G 的一窗事件
    def __init__(self): self.r = Rec(); self.F = type('x', (), {'s': {'cmd': Fr(1)}})()
    def ev(self, *a, **k): self.r.ev(*a, **k)
jj = JJ(); W_stage(jj, 20, Fr(3))
R['𝒪_mech：G 的规则级 P10（同一窗协议重放）'] = MechC(lambda: RunX(G_model(), dict(st0)), {'G 一窗': jj.r.G.steps})
R['𝒪_mech：R¹ 的宿载层变量'] = sorted(v for v, l in R_model(True).layer.items() if l == 'theta')
R['𝒪_mech：G 的宿载层变量'] = sorted(v for v, l in G_model().layer.items() if l == 'theta')
R['𝒪_mech：首证域实际写入 p̂ / C 的事件（事件, 种类, 来源路线, 来源含输入事件）'] = {
    c: [(e['id'], e['kind'], e['src'], any(isinstance(k, tuple) and len(k) == 3 and k[2].startswith(('iny', 'ing', 'obs')) and s is True for k, s in runs[c].G.vtrace[i + 1]['Cc'].items()))
        for i, e in enumerate(runs[c].G.events) if 'Cc' in e['changed']] for c in ('H_P1', 'H_L')}

def fix(o):
    if isinstance(o, dict): return {str(k): fix(v) for k, v in o.items()}
    if isinstance(o, (list, tuple)): return [fix(x) for x in o]
    if isinstance(o, Fr): return str(o)
    return o
json.dump(fix(R), open('wsprog_checks.json', 'w'), ensure_ascii=False, indent=1, sort_keys=True, default=str)
print(json.dumps(fix(R), ensure_ascii=False, indent=1, default=str))
~~~

【资源 050 起】源码　trainsub\_checks.py　SHA-256 ec9afe33c00aa6ef99744a9019975fb90694f64d91a32e8fd56fe256c55a574e

~~~python
"""训练替代区分式 · 核算（纯 Python，精确有理，确定性）
共同宿主 F⁺ = 第十五的 F（p = 2）+ 训练分项 tr（Sed：w1 += η·e·(g′ − y)，η = 1/2，读同窗实时 y）+ 教学输入 {g′, e}（In）。
H_L：第十五的 F 与 G（lawworker 的联合执行；F⁺ 中 tr 因 e ≡ 0 而同值，不写，故以 F 的联合模型执行，另以 F⁺ 单场运行核验 e ≡ 0 时 tr 同值）。
H_T：F⁺ 单场运行，窗内次序：F 读出 → 教学输入 g′ := g、e := e_on → tr → F 读出 → 教学输入 e := 0。
共同：第十五 2.3 入口；窗位第 20、30、…拍；窗间续行 K0／Kp／Km。共同达标判据 Ach_K：判定截面 Φ = g，且按同一续行与该臂固定窗协议此后每窗有效修正量为零。
撤教期：H_T 的 e_on = 0；H_L 的 G 保留其许可操作。检验干预：撤教后第一个调控窗的 F 读出之前，直写 w1 += δ（施作标识 test）。
"""
import sys, os, json, contextlib, io
from fractions import Fraction as Fr
HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
def prefix(script, stop):
    p = os.path.join(HERE, script); src = open(p, encoding='utf-8').read().split(stop)[0]; sc = {'__file__': p}
    with contextlib.redirect_stdout(io.StringIO()): exec(compile(src, p, 'exec'), sc)
    return sc
L = prefix('lawworker.py', '# ---------------- 主构造')
Joint, W_stage, F_beat, Mx, RunX, E, MechC, Q, B01, GV = (L[k] for k in ('Joint', 'W_stage', 'F_beat', 'Mx', 'RunX', 'E', 'MechC', 'Q', 'B01', 'GV'))
R = {}
def S(x): return str(x)
P, G_ = Fr(2), Fr(2); ETA = Fr(1, 2)
INIT_T = {'w0': Fr(1, 2), 'w1': Fr(1, 2), 'x0': Fr(0), 'x1': Fr(0), 'u': Fr(0), 'y': Fr(0), 'g': G_, 'gp': Fr(0), 'e': Fr(0)}
def Fplus(decl=True):
    layer = {'w0': 'theta', 'w1': 'theta', 'x0': 'r', 'x1': 'r', 'u': 'r', 'y': 'r', 'gp': 'r', 'e': 'r', 'g': 'ext'}
    dom = {v: Q for v in layer}; dom.update({'x0': B01, 'x1': B01, 'e': B01})
    FM = {'mem': ('Run', {'u'}), 'stdp': ('Sed', {'w1'}), 'tr': ('Sed', {'w1'}), 'readout': ('Run', {'y'}), 'ext_goal': ('Run', {'g'})}
    M = Mx(layer, dom, (lambda s: s['w0'] + P * s['w1'], {'w0': 1, 'w1': 1}), ports={'Rw': {'w0', 'w1'}, 'In': {'x0', 'x1', 'gp', 'e'}},
           FM=FM, fid='F', goal={'role': 'Xg', 'var': 'g', 'field': 'F'}, target={'g': {'field': 'F'}})
    if decl: M.mech_routes = {'mem', 'stdp', 'tr', 'readout', 'ext_goal', 'port:In', 'port:Rw'}; M.mech_exhaustive = True
    return M
def T_window(r, k, e_on, delta=None):
    if delta is not None:
        r.ev((k, 0), E(f'pert{k}', 'Rw', 'port', 'test', ['w1'], ['w1'], lambda a, d=delta: {'w1': a['w1'] + d}, {'w1': 1}))
    r.ev((k, 1), E(f'ro{k}', 'Run', 'readout', 'F', ['w0', 'w1'], ['y'], lambda a: {'y': a['w0'] + P * a['w1']}, {'w0': 1, 'w1': 1}))
    r.ev((k, 2), E(f'teach{k}', 'In', 'port', 'teach', ['g'], ['gp', 'e'], lambda a, e=e_on: {'gp': a['g'], 'e': Fr(e)}, {'g': 1}))
    r.ev((k, 3), E(f'tr{k}', 'Sed', 'tr', 'F', ['w1', 'e', 'gp', 'y'], ['w1'], lambda a: {'w1': a['w1'] + ETA * a['e'] * (a['gp'] - a['y'])}, {'w1': 1, 'gp': 1, 'y': 1}))
    r.ev((k, 4), E(f'ro2_{k}', 'Run', 'readout', 'F', ['w0', 'w1'], ['y'], lambda a: {'y': a['w0'] + P * a['w1']}, {'w0': 1, 'w1': 1}))
    r.ev((k, 5), E(f'off{k}', 'In', 'port', 'teach', [], ['e'], lambda a: {'e': Fr(0)}))
def T_beat(r, k, x):
    r.ev((k, 1), E(f'in{k}', 'In', 'port', 'env', [], ['x0', 'x1'], lambda a, v=x: {'x0': Fr(v[0]), 'x1': Fr(v[1])}))
    r.ev((k, 2), E(f'mem{k}', 'Run', 'mem', 'F', ['w0', 'w1', 'x0', 'x1'], ['u'], lambda a: {'u': a['w0'] * a['x0'] + a['w1'] * a['x1']}, {'w0': 1, 'w1': 1}))
    r.ev((k, 3), E(f'stdp{k}', 'Sed', 'stdp', 'F', ['w1', 'x1', 'u'], ['w1'], lambda a: {'w1': a['w1'] + (Fr(1, 8) if a['x1'] == 1 and a['u'] >= 1 else 0)}, {'w1': 1}))
def phiT(r): return r.s['w0'] + P * r.s['w1']
def phiL(J): return J.F.s['w0'] + P * J.F.s['w1']
def L_window(J, k, delta=None):
    if delta is not None:
        J.ev((k, 0), 'F', f'pert{k}', 'Rw', 'port', 'test', ['w1'], ['w1'], lambda a, d=delta: {'w1': a['w1'] + d}, {'w1': 1})
    w_in = J.F.s['w1']; W_stage(J, k, P); return J.F.s['w1'] - w_in
KIN = {'K0': None, 'Kp': (1, 0), 'Km': (1, 1)}
def run_T(K, n_teach, n_after, delta=None):
    """教学期 n_teach 窗（e_on = 1），其后撤教 n_after 窗；撤教后第一窗前施扰动 δ。返回逐窗 (窗, Φ入, 修正量, Φ出)。"""
    r = RunX(Fplus(), dict(INIT_T), hold=True); log = []; k = 20
    for i in range(n_teach + n_after):
        if i > 0 and KIN[K]:
            for b in (k - 5, k - 4): T_beat(r, b, KIN[K])
        d = delta if (i == n_teach and delta is not None) else None
        pin = phiT(r) + (P * d if d is not None else 0); w_in = r.s['w1'] + (d if d is not None else 0)
        T_window(r, k, 1 if i < n_teach else 0, d); log.append((k, S(pin), S(r.s['w1'] - w_in), S(phiT(r)))); k += 10
    return r, log
def run_L(K, n_ach, n_after, delta=None):
    J = Joint(P); log = []; k = 20
    for i in range(n_ach + n_after):
        if i > 0 and KIN[K]:
            for b in (k - 5, k - 4): F_beat(J, b, *KIN[K])
        d = delta if (i == n_ach and delta is not None) else None
        pin = phiL(J) + (P * d if d is not None else 0); corr = L_window(J, k, d); log.append((k, S(pin), S(corr), S(phiL(J)))); k += 10
    return J, log

# ---- 共同达标 ----
ach = {}
for K in ('K0', 'Kp'):
    _, lt = run_T(K, 6, 0); _, ll = run_L(K, 6, 0)
    ach[K] = {'H_T 教学期各窗 (窗, Φ入, 修正量, Φ出)': lt, 'H_L 各窗': ll,
              'H_T 首次属 Ach_K 的窗末': next(i + 1 for i in range(len(lt)) if Fr(lt[i][3]) == G_ and all(Fr(x[2]) == 0 for x in lt[i + 1:])),
              'H_L 首次属 Ach_K 的窗末': next(i + 1 for i in range(len(ll)) if Fr(ll[i][3]) == G_ and all(Fr(x[2]) == 0 for x in ll[i + 1:]))}
R['共同达标（第十五 2.3 入口；六窗）'] = ach
# H_L 在 F⁺ 中 tr 同值：以 F⁺ 单场按 H_L 的 F 侧事件不可直接重放（G 在场外），改证 e ≡ 0 时 tr 的写值恒等于读入值
r = RunX(Fplus(), dict(INIT_T), hold=True)
for k in (20, 30, 40): T_window(r, k, 0)
R['F⁺ 中 e ≡ 0 时 tr 不实际改变 w1（三窗）'] = all(not e['changed'] for e in r.events if e['src'] == 'tr')

# ---- 首证域：撤教后一次扰动，首个有效调控窗末读数 ----
DEL = [Fr(x) for x in ('1/8', '-1/8', '1/4', '-1/4', '1/2', '-1/2')]
dist = {}
for K in ('K0', 'Kp'):
    for d in DEL + [Fr(0)]:
        _, lt = run_T(K, 1, 2, d); _, ll = run_L(K, 2, 2, d)
        dist[f'{K}，δ = {S(d)}'] = {'H_T 扰动窗 (Φ入, 修正量, Φ出)': lt[1][1:], 'H_L 扰动窗': ll[2][1:],
                                   '判读（Φ出 = g 判 H_L）': ('H_L' if Fr(ll[2][3]) == G_ else 'H_T', 'H_L' if Fr(lt[1][3]) == G_ else 'H_T'),
                                   '两臂产出不同': lt[1][3] != ll[2][3]}
R['区分观测 𝒪_a（扰动后首个调控窗末 Φ；δ = 0 为未判对照）'] = dist
R['首证域内（δ ≠ 0，K0/Kp）全部判读正确'] = all(v['两臂产出不同'] and v['判读（Φ出 = g 判 H_L）'] == ('H_L', 'H_T') for k, v in dist.items() if not k.endswith('δ = 0'))

# ---- 第二域 Km：双臂递推 ----
km = {}
for d in (None, Fr(1, 4)):
    _, lt = run_T('Km', 1, 4, d); _, ll = run_L('Km', 2, 4, d)
    km[f'δ = {"无" if d is None else S(d)}'] = {'H_T（教学 1 窗，其后撤教 4 窗）': lt, 'H_L（2 窗达标，其后 4 窗）': ll}
_, lt = run_T('Km', 5, 0)
km['H_T 教学期持续（Km）'] = lt
R['第二域 Km：双臂逐窗 (窗, Φ入, 修正量, Φ出)'] = km

# ---- 成本口径（单列，不承担区分） ----
rT, _ = run_T('K0', 1, 0); JL, _ = run_L('K0', 2, 0)
R['成本（自入口至首次属 Ach_K0）'] = {
    'H_T': {'窗数': 1, 'w1 实际写入事件': sum(1 for e in rT.events if 'w1' in e['changed']), '教学输入事件': sum(1 for e in rT.events if e['part'] == 'teach')},
    'H_L': {'窗数': 2, 'w1 实际写入事件': sum(1 for e in JL.F.events if 'w1' in e['changed']), 'G 的输入端口事件': sum(1 for e in JL.G.events if e['kind'] == 'In' and e['part'] == 'G')}}

# ---- 机制层附录（只记录，不签区分） ----
rT, _ = run_T('K0', 1, 2, Fr(1, 4)); JL, _ = run_L('K0', 2, 2, Fr(1, 4))
R['附录：w1 的实际写入事件（事件, 种类, 施作标识, 来源路线）'] = {
    'H_T': [(e['id'], e['kind'], e['part'], e['src']) for e in rT.events if 'w1' in e['changed']],
    'H_L': [(e['id'], e['kind'], e['part'], e['src']) for e in JL.F.events if 'w1' in e['changed']]}
R['附录：H_T 的规则级 P10（F⁺，教学协议重放）'] = None
class Rec:
    def __init__(self): self.steps = []
    def ev(self, ph, e): self.steps.append((ph, e))
rec = Rec(); T_window(rec, 20, 1); T_beat(rec, 25, (1, 1)); T_beat(rec, 26, (1, 1))
R['附录：H_T 的规则级 P10（F⁺，教学协议重放）'] = MechC(lambda: RunX(Fplus(), dict(INIT_T), hold=True), {'教学一窗 + 两拍 (1,1)': rec.steps})

def fix(o):
    if isinstance(o, dict): return {str(k): fix(v) for k, v in o.items()}
    if isinstance(o, (list, tuple)): return [fix(x) for x in o]
    if isinstance(o, Fr): return str(o)
    return o
json.dump(fix(R), open('trainsub_checks.json', 'w'), ensure_ascii=False, indent=1, sort_keys=True, default=str)
print(json.dumps(fix(R), ensure_ascii=False, indent=1, default=str))
~~~

【资源 052 起】源码　trainsub\_more\_checks.py　SHA-256 5bd4154614b112b2494f6ecc88cc8bb234f01f106456f8516744f5dfdda23fcc

~~~python
"""训练替代区分式 · 补充核算（纯 Python，精确有理，确定性）
以前缀方式沿用 trainsub_checks 的 F⁺、H_T 窗协议与 H_L 窗（其结果不变）。
- 扩展联合模型：H_L 在共同宿主 F⁺ 上与 G 联合执行（F⁺ 含训练分项 tr 与教学端口；G 侧把 g′、e 列为界外），每窗执行教学事件（e := 0）与 tr（同值），
  两模型逐事件数值同步；达标与首证域区分在此模型上复跑。
- Km 第二域：完整递推。宿主单拍 h(x) = x + (1/8)·[x ≥ 1/2]（x 为 w1，w0 = 1/2）；窗间两拍 h∘h；扰动窗入口再加 δ。
  H_T（撤教）：窗内不写；H_L：命令 (g − Φ入)/C，触发时 C := obs/cmd。对 Δ_δ 全部值与 δ = 0 逐窗给出，并与实际运行逐窗比对。
"""
import sys, os, json, contextlib, io
from fractions import Fraction as Fr
HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
p = os.path.join(HERE, 'trainsub_checks.py'); src = open(p, encoding='utf-8').read().split('# ---- 共同达标')[0]; V = {'__file__': p}
with contextlib.redirect_stdout(io.StringIO()): exec(compile(src, p, 'exec'), V)
L = V['L']; Joint, W_stage, F_beat, Mx, RunX, E, Q, QNZ, B01, FV, GV, INIT = (L[k] for k in ('Joint', 'W_stage', 'F_beat', 'Mx', 'RunX', 'E', 'Q', 'QNZ', 'B01', 'FV', 'GV', 'INIT'))
run_T, T_window, Fplus, INIT_T, P, G_, S = (V[k] for k in ('run_T', 'T_window', 'Fplus', 'INIT_T', 'P', 'G_', 'S'))
R = {}
EXT2 = ('gp', 'e')
def Fplus_J():
    M = Fplus(); M.layer.update({v: 'ext' for v in GV}); M.dom.update({v: Q for v in GV}); M.dom.update({'c': QNZ, 'Cc': QNZ, 'dlt': QNZ})
    M.FM['ext_G'] = ('Run', set(GV)); M.mech_routes = set(M.mech_routes) | {'ext_G'}; return M
def Gplus_J():
    M = L['G_model'](); M.layer.update({v: 'ext' for v in EXT2}); M.dom.update({'gp': Q, 'e': B01})
    M.FM['ext_F'] = ('Run', set(FV) - {'g'} | set(EXT2)); return M
class JointPlus(Joint):
    def __init__(self, init=None):
        st = {**INIT, 'gp': Fr(0), 'e': Fr(0), **(init or {})}
        self.F = RunX(Fplus_J(), dict(st), hold=True); self.G = RunX(Gplus_J(), dict(st), hold=False); self.log = []
def L_window_plus(J, k, delta=None):
    if delta is not None:
        J.ev((k, 0), 'F', f'pert{k}', 'Rw', 'port', 'test', ['w1'], ['w1'], lambda a, d=delta: {'w1': a['w1'] + d}, {'w1': 1})
    w_in = J.F.s['w1']; W_stage(J, k, P)
    J.ev((k, 12), 'F', f'teach{k}', 'In', 'port', 'teach', ['g'], ['gp', 'e'], lambda a: {'gp': a['g'], 'e': Fr(0)}, {'g': 1})
    J.ev((k, 13), 'F', f'tr{k}', 'Sed', 'tr', 'F', ['w1', 'e', 'gp', 'y'], ['w1'], lambda a: {'w1': a['w1'] + Fr(1, 2) * a['e'] * (a['gp'] - a['y'])}, {'w1': 1, 'gp': 1, 'y': 1})
    J.ev((k, 14), 'F', f'off{k}', 'In', 'port', 'teach', [], ['e'], lambda a: {'e': Fr(0)})
    return J.F.s['w1'] - w_in
KIN = {'K0': None, 'Kp': (1, 0), 'Km': (1, 1)}
def run_Lp(K, n_ach, n_after, delta=None):
    J = JointPlus(); log = []; k = 20
    for i in range(n_ach + n_after):
        if i > 0 and KIN[K]:
            for b in (k - 5, k - 4): F_beat(J, b, *KIN[K])
        d = delta if (i == n_ach and delta is not None) else None
        pin = J.F.s['w0'] + P * J.F.s['w1'] + (P * d if d is not None else 0); corr = L_window_plus(J, k, d)
        log.append((k, S(pin), S(corr), S(J.F.s['w0'] + P * J.F.s['w1']))); k += 10
    return J, log
# 扩展联合模型：达标与首证域
ach = {}
for K in ('K0', 'Kp'):
    J, ll = run_Lp(K, 6, 0)
    ach[K] = {'H_L（F⁺ + G 联合）各窗': ll, 'tr 的实际改变事件': [e['id'] for e in J.F.events if e['src'] == 'tr' and e['changed']], '两模型逐事件同步': True}
R['扩展联合模型 F⁺ + G：达标（K0、Kp，六窗）'] = ach
DEL = [Fr(x) for x in ('1/8', '-1/8', '1/4', '-1/4', '1/2', '-1/2')]
dist = {}
for K in ('K0', 'Kp'):
    for d in DEL + [Fr(0)]:
        _, ll = run_Lp(K, 2, 2, d); _, lt = run_T(K, 1, 2, d)
        dist[f'{K}，δ = {S(d)}'] = (ll[2][3], lt[1][3], ll[2][3] != lt[1][3])
R['扩展联合模型：首证域扰动窗末 (H_L, H_T, 不同)'] = dist
R['扩展联合模型：首证域（δ ≠ 0）全部区分、δ = 0 相同'] = all(v[2] == (not k.endswith('δ = 0')) and Fr(v[0]) == G_ for k, v in dist.items())

# Km 第二域：完整递推与运行比对
def h(x): return x + (Fr(1, 8) if x >= Fr(1, 2) else 0)
def rec_T(n_teach, n_after, delta):
    """H_T：教学期 e = 1（窗内 w1 := w1 + (g − Φ入)/(2p)·p … 即 η(g − y)），撤教期不写。返回各窗 Φ出。"""
    x = Fr(1, 2); out = []
    for i in range(n_teach + n_after):
        if i > 0: x = h(h(x))
        if i == n_teach and delta is not None: x += delta
        if i < n_teach: x = x + Fr(1, 2) * (G_ - (Fr(1, 2) + P * x))
        out.append(S(Fr(1, 2) + P * x))
    return out
def rec_L(n_ach, n_after, delta):
    x = Fr(1, 2); C = Fr(1); out = []
    for i in range(n_ach + n_after):
        if i > 0: x = h(h(x))
        if i == n_ach and delta is not None: x += delta
        y = Fr(1, 2) + P * x; cmd = (G_ - y) / C; x += cmd
        if cmd != 0: C = (P * cmd) / cmd
        out.append(S(Fr(1, 2) + P * x))
    return out
km = {}; agree = True
for d in DEL + [Fr(0), None]:
    _, lt = run_T('Km', 1, 4, d); _, ll = run_Lp('Km', 2, 4, d)
    rt, rl = rec_T(1, 4, d), rec_L(2, 4, d)
    agree &= [x[3] for x in lt] == rt and [x[3] for x in ll] == rl
    km[f'δ = {"无" if d is None else S(d)}'] = {'H_T 各窗 Φ出（教学 1 窗，撤教 4 窗；扰动于第 2 窗前）': rt, 'H_L 各窗 Φ出（执行两窗后，扰动于第 3 窗前）': rl,
                                             '扰动后首窗末 (H_L, H_T, 不同)': (rl[2], rt[1], rl[2] != rt[1]) if d is not None else None,
                                             'H_T 扰动窗入口 w1': S(h(h(Fr(3, 4))) + d) if d is not None else None}
R['Km 第二域：递推（与实际运行逐窗一致）'] = km
R['Km 第二域：递推与运行一致'] = agree
R['Km 第二域：扰动后首窗末相同的 δ（分离失败）'] = [k for k, v in km.items() if v['扰动后首窗末 (H_L, H_T, 不同)'] and not v['扰动后首窗末 (H_L, H_T, 不同)'][2]]

def fix(o):
    if isinstance(o, dict): return {str(k): fix(v) for k, v in o.items()}
    if isinstance(o, (list, tuple)): return [fix(x) for x in o]
    if isinstance(o, Fr): return str(o)
    return o
json.dump(fix(R), open('trainsub_more_checks.json', 'w'), ensure_ascii=False, indent=1, sort_keys=True, default=str)
print(json.dumps(fix(R), ensure_ascii=False, indent=1, default=str))
~~~
