【资源 054 起】源码　text\_checks\_21\_23.py　SHA-256 b901a6a6efff4d92680221ca43b2d51f568443019ba998ce9463fbd424599013

~~~python
"""第二十一、第二十二、第二十三正文所列各例的核算（纯 Python，精确有理，确定性）
第二十一：首窗读数差观测不到达的联合运行（H_L 两窗）；正常两窗与扰动全组沿用 trainsub_more_checks。
第二十一：H_L 扩展联合模型每窗的教学端口事件与相位。
第二十二：p = 0 时两臂在第 1 窗的更新事件越出非零类型（共同合法前缀止于该事件之前）；首证域两窗耦合。
第二十二：G 的 P10 见证事件表（输入、第一次实际沉积、读入与 C 版本）。
第二十三：同值写入保持 Q（两域）；延后教学时首次失效的实际拍号等于首个教学拍的拍号；持续验证覆盖两拍以上。
"""
import sys, os, json, contextlib, io
from fractions import Fraction as Fr
HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
def prefix(script, stop):
    p = os.path.join(HERE, script); src = open(p, encoding='utf-8').read().split(stop)[0]; sc = {'__file__': p}
    with contextlib.redirect_stdout(io.StringIO()): exec(compile(src, p, 'exec'), sc)
    return sc
R = {}
def S(x): return str(x)
# ---- 第二十一 ----
T2 = prefix('trainsub_more_checks.py', '# 扩展联合模型：达标与首证域')
JointPlus, L_window_plus, W_stage, P = T2['JointPlus'], T2['L_window_plus'], T2['W_stage'], T2['P']
J = JointPlus(); out = []
for k in (20, 30):
    W_stage(J, k, P, observe=False)
    out.append((k, S(J.F.s['w0'] + P * J.F.s['w1']), S(J.G.s['Cc'])))
R['第二十一 首窗读数差观测不到达（H_L 两窗：窗, Φ出, C）'] = out
J = JointPlus(); L_window_plus(J, 20)
R['第二十一 H_L 每窗事件（扩展联合模型；事件, 相位, 施作标识, 写值）'] = [(e['id'], str(e['phase']), e['part'], e['out']) for e in J.F.events]
# ---- 第二十二 ----
WS = prefix('wsprog_checks.py', 'C3 = (')
run = WS['run']
p0 = {}
for c in ('H_P1', 'H_L'):
    try: run(c, 0, 3); p0[c] = '未拒（错误）'
    except Exception as ex: p0[c] = f'第 1 窗被拒：{ex}'
R['第二十二 p = 0、g = 3：两臂第 1 窗更新事件'] = p0
a, b = run('H_P1', 3, 3), run('H_L', 3, 3)
R['第二十二 首证域两窗耦合（𝒪_beh 相同）'] = WS['proj_beh'](a) == WS['proj_beh'](b)
G = b.G
R['第二十二 G 的 P10 见证事件表（事件, 种类, 读入, 实际改变, 写后版本来源中的输入事件）'] = [
    (e['id'], e['kind'], e['reads'], e['changed'],
     sorted(str(k[2]) for v in e['changed'] for k, s in G.vtrace[i + 1][v].items() if isinstance(k, tuple) and len(k) == 3 and str(k[2]).startswith(('iny', 'ing', 'obs')) and s is True))
    for i, e in enumerate(G.events) if e['phase'][0] == 20 and e['kind'] in ('In', 'Sed', 'Run') and e['src'] in ('port', 'lms', 'consol', 'eff', 'plan')]
# ---- 第二十三 ----
ST = prefix('stab_checks.py', '# ---- Δ1')
DOM, FORM0, sect = ST['DOM'], ST['FORM0'], ST['sect']
same = {}
for k, (h0, tau) in DOM.items():
    h = h0.clone(); h.op(('r', 'u', h0.st['u'])); x = sect(h, tau, FORM0[k])
    same[k] = {'c = u₀': S(h0.st['u']), '直写后 Q': x['Q'], '固定对象': x['固定对象']}
R['第二十三 同值写入保持（两域）'] = same
late = {}
for k, (h0, tau) in DOM.items():
    for n in (0, 2, 4):
        h = h0.clone(); seq = [('A', 0)] * n + [('A', 1), ('AB', 0)]; fail = None
        for i, (s_, t) in enumerate(seq, 1):
            h.op(('b', s_, t)); x = sect(h, tau, FORM0[k])
            if fail is None and not (x['Q∧对齐'] and x['固定对象']): fail = i
        late[f'{k}，教学前 {n} 拍 t = 0'] = {'首个教学拍的拍号': n + 1, '首次失效拍号': fail}
R['第二十三 延后教学：首次失效拍号'] = late
h0, tau = DOM['双正 (1,1,0)']; h = h0.clone(); h.op(('r', 'u', Fr(1, 2))); tr = []
for s_ in ('AB', 'AB', 'A', 'B', 'A', 'B', 'AB'):
    h.op(('b', s_, 0)); x = sect(h, tau, FORM0['双正 (1,1,0)']); tr.append((s_, x['Q∧对齐'] and x['固定对象']))
R['第二十三 持续验证（双正 c = 1/2；复归后覆盖三拍）'] = tr

def fix(o):
    if isinstance(o, dict): return {str(k): fix(v) for k, v in o.items()}
    if isinstance(o, (list, tuple)): return [fix(x) for x in o]
    if isinstance(o, Fr): return str(o)
    return o
json.dump(fix(R), open('text_checks_21_23.json', 'w'), ensure_ascii=False, indent=1, sort_keys=True, default=str)
print(json.dumps(fix(R), ensure_ascii=False, indent=1, default=str))
~~~

【资源 056 起】源码　stab\_more\_checks.py　SHA-256 fa0228bb9a5af95c2561671cd032711e4d110396647c99bf7b0c2b7a100680d8

~~~python
"""一般化结构稳定式 · 补充核算（纯 Python，精确有理，确定性）
以前缀方式沿用 stab_checks 的宿主、两域初态与判定截面（其结果不变）。
- Δ2：逐截面分报 Q 与 Q∧对齐；间隙 d = u − θ 的上界 1/2 与「每个教学拍末 D 失效」逐拍核；引理「d > 0 且 t = 0 的拍不产生任何实际写入」；
  单正域 Q 成立时对齐 ⟺ 负单输入 Y 满足 w_Y < 1 或 a_Y ≥ 1。
- 恢复计时：首次失效起的总计时（不重置）与最后一次深度写入后的留存（重置）分列；同一复归前缀的三种后续——未覆盖、恰覆盖两拍、继续教学。
"""
import sys, os, json, itertools, contextlib, io
from fractions import Fraction as Fr
HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
p = os.path.join(HERE, 'stab_checks.py'); src = open(p, encoding='utf-8').read().split('# ---- Δ1')[0]; V = {'__file__': p}
with contextlib.redirect_stdout(io.StringIO()): exec(compile(src, p, 'exec'), V)
DOM, FORM0, sect, thr, aligned, inSigma, z, S = (V[k] for k in ('DOM', 'FORM0', 'sect', 'thr', 'aligned', 'inSigma', 'z', 'S'))
R = {}
STEPS = [(s_, t) for s_ in ('A', 'B', 'AB') for t in (0, 1)]
def gap(h): return h.st['u'] - thr(h)
def writes(h0, h1): return any(h0.st[x] != h1.st[x] for x in ('wA', 'wB', 'aA', 'aB', 'u'))
d2 = {}
for k, (h0, tau) in DOM.items():
    P = [x for x in ('A', 'B') if tau[x] == 1]; Y = [x for x in ('A', 'B') if tau[x] == 0]
    c = {'序列数': 0, '截面数': 0, '间隙 d ≤ 1/2 全程': 0, '教学拍数': 0, '教学拍末 D 失效': 0, 'd > 0 且 t = 0 的拍': 0, '其中无实际写入': 0,
         '末截面 Q 复归（失效后）': 0, '末截面 Q∧对齐复归（失效后）': 0, 'Q 成立截面': 0, '其中对齐判据（负单输入 w_Y < 1 或 a_Y ≥ 1）与实际一致': 0}
    ex = None
    for L in range(1, 6):
        for seq in itertools.product(STEPS, repeat=L):
            h = h0.clone(); failed = False; c['序列数'] += 1; ok_gap = True
            for s_, t in seq:
                pre = h.clone(); d_pre = gap(h); h.op(('b', s_, t)); c['截面数'] += 1
                ok_gap &= gap(h) <= Fr(1, 2)
                if t == 1:
                    c['教学拍数'] += 1; c['教学拍末 D 失效'] += not (h.st['u'] > thr(h))
                elif d_pre > 0:
                    c['d > 0 且 t = 0 的拍'] += 1; c['其中无实际写入'] += not writes(pre, h)
                x = sect(h, tau, FORM0[k])
                if not (x['Q∧对齐'] and x['固定对象']): failed = True
                if x['Q']:
                    c['Q 成立截面'] += 1
                    pred = all(h.st['w' + y] < 1 or h.st['a' + y] >= 1 for y in Y) if Y else True
                    c['其中对齐判据（负单输入 w_Y < 1 或 a_Y ≥ 1）与实际一致'] += (pred == aligned(h.st, tau))
            c['间隙 d ≤ 1/2 全程'] += ok_gap
            x = sect(h, tau, FORM0[k])
            if failed and x['Q'] and x['固定对象']:
                c['末截面 Q 复归（失效后）'] += 1
                if x['Q∧对齐']: c['末截面 Q∧对齐复归（失效后）'] += 1
                elif ex is None: ex = [f'{s_}(t={t})' for s_, t in seq]
    c['Q 复归而 Q∧对齐不成立之例'] = ex
    d2[k] = c
R['Δ2（长度 ≤ 5 的全部 t ∈ {0,1} 序列）：间隙不变量、教学拍失效、Q 与 Q∧对齐分报'] = d2
# 例：单正入口 B(t=1)、AB、A、A
h0, tau = DOM['单正 (1,0,0)']; h = h0.clone()
tr = []
for s_, t in (('B', 1), ('AB', 0), ('A', 0), ('A', 0)):
    h.op(('b', s_, t)); x = sect(h, tau, FORM0['单正 (1,0,0)'])
    tr.append((f'{s_}(t={t})', {k: S(h.st[k]) for k in ('wB', 'aB', 'u')}, x['D'], x['mem'], x['Q'], x['Q∧对齐'], [z(h.st, s) for s in ('A', 'B', 'AB')]))
R['例：单正 B(t=1)、AB、A、A（截面：输入, (w_B, a_B, u), D, mem, Q, Q∧对齐, 输出 (A,B,AB)）'] = tr

# ---- 恢复计时与持续验证 ----
def timeline(h0, tau, f0, ops):
    h = h0.clone(); first_fail = None; last_depth = None; out = []
    for i, o in enumerate(ops):
        u_pre = h.st['u']; h.op(o); x = sect(h, tau, f0)
        if first_fail is None and not x['Q∧对齐']: first_fail = i
        if h.st['u'] != u_pre: last_depth = i
        out.append((o[1] if o[0] == 'b' else f'{o[1]}:={S(o[2])}', S(h.st['u']), x['D'], x['mem'], x['Q∧对齐'] and x['固定对象'],
                    None if first_fail is None else i - first_fail, None if last_depth is None else i - last_depth))
    return out
h0, tau = DOM['双正 (1,1,0)']; f0 = FORM0['双正 (1,1,0)']
pre = [('r', 'u', Fr(1, 4)), ('b', 'A', 0), ('b', 'AB', 0), ('b', 'B', 0), ('b', 'AB', 0), ('b', 'A', 0), ('b', 'B', 0)]
R['恢复计时（双正，c = 1/4；截面：事件, u, D, mem, 固定对象 Q∧对齐, 距首次失效的事件数（不重置）, 距最后深度写入的事件数（重置））'] = {
    '复归前缀（AB 延后、阈前两次实际写入）': timeline(h0, tau, f0, pre),
    '未覆盖：复归即止': timeline(h0, tau, f0, pre)[-1:],
    '恰覆盖：复归后再两拍 t = 0': timeline(h0, tau, f0, pre + [('b', 'AB', 0), ('b', 'A', 0)])[-3:],
    '继续教学：复归后一拍 A(t=1)': timeline(h0, tau, f0, pre + [('b', 'A', 1)])[-2:]}

def fix(o):
    if isinstance(o, dict): return {str(k): fix(v) for k, v in o.items()}
    if isinstance(o, (list, tuple)): return [fix(x) for x in o]
    if isinstance(o, Fr): return str(o)
    return o
json.dump(fix(R), open('stab_more_checks.json', 'w'), ensure_ascii=False, indent=1, sort_keys=True, default=str)
print(json.dumps(fix(R), ensure_ascii=False, indent=1, default=str))
~~~

【资源 058 起】源码　lawreach\_net.py　SHA-256 2b8f57ff2c14d50587eba8c45e01152d5835e586c47c8bb22a0342fad82cc501

~~~python
"""脉冲网络调宿律候选可达式 · 被调网络与运行层核算（纯 Python，精确有理，确定性）
被调网络 N（算法层、离散拍）：输入 A、B；输出单元 z；突触 syn_A、syn_B（宿载值 wA、wB）；律层可塑参数 κ（结构成分 rule_κ）。
  每拍：输入（sA、sB 由字母；教学电流 I，界外）→ 积分 vt = λ·v + wA·sA + wB·sB + I → 放电 z = [vt ≥ θ]、复位 → 赫布沉积 wX := min(hi, wX + κ·sX·z)。
  λ = 1/2，θ = 1，hi = 2；入口 wA = wB = 1/4，κ = 0，v = 0。
许可操作：施作者直写 κ（宿载直接变换，落在律层可塑参数，不落在目标迹应 wA）；教学输入（输入端口：字母与教学电流）；场自身的赫布沉积。直接置 w、擦除不在许可集。
三本账：直接变换（Rw 端口事件）、教学输入（In 端口事件）、沉积（Sed 事件）分开记。
目标律（事前申报）χ_L = (L, I, Rel(L), Γ_judge)：L「A 到来（无教学电流）则 z 放电」；I = {A}；Rel(L) 单职责 d：Ante = A 到来、I = 0，Anchor = {wA}，Read = z，Type = 确定、set；
  Γ_judge = 归约 R11（确定、set、单入口、单职责子情形下 (4.7) 各分量的计算读法）。
核算：一、直写承担链（版本路径与依赖）；二、三臂（直写保持、直写后撤回、对照：同教学不直写）与三本账；
     三、归约 R11 下逐截面对候选承担域全体的判词（起点负判、首次取得、撤引后支配）；四、撤引后运行、复走；五、不可达边界。
"""
import sys, os, json, runpy, tempfile, contextlib, io, itertools
from fractions import Fraction as Fr
HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)); ARGV = list(sys.argv)
with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp, contextlib.redirect_stdout(io.StringIO()):
    cwd = os.getcwd(); os.chdir(tmp); sys.argv = ['full', os.path.join(tmp, 'o.json')]
    X = runpy.run_path(os.path.join(HERE, 'shiyin_full.py')); os.chdir(cwd); sys.argv = ARGV
Mx, RunX, E, versions_of, vanc_at, version_path = (X[k] for k in ('Mx', 'RunX', 'E', 'versions_of', 'vanc_at', 'version_path'))
R = {}
def S(x): return str(x)
LAM, TH, HI = Fr(1, 2), Fr(1), Fr(2)
PAT = {'A': (1, 0), 'B': (0, 1), 'AB': (1, 1), 'none': (0, 0)}
WG = ('pred', lambda x: 0 <= x <= HI, [Fr(j, 8) for j in range(0, 17)])
NUM = ('pred', lambda x: True, [Fr(j, 8) for j in range(-4, 33)])
B01 = ('fin', {Fr(0), Fr(1)})

# ======================= 模型（运行层，供版本路径） =======================
def model():
    lay = {'wA': 'theta', 'wB': 'theta', 'kappa': 'theta', 'v': 'r', 'vt': 'r', 'z': 'r', 'sA': 'r', 'sB': 'r', 'I': 'r'}
    dom = {'wA': WG, 'wB': WG, 'kappa': ('fin', {Fr(0), Fr(1, 4), Fr(1, 2)}), 'v': NUM, 'vt': NUM, 'z': B01, 'sA': B01, 'sB': B01, 'I': ('fin', {Fr(0), Fr(1)})}
    FM = {'integ': ('Run', {'vt'}), 'fire': ('Run', {'z', 'v'}), 'heb': ('Sed', {'wA', 'wB'})}
    M = Mx(lay, dom, (lambda s: s['wA'], {'wA': 1}), ports={'In': {'sA', 'sB', 'I'}, 'Rw': {'kappa'}}, FM=FM, fid='N')
    M.mech_routes = {'integ', 'fire', 'heb', 'port:In', 'port:Rw'}; M.mech_exhaustive = True
    return M
def st0(): return {'wA': Fr(1, 4), 'wB': Fr(1, 4), 'kappa': Fr(0), 'v': Fr(0), 'vt': Fr(0), 'z': Fr(0), 'sA': Fr(0), 'sB': Fr(0), 'I': Fr(0)}
def beat(r, k, letter, I):
    s = PAT[letter]
    r.ev((k, 1), E(f'in{k}', 'In', 'port', 'env', [], ['sA', 'sB', 'I'], lambda v, s=s, I=I: {'sA': Fr(s[0]), 'sB': Fr(s[1]), 'I': Fr(I)}))
    r.ev((k, 2), E(f'integ{k}', 'Run', 'integ', 'F', ['v', 'wA', 'wB', 'sA', 'sB', 'I'], ['vt'], lambda v: {'vt': LAM * v['v'] + v['wA'] * v['sA'] + v['wB'] * v['sB'] + v['I']},
                   {'v': 1, 'wA': 1, 'wB': 1, 'sA': 1, 'sB': 1, 'I': 1}))
    r.ev((k, 3), E(f'fire{k}', 'Run', 'fire', 'F', ['vt'], ['z', 'v'], lambda v: {'z': Fr(1), 'v': Fr(0)} if v['vt'] >= TH else {'z': Fr(0), 'v': v['vt']}))
    for X_ in ('A', 'B'):
        r.ev((k, 4 if X_ == 'A' else 5), E(f'heb{X_}{k}', 'Sed', 'heb', 'F', [f'w{X_}', f's{X_}', 'z', 'kappa'], [f'w{X_}'],
                                            lambda v, X_=X_: {f'w{X_}': min(HI, v[f'w{X_}'] + v['kappa'] * v[f's{X_}'] * v['z'])}))
def dwrite(r, k, val): r.ev((k, 0), E(f'dw{k}', 'Rw', 'port', 'P', [], ['kappa'], lambda v, val=val: {'kappa': Fr(val)}))
TEACH = [('A', 1)] * 3
POST = [('A', 0), ('none', 0), ('B', 0), ('A', 0), ('AB', 0), ('none', 0), ('none', 0), ('A', 0)]
def arm(kind):
    """kind：'保持'（第 0 拍直写 κ := 1/4，撤引后 κ 保留）；'撤回'（撤引时直写 κ := 0）；'对照'（同教学，不直写，κ 保持入口值 0）。"""
    r = RunX(model(), st0()); k = 1; marks = {'入口': len(r.events)}
    if kind != '对照': dwrite(r, k, Fr(1, 4))
    marks['直写后'] = len(r.events)
    for j, (letter, I) in enumerate(TEACH):
        beat(r, k, letter, I); marks[f'教学第 {j + 1} 拍后'] = len(r.events); k += 1
    if kind == '撤回': dwrite(r, k, 0)
    marks['撤引点'] = len(r.events); w_k = k
    for j, (letter, I) in enumerate(POST):
        beat(r, k, letter, I); marks[f'撤引后第 {j + 1} 拍后（{letter}）'] = len(r.events); k += 1
    return r, marks, w_k
ARMS = {a: arm(a) for a in ('保持', '撤回', '对照')}
def state_at(r, n): return r.trace[n][1]

# ======================= 一、直写承担链 =======================
r, marks, wk = ARMS['保持']
dw = next(e for e in r.events if e['kind'] == 'Rw'); kv = next(k for k in versions_of(r, dw) if k[0] == 'kappa')
hebs = [e for e in r.events if e['src'] == 'heb' and e['changed']]
chain = []
for e in hebs:
    v = next(k for k in versions_of(r, e) if k[0] in e['changed'])
    chain.append({'事件': e['id'], '所写版本': '|'.join(map(str, v)), '写值': e['out'], '读入 κ 版本': '|'.join(map(str, e['read_versions']['kappa'])),
                  'κ 依赖': e['deps'].get((e['changed'][0], 'kappa')), '来源含直写版本': vanc_at(r, e)[e['changed'][0]].get(kv) is True})
first_post_fire = next(e for e in r.events if e['src'] == 'fire' and int(e['id'][4:]) >= wk and e['out']['z'] == '1')
integ_post = next(e for e in r.events if e['id'] == 'integ' + first_post_fire['id'][4:])
zv = next(k for k in versions_of(r, integ_post) if k[0] == 'vt')          # z 同值写入不立新版本，取同拍积分版本
R['一 直写承担链（保持臂）'] = {'直写版本': '|'.join(map(str, kv)), '沉积写入（教学段与撤引后）': chain,
    '势变化（申报 Φ = wA）': {'入口': '1/4', '教学段末': S(state_at(r, marks['撤引点'])['wA'])},
    '撤引后首次放电事件': first_post_fire['id'], '同拍积分 vt 版本来源含直写版本': vanc_at(r, integ_post)['vt'].get(kv) is True,
    '放电读入的 vt 版本': '|'.join(map(str, first_post_fire['read_versions']['vt'])), '放电对 vt 依赖': first_post_fire['deps'].get(('z', 'vt')),
    '版本路径（直写 κ → 沉积 wA → 积分）': version_path(r, kv, zv)}

# ======================= 二、三臂与三本账 =======================
def ledger(r):
    return {'直接变换（Rw）': [(e['id'], e['out']) for e in r.events if e['kind'] == 'Rw'],
            '教学输入（In，I = 1 者）': [e['id'] for e in r.events if e['kind'] == 'In' and e['out']['I'] == '1'],
            '沉积（Sed，实际改变）': [(e['id'], e['out']) for e in r.events if e['kind'] == 'Sed' and e['changed']],
            '直接置 w 事件': [e['id'] for e in r.events if e['kind'] != 'Sed' and any(v in ('wA', 'wB') for v in e['changed'])]}
R['二 三臂三本账'] = {a: ledger(ARMS[a][0]) for a in ARMS}
R['二′ 两臂终点（wA, κ）'] = {a: (S(ARMS[a][0].s['wA']), S(ARMS[a][0].s['kappa'])) for a in ARMS}

# ======================= 三、归约 R11 下的判词 =======================
COMP = {'wA': 'syn_A', 'wB': 'syn_B', 'kappa': 'rule_κ'}             # 结构状态量 → 宿主成分
EXISTING = ['syn_A', 'syn_B', 'rule_κ']
REACH = {'A': {'syn_A'}}                                              # 输入 A 的界面经输入线可达的成分（申报结构关系）
GRID_W = [Fr(j, 4) for j in range(0, 9)]; GRID_V = [Fr(j, 4) for j in range(0, 4)]   # AdmVar：w ∈ [0, 2]，v ∈ [0, θ)（放电复位后的合法值）
def z_of(nu, c): return 1 if LAM * nu['v'] + nu['wA'] * c[0] + nu['wB'] * c[1] + c[2] >= TH else 0
def actarg(nu, c, avail):
    """确定型 ActArg（定义 3.10，AdmVar 取申报格点）：avail 为可用变量（受限上下文中 P 外结构量不可用，按定义 3.18 取最不利合法值处理为自由）。"""
    keys = ['v', 'wA', 'wB']; y0 = z_of(nu, c); dom = {'v': GRID_V, 'wA': GRID_W, 'wB': GRID_W}
    def suff(C):
        free = [k for k in keys if k not in C]
        return all(z_of({**nu, **dict(zip(free, vals))}, c) == y0 for vals in itertools.product(*[dom[k] for k in free]))
    mins = [set(C) for n in range(0, 4) for C in itertools.combinations(keys, n) if suff(C)]
    mins = [C for C in mins if not any(D < C for D in mins)]
    return y0, mins
def realize(sigma, c, P, replay):
    """返回 (Read 值, Bear) 若在 P 上实现 d（Read = 1，链闭合于 P）；否则 None。replay：再入上下文取 Freeze（v := 0）。"""
    nu = {'v': Fr(0) if replay else sigma['v'], 'wA': sigma['wA'], 'wB': sigma['wB']}
    y0, mins = actarg(nu, c, None)
    if y0 != 1: return None
    for C in mins:
        comp = {COMP[k] for k in C if k in COMP}
        if comp and comp <= set(P) and 'v' not in C: return (y0, comp)
    return None
def judge(sigma, Rset, letter='A'):
    c = PAT[letter] + (0,)
    out = {}
    role = realize(sigma, c, Rset, False); rep = realize(sigma, c, Rset, True)
    out['CurrentCarrier'] = int(bool(Rset) and set(Rset) <= set(EXISTING))
    out['CarrierRealizes'] = int(role is not None)
    out['CarrierNonInstant'] = int(role is not None and role[1] <= set(EXISTING))           # Estab：θ 成分在 Freeze 中保留
    out['EstWitness'] = int(out['CurrentCarrier'] and out['CarrierRealizes'] and out['CarrierNonInstant'])
    out['DynamicRole'] = int(role is not None)                                              # RolePath：w → vt → z，Comp ⊆ Supp
    ok_reach = rep is not None and rep[1] <= REACH[letter]
    # ΩOK（申报复走协议）：自 Freeze 起 (A, none, A) 三拍，A 拍均放电
    def replay_ok():
        s = dict(sigma); s['v'] = Fr(0); res = []
        for L_ in ('A', 'none', 'A'):
            vt = LAM * s['v'] + s['wA'] * PAT[L_][0] + s['wB'] * PAT[L_][1]; z = 1 if vt >= TH else 0; s['v'] = Fr(0) if z else vt
            if L_ == 'A': res.append(z)
        return all(res)
    out['StableReplay'] = int(rep is not None)
    out['ΩSupport'] = int(rep is not None and ok_reach and replay_ok())
    out['Bind'] = int(out['ΩSupport'] and rep is not None and role is not None and rep[1] == role[1])
    out['Govern'] = int(out['DynamicRole'] and out['ΩSupport'] and out['Bind'])
    out['∈𝒮'] = int(out['EstWitness'] and out['Govern'])
    return out
CANDS = [list(C) for n in range(1, 4) for C in itertools.combinations(EXISTING, n)]
def sections(a):
    r, marks, _ = ARMS[a]; out = {}
    for name, n in marks.items():
        sg = state_at(r, n); per = {'{' + ','.join(C) + '}': judge(sg, C) for C in CANDS}
        sol = [k for k, v in per.items() if v['∈𝒮']]
        out[name] = {'状态 (wA, wB, κ, v)': (S(sg['wA']), S(sg['wB']), S(sg['kappa']), S(sg['v'])), '𝒮（归约 R11）': sol,
                     '极小元': [k for k in sol if not any(set(j.strip('{}').split(',')) < set(k.strip('{}').split(',')) for j in sol)],
                     '逐候选（EstWitness, Govern）': {k: (v['EstWitness'], v['Govern']) for k, v in per.items()}}
    return out
SEC = {a: sections(a) for a in ARMS}
R['三 逐截面判词（归约 R11；候选承担域 = Existing 的全部非空子集）'] = SEC
def first_hit(a):
    for name, v in SEC[a].items():
        if v['𝒮（归约 R11）']: return name
    return None
R['三′ 首次取得截面与此前截面'] = {a: {'首次 𝒮 ≠ ∅': first_hit(a), '此前截面 𝒮 全空': all(not v['𝒮（归约 R11）'] for n, v in SEC[a].items() if n == first_hit(a) or True) if first_hit(a) is None else
                                    all(not SEC[a][n]['𝒮（归约 R11）'] for n in list(SEC[a])[:list(SEC[a]).index(first_hit(a))])} for a in ARMS}
R['三″ 撤引后各截面 𝒮 ≠ ∅'] = {a: all(v['𝒮（归约 R11）'] for n, v in SEC[a].items() if n.startswith('撤引')) for a in ARMS}
# 起点逐项（syn_A 单元素候选的各分量，入口截面）
R['三‴ 起点逐项（候选 {syn_A}，入口截面）'] = judge(state_at(ARMS['保持'][0], 0), ['syn_A'])

# ======================= 四、撤引后运行与复走 =======================
post = {}
for a in ('保持', '撤回'):
    r, marks, wk = ARMS[a]
    post[a] = {'撤引后各拍（拍, 字母, z, wA, κ）': [(e['id'][4:], next(x['out'] for x in r.events if x['id'] == f"in{e['id'][4:]}"), e['out']['z'],
                                                state_at(r, r.events.index(e) + 3)['wA'].__str__(), S(state_at(r, r.events.index(e) + 3)['kappa']))
                                               for e in r.events if e['src'] == 'fire' and int(e['id'][4:]) >= wk],
               '撤引后 W 内实际沉积': [(e['id'], e['out']) for e in r.events if e['src'] == 'heb' and e['changed'] and int(e['id'][4:]) >= wk],
               '撤引后施作事件（Rw、I = 1）': [e['id'] for e in r.events if (e['kind'] == 'Rw' or (e['kind'] == 'In' and e['out']['I'] == '1')) and int(''.join(ch for ch in e['id'] if ch.isdigit())) > wk]}
R['四 撤引后运行（继续运行：场自身动力照常）'] = post

# ======================= 五、不可达边界 =======================
def reach_set(kappas, letters, Is, depth):
    """自入口状态，按许可（κ 可写值集、字母集、教学电流集）作 BFS；返回 depth 拍内是否有状态使 z 在无教学的 A 拍放电。"""
    start = (Fr(1, 4), Fr(1, 4), Fr(0), Fr(0)); seen = {start}; front = [start]
    def step(s, letter, I):
        wA, wB, k, v = s; sA, sB = PAT[letter]; vt = LAM * v + wA * sA + wB * sB + I; z = 1 if vt >= TH else 0
        return (min(HI, wA + k * sA * z), min(HI, wB + k * sB * z), k, Fr(0) if z else vt)
    for _ in range(depth):
        nxt = []
        for s in front:
            for k in kappas:
                s1 = (s[0], s[1], k, s[3])
                for letter in letters:
                    for I in Is:
                        t = step(s1, letter, I)
                        if t not in seen: seen.add(t); nxt.append(t)
        front = nxt
    hit = [s for s in seen if LAM * Fr(0) + s[0] >= TH]
    return {'可达状态数': len(seen), '存在 wA ≥ θ 的状态（Freeze 后 A 放电）': bool(hit), '最大 v': S(max(s[3] for s in seen))}
R['五 不可达边界'] = {
    '可塑性冻结（κ 不许写，保持 0；字母全集、教学电流 {0,1}）': reach_set([Fr(0)], list(PAT), [0, 1], 8),
    '教学输入族不足（κ 可写 {0, 1/4, 1/2}；无教学电流）': reach_set([Fr(0), Fr(1, 4), Fr(1, 2)], list(PAT), [0], 8),
    '许可齐备（κ 可写，教学电流 {0,1}）': reach_set([Fr(0), Fr(1, 4)], ['A', 'none'], [0, 1], 4)}

def fix(o):
    if isinstance(o, dict): return {str(k): fix(v) for k, v in o.items()}
    if isinstance(o, (list, tuple)): return [fix(x) for x in o]
    if isinstance(o, Fr): return str(o)
    return o
json.dump(fix(R), open(os.path.join(HERE, 'lawreach_net.json'), 'w'), ensure_ascii=False, indent=1, sort_keys=True, default=str)
print(json.dumps(fix(R), ensure_ascii=False, indent=1, default=str))
~~~

【资源 060 起】源码　lawreach\_cand.py　SHA-256 78cc0592c6b3c72fa58246785ce71903d8bd0f9222f498fca7fb2a301f67f34b

~~~python
"""脉冲网络调宿律候选可达式 · 律候选判据核算（纯 Python，精确有理，确定性）
沿用 lawreach_net 的网络 N、三臂与运行层（前缀执行至「三、归约 R11」之前；其承担链、三本账、不可达结果不变）。
律候选判据 Cand（事前申报，不声称为《律数理式》(4.7) 的归约）：
  K0 承载：R ⊆ Existing = {syn_A, syn_B, rule_κ}，R ≠ ∅。
  K1 单独承担：上下文 c =（A 到来，I = 0）；R 外各量取全部合法值——v ∈ [0, θ)（放电间合法域：v ≥ 0，放电即复位，故拍末 v < θ；该域为区间全体，不取格点），
     R 外的 w ∈ [0, hi]，κ ∈ [0, 1/2]——z 恒为 1。vt = λ·v + wA·sA + wB·sB 对各量单调不减，故 K1 ⇔ 各自由量取下确界时 vt ≥ θ（解析判定，精确）。
  K1′ 极小：R 满足 K1 且无真子集满足 K1。
  K2 非即时：R 的成分均为 θ 层（Freeze 保留），且 K1 在冻结上下文（v := 0）下成立。
  K3 场内形成：R 中承担成分的当前版本由沉积事件写成、来源含输入端口事件，且入口截面 K1 不成立。
  K4 撤引后真实续行：撤引后实际运行（场自身沉积照常）的每个 A 拍放电，且各撤引后截面 K1′ 的解不变。
  K5 复走，两协议分报：(D) 诊断协议——自冻结快照、可塑性冻结，(A, none, A)；(C) 真实续行协议——自撤引后各截面克隆全状态（含 κ），
     沉积照常，{A, B, AB, none}⁴ 全部 256 条序列（I = 0），每个 A 拍放电。
Cand(R, t) = K0 ∧ K1′ ∧ K2 ∧ K3（截面资格）；撤引后另报 K4、K5。
"""
import os, json, contextlib, io, itertools
from fractions import Fraction as Fr
HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
def prefix(script, stop):
    p = os.path.join(HERE, script); src = open(p, encoding='utf-8').read().split(stop)[0]; sc = {'__file__': p}
    with contextlib.redirect_stdout(io.StringIO()): exec(compile(src, p, 'exec'), sc)
    return sc
V1 = prefix('lawreach_net.py', '# ======================= 三、归约 R11')
ARMS, state_at, LAM, TH, HI, PAT = (V1[k] for k in ('ARMS', 'state_at', 'LAM', 'TH', 'HI', 'PAT'))
S = str; R = {}
COMP = {'wA': 'syn_A', 'wB': 'syn_B', 'kappa': 'rule_κ'}; EXISTING = ['syn_A', 'syn_B', 'rule_κ']
LOW = {'v': Fr(0), 'wA': Fr(0), 'wB': Fr(0), 'kappa': Fr(0)}               # 合法域下确界（v ∈ [0, θ)，w ∈ [0, hi]，κ ∈ [0, 1/2]）
def K1(sigma, Rset, frozen=False, letter='A'):
    sA, sB = PAT[letter]; fixed = {k for k, c in COMP.items() if c in Rset}
    val = {k: (sigma[k] if k in fixed else LOW[k]) for k in ('wA', 'wB', 'kappa')}
    v = Fr(0)                                                               # v 不属结构成分，恒自由，取下确界 0
    inf_vt = LAM * v + val['wA'] * sA + val['wB'] * sB
    actual = LAM * (Fr(0) if frozen else sigma['v']) + sigma['wA'] * sA + sigma['wB'] * sB
    return actual >= TH and inf_vt >= TH
CANDS = [list(C) for n in range(1, 4) for C in itertools.combinations(EXISTING, n)]
def minimal(sigma, frozen=False):
    sat = [C for C in CANDS if K1(sigma, C, frozen)]
    return [C for C in sat if not any(set(D) < set(C) for D in sat)]
def formed(r, n, comp_var):
    vq = r.vtrace[n][comp_var]
    ins = {e['id'] for e in r.events if e['kind'] == 'In'}
    cur = r.trace[n][1][comp_var] != r.trace[0][1][comp_var]
    return cur and any(isinstance(k, tuple) and len(k) == 3 and k[2] in ins and s is True for k, s in vq.items())
def cand_sections(a):
    r, marks, _ = ARMS[a]; out = {}; entry_ok = bool(minimal(state_at(r, 0)))
    for name, n in marks.items():
        sg = state_at(r, n); mins = minimal(sg)
        res = []
        for C in mins:
            k2 = all(c in EXISTING for c in C) and K1(sg, C, frozen=True)
            k3 = (not entry_ok) and all(formed(r, n, k) for k, c in COMP.items() if c in C and k != 'kappa')
            if k2 and k3: res.append('{' + ','.join(C) + '}')
        out[name] = {'状态 (wA, wB, κ, v)': (S(sg['wA']), S(sg['wB']), S(sg['kappa']), S(sg['v'])),
                     'K1′ 极小解': ['{' + ','.join(C) + '}' for C in mins], 'Cand 成立的承载': res}
    return out
SEC = {a: cand_sections(a) for a in ARMS}
R['一 逐截面律候选判定（候选承担域 7 个子集全体）'] = SEC
def first(a):
    names = list(SEC[a]); hit = next((n for n in names if SEC[a][n]['Cand 成立的承载']), None)
    return {'首次 Cand 成立截面': hit, '此前截面全空': hit is None or all(not SEC[a][n]['Cand 成立的承载'] for n in names[:names.index(hit)])}
R['一′ 首次成立与此前截面'] = {a: first(a) for a in ARMS}
R['一″ 起点（入口截面）逐候选 K1'] = {'{' + ','.join(C) + '}': K1(state_at(ARMS['保持'][0], 0), C) for C in CANDS}
# 格点反例：合法 v = 7/8；教学第 2 拍后 wA = 3/4 时，A 到来是否放电、{syn_A} 是否单独承担
v = Fr(0)
for _ in range(3): v = LAM * v + Fr(1, 4) + Fr(1, 4)
sg2 = state_at(ARMS['保持'][0], ARMS['保持'][1]['教学第 2 拍后'])
R['一‴ 合法放电间 v 与 K1'] = {'AB × 3（κ = 0，I = 0）所得 v': S(v), 'v ∈ [0, θ)': Fr(0) <= v < TH,
    '教学第 2 拍后 wA': S(sg2['wA']), '取 v = 7/8 时 A 到来放电': LAM * v + sg2['wA'] >= TH, '{syn_A} 单独承担（K1）': K1(sg2, ['syn_A'])}

# 撤引后 K4、K5
def step(s, letter, I=0, plastic=True):
    sA, sB = PAT[letter]; vt = LAM * s['v'] + s['wA'] * sA + s['wB'] * sB + I; z = 1 if vt >= TH else 0
    t = dict(s); t['v'] = Fr(0) if z else vt
    if plastic:
        t['wA'] = min(HI, s['wA'] + s['kappa'] * sA * z); t['wB'] = min(HI, s['wB'] + s['kappa'] * sB * z)
    return t, z
post = {}
for a in ('保持', '撤回'):
    r, marks, wk = ARMS[a]; names = [n for n in marks if n.startswith('撤引')]
    k4_fire = all(e['out']['z'] == '1' for e in r.events if e['src'] == 'fire' and int(e['id'][4:]) >= wk
                  and next(x for x in r.events if x['id'] == 'in' + e['id'][4:])['out']['sA'] == '1')
    k4_sol = all(SEC[a][n]['Cand 成立的承载'] == ['{syn_A}'] for n in names)
    diag, real, nseq = True, True, 0
    for n in names:
        sg = {k: state_at(r, marks[n])[k] for k in ('wA', 'wB', 'kappa', 'v')}
        s = dict(sg); s['v'] = Fr(0)
        for L_ in ('A', 'none', 'A'):
            s, z = step(s, L_, plastic=False)
            if L_ == 'A' and not z: diag = False
        for seq in itertools.product(list(PAT), repeat=4):
            s = dict(sg); nseq += 1
            for L_ in seq:
                s, z = step(s, L_)
                if PAT[L_][0] and not z: real = False
    post[a] = {'K4 撤引后实际 A 拍均放电': k4_fire, 'K4 撤引后各截面 Cand 解恒为 {syn_A}': k4_sol,
               'K5(D) 诊断复走（冻结可塑性，自冻结快照）': diag, 'K5(C) 真实续行复走（沉积照常，256 序列 × 截面）': real, '真实续行序列总数': nseq}
R['二 撤引后 K4、K5'] = post
R['三 签署'] = {'强度': '律候选档（明示判据 Cand；不声称归约到《律数理式》(4.7)）',
             '正式档未闭合处（保留）': ['ΩOK 须读所需续行段 ReqSeg 上的 Ω_t（定义 3.29）', 'Bind 须同一再入实现见证兼满足 ΩWit 与 Scheme', '受限评价、锚定、链闭合与 Bear 的桥接（定义 3.18—3.23）'],
             '正式 Law': '不签'}

def fix(o):
    if isinstance(o, dict): return {str(k): fix(v) for k, v in o.items()}
    if isinstance(o, (list, tuple)): return [fix(x) for x in o]
    if isinstance(o, Fr): return str(o)
    return o
json.dump(fix(R), open(os.path.join(HERE, 'lawreach_cand.json'), 'w'), ensure_ascii=False, indent=1, sort_keys=True, default=str)
print(json.dumps(fix(R), ensure_ascii=False, indent=1, default=str))
~~~

【资源 062 起】源码　lawreach\_table.py　SHA-256 de38d9255a7fc4ee1a01f24943e64d77e88325c5bd5cafea9785350da74a3680

~~~python
"""脉冲网络调宿律候选可达式 · 许可值表与判据域核算（纯 Python，精确有理，确定性）
许可值表：输入字母 {A, B, AB, 静}；教学电流 I ∈ {0, 1}；κ 的可写值 {0, 1/4, 1/2}。沉积是 N 的动力步，外部操作只有直写 κ 与输入。
K1 的自由量域：v ∈ [0, θ)（由 v ≥ 0 的归纳与放电复位得出），R 外的 w ∈ [0, hi]，κ 取可写集 {0, 1/4, 1/2}（κ 不进积分读出，取值不影响 K1）。
本程序：(一) 前缀执行 lawreach_cand 至输出之前，K1、起点、首次取得、K4、K5 在上述域下照原算；
        (二) 全许可域内 8 拍穷举：v ∈ [0, θ) 恒成立（v ≥ 0 归纳的有限核对）；
        (三) 两条负界与许可齐备子协议的有限穷举，计数含入口状态、至多 8（或 4）拍。
"""
import os, json, contextlib, io, itertools
from fractions import Fraction as Fr
HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
def prefix(script, stop):
    p = os.path.join(HERE, script); src = open(p, encoding='utf-8').read().split(stop)[0]; sc = {'__file__': p}
    with contextlib.redirect_stdout(io.StringIO()): exec(compile(src, p, 'exec'), sc)
    return sc
V2 = prefix('lawreach_cand.py', 'def fix(o):')
R = dict(V2['R']); PAT, LAM, TH, HI = V2['PAT'], V2['LAM'], V2['TH'], V2['HI']
KAPPA_W = [Fr(0), Fr(1, 4), Fr(1, 2)]; I_W = [Fr(0), Fr(1)]; LET = list(PAT)
def step(s, letter, I):
    wA, wB, k, v = s; sA, sB = PAT[letter]; vt = LAM * v + wA * sA + wB * sB + I; z = 1 if vt >= TH else 0
    return (min(HI, wA + k * sA * z), min(HI, wB + k * sB * z), k, Fr(0) if z else vt)
def reach(kappas, letters, Is, depth):
    start = (Fr(1, 4), Fr(1, 4), Fr(0), Fr(0)); seen = {start}; front = [start]
    for _ in range(depth):
        nxt = []
        for s in front:
            for k in kappas:
                s1 = (s[0], s[1], k, s[3])
                for L_ in letters:
                    for I in Is:
                        t = step(s1, L_, I)
                        if t not in seen: seen.add(t); nxt.append(t)
        front = nxt
    return seen
full = reach(KAPPA_W, LET, I_W, 8)
R['四 全许可域 8 拍内 v 的范围（含入口）'] = {'可达状态数': len(full), 'v 最小': str(min(s[3] for s in full)), 'v 最大': str(max(s[3] for s in full)),
                                      'v ∈ [0, θ) 全真': all(Fr(0) <= s[3] < TH for s in full)}
def rep(seen): return {'可达状态数（含入口）': len(seen), '存在 wA ≥ θ': any(s[0] >= TH for s in seen), 'v 最大': str(max(s[3] for s in seen))}
R['五 不可达与许可齐备子协议（有限穷举）'] = {
    '命题 1 协议：κ 恒 0，四字母，I ∈ {0, 1}，至多 8 拍': rep(reach([Fr(0)], LET, I_W, 8)),
    '命题 2 协议：κ ∈ {0, 1/4, 1/2}，四字母，I = 0，至多 8 拍': rep(reach(KAPPA_W, LET, [Fr(0)], 8)),
    '许可齐备子协议：κ ∈ {0, 1/4}，字母 {A, 静}，I ∈ {0, 1}，至多 4 拍': rep(reach([Fr(0), Fr(1, 4)], ['A', 'none'], I_W, 4))}
R['六 口径'] = {'K5 截面': '撤引点与其后 8 拍末，共 9 截面；诊断与真实续行同用此 9 截面', '许可值表': {'字母': LET, 'I': ['0', '1'], 'κ 可写': ['0', '1/4', '1/2']}}
def fix(o):
    if isinstance(o, dict): return {str(k): fix(v) for k, v in o.items()}
    if isinstance(o, (list, tuple)): return [fix(x) for x in o]
    if isinstance(o, Fr): return str(o)
    return o
json.dump(fix(R), open(os.path.join(HERE, 'lawreach_table.json'), 'w'), ensure_ascii=False, indent=1, sort_keys=True, default=str)
print(json.dumps(fix(R), ensure_ascii=False, indent=1, default=str))
~~~

~~~python
"""忆阻材料网络 · 申报模型资格判定核算（纯 Python，精确有理，确定性）
申报模型 𝔐_R（算法层、离散拍、模型模式）：
  交叉阵列 n_in 行 × n_out 列；器件电导 G_ij ∈ [lo, hi]（结构位），基线 b；列节点为积分发放比较电路。
  每拍：(1) 行读脉冲 s_i ∈ {0,1}（字母决定），列注入教学电流 I_j（界外）；(2) 列电容积分 v~_j = λ·v_j + Σ_i G_ij·s_i + I_j（读拍前 G）；
  (3) 发放 g_j = [v~_j ≥ θ]；(4) 复位 v_j := 0 若 g_j，否则 v~_j；
  (5) 器件电压 V_ij = V_pre·s_i + bp·V_post·g_j（bp：列回传脉冲开关，协议申报）；
  (6) 阈值型状态方程：ΔG_ij = α·[V_ij ≥ V_set] − β·[V_ij ≤ −V_rst] − ρ·(G_ij − b)（ρ：易失弛豫；读拍前 G）；夹限到 [lo, hi]。
申报参数族：V_pre = V_post = 1/2，V_set = 1，V_rst = 1（脉冲非负，复位支不触发），lo = 0，hi = 2。
𝒟₁ 单拍设定取 LOC 合法域 [b, hi]（1/4 格点）；形成侧按 PS 认证 PLUS 分项（同拍撤除后 PLUS 为 0 且不新增放电），净增量另报；
直写为拍间实际状态转换（writes），实际与诊断分支都读写后态；族条件按 bp 两值写出。
核算：一、𝒦_g 成员资格（与按定义 2 独立实现的一般形逐拍比较；越出族者报不属适用域）；
     二、规则级 P10（shiyin_full 的 MechC，机制路线申报穷尽 / 未申报）；
     三、主实例的实际窗分证（TraceSet、Part、HDyn 同一 T；𝒟₁ 单拍设定；同拍撤除 M1₀）与 Fld 判词；
     四、负例与边界（无形成；非易失只读窗；易失只读窗）。
"""
import sys, os, json, runpy, tempfile, contextlib, io, itertools, random
from fractions import Fraction as Fr
HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)); ARGV = list(sys.argv)
R = {}
def S(x): return str(x)

# ======================= 申报模型（物理形） =======================
class Prm:
    def __init__(s, n_in=2, n_out=1, lam=Fr(1, 2), th=Fr(1), alpha=Fr(1, 4), beta=Fr(1, 4), rho=Fr(1, 8), b=Fr(1, 4),
                 lo=Fr(0), hi=Fr(2), Vpre=Fr(1, 2), Vpost=Fr(1, 2), Vset=Fr(1), Vrst=Fr(1)):
        s.n_in, s.n_out, s.lam, s.th, s.alpha, s.beta, s.rho, s.b, s.lo, s.hi = n_in, n_out, Fr(lam), Fr(th), Fr(alpha), Fr(beta), Fr(rho), Fr(b), Fr(lo), Fr(hi)
        s.Vpre, s.Vpost, s.Vset, s.Vrst = Fr(Vpre), Fr(Vpost), Fr(Vset), Fr(Vrst)
    def d(s): return {k: S(v) for k, v in vars(s).items()}
PAT = {'A': (1, 0), 'B': (0, 1), 'AB': (1, 1), 'none': (0, 0)}
X2 = [(0, 0), (1, 0)]                                                # n_in = 2、n_out = 1 的结构位

def init(p): return {'G': {(i, j): p.b for i in range(p.n_in) for j in range(p.n_out)}, 'v': [Fr(0)] * p.n_out}
def phys_step(p, st, letter, I, bp=1, setG=None, drop=None):
    """一拍。setG：𝒟₁ 单拍设定（拍前改写所读电导）；drop：同拍撤除（'letter' 撤输入字母，'I' 撤教学电流）。返回 (新状态, 拍内记录)。"""
    G = dict(st['G']); v = list(st['v'])
    if setG: G.update(setG)
    s = PAT['none' if drop == 'letter' else letter]; I = [Fr(0)] * p.n_out if drop == 'I' else [Fr(x) for x in I]
    vt = [p.lam * v[j] + sum(G[(i, j)] * s[i] for i in range(p.n_in)) + I[j] for j in range(p.n_out)]
    g = [1 if vt[j] >= p.th else 0 for j in range(p.n_out)]
    v2 = [Fr(0) if g[j] else vt[j] for j in range(p.n_out)]
    G2, contrib, parts = {}, {}, {}
    for (i, j), w in G.items():
        V = p.Vpre * s[i] + bp * p.Vpost * g[j]
        pot = p.alpha if V >= p.Vset else Fr(0); dep = -p.beta if V <= -p.Vrst else Fr(0); rel = -p.rho * (w - p.b)
        Sx = w + pot + dep + rel; G2[(i, j)] = min(p.hi, max(p.lo, Sx))
        contrib[(i, j)] = G2[(i, j)] - w; parts[(i, j)] = {'pot': pot, 'dep': dep, 'rel': rel, 'clamp': Sx != G2[(i, j)]}
    return {'G': G2, 'v': v2}, {'s': s, 'I': I, 'vt': vt, 'g': g, 'c': contrib, 'parts': parts, 'G_read': G}

# ======================= 一、𝒦_g 成员资格 =======================
def kg_step(q, o, s, I):
    """《候选宿载场具化判定式》定义 2 的独立实现（字面顺序）。q：成员参数；o = (w, v, x, y)。"""
    w, v, x, y = o; n_in, n_out = q['n_in'], q['n_out']
    vt = [q['lam'][j] * v[j] + sum(w[(i, j)] * s[i] for i in range(n_in)) + I[j] for j in range(n_out)]
    g = [1 if vt[j] >= q['th'][j] else 0 for j in range(n_out)]
    v2 = [q['vr'][j] if g[j] else vt[j] for j in range(n_out)]
    x2 = [q['dx'][i] * x[i] + s[i] for i in range(n_in)]; y2 = [q['dy'][j] * y[j] + g[j] for j in range(n_out)]
    pl, bap = q['plastic'], q['bap']; w2 = {}
    for (i, j), wij in w.items():
        Pp = q['Ap'] * x2[i] if (pl and bap and g[j]) else Fr(0)
        Pm = -q['Am'] * y2[j] if (pl and bap and s[i]) else Fr(0)
        Pr = -q['rho'] * (wij - q['b'][(i, j)]) if pl else Fr(0)
        w2[(i, j)] = min(q['hi'][(i, j)], max(q['lo'][(i, j)], wij + Pp + Pm + Pr))
    return (w2, v2, x2, y2), g
def as_kg(p, bp=1):
    """申报物理参数 → 𝒦_g 成员参数（映射：d_x = d_y = 0，A₊ = α，A₋ = 0，ρ 同，θ、λ 同，v_reset = 0，plastic = 1，bap = bp）。
    映射成立的条件（族条件）：V_pre < V_set，bp·V_post < V_set，V_pre + bp·V_post ≥ V_set（只在共现时增强）；脉冲非负（复位支不触发）。"""
    X = [(i, j) for i in range(p.n_in) for j in range(p.n_out)]
    return {'n_in': p.n_in, 'n_out': p.n_out, 'lam': [p.lam] * p.n_out, 'th': [p.th] * p.n_out, 'vr': [Fr(0)] * p.n_out,
            'dx': [Fr(0)] * p.n_in, 'dy': [Fr(0)] * p.n_out, 'Ap': p.alpha, 'Am': Fr(0), 'rho': p.rho, 'plastic': True, 'bap': bool(bp),
            'b': {k: p.b for k in X}, 'lo': {k: p.lo for k in X}, 'hi': {k: p.hi for k in X}}
def fam_cond(p, bp=1):
    """族条件：V_pre < V_set，V_post < V_set；bp = 1 时 V_pre + V_post ≥ V_set（共现达阈）；bp = 0 时增强分项恒零。"""
    return p.Vpre < p.Vset and p.Vpost < p.Vset and (bp == 0 or p.Vpre + p.Vpost >= p.Vset)
def compare(p, hist, bp=1):
    q = as_kg(p, bp); st = init(p); o = (dict(st['G']), list(st['v']), [Fr(0)] * p.n_in, [Fr(0)] * p.n_out); bad = clamp = eq = 0
    for letter, I in hist:
        st, rec = phys_step(p, st, letter, I, bp=bp); o, g = kg_step(q, o, PAT[letter], [Fr(x) for x in I])
        if st['G'] != o[0] or st['v'] != o[1] or rec['g'] != g: bad += 1
        clamp += sum(1 for k in rec['parts'] if rec['parts'][k]['clamp']); eq += sum(1 for j in range(p.n_out) if rec['vt'][j] == p.th)
    return bad, clamp, eq
rng = random.Random(20261111); n_beats = n_bad = n_clamp = n_eq = n_hist = 0
FAM = dict(lam=[Fr(0), Fr(1, 2), Fr(3, 4)], th=[Fr(1), Fr(3, 2)], alpha=[Fr(1, 8), Fr(1, 4), Fr(3, 8)], rho=[Fr(0), Fr(1, 16), Fr(1, 8)], b=[Fr(1, 4), Fr(1, 2)])
for _ in range(400):
    kw = {k: rng.choice(v) for k, v in FAM.items()}; n_out = rng.choice([1, 2]); p = Prm(n_out=n_out, **kw); bp = rng.choice([0, 1])
    L = rng.randint(4, 16); hist = [(rng.choice(list(PAT)), [rng.choice([0, Fr(1, 2), 1]) for _ in range(n_out)]) for _ in range(L)]
    bad, cl, eq = compare(p, hist, bp); n_hist += 1; n_beats += L; n_bad += bad; n_clamp += cl; n_eq += eq
R['一 𝒦_g 成员资格（申报族内随机史，物理形 vs 定义 2 独立实现；逐拍比较 G、v 与发放，x′、y′ 为映射后资格迹）'] = {'史数': n_hist, '拍数': n_beats, '不一致拍': n_bad, '夹限生效处': n_clamp, '积分恰等于阈值处': n_eq}
# 越出族：V_pre ≥ V_set（单有输入即增强，增强不再以发放为条件）
p_out = Prm(Vpre=Fr(1)); hist = [('A', [0]), ('A', [0]), ('none', [0])]
R['一′ 越出申报族（V_pre = 1 ≥ V_set）'] = {'族条件': fam_cond(p_out), '与定义 2 逐拍不一致数（按同一映射）': compare(p_out, hist)[0],
    '判词': '判定路径未覆盖（不属 𝒦_g：增强分项不经发放条件；不读作 0）'}

# ======================= 二、规则级 P10 =======================
with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp, contextlib.redirect_stdout(io.StringIO()):
    cwd = os.getcwd(); os.chdir(tmp); sys.argv = ['full', os.path.join(tmp, 'o.json')]
    X = runpy.run_path(os.path.join(HERE, 'shiyin_full.py')); os.chdir(cwd); sys.argv = ARGV
Mx, RunX, E, MechC, versions_of, vanc_at, version_path = (X[k] for k in ('Mx', 'RunX', 'E', 'MechC', 'versions_of', 'vanc_at', 'version_path'))
P0 = Prm()
GD = ('pred', lambda x: P0.lo <= x <= P0.hi, [Fr(j, 32) for j in range(0, 65)])
NUM = ('pred', lambda x: True, [Fr(j, 8) for j in range(-8, 33)])
B01 = ('fin', {Fr(0), Fr(1)})
ROUTES = {'integ', 'fire', 'dev', 'port:In'}
def mx(decl=True):
    lay = {'G00': 'theta', 'G10': 'theta', 'v': 'r', 'vt': 'r', 'g': 'r', 's0': 'r', 's1': 'r', 'I': 'r'}
    dom = {'G00': GD, 'G10': GD, 'v': NUM, 'vt': NUM, 'g': B01, 's0': B01, 's1': B01, 'I': ('fin', {Fr(0), Fr(1, 2), Fr(1)})}
    FM = {'integ': ('Run', {'vt'}), 'fire': ('Run', {'g', 'v'}), 'dev': ('Sed', {'G00', 'G10'})}
    M = Mx(lay, dom, (lambda s: s['G00'] + s['G10'], {'G00': 1, 'G10': 1}), ports={'In': {'s0', 's1', 'I'}}, FM=FM, fid='𝔐_R')
    if decl: M.mech_routes = set(ROUTES); M.mech_exhaustive = True
    return M
def st0(): return {'G00': P0.b, 'G10': P0.b, 'v': Fr(0), 'vt': Fr(0), 'g': Fr(0), 's0': Fr(0), 's1': Fr(0), 'I': Fr(0)}
def dev_fn(i, p=P0):
    def f(v):
        w = v[f'G{i}0']; V = p.Vpre * v[f's{i}'] + p.Vpost * v['g']
        Sx = w + (p.alpha if V >= p.Vset else 0) - (p.beta if V <= -p.Vrst else 0) - p.rho * (w - p.b)
        return {f'G{i}0': min(p.hi, max(p.lo, Sx))}
    return f
def steps_for(k, letter, I):
    s = PAT[letter]
    return [((k, 1), E(f'in{k}', 'In', 'port', 'env', [], ['s0', 's1', 'I'], lambda v, s=s, I=I: {'s0': Fr(s[0]), 's1': Fr(s[1]), 'I': Fr(I)})),
            ((k, 2), E(f'integ{k}', 'Run', 'integ', 'F', ['v', 'G00', 'G10', 's0', 's1', 'I'], ['vt'],
                       lambda v: {'vt': P0.lam * v['v'] + v['G00'] * v['s0'] + v['G10'] * v['s1'] + v['I']}, {'G00': 1, 'G10': 1, 'v': 1, 's0': 1, 's1': 1, 'I': 1})),
            ((k, 3), E(f'fire{k}', 'Run', 'fire', 'F', ['vt'], ['g', 'v'], lambda v: {'g': Fr(1), 'v': Fr(0)} if v['vt'] >= P0.th else {'g': Fr(0), 'v': v['vt']})),
            ((k, 4), E(f'dev0_{k}', 'Sed', 'dev', 'F', ['G00', 's0', 'g'], ['G00'], dev_fn(0))),
            ((k, 5), E(f'dev1_{k}', 'Sed', 'dev', 'F', ['G10', 's1', 'g'], ['G10'], dev_fn(1)))]
TEACH = sum((steps_for(k, 'A', 1) for k in range(1, 5)), [])
R['二 规则级 P10（教学协议：(A + 教学电流 1) × 4 重放；申报路线穷尽）'] = MechC(lambda: RunX(mx(), st0()), {'教学协议': TEACH})
R['二′ 机制未申报时'] = MechC(lambda: RunX(mx(False), st0()), {'教学协议': TEACH})

# ======================= 三、主实例：实际窗分证 =======================
FORM = [('A', [1])] * 4
WIN = [('A', [0]), ('A', [0]), ('none', [0])] * 2
def run(p, hist, bp_of=lambda k: 1, writes=None):
    """writes：{拍下标 k: {位: 值}}——施作者直写，作为第 k 拍之前的拍间实际状态转换；tr[k] 即写后态，实际与诊断分支都读它。"""
    writes = writes or {}; st = init(p); tr = [st]; recs = []
    for k, (letter, I) in enumerate(hist):
        if k in writes: st = {'G': {**st['G'], **writes[k]}, 'v': list(st['v'])}; tr[k] = st
        st, rec = phys_step(p, st, letter, I, bp=bp_of(k)); tr.append(st); recs.append(rec)
    return tr, recs
def subsets(X): return [list(c) for n in range(1, len(X) + 1) for c in itertools.combinations(X, n)]
def LOC(p): return [Fr(j, 4) for j in range(0, 9) if p.b <= Fr(j, 4) <= p.hi]   # 𝒟₁ 单拍设定：LOC 合法域 [b, hi] 上 1/4 格点
def judge(p, hist, w0, bp_of=lambda k: 1, writes=None):
    """hist 中下标 ≥ w0 的拍为窗 W。逐 T 判 TraceSet（q、r）、Part、HDyn；Fld = ∃T 三项同真。"""
    tr, recs = run(p, hist, bp_of, writes); W = range(w0, len(hist)); X = sorted(tr[0]['G'])
    res = {}
    for T in subsets(X):
        key = '{' + ', '.join(f'G{i}{j}' for i, j in T) + '}'
        q = all(tr[k + 1]['G'][x] != p.b for k in W for x in T) and all(tr[w0]['G'][x] != p.b for x in T)
        r_w = []
        for k in range(0, w0):                                   # 形成史 PS 认证：实际 PLUS ≠ 0；同拍撤除入场分量后 PLUS = 0，且不新增放电（M1₀）；净增量另报
            for x in T:
                if recs[k]['parts'][x]['pot'] == 0: continue
                for drop in ('letter', 'I'):
                    _, alt = phys_step(p, tr[k], *hist[k], bp=bp_of(k), drop=drop)
                    if alt['parts'][x]['pot'] == 0 and all(a_ <= b_ for a_, b_ in zip(alt['g'], recs[k]['g'])):
                        r_w.append({'拍': k, '位': f'G{x[0]}{x[1]}', '撤除': f'撤{drop}', 'PLUS 实/撤': (S(recs[k]['parts'][x]['pot']), S(alt['parts'][x]['pot'])),
                                    '净增量 实/撤': (S(recs[k]['c'][x]), S(alt['c'][x])), '撤后放电': alt['g']}); break
        part_w, hdyn_w, hself = None, None, None
        for k in W:
            for x in T:
                for val in LOC(p):
                    if val == tr[k]['G'][x]: continue
                    _, alt = phys_step(p, tr[k], *hist[k], bp=bp_of(k), setG={x: val})
                    if part_w is None and recs[k]['s'][x[0]] and (alt['vt'] != recs[k]['vt'] or alt['g'] != recs[k]['g']):
                        part_w = {'拍': k, '设定': f'G{x[0]}{x[1]} := {val}', '积分 实/设': (S(recs[k]['vt'][0]), S(alt['vt'][0])), '发放 实/设': (recs[k]['g'], alt['g'])}
                    if hdyn_w is None:
                        for y in T:
                            if recs[k]['c'][y] == 0: continue
                            via = 'pot' if alt['parts'][y]['pot'] != recs[k]['parts'][y]['pot'] else ('rel' if alt['parts'][y]['rel'] != recs[k]['parts'][y]['rel'] else None)
                            if via == 'rel' and x == y and hself is None:
                                hself = {'拍': k, '作用位': f'G{y[0]}{y[1]}', '实际分项 c': S(recs[k]['c'][y]), '设定': f'G{x[0]}{x[1]} := {val}'}
                            if via and (x != y or via == 'pot'):
                                hdyn_w = {'拍': k, '作用位': f'G{y[0]}{y[1]}', '实际分项 c': S(recs[k]['c'][y]), '分项构成': {kk: S(vv) for kk, vv in recs[k]['parts'][y].items()},
                                          '设定': f'G{x[0]}{x[1]} := {val}', '设定下分项构成': {kk: S(vv) for kk, vv in alt['parts'][y].items()},
                                          '途径': '界内：G → 积分 → 发放 → 器件电压 → ΔG' if via == 'pot' else '器件自弛豫（读本位 G）'}
                                break
        ts = q and bool(r_w)
        res[key] = {'TraceSet': ts, 'q（W 内留存非零）': q, 'r 见证（PS：PLUS 分项；净增量另报）': r_w[:3], 'Part': part_w is not None, 'Part 见证': part_w,
                    'HDyn': hdyn_w is not None, 'HDyn 见证': hdyn_w, '三项同真': ts and part_w is not None and hdyn_w is not None,
                    '只经自弛豫的作用（读法未定，不计入 HDyn）': hself if hdyn_w is None else None}
    fld = any(v['三项同真'] for v in res.values())
    selfonly = (not fld) and any(v['TraceSet'] and v['Part'] and v['只经自弛豫的作用（读法未定，不计入 HDyn）'] for v in res.values())
    G_end = lambda k, x: recs[k]['G_read'][x] + recs[k]['c'][x]               # 拍末值（不含其后拍间直写）
    traj = [(k, hist[k][0], S(hist[k][1][0]), recs[k]['g'][0], S(G_end(k, (0, 0))), S(G_end(k, (1, 0)))) for k in range(len(hist))]
    return {'轨迹（拍, 字母, 教学电流, 发放, G00, G10）': traj, '拍间直写（写前 → 写后）': {k: {f'G{x[0]}{x[1]}': (S(G_end(k - 1, x)), S(v)) for x, v in w_.items()} for k, w_ in (writes or {}).items()}, '逐 T': res, 'Fld': 1 if fld else ('⊥' if selfonly else 0)}
J = judge(P0, FORM + WIN, len(FORM))
J['判词'] = '1（模型模式；申报协议与已认证见证）' if J['Fld'] else '0 须 𝔅₀ 前件'
R['三 主实例（易失 ρ = 1/8；形成 (A + 1) × 4；W = (A, A, 静) × 2）'] = J

# ======================= 四、负例与边界 =======================
B0 = '𝔅₀ 前件：𝒟₁ 申报、机制清单申报穷尽、记录忠实（模型模式〔构〕）；负判由解析论证承担，有限格点核算只作一致性核对'
J1 = judge(P0, [('A', [0])] * 4 + WIN, 4)
J1['判词'] = ('1' if J1['Fld'] else '0（' + B0 + '）'); J1['解析'] = '无教学电流时 wA = b 下 v < θ 恒成立，从不发放，PLUS 恒零；G = b 时弛豫为零：全程无结构位偏离基线，T_max = ∅，TraceSet 对一切 T 不成立'
R['四.1 无形成（无教学电流）'] = J1
P_nv = Prm(rho=Fr(0))
J2 = judge(P_nv, FORM + WIN, len(FORM), bp_of=lambda k: 1 if k < len(FORM) else 0)
J2['判词'] = ('1' if J2['Fld'] else '0（' + B0 + '）'); J2['解析'] = 'W 内 bp = 0：器件电压 ≤ V_pre = 1/2 < V_set，PLUS 恒零；ρ = 0：弛豫为零；复位支不触发：W 内一切结构位无有效事件，HDyn 对一切 T 不成立'
R['四.2 非易失（ρ = 0）只读窗（W 内关闭回传脉冲，器件电压 ≤ 1/2 < V_set）'] = J2
J3 = judge(P0, FORM + WIN, len(FORM), bp_of=lambda k: 1 if k < len(FORM) else 0)
J3['判词'] = {1: '1', 0: '0（' + B0 + '）', '⊥': '⊥（W 内作用于 T 的只有器件自弛豫：读本位迹应的弛豫是否承担 HDyn 的出自迹应之作用，本核算不定读法；不读作 0）'}[J3['Fld']]
R['四.3 易失（ρ = 1/8）只读窗'] = J3

R['五 真实材料'] = {'判词': '⊥', '原因': ['模型判词只判申报模型 𝔐_R（算法层）；物质宿载的读回需实施保真与模型如实的对应物（《候选宿载场具化判定式》5.5），未给',
                    '《物质场具化判定式》已登记：忆阻纳米线网络「复位网络等同原始网络」的前提待验证；本核算不给物质观测']}

def fix(o):
    if isinstance(o, dict): return {str(k): fix(v) for k, v in o.items()}
    if isinstance(o, (list, tuple)): return [fix(x) for x in o]
    if isinstance(o, Fr): return str(o)
    return o
json.dump(fix(R), open(os.path.join(HERE, 'memr_checks.json'), 'w'), ensure_ascii=False, indent=1, sort_keys=True, default=str)
print(json.dumps(fix(R), ensure_ascii=False, indent=1, default=str))
~~~

【资源 066 起】源码　memr\_tune.py　SHA-256 36c7ead3899020f6216f84e2874d99628e12a0b6f35c15ab1710b82122f0e903

~~~python
"""忆阻材料网络势引可能式 · 核算（纯 Python，精确有理，确定性）
沿用 memr_checks 的申报模型 𝔐_R 与资格判定（前缀执行至「四、负例与边界」之前；LOC 合法域、PS 的 PLUS 认证、直写为拍间状态转换）。
许可类：输入端口（行脉冲字母、列教学电流）；直写端口 Rw（施作者编程脉冲把某器件电导直接写到申报值，落在结构位 G_ij）。B、场规则、层级不变。
资格与施作在同一历史中。
核算：
  一、C^宿 正向构造：目标设定 g（目标电导）→ 直写 G00 := g → 窗内场自身运行与沉积；读数 Φ := G00（A 通路的宿载驱动）。
     逐项：Org₀、Rewrite、Eff、Bear、Bear_R,W（承担起于直写）、GoalCond（目标经写值链决定直写）、ΔΦ_W ≠ 0、入口驱动对照；直写之后的实际窗 Fld 重判。
  二、对照：同一施作者只经输入端口（字母 + 教学电流）把 G00 推到同一量级——Rewrite = 0，报为输入与沉积，不计调宿。
  三、C^谐 否定：𝔐_R 的变量表与端口表穷尽检查——无动态层门变量、无门控端口；传输读取的量逐一归层；许可类下 Gate 恒 0。
  边界例：(b) 静拍在窗前、无目标事件；另列边界例与对照。
"""
import os, sys, json, contextlib, io, runpy, tempfile, itertools
from fractions import Fraction as Fr
HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
def prefix(script, stop):
    p = os.path.join(HERE, script); src = open(p, encoding='utf-8').read().split(stop)[0]; sc = {'__file__': p}
    cwd = os.getcwd()
    with contextlib.redirect_stdout(io.StringIO()): exec(compile(src, p, 'exec'), sc)
    os.chdir(cwd); return sc
M = prefix('memr_checks.py', '# ======================= 四、负例与边界')
Prm, phys_step, PAT, P0, FORM, WIN = (M[k] for k in ('Prm', 'phys_step', 'PAT', 'P0', 'FORM', 'WIN'))
Mx, RunX, E, Org0, Rewrite, Gate, BearW, GoalCond, in_W, version_path, versions_of, Eff, Bear = (M['X'][k] for k in ('Mx', 'RunX', 'E', 'Org0', 'Rewrite', 'Gate', 'BearW', 'GoalCond', 'in_W', 'version_path', 'versions_of', 'Eff', 'Bear'))
S = str; R = {}
GD, NUM, B01 = M['GD'], M['NUM'], M['B01']

# ======================= 模型（加直写端口与目标声明；场规则同 𝔐_R） =======================
def mx():
    lay = {'G00': 'theta', 'G10': 'theta', 'v': 'r', 'vt': 'r', 'g': 'r', 's0': 'r', 's1': 'r', 'I': 'r', 'gol': 'ext'}
    dom = {'G00': GD, 'G10': GD, 'v': NUM, 'vt': NUM, 'g': B01, 's0': B01, 's1': B01, 'I': ('fin', {Fr(0), Fr(1)}), 'gol': ('fin', {Fr(0), Fr(1, 4), Fr(1), Fr(5, 4)})}
    FM = {'integ': ('Run', {'vt'}), 'fire': ('Run', {'g', 'v'}), 'dev': ('Sed', {'G00', 'G10'}), 'ext_goal': ('Run', {'gol'})}
    m = Mx(lay, dom, (lambda s: s['G00'], {'G00': 1}), ports={'In': {'s0', 's1', 'I'}, 'Rw': {'G00', 'G10'}}, FM=FM, fid='𝔐_R',
           goal={'role': 'Xg', 'var': 'gol', 'field': '𝔐_R'}, target={'gol': {'field': '𝔐_R', 'rel': '器件电导'}})
    m.mech_routes = {'integ', 'fire', 'dev', 'ext_goal', 'port:In', 'port:Rw'}; m.mech_exhaustive = True
    return m
def st0(): return {'G00': P0.b, 'G10': P0.b, 'v': Fr(0), 'vt': Fr(0), 'g': Fr(0), 's0': Fr(0), 's1': Fr(0), 'I': Fr(0), 'gol': Fr(0)}
p = P0
def dev(i):
    def f(v):
        w = v[f'G{i}0']; V = p.Vpre * v[f's{i}'] + p.Vpost * v['g']
        Sx = w + (p.alpha if V >= p.Vset else 0) - (p.beta if V <= -p.Vrst else 0) - p.rho * (w - p.b)
        return {f'G{i}0': min(p.hi, max(p.lo, Sx))}
    return f
def beat(r, k, letter, I, part_in='env'):
    s = PAT[letter]
    r.ev((k, 2), E(f'in{k}', 'In', 'port', part_in, [], ['s0', 's1', 'I'], lambda v, s=s, I=I: {'s0': Fr(s[0]), 's1': Fr(s[1]), 'I': Fr(I)}))
    r.ev((k, 3), E(f'integ{k}', 'Run', 'integ', 'F', ['v', 'G00', 'G10', 's0', 's1', 'I'], ['vt'],
                   lambda v: {'vt': p.lam * v['v'] + v['G00'] * v['s0'] + v['G10'] * v['s1'] + v['I']}, {'G00': 1, 'G10': 1, 'v': 1, 's0': 1, 's1': 1, 'I': 1}))
    r.ev((k, 4), E(f'fire{k}', 'Run', 'fire', 'F', ['vt'], ['g', 'v'], lambda v: {'g': Fr(1), 'v': Fr(0)} if v['vt'] >= p.th else {'g': Fr(0), 'v': v['vt']}))
    r.ev((k, 5), E(f'dev0_{k}', 'Sed', 'dev', 'F', ['G00', 's0', 'g'], ['G00'], dev(0)))
    r.ev((k, 6), E(f'dev1_{k}', 'Sed', 'dev', 'F', ['G10', 's1', 'g'], ['G10'], dev(1)))
def forms(r, W):
    s_in = r.trace[0][1]; s_end = r.trace[-1][1]
    return {'Org₀': Org0(r), 'Rewrite(P)': Rewrite(r, 'P'), 'Gate(P)': Gate(r, 'P'), 'Bear_R,W(P)': BearW(r, 'P', {'Rw'}, W),
            'Bear_W(P，全部施作种类)': BearW(r, 'P', {'Rw', 'In', 'Gt'}, W),
            'GoalCond(P；Rw × θ；W)': GoalCond(r, 'P', kinds={'Rw'}, layers=('theta',), W=W), 'ΔΦ_W': S(r.M.phi(s_end) - r.M.phi(s_in)),
            'Φ 入、Φ 末': (S(r.M.phi(s_in)), S(r.M.phi(s_end)))}
TARGET = Fr(5, 4)
# 一 C^宿 正向
r1 = RunX(mx(), st0())
r1.ev((1, 0), E('G1', 'Run', 'ext_goal', 'Xg', [], ['gol'], lambda v: {'gol': TARGET}))
r1.ev((1, 1), E('rw1', 'Rw', 'port', 'P', ['gol'], ['G00'], lambda v: {'G00': v['gol']}))
for k, (L_, I) in enumerate([('A', 0), ('A', 0), ('none', 0), ('A', 0)], 1): beat(r1, k, L_, I[0] if isinstance(I, list) else I)
W1 = ((1, 0), (4, 9))
rw = next(e for e in r1.events if e['id'] == 'rw1'); gv = next(k for k in versions_of(r1, rw) if k[0] == 'G00')
dv = [e for e in r1.events if e['src'] == 'dev' and 'G00' in e['changed']]
R['一 C^宿 正向（目标电导 5/4；直写 G00；窗内 A、A、静、A）'] = {**forms(r1, W1),
    '直写版本': '|'.join(map(str, gv)), '窗内沉积（G00）': [(e['id'], e['out']) for e in dv],
    '首拍积分读入直写版本': next(e for e in r1.events if e['id'] == 'integ1')['read_versions']['G00'] == gv,
    '直写 → 首拍沉积版本路径': version_path(r1, gv, next(k for k in versions_of(r1, dv[0]) if k[0] == 'G00')) if dv else None,
    '各拍发放': [e['out']['g'] for e in r1.events if e['src'] == 'fire']}
# 一′ 直写后的实际窗 Fld 重判（物理形；写入施于形成段后的一个静拍之前；窗 = 其后 W）
judge = M['judge']
JW = judge(P0, FORM + [('none', [0])] + WIN, len(FORM) + 1, writes={len(FORM): {(0, 0): Fr(7, 4)}})
R['一′ 边界例 (b)：形成段 → 静拍前直写 G00 := 7/4（静拍在窗前；物理形，无目标事件）→ W'] = {'Fld': JW['Fld'], '{G00} 三项': {k: JW['逐 T']['{G00}'][k] for k in ('TraceSet', 'Part', 'HDyn')},
    'HDyn 途径': (JW['逐 T']['{G00}']['HDyn 见证'] or {}).get('途径')}
# 二 对照：只经输入端口
r2 = RunX(mx(), st0())
r2.ev((1, 0), E('G1', 'Run', 'ext_goal', 'Xg', [], ['gol'], lambda v: {'gol': TARGET}))
for k in range(1, 5): beat(r2, k, 'A', 1, part_in='P')
W2 = ((1, 0), (4, 9))
R['二 对照：施作者只经输入端口（A + 教学电流）× 4'] = {**forms(r2, W2), 'G00 末值': S(r2.s['G00']),
    '施作者事件种类': sorted({e['kind'] for e in r2.events if e['part'] == 'P'}), '归类': '输入（施作者事件只在 In 端口）与沉积（dev 由器件状态方程写入），不计调宿'}
# 三 C^谐 否定：变量、端口与传输读取的穷尽检查
m = mx()
trans_reads = ['v', 'G00', 'G10', 's0', 's1', 'I']
R['三 C^谐：𝔐_R 的门控对象穷尽检查'] = {
    '层表': {v: L for v, L in sorted(m.layer.items())}, 'm 层（动态通透）变量': [v for v, L in m.layer.items() if L == 'm'],
    '端口表': {k: sorted(v) for k, v in m.ports.items()}, '门控端口 Gt': 'Gt' in m.ports,
    '传输（积分）读取的量与层': {v: m.layer[v] for v in trans_reads},
    '连接 ij 的通透量': 'G_ij（θ 层，沉积度）；读出逐连接只经 G_ij·s_i，无逐连接的动态因子',
    '许可类下 Gate(P)': {'一': Gate(r1, 'P'), '二': Gate(r2, 'P')},
    '教学电流 I 的归类': '加在列节点、经节点自身积分规则处理——输入；不在任何连接上，不构成连接通阻'}

# ======================= 四、同一历史：形成前缀 → 目标设定与直写 5/4 → A、A、静、A =======================
H4 = FORM + [('A', [0]), ('A', [0]), ('none', [0]), ('A', [0])]; WK = len(FORM)
# 4.1 物理形：资格判定（窗 = 直写后四拍；直写施于第 5 拍之前，为许可 Rw，不入 r 见证的信号撤除）
J4 = judge(P0, H4, WK, writes={WK: {(0, 0): TARGET}})
t0 = J4['逐 T']['{G00}']
R['四.1 同一历史的场具化资格（物理形，模型判定程序）'] = {'Fld': J4['Fld'], '轨迹（拍, 字母, 教学电流, 发放, G00, G10）': J4['轨迹（拍, 字母, 教学电流, 发放, G00, G10）'], '拍间直写': J4['拍间直写（写前 → 写后）'],
    '{G00}': {k: t0[k] for k in ('TraceSet', 'q（W 内留存非零）', 'r 见证（PS：PLUS 分项；净增量另报）', 'Part', 'Part 见证', 'HDyn', 'HDyn 见证')},
    'r 见证所在拍均在直写之前（形成前缀）': all(w['拍'] < WK for w in t0['r 见证（PS：PLUS 分项；净增量另报）'])}
# 4.2 事件形：同一历史的完整事件重放与势引逐项
r4 = RunX(mx(), st0())
for k in range(1, WK + 1): beat(r4, k, 'A', 1)
k = WK + 1
r4.ev((k, 0), E(f'G{k}', 'Run', 'ext_goal', 'Xg', [], ['gol'], lambda v: {'gol': TARGET}))
r4.ev((k, 1), E(f'rw{k}', 'Rw', 'port', 'P', ['gol'], ['G00'], lambda v: {'G00': v['gol']}))
for j, L_ in enumerate(['A', 'A', 'none', 'A']): beat(r4, k + j, L_, 0)
W4 = ((k, 0), (k + 3, 9))
n_in = next(i for i, e in enumerate(r4.events) if e['id'] == f'G{k}')
s_in, s_end = r4.trace[n_in][1], r4.trace[-1][1]
rw4 = next(e for e in r4.events if e['id'] == f'rw{k}'); gv4 = next(x for x in versions_of(r4, rw4) if x[0] == 'G00')
dv4 = [e for e in r4.events if e['src'] == 'dev' and 'G00' in e['changed'] and e['phase'] > rw4['phase']]
ins = {e['id'] for e in r4.events if e['kind'] == 'In'}
anc_entry = r4.vtrace[n_in]['G00']
entry_ver = max((x for x in anc_entry if isinstance(x, tuple) and x[0] == 'G00' and isinstance(x[1], int) and anc_entry[x] is True), key=lambda x: x[1])
R['四.2 同一历史的势引逐项（事件重放）'] = {
    '窗入口 Φ（G00）': S(r4.M.phi(s_in)), '窗末 Φ': S(r4.M.phi(s_end)), 'ΔΦ_W': S(r4.M.phi(s_end) - r4.M.phi(s_in)),
    '入口势的承担组织': {'承担成分': 'syn G00（器件 00 的电导）', '入口版本': S(entry_ver),
                          '入口版本由沉积写成且来源含输入事件': any(isinstance(x, tuple) and len(x) == 3 and x[2] in ins and anc_entry[x] is True for x in anc_entry)},
    'Org₀（辅助）': Org0(r4, (k, 0)), 'Eff(P)': Eff(r4, 'P'), 'Bear(P)': Bear(r4, 'P'),
    '窗首拍 A 下驱动（直写前状态 / 实际）': (S(M['P0'].lam * 0 + s_in['G00']), S(next(e for e in r4.events if e['id'] == f'integ{k}')['out']['vt'])), 'Rewrite(P)': Rewrite(r4, 'P'), 'Gate(P)': Gate(r4, 'P'), 'Bear_R,W(P)': BearW(r4, 'P', {'Rw'}, W4),
    'GoalCond(P；Rw × θ；W)': GoalCond(r4, 'P', kinds={'Rw'}, layers=('theta',), W=W4),
    '目标指向（GoalRep/Target）': (r4.M.goal['field'], r4.M.target['gol']['field'], r4.M.fid),
    '直写版本': '|'.join(map(str, gv4)),
    '直写 → 首拍沉积版本路径': version_path(r4, gv4, next(x for x in versions_of(r4, dv4[0]) if x[0] == 'G00')),
    '事件身份分列（事件, 种类, 部分）': {'形成前缀沉积': [e['id'] for e in r4.events if e['src'] == 'dev' and e['changed'] and e['phase'] < rw4['phase']],
                                   '许可直写': [(e['id'], e['kind'], e['part']) for e in r4.events if e['kind'] == 'Rw'],
                                   '窗内沉积': [e['id'] for e in dv4]},
    '各拍发放': [e['out']['g'] for e in r4.events if e['src'] == 'fire']}

def fix(o):
    if isinstance(o, dict): return {str(k): fix(v) for k, v in o.items()}
    if isinstance(o, (list, tuple)): return [fix(x) for x in o]
    if isinstance(o, Fr): return str(o)
    return o
json.dump(fix(R), open(os.path.join(HERE, 'memr_tune.json'), 'w'), ensure_ascii=False, indent=1, sort_keys=True, default=str)
print(json.dumps(fix(R), ensure_ascii=False, indent=1, default=str))
~~~

【资源 068 起】源码　memr\_bounds.py　SHA-256 608fba8031a26d379d8612b582a325e5b152eca95ba2e1b068e7d041f421a87f

~~~python
"""忆阻材料网络 · 申报模型资格判定核算，含族条件边界（纯 Python，精确有理，确定性）
越出族条件只报映射边界与「判定路径未覆盖」，不断言不属 𝒦_g；补 bp = 0、V_post = V_set 的映射例。其余同 memr_checks：
申报模型 𝔐_R（算法层、离散拍、模型模式）：
  交叉阵列 n_in 行 × n_out 列；器件电导 G_ij ∈ [lo, hi]（结构位），基线 b；列节点为积分发放比较电路。
  每拍：(1) 行读脉冲 s_i ∈ {0,1}（字母决定），列注入教学电流 I_j（界外）；(2) 列电容积分 v~_j = λ·v_j + Σ_i G_ij·s_i + I_j（读拍前 G）；
  (3) 发放 g_j = [v~_j ≥ θ]；(4) 复位 v_j := 0 若 g_j，否则 v~_j；
  (5) 器件电压 V_ij = V_pre·s_i + bp·V_post·g_j（bp：列回传脉冲开关，协议申报）；
  (6) 阈值型状态方程：ΔG_ij = α·[V_ij ≥ V_set] − β·[V_ij ≤ −V_rst] − ρ·(G_ij − b)（ρ：易失弛豫；读拍前 G）；夹限到 [lo, hi]。
申报参数族：V_pre = V_post = 1/2，V_set = 1，V_rst = 1（脉冲非负，复位支不触发），lo = 0，hi = 2。
𝒟₁ 单拍设定取 LOC 合法域 [b, hi]（1/4 格点）；形成侧按 PS 认证 PLUS 分项（同拍撤除后 PLUS 为 0 且不新增放电），净增量另报；
直写为拍间实际状态转换（writes），实际与诊断分支都读写后态；族条件按 bp 两值写出。
核算：一、𝒦_g 成员资格（与按定义 2 独立实现的一般形逐拍比较）；
     二、规则级 P10（shiyin_full 的 MechC，机制路线申报穷尽 / 未申报）；
     三、主实例的实际窗分证（TraceSet、Part、HDyn 同一 T；𝒟₁ 单拍设定；同拍撤除 M1₀）与 Fld 判词；
     四、负例与边界（无形成；非易失只读窗；易失只读窗）。
"""
import sys, os, json, runpy, tempfile, contextlib, io, itertools, random
from fractions import Fraction as Fr
HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)); ARGV = list(sys.argv)
R = {}
def S(x): return str(x)

# ======================= 申报模型（物理形） =======================
class Prm:
    def __init__(s, n_in=2, n_out=1, lam=Fr(1, 2), th=Fr(1), alpha=Fr(1, 4), beta=Fr(1, 4), rho=Fr(1, 8), b=Fr(1, 4),
                 lo=Fr(0), hi=Fr(2), Vpre=Fr(1, 2), Vpost=Fr(1, 2), Vset=Fr(1), Vrst=Fr(1)):
        s.n_in, s.n_out, s.lam, s.th, s.alpha, s.beta, s.rho, s.b, s.lo, s.hi = n_in, n_out, Fr(lam), Fr(th), Fr(alpha), Fr(beta), Fr(rho), Fr(b), Fr(lo), Fr(hi)
        s.Vpre, s.Vpost, s.Vset, s.Vrst = Fr(Vpre), Fr(Vpost), Fr(Vset), Fr(Vrst)
    def d(s): return {k: S(v) for k, v in vars(s).items()}
PAT = {'A': (1, 0), 'B': (0, 1), 'AB': (1, 1), 'none': (0, 0)}
X2 = [(0, 0), (1, 0)]                                                # n_in = 2、n_out = 1 的结构位

def init(p): return {'G': {(i, j): p.b for i in range(p.n_in) for j in range(p.n_out)}, 'v': [Fr(0)] * p.n_out}
def phys_step(p, st, letter, I, bp=1, setG=None, drop=None):
    """一拍。setG：𝒟₁ 单拍设定（拍前改写所读电导）；drop：同拍撤除（'letter' 撤输入字母，'I' 撤教学电流）。返回 (新状态, 拍内记录)。"""
    G = dict(st['G']); v = list(st['v'])
    if setG: G.update(setG)
    s = PAT['none' if drop == 'letter' else letter]; I = [Fr(0)] * p.n_out if drop == 'I' else [Fr(x) for x in I]
    vt = [p.lam * v[j] + sum(G[(i, j)] * s[i] for i in range(p.n_in)) + I[j] for j in range(p.n_out)]
    g = [1 if vt[j] >= p.th else 0 for j in range(p.n_out)]
    v2 = [Fr(0) if g[j] else vt[j] for j in range(p.n_out)]
    G2, contrib, parts = {}, {}, {}
    for (i, j), w in G.items():
        V = p.Vpre * s[i] + bp * p.Vpost * g[j]
        pot = p.alpha if V >= p.Vset else Fr(0); dep = -p.beta if V <= -p.Vrst else Fr(0); rel = -p.rho * (w - p.b)
        Sx = w + pot + dep + rel; G2[(i, j)] = min(p.hi, max(p.lo, Sx))
        contrib[(i, j)] = G2[(i, j)] - w; parts[(i, j)] = {'pot': pot, 'dep': dep, 'rel': rel, 'clamp': Sx != G2[(i, j)]}
    return {'G': G2, 'v': v2}, {'s': s, 'I': I, 'vt': vt, 'g': g, 'c': contrib, 'parts': parts, 'G_read': G}

# ======================= 一、𝒦_g 成员资格 =======================
def kg_step(q, o, s, I):
    """《候选宿载场具化判定式》定义 2 的独立实现（字面顺序）。q：成员参数；o = (w, v, x, y)。"""
    w, v, x, y = o; n_in, n_out = q['n_in'], q['n_out']
    vt = [q['lam'][j] * v[j] + sum(w[(i, j)] * s[i] for i in range(n_in)) + I[j] for j in range(n_out)]
    g = [1 if vt[j] >= q['th'][j] else 0 for j in range(n_out)]
    v2 = [q['vr'][j] if g[j] else vt[j] for j in range(n_out)]
    x2 = [q['dx'][i] * x[i] + s[i] for i in range(n_in)]; y2 = [q['dy'][j] * y[j] + g[j] for j in range(n_out)]
    pl, bap = q['plastic'], q['bap']; w2 = {}
    for (i, j), wij in w.items():
        Pp = q['Ap'] * x2[i] if (pl and bap and g[j]) else Fr(0)
        Pm = -q['Am'] * y2[j] if (pl and bap and s[i]) else Fr(0)
        Pr = -q['rho'] * (wij - q['b'][(i, j)]) if pl else Fr(0)
        w2[(i, j)] = min(q['hi'][(i, j)], max(q['lo'][(i, j)], wij + Pp + Pm + Pr))
    return (w2, v2, x2, y2), g
def as_kg(p, bp=1):
    """申报物理参数 → 𝒦_g 成员参数（映射：d_x = d_y = 0，A₊ = α，A₋ = 0，ρ 同，θ、λ 同，v_reset = 0，plastic = 1，bap = bp）。
    映射成立的条件（族条件）：V_pre < V_set，bp·V_post < V_set，V_pre + bp·V_post ≥ V_set（只在共现时增强）；脉冲非负（复位支不触发）。"""
    X = [(i, j) for i in range(p.n_in) for j in range(p.n_out)]
    return {'n_in': p.n_in, 'n_out': p.n_out, 'lam': [p.lam] * p.n_out, 'th': [p.th] * p.n_out, 'vr': [Fr(0)] * p.n_out,
            'dx': [Fr(0)] * p.n_in, 'dy': [Fr(0)] * p.n_out, 'Ap': p.alpha, 'Am': Fr(0), 'rho': p.rho, 'plastic': True, 'bap': bool(bp),
            'b': {k: p.b for k in X}, 'lo': {k: p.lo for k in X}, 'hi': {k: p.hi for k in X}}
def fam_cond(p, bp=1):
    """族条件：V_pre < V_set，V_post < V_set；bp = 1 时 V_pre + V_post ≥ V_set（共现达阈）；bp = 0 时增强分项恒零。"""
    return p.Vpre < p.Vset and p.Vpost < p.Vset and (bp == 0 or p.Vpre + p.Vpost >= p.Vset)
def compare(p, hist, bp=1):
    q = as_kg(p, bp); st = init(p); o = (dict(st['G']), list(st['v']), [Fr(0)] * p.n_in, [Fr(0)] * p.n_out); bad = clamp = eq = 0
    for letter, I in hist:
        st, rec = phys_step(p, st, letter, I, bp=bp); o, g = kg_step(q, o, PAT[letter], [Fr(x) for x in I])
        if st['G'] != o[0] or st['v'] != o[1] or rec['g'] != g: bad += 1
        clamp += sum(1 for k in rec['parts'] if rec['parts'][k]['clamp']); eq += sum(1 for j in range(p.n_out) if rec['vt'][j] == p.th)
    return bad, clamp, eq
rng = random.Random(20261111); n_beats = n_bad = n_clamp = n_eq = n_hist = 0
FAM = dict(lam=[Fr(0), Fr(1, 2), Fr(3, 4)], th=[Fr(1), Fr(3, 2)], alpha=[Fr(1, 8), Fr(1, 4), Fr(3, 8)], rho=[Fr(0), Fr(1, 16), Fr(1, 8)], b=[Fr(1, 4), Fr(1, 2)])
for _ in range(400):
    kw = {k: rng.choice(v) for k, v in FAM.items()}; n_out = rng.choice([1, 2]); p = Prm(n_out=n_out, **kw); bp = rng.choice([0, 1])
    L = rng.randint(4, 16); hist = [(rng.choice(list(PAT)), [rng.choice([0, Fr(1, 2), 1]) for _ in range(n_out)]) for _ in range(L)]
    bad, cl, eq = compare(p, hist, bp); n_hist += 1; n_beats += L; n_bad += bad; n_clamp += cl; n_eq += eq
R['一 𝒦_g 成员资格（申报族内随机史，物理形 vs 定义 2 独立实现；逐拍比较 G、v 与发放，x′、y′ 为映射后资格迹）'] = {'史数': n_hist, '拍数': n_beats, '不一致拍': n_bad, '夹限生效处': n_clamp, '积分恰等于阈值处': n_eq}
# 越出族：V_pre ≥ V_set（单有输入即增强，增强不再以发放为条件）
p_out = Prm(Vpre=Fr(1)); hist = [('A', [0]), ('A', [0]), ('none', [0])]
R['一′ 越出所给充分条件（V_pre = 1 ≥ V_set）'] = {'充分条件': fam_cond(p_out), '与本段映射（定义 2 一般形）逐拍不一致数': compare(p_out, hist)[0],
    '判词': '映射边界：未发放时单有行脉冲即可增强，不满足本段的发放门控映射；判定路径未覆盖（不断言不属 𝒦_g；不读作 0）'}
p_b0 = Prm(Vpost=Fr(1)); rng0 = random.Random(7); hist0 = [(rng0.choice(list(PAT)), [rng0.choice([0, Fr(1, 2), 1])]) for _ in range(40)]
R['一″ 越出所给充分条件而仍可映射（bp = 0，V_pre = 1/2，V_post = V_set = 1）'] = {'充分条件（按 bp = 1 读）': fam_cond(p_b0, 1), '充分条件（bp = 0）': fam_cond(p_b0, 0),
    '按 bap = 0 映射逐拍不一致数（40 拍）': compare(p_b0, hist0, bp=0)[0], '说明': 'bp·V_post·g 恒为零，V = V_pre·s < V_set，PLUS 恒零，与定义 2 的 bap = 0 相同'}

# ======================= 二、规则级 P10 =======================
with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp, contextlib.redirect_stdout(io.StringIO()):
    cwd = os.getcwd(); os.chdir(tmp); sys.argv = ['full', os.path.join(tmp, 'o.json')]
    X = runpy.run_path(os.path.join(HERE, 'shiyin_full.py')); os.chdir(cwd); sys.argv = ARGV
Mx, RunX, E, MechC, versions_of, vanc_at, version_path = (X[k] for k in ('Mx', 'RunX', 'E', 'MechC', 'versions_of', 'vanc_at', 'version_path'))
P0 = Prm()
GD = ('pred', lambda x: P0.lo <= x <= P0.hi, [Fr(j, 32) for j in range(0, 65)])
NUM = ('pred', lambda x: True, [Fr(j, 8) for j in range(-8, 33)])
B01 = ('fin', {Fr(0), Fr(1)})
ROUTES = {'integ', 'fire', 'dev', 'port:In'}
def mx(decl=True):
    lay = {'G00': 'theta', 'G10': 'theta', 'v': 'r', 'vt': 'r', 'g': 'r', 's0': 'r', 's1': 'r', 'I': 'r'}
    dom = {'G00': GD, 'G10': GD, 'v': NUM, 'vt': NUM, 'g': B01, 's0': B01, 's1': B01, 'I': ('fin', {Fr(0), Fr(1, 2), Fr(1)})}
    FM = {'integ': ('Run', {'vt'}), 'fire': ('Run', {'g', 'v'}), 'dev': ('Sed', {'G00', 'G10'})}
    M = Mx(lay, dom, (lambda s: s['G00'] + s['G10'], {'G00': 1, 'G10': 1}), ports={'In': {'s0', 's1', 'I'}}, FM=FM, fid='𝔐_R')
    if decl: M.mech_routes = set(ROUTES); M.mech_exhaustive = True
    return M
def st0(): return {'G00': P0.b, 'G10': P0.b, 'v': Fr(0), 'vt': Fr(0), 'g': Fr(0), 's0': Fr(0), 's1': Fr(0), 'I': Fr(0)}
def dev_fn(i, p=P0):
    def f(v):
        w = v[f'G{i}0']; V = p.Vpre * v[f's{i}'] + p.Vpost * v['g']
        Sx = w + (p.alpha if V >= p.Vset else 0) - (p.beta if V <= -p.Vrst else 0) - p.rho * (w - p.b)
        return {f'G{i}0': min(p.hi, max(p.lo, Sx))}
    return f
def steps_for(k, letter, I):
    s = PAT[letter]
    return [((k, 1), E(f'in{k}', 'In', 'port', 'env', [], ['s0', 's1', 'I'], lambda v, s=s, I=I: {'s0': Fr(s[0]), 's1': Fr(s[1]), 'I': Fr(I)})),
            ((k, 2), E(f'integ{k}', 'Run', 'integ', 'F', ['v', 'G00', 'G10', 's0', 's1', 'I'], ['vt'],
                       lambda v: {'vt': P0.lam * v['v'] + v['G00'] * v['s0'] + v['G10'] * v['s1'] + v['I']}, {'G00': 1, 'G10': 1, 'v': 1, 's0': 1, 's1': 1, 'I': 1})),
            ((k, 3), E(f'fire{k}', 'Run', 'fire', 'F', ['vt'], ['g', 'v'], lambda v: {'g': Fr(1), 'v': Fr(0)} if v['vt'] >= P0.th else {'g': Fr(0), 'v': v['vt']})),
            ((k, 4), E(f'dev0_{k}', 'Sed', 'dev', 'F', ['G00', 's0', 'g'], ['G00'], dev_fn(0))),
            ((k, 5), E(f'dev1_{k}', 'Sed', 'dev', 'F', ['G10', 's1', 'g'], ['G10'], dev_fn(1)))]
TEACH = sum((steps_for(k, 'A', 1) for k in range(1, 5)), [])
R['二 规则级 P10（教学协议：(A + 教学电流 1) × 4 重放；申报路线穷尽）'] = MechC(lambda: RunX(mx(), st0()), {'教学协议': TEACH})
R['二′ 机制未申报时'] = MechC(lambda: RunX(mx(False), st0()), {'教学协议': TEACH})

# ======================= 三、主实例：实际窗分证 =======================
FORM = [('A', [1])] * 4
WIN = [('A', [0]), ('A', [0]), ('none', [0])] * 2
def run(p, hist, bp_of=lambda k: 1, writes=None):
    """writes：{拍下标 k: {位: 值}}——施作者直写，作为第 k 拍之前的拍间实际状态转换；tr[k] 即写后态，实际与诊断分支都读它。"""
    writes = writes or {}; st = init(p); tr = [st]; recs = []
    for k, (letter, I) in enumerate(hist):
        if k in writes: st = {'G': {**st['G'], **writes[k]}, 'v': list(st['v'])}; tr[k] = st
        st, rec = phys_step(p, st, letter, I, bp=bp_of(k)); tr.append(st); recs.append(rec)
    return tr, recs
def subsets(X): return [list(c) for n in range(1, len(X) + 1) for c in itertools.combinations(X, n)]
def LOC(p): return [Fr(j, 4) for j in range(0, 9) if p.b <= Fr(j, 4) <= p.hi]   # 𝒟₁ 单拍设定：LOC 合法域 [b, hi] 上 1/4 格点
def judge(p, hist, w0, bp_of=lambda k: 1, writes=None):
    """hist 中下标 ≥ w0 的拍为窗 W。逐 T 判 TraceSet（q、r）、Part、HDyn；Fld = ∃T 三项同真。"""
    tr, recs = run(p, hist, bp_of, writes); W = range(w0, len(hist)); X = sorted(tr[0]['G'])
    res = {}
    for T in subsets(X):
        key = '{' + ', '.join(f'G{i}{j}' for i, j in T) + '}'
        q = all(tr[k + 1]['G'][x] != p.b for k in W for x in T) and all(tr[w0]['G'][x] != p.b for x in T)
        r_w = []
        for k in range(0, w0):                                   # 形成史 PS 认证：实际 PLUS ≠ 0；同拍撤除入场分量后 PLUS = 0，且不新增放电（M1₀）；净增量另报
            for x in T:
                if recs[k]['parts'][x]['pot'] == 0: continue
                for drop in ('letter', 'I'):
                    _, alt = phys_step(p, tr[k], *hist[k], bp=bp_of(k), drop=drop)
                    if alt['parts'][x]['pot'] == 0 and all(a_ <= b_ for a_, b_ in zip(alt['g'], recs[k]['g'])):
                        r_w.append({'拍': k, '位': f'G{x[0]}{x[1]}', '撤除': f'撤{drop}', 'PLUS 实/撤': (S(recs[k]['parts'][x]['pot']), S(alt['parts'][x]['pot'])),
                                    '净增量 实/撤': (S(recs[k]['c'][x]), S(alt['c'][x])), '撤后放电': alt['g']}); break
        part_w, hdyn_w, hself = None, None, None
        for k in W:
            for x in T:
                for val in LOC(p):
                    if val == tr[k]['G'][x]: continue
                    _, alt = phys_step(p, tr[k], *hist[k], bp=bp_of(k), setG={x: val})
                    if part_w is None and recs[k]['s'][x[0]] and (alt['vt'] != recs[k]['vt'] or alt['g'] != recs[k]['g']):
                        part_w = {'拍': k, '设定': f'G{x[0]}{x[1]} := {val}', '积分 实/设': (S(recs[k]['vt'][0]), S(alt['vt'][0])), '发放 实/设': (recs[k]['g'], alt['g'])}
                    if hdyn_w is None:
                        for y in T:
                            if recs[k]['c'][y] == 0: continue
                            via = 'pot' if alt['parts'][y]['pot'] != recs[k]['parts'][y]['pot'] else ('rel' if alt['parts'][y]['rel'] != recs[k]['parts'][y]['rel'] else None)
                            if via == 'rel' and x == y and hself is None:
                                hself = {'拍': k, '作用位': f'G{y[0]}{y[1]}', '实际分项 c': S(recs[k]['c'][y]), '设定': f'G{x[0]}{x[1]} := {val}'}
                            if via and (x != y or via == 'pot'):
                                hdyn_w = {'拍': k, '作用位': f'G{y[0]}{y[1]}', '实际分项 c': S(recs[k]['c'][y]), '分项构成': {kk: S(vv) for kk, vv in recs[k]['parts'][y].items()},
                                          '设定': f'G{x[0]}{x[1]} := {val}', '设定下分项构成': {kk: S(vv) for kk, vv in alt['parts'][y].items()},
                                          '途径': '界内：G → 积分 → 发放 → 器件电压 → ΔG' if via == 'pot' else '器件自弛豫（读本位 G）'}
                                break
        ts = q and bool(r_w)
        res[key] = {'TraceSet': ts, 'q（W 内留存非零）': q, 'r 见证（PS：PLUS 分项；净增量另报）': r_w[:3], 'Part': part_w is not None, 'Part 见证': part_w,
                    'HDyn': hdyn_w is not None, 'HDyn 见证': hdyn_w, '三项同真': ts and part_w is not None and hdyn_w is not None,
                    '只经自弛豫的作用（读法未定，不计入 HDyn）': hself if hdyn_w is None else None}
    fld = any(v['三项同真'] for v in res.values())
    selfonly = (not fld) and any(v['TraceSet'] and v['Part'] and v['只经自弛豫的作用（读法未定，不计入 HDyn）'] for v in res.values())
    G_end = lambda k, x: recs[k]['G_read'][x] + recs[k]['c'][x]               # 拍末值（不含其后拍间直写）
    traj = [(k, hist[k][0], S(hist[k][1][0]), recs[k]['g'][0], S(G_end(k, (0, 0))), S(G_end(k, (1, 0)))) for k in range(len(hist))]
    return {'轨迹（拍, 字母, 教学电流, 发放, G00, G10）': traj, '拍间直写（写前 → 写后）': {k: {f'G{x[0]}{x[1]}': (S(G_end(k - 1, x)), S(v)) for x, v in w_.items()} for k, w_ in (writes or {}).items()}, '逐 T': res, 'Fld': 1 if fld else ('⊥' if selfonly else 0)}
J = judge(P0, FORM + WIN, len(FORM))
J['判词'] = '1（模型模式；申报协议与已认证见证）' if J['Fld'] else '0 须 𝔅₀ 前件'
R['三 主实例（易失 ρ = 1/8；形成 (A + 1) × 4；W = (A, A, 静) × 2）'] = J

# ======================= 四、负例与边界 =======================
B0 = '𝔅₀ 前件：𝒟₁ 申报、机制清单申报穷尽、记录忠实（模型模式〔构〕）；负判由解析论证承担，有限格点核算只作一致性核对'
J1 = judge(P0, [('A', [0])] * 4 + WIN, 4)
J1['判词'] = ('1' if J1['Fld'] else '0（' + B0 + '）'); J1['解析'] = '无教学电流时 wA = b 下 v < θ 恒成立，从不发放，PLUS 恒零；G = b 时弛豫为零：全程无结构位偏离基线，T_max = ∅，TraceSet 对一切 T 不成立'
R['四.1 无形成（无教学电流）'] = J1
P_nv = Prm(rho=Fr(0))
J2 = judge(P_nv, FORM + WIN, len(FORM), bp_of=lambda k: 1 if k < len(FORM) else 0)
J2['判词'] = ('1' if J2['Fld'] else '0（' + B0 + '）'); J2['解析'] = 'W 内 bp = 0：器件电压 ≤ V_pre = 1/2 < V_set，PLUS 恒零；ρ = 0：弛豫为零；复位支不触发：W 内一切结构位无有效事件，HDyn 对一切 T 不成立'
R['四.2 非易失（ρ = 0）只读窗（W 内关闭回传脉冲，器件电压 ≤ 1/2 < V_set）'] = J2
J3 = judge(P0, FORM + WIN, len(FORM), bp_of=lambda k: 1 if k < len(FORM) else 0)
J3['判词'] = {1: '1', 0: '0（' + B0 + '）', '⊥': '⊥（W 内作用于 T 的只有器件自弛豫：读本位迹应的弛豫是否承担 HDyn 的出自迹应之作用，本核算不定读法；不读作 0）'}[J3['Fld']]
R['四.3 易失（ρ = 1/8）只读窗'] = J3

R['五 真实材料'] = {'判词': '⊥', '原因': ['模型判词只判申报模型 𝔐_R（算法层）；物质宿载的读回需实施保真与模型如实的对应物（《候选宿载场具化判定式》5.5），未给',
                    '《物质场具化判定式》已登记：忆阻纳米线网络「复位网络等同原始网络」的前提待验证；本核算不给物质观测']}

def fix(o):
    if isinstance(o, dict): return {str(k): fix(v) for k, v in o.items()}
    if isinstance(o, (list, tuple)): return [fix(x) for x in o]
    if isinstance(o, Fr): return str(o)
    return o
json.dump(fix(R), open(os.path.join(HERE, 'memr_bounds.json'), 'w'), ensure_ascii=False, indent=1, sort_keys=True, default=str)
print(json.dumps(fix(R), ensure_ascii=False, indent=1, default=str))
~~~
