~~~python
"""一般化结构起势构造式 · 核算（纯 Python，精确有理，确定性）
宿主、读出、写入律、资格区间与申报史族 𝓗 同第二十（source_checks，前缀执行）。
预算表含「恢复对齐」列；网格分类用正文编号（命题 6、推论 5），越阈组细分申报域内／κ = 0 边界／u0 > 2 外域。
构造程序 prime(来源证书, 新场入口, 分支, 预算)：
  0 申报域：新场入口 = E0；分支 ∈ {全量, 成员}；预算为非负整数；否则拒绝。
  1 来源核验：来源证书 = (旧场入口, 操作序列)；入口须属 𝓗 的 τ = (1,1,0) 入口集，操作须属 𝓗 的合法操作集；
    由入口重放操作序列求旧场 Hist，再求 Σ_mem(1,1,0) ∧ 对齐；不成立则拒绝。
  2 新场条件：R1、R2、R4、单输入正确放电、κ > 0。
  3 取值：全量——u0 := 旧场重放所得 u；成员——u0 := θ_D(N)；u0 须属预搭端口许可值域 U_pre = {k/4：k = 0..8}，
    全量支另核 u0 > θ_D(N)（在来源域内恒真，防御检查）。
  4 预搭：第 1 拍前一次直写 u := u0。
  5 形成与承担段：全量——AB、A、B；成员——m* 个 AB、A、B。
  6 扰动：固定许可扰动 u := 1/2（第二十三 Δ3 的一点）。
  7 续行与验证：预算 N_b 计扰动后的全部实际拍（含验证拍）。未对齐则 AB；对齐而未达 Σ_mem 则 A、B 交替；
    达对齐 ∧ Σ_mem 后执行一个验证 AB 拍。验证拍执行完且拍末对齐 ∧ Σ_mem、实际窗判 1 → 完成；预算用尽即停 → 超预算。
  8 实际窗：扰动后已执行的实际拍；TraceSet、HDyn、Part 由实际事件的拍前、拍后状态求得（诊断替代值只在拍前状态的副本上算），
    留存为同一位 u 自 TraceSet 拍至窗末不回基线；实际窗判 = 三项 ∧ 留存。
"""
import os, json, contextlib, io, itertools
from fractions import Fraction as Fr
HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
def prefix(script, stop):
    p = os.path.join(HERE, script); src = open(p, encoding='utf-8').read().split(stop)[0]; sc = {'__file__': p}
    with contextlib.redirect_stdout(io.StringIO()): exec(compile(src, p, 'exec'), sc)
    return sc
sc = prefix('source_checks.py', '# ---- 起点与默认来源的排除')
Hist, aligned, inSigma, z, zI, v, beat, OPS, ent = (sc[k] for k in ('Hist', 'aligned', 'inSigma', 'z', 'zI', 'v', 'beat', 'OPS', 'ent'))
S = str; R = {}
TAU = {'A': 1, 'B': 1, 'AB': 0}
E0 = {'wA': Fr(1), 'wB': Fr(1), 'aA': Fr(1, 2), 'aB': Fr(1, 2), 'u': Fr(0), 'kap': Fr(1, 2)}
H_ENTRIES = [ent(Fr(0)), ent(Fr(1, 2))]          # 𝓗：τ = (1,1,0) 的入口集
H_OPS = OPS                                        # 𝓗：合法操作集
U_PRE = [Fr(k, 4) for k in range(9)]               # 预搭端口许可值域
PERTURB = Fr(1, 2)
GRID = [Fr(j, 4) for j in range(9)]                # 诊断设定域
def readout(st): return tuple(z(st, s) for s in ('A', 'B', 'AB'))
def sect(h): return {'读出': readout(h.st), '对齐': aligned(h.st, TAU), 'Σ': inSigma(h.st, TAU), 'mem(u)': h.mem('u'),
                     'Σ_mem': inSigma(h.st, TAU) and h.mem('u'), 'u': S(h.st['u']), 'u 区间（形成拍, 起点）': h.form.get('u')}
def act(h, o, tag):
    st = dict(h.st); h.op(o)
    if o[0] == 'b':
        return {'拍': tag, '事件': f'{o[1]}(t={o[2]})', '募集 z_I': zI(st, o[1]), '膜驱动（读拍前 u = ' + S(st['u']) + '）': S(v(st, o[1])),
                '放电': z(st, o[1]), 'Δu': S(h.st['u'] - st['u']), '_pre': st, '_post': dict(h.st), '_o': o}
    return {'拍': tag, '事件': f'直写 {o[1]} := {o[2]}', '写前': S(st[o[1]]), '写后': S(h.st[o[1]])}
def window(evs):
    """实际窗：evs 为扰动后已执行的实际拍事件（含拍前、拍后状态）。"""
    tr = part = hd = None
    for e in evs:
        st, post, s_ = e['_pre'], e['_post'], e['_o'][1]; du = post['u'] - st['u']
        if tr is None:
            if du != 0 and s_ == 'AB' and all(beat(st, x, 0)['u'] == st['u'] for x in ('A', 'B')):
                tr = {'拍': e['拍'], 'Δu': S(du), '诊断（同拍前状态改输入 A／B）': 'Δu = 0'}
            continue
        if hd is None and du != 0:
            for c in GRID:
                if c != st['u'] and beat({**st, 'u': c}, s_, 0)['u'] - c != du:
                    hd = {'拍': e['拍'], 'Δu': S(du), '诊断设定': f'u := {c}', '诊断下 Δu': S(beat({**st, 'u': c}, s_, 0)['u'] - c)}; break
        if part is None and s_ == 'AB' and z({**st, 'u': Fr(0)}, s_) != z(st, s_):
            part = {'拍': e['拍'], '放电 实/诊断 u := 0': (z(st, s_), z({**st, 'u': Fr(0)}, s_))}
    us = [(e['拍'], S(e['_post']['u'])) for e in evs]
    keep = bool(tr) and all(e['_post']['u'] != E0['u'] for e in evs if e['拍'] >= tr['拍'])
    return {'TraceSet': tr, 'HDyn': hd, 'Part': part, '窗内 u（拍末）': us,
            '留存（位 u，自 TraceSet 拍至窗末不回基线 0）': keep, '实际窗判': 1 if (tr and hd and part and keep) else '未交齐'}
def replay(cert):
    entry, ops = cert; h = Hist(dict(entry))
    for o in ops: h.op(o)
    return h
def prime(cert, entry, branch, budget=12):
    rec = {'分支': branch}
    if entry != E0: return {**rec, '结果': '拒绝：新场入口不在申报域（申报入口 E0）'}
    if branch not in ('全量', '成员'): return {**rec, '结果': '拒绝：分支未申报'}
    if not (isinstance(budget, int) and not isinstance(budget, bool) and budget >= 0): return {**rec, '结果': f'拒绝：预算 {budget!r} 不在申报域（非负整数）'}
    oe, ops = cert
    if oe not in H_ENTRIES: return {**rec, '结果': '拒绝：旧场入口不属申报史族'}
    if any(o not in H_OPS for o in ops): return {**rec, '结果': '拒绝：旧场操作不属申报史族的合法操作集'}
    old = replay(cert)
    src = {'旧场入口 κ': S(oe['kap']), '旧场操作序列': [' '.join(map(str, o)) for o in ops], '重放 Σ_mem(1,1,0)': inSigma(old.st, TAU) and old.mem('u'),
           '重放 对齐': aligned(old.st, TAU), '重放 u': S(old.st['u']), '重放 u 区间（形成拍, 起点, κ 来源）': old.form.get('u'), '重放 w': (S(old.st['wA']), S(old.st['wB']))}
    rec['来源核验'] = src
    if not (src['重放 Σ_mem(1,1,0)'] and src['重放 对齐']): return {**rec, '结果': '拒绝：来源资格不成立'}
    cond = {'R1': zI(entry, 'AB') == 1, 'R2': zI(entry, 'A') == 0 and zI(entry, 'B') == 0, 'R4': entry['wA'] > 0 and entry['wB'] > 0,
            '单输入正确放电': entry['wA'] >= 1 and entry['wB'] >= 1, 'κ > 0': entry['kap'] > 0}
    rec['新场条件'] = cond
    if not all(cond.values()): return {**rec, '结果': '拒绝：新场条件不成立'}
    th = entry['wA'] + entry['wB'] - 1
    if branch == '全量':
        u0 = old.st['u']
        if not u0 > th: return {**rec, '结果': f'拒绝：全量写值 {u0} 不大于 θ_D = {th}'}
        why = f'旧场重放 u = {u0}（> θ_D = {th}）'; form = [('b', 'AB', 0), ('b', 'A', 0), ('b', 'B', 0)]
    else:
        u0 = th; why = f'θ_D = w_A + w_B − 1 = {th}（新场入口）'
        m = (th - u0) // entry['kap'] + 1; form = [('b', 'AB', 0)] * int(m) + [('b', 'A', 0), ('b', 'B', 0)]
    if u0 not in U_PRE: return {**rec, '结果': f'拒绝：写值 {u0} 不在预搭端口许可值域'}
    h = Hist(dict(entry)); secs = [('预搭前入口', sect(h))]
    ev = [act(h, ('r', 'u', u0), '预搭')]; secs.append(('预搭后入口', sect(h)))
    rec['预搭'] = {'源值': S(old.st['u']), '写值': S(u0), '派生依据': why, '许可值域': 'U_pre = {k/4：k = 0..8}'}
    rec['入口诊断（预搭后、拍前计算的 AB 膜驱动；非实际事件）'] = S(v(h.st, 'AB'))
    for i, o in enumerate(form, 1): ev.append(act(h, o, f'形成第 {i} 拍')); secs.append((f'形成与承担段第 {i} 拍末（{o[1]}）', sect(h)))
    ev.append(act(h, ('r', 'u', PERTURB), '扰动')); secs.append((f'扰动 u := {PERTURB} 后', sect(h)))
    post = []; k = 0; verified = False; status = '超预算'
    while k < budget:
        k += 1; reached = aligned(h.st, TAU) and inSigma(h.st, TAU) and h.mem('u')
        o = ('b', 'AB', 0) if (reached or not aligned(h.st, TAU)) else (('b', 'A', 0) if k % 2 else ('b', 'B', 0))
        e = act(h, o, k); ev.append(e); post.append(e); secs.append((f'续行第 {k} 拍末（{o[1]}{"，验证拍" if reached else ""}）', sect(h)))
        if reached: verified = True; break
    w = window(post)
    if verified and aligned(h.st, TAU) and inSigma(h.st, TAU) and h.mem('u') and w['实际窗判'] == 1: status = '完成'
    first = lambda key, xs: next((n for n, x in xs if x[key]), None)
    pre_secs = [x for x in secs if x[0].startswith(('预搭', '形成'))]
    psecs = [x for x in secs if x[0].startswith('续行')]
    cut = lambda e: {k_: x for k_, x in e.items() if not k_.startswith('_')}
    had = old_interval = h_before_interval = None
    return {**rec, '实际事件（逐项）': [cut(e) for e in ev], '截面': secs,
            '扰动前：首次对齐': first('对齐', pre_secs), '扰动前：首次 Σ_mem': first('Σ_mem', pre_secs),
            '扰动后：失配': not secs[[n for n, _ in secs].index(next(n for n, _ in secs if n.startswith('扰动')))][1]['对齐'],
            '扰动后：首次恢复对齐': first('对齐', psecs), '扰动后：首次满足 Σ_mem': first('Σ_mem', psecs),
            '扰动后续行事件（实际）': [x['事件'] for x in post], '验证拍已执行': verified,
            '实际窗': w, '结果': status}
def interval_reading(r):
    """按截面记录读出 Σ_mem 的区间来源：扰动后 u 是否仍有区间。"""
    pert = next(x for n, x in r['截面'] if n.startswith('扰动'))
    return '原区间延续，扰动后重新满足留存' if pert['u 区间（形成拍, 起点）'] else '扰动后由场自身沉积开启新区间（首次形成）'
# ---- 主实例（来源证书）
S1 = (dict(E0), [('b', 'AB', 0)] * 3 + [('b', 'A', 0), ('b', 'B', 0)])
O2 = (dict(E0), S1[1] + [('r', 'u', Fr(2)), ('b', 'A', 0), ('b', 'B', 0)])
R['一 主实例'] = {}
for nm, c in (('旧场 S1（u = 3/2）', S1), ('旧场 S1 后区间内直写加深至 2 并留存（u = 2）', O2)):
    for br in ('全量', '成员'):
        r = prime(c, E0, br); r['Σ_mem 读法（由截面）'] = interval_reading(r); R['一 主实例'][f'{nm}，{br}'] = r
# ---- 预算阈值
R['二 预算 0—6'] = {f'{br} 预算 {b}': {'结果': (r := prime(S1, E0, br, b))['结果'], '续行实际事件': r['扰动后续行事件（实际）'],
                     '实际窗判': r['实际窗']['实际窗判'], 'TraceSet/HDyn/Part 拍': tuple((r['实际窗'][x] or {}).get('拍') for x in ('TraceSet', 'HDyn', 'Part')),
                     '扰动后首次恢复对齐': r['扰动后：首次恢复对齐'], '扰动后首次 Σ_mem': r['扰动后：首次满足 Σ_mem']} for br in ('全量', '成员') for b in range(7)}
# ---- 拒绝
wide = ({**E0, 'wA': Fr(2), 'wB': Fr(2), 'kap': Fr(1)}, [('b', 'AB', 0)] * 4 + [('b', 'A', 0), ('b', 'B', 0)])
R['三 拒绝'] = {
    '预算缺省（取 12）': prime(S1, E0, '全量')['结果'],
    '旧场直写 u := 3/2 后 A、B（无资格区间）': prime((dict(E0), [('r', 'u', Fr(3, 2)), ('b', 'A', 0), ('b', 'B', 0)]), E0, '全量')['结果'],
    '旧场入口 w = (2,2)、κ = 1、AB 四拍、A、B（申报史族外）': prime(wide, E0, '全量')['结果'],
    '旧场操作含直写 u := 4（合法操作集外）': prime((dict(E0), S1[1] + [('r', 'u', Fr(4)), ('b', 'A', 0), ('b', 'B', 0)]), E0, '全量')['结果'],
    '新场入口违 R2（a = (1,1)）': prime(S1, {**E0, 'aA': Fr(1), 'aB': Fr(1)}, '全量')['结果'],
    '新场入口 w_A = 2': prime(S1, {**E0, 'wA': Fr(2)}, '全量')['结果'],
    '新场入口 κ = 1/32': prime(S1, {**E0, 'kap': Fr(1, 32)}, '成员')['结果'],
    '分支未申报': prime(S1, E0, '其他')['结果'],
    '预算 None': prime(S1, E0, '全量', None)['结果'],
    '预算 7/2': prime(S1, E0, '全量', Fr(7, 2))['结果'],
    '预算 −1': prime(S1, E0, '全量', -1)['结果']}
# ---- 申报史族内全部合格来源（长度 ≤ 6）
qual = {}; tot = 0; vals = set(); same = True; ref = None
for oe in H_ENTRIES:
    for n in range(7):
        for ops in itertools.product(H_OPS, repeat=n):
            tot += 1; h = replay((oe, list(ops))); vals.add(h.st['u'])
            if not (inSigma(h.st, TAU) and h.mem('u') and aligned(h.st, TAU)): continue
            for br in ('全量', '成员'):
                r = prime((oe, list(ops)), E0, br, 5)
                key = (br, S(h.st['u']), r['结果'], tuple(r['扰动后续行事件（实际）']), r['扰动后：首次恢复对齐'], r['扰动后：首次满足 Σ_mem'], r['实际窗']['实际窗判'])
                qual[key] = qual.get(key, 0) + 1
                tail = [{k_: x for k_, x in e.items() if k_ != '拍'} for e in r['实际事件（逐项）'] if isinstance(e['拍'], int)]
                ref = ref or tail; same = same and tail == ref
R['四 申报史族内合格来源（长度 ≤ 6，两入口）'] = {'序列总数': tot, '可达 u 值': sorted(S(x) for x in vals),
    '合格来源 × 分支 → (分支, 旧 u, 结果, 续行事件, 恢复拍, Σ_mem 拍, 实际窗判)：计数': {' | '.join(map(str, k)): c for k, c in sorted(qual.items(), key=str)},
    '扰动后实际事件在全部合格来源与两支上逐项相同': same}
# ---- 自形成基线
h = Hist(dict(E0)); base = []
for i, o in enumerate([('b', 'AB', 0)] * 3 + [('b', 'A', 0), ('b', 'B', 0)], 1):
    u_ = h.st['u']; h.op(o); base.append((i, o[1], sect(h), h.st['u'] != u_))
R['五 自形成基线'] = {'首次对齐拍': next(i for i, _, x, _ in base if x['对齐']), '首次 Σ_mem 拍': next(i for i, _, x, _ in base if x['Σ_mem']), '形成写入': sum(w for *_, w in base)}
# ---- 闭式网格（命题 2）
grid = []; bad = 0
for j in range(25):
    for kap in [Fr(0), Fr(1, 32), Fr(1, 16), Fr(1, 8), Fr(1, 4), Fr(1, 2), Fr(1)]:
        u0 = Fr(j, 8); h = Hist({**E0, 'kap': kap}); h.op(('r', 'u', u0))
        fa = 0 if aligned(h.st, TAU) else None; fm = None; wr = 0; nab = 0
        for step in range(1, 36):
            o = ('b', 'AB', 0) if fa is None else (('b', 'A', 0) if step % 2 else ('b', 'B', 0))
            p = h.st['u']; h.op(o); wr += p != h.st['u']
            if fa is None and aligned(h.st, TAU): fa = step
            if inSigma(h.st, TAU) and h.mem('u'): fm = step; break
        cls = (('越阈·推论 5 申报域内' if (kap > 0 and u0 <= 2) else ('越阈·κ = 0 边界' if kap == 0 else '越阈·u0 > 2 外域')) if u0 > 1 else ('κ = 0 边界（u0 ≤ 1）' if kap == 0 else '命题 6'))
        pred = (0, None, 0) if u0 > 1 else ((None, None, 0) if kap == 0 else ((m := int((1 - u0) // kap) + 1), m + 2, m))
        ok = (fa, fm, wr) == pred; bad += not ok
        grid.append({'u0': S(u0), 'κ': S(kap), '类': cls, '首次对齐拍': fa, '首次 Σ_mem 拍': fm, '形成写入': wr, '闭式': pred, '相符': ok})
R['六 闭式网格'] = {'点数': len(grid), '不符': bad, '分类计数': {c: sum(g['类'] == c for g in grid) for c in ('命题 6', 'κ = 0 边界（u0 ≤ 1）', '越阈·推论 5 申报域内', '越阈·κ = 0 边界', '越阈·u0 > 2 外域')}, '逐点': grid}
def fix(o):
    if isinstance(o, dict): return {str(k): fix(x) for k, x in o.items()}
    if isinstance(o, (list, tuple)): return [fix(x) for x in o]
    if isinstance(o, Fr): return str(o)
    return o
json.dump(fix(R), open(os.path.join(HERE, 'struct_prime.json'), 'w'), ensure_ascii=False, indent=1, sort_keys=True, default=str)
print(json.dumps(fix({k: x for k, x in R.items() if k not in ('六 闭式网格',)}), ensure_ascii=False, indent=1, default=str)[:20000])
print('grid', R['六 闭式网格']['点数'], R['六 闭式网格']['不符'], R['六 闭式网格']['分类计数'])
~~~

~~~python
"""脉冲沉积场：学时调谐经抑与去抑、反复流过而间接冲出沟（固化即沉积），用时收束不固化
基底：24 频道；入口通路 w 与频道间沟线 W 同一可塑规则；零和反馈抑制 H = 0.1，无延迟；每内容学 20 次。
不加调谐场可塑性，不写固化规则。
学时输入带杂散：内容频道放电率 0.5；其两侧 ±1 频道 0.5、±2 频道 0.3（按 4 种杂散式样循环，模拟每次呈现的差异，水往两侧乱流）。
学时调谐（瞩力）：内容频道以外的频道每拍受抑 1.0（加高两侧），内容频道不抑（去抑）。
参数：杂散强到能使场元实际放电、能沉积；η = 0.001；用时读出驱动乘 1.2（使固定对照可放电）。
用时：同一声源的带杂散 C 声（同学时统计），或宽带（全频道放电率 0.3）；不加调谐；可塑性开（用也照常沉积）。
"""
import json, os, sys
import numpy as np
HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
NC = 24; T = 30; H = 0.1; REPS = 20
P = dict(eta=0.001, w0=0.7, wmax=1.6, r0=0.02, rmax=0.5, decay=0.0001, leak=0.7)
SOUND = {'A': [6], 'B': [6, 12, 18], 'C': [6, 9, 12, 15, 18, 21]}
NOISE = [(1, 1, 2, 2), (1, 0, 2, 0), (0, 1, 0, 2), (1, 1, 0, 0)]   # 各式样：左1、右1、左2、右2 是否有杂散

SCALE = 1.0
def rates(chans, k=0, noisy=True):
    r = np.zeros(NC)
    if noisy:
        l1, r1, l2, r2 = NOISE[k % 4]
        for c in chans:
            for off, on, val in ((-1, l1, 0.5), (1, r1, 0.5), (-2, l2, 0.3), (2, r2, 0.3)):
                if on and 0 <= c + off < NC: r[c + off] = max(r[c + off], val)
    for c in chans: r[c] = 0.5
    return r * SCALE
def tune_vec(chans, strength=1.0):
    g = np.full(NC, strength); g[list(chans)] = 0.0; return g

class Field:
    def __init__(s, plastic=True):
        s.w = np.full(NC, P['w0']); s.W = np.full((NC, NC), P['r0']); np.fill_diagonal(s.W, 0); s.plastic = plastic
    def present(s, rate, g=None, learn=True):
        acc = np.zeros(NC); v = np.zeros(NC); y = np.zeros(NC); sF = np.zeros(NC); cnt = np.zeros(NC); first = np.full(NC, -1)
        for t in range(1, T + 1):
            acc += rate; sR = (acc >= 1).astype(float); acc -= sR
            v = P['leak'] * v + s.w * sR + s.W @ sF - H * sF.sum() - (g if g is not None else 0)
            v = np.maximum(v, 0); sn = (v >= 1).astype(float); v = np.where(sn > 0, 0, v); y = 0.8 * y + sn
            if learn and s.plastic:
                s.w = s.w + P['eta'] * sR * y * (P['wmax'] - s.w) / P['wmax']; s.w -= P['decay'] * (s.w - P['w0'])
                dW = P['eta'] * np.outer(y, sF) * (P['rmax'] - s.W) / P['rmax']; np.fill_diagonal(dW, 0)
                s.W = s.W + dW; s.W -= P['decay'] * (s.W - P['r0']); np.fill_diagonal(s.W, 0)
            first = np.where((first < 0) & (sn > 0), t, first); cnt += sn; sF = sn
        return cnt, first
    def readout(s, rate, k=0.0, sub=10, beats=30):
        rate = rate * 1.2   # 用时读出驱动（同第二步 I = 0.6）
        lam = P['leak'] ** (1 / sub); v = np.zeros(NC); first = np.full(NC, -1.0); cnt = np.zeros(NC); sF = np.zeros(NC)
        for n in range(beats * sub):
            v = lam * v + (s.w * rate - k) / sub + s.W @ sF - H * sF.sum()
            v = np.maximum(v, 0); sn = (v >= 1).astype(float); v = np.where(sn > 0, 0, v)
            first = np.where((first < 0) & (sn > 0), (n + 1) / sub, first); cnt += sn; sF = sn
        return cnt, first

def learn(F, seq, tuned=True):
    for name in seq:
        for r in range(REPS): F.present(rates(SOUND[name], r), tune_vec(SOUND[name]) if tuned else None)
def onC(cnt):
    tot = cnt.sum(); return round(float(cnt[SOUND['C']].sum() / tot), 3) if tot > 0 else None
def cosC(cnt):
    c = np.zeros(NC); c[SOUND['C']] = 1; n = np.linalg.norm(cnt); return round(float(cnt @ c / (n * np.linalg.norm(c))), 3) if n > 0 else None
def fired(cnt): return [int(c) for c in range(NC) if cnt[c] > 0]
def depth(F): return {'内容频道': {c: round(float(F.w[c]), 3) for c in SOUND['C']}, '杂散频道均值': round(float(np.mean(F.w[[c for c in range(NC) if c not in SOUND['C'] and min(abs(c - k) for k in SOUND['C']) <= 2]])), 3), '未经频道均值': round(float(np.mean(F.w[[c for c in range(NC) if min(abs(c - k) for k in SOUND['C']) > 2]])), 3)}
def default_use(F, label):
    out = {}
    for nm, rt in (('带杂散 C 声', rates(SOUND['C'], 0)), ('宽带声', np.full(NC, 0.3))):
        cnt, _ = F.readout(rt); out[nm] = {'所出频道': fired(cnt), '放电落在 C 频道的比例': onC(cnt), '与 C 的相似度': cosC(cnt)}
    return out
def peel(F):
    seq = []; prev = None
    for i in range(0, 25):
        k = round(0.025 * i, 3); cnt, _ = F.readout(rates(SOUND['C'], 0, noisy=False), k=k); f = fired(cnt)
        if f != prev: seq.append((k, f)); prev = f
    return seq

R = {}
def experiment(tuned=True, seq=('A', 'B', 'C'), plastic=True):
    F = Field(plastic); learn(F, seq, tuned); res = {'学后深度': depth(F), '用时默认（不调谐）': default_use(F, '')}
    cnt, first = F.readout(rates(SOUND['C'], 0))
    res['深者先得其流（带杂散 C 声，各道首放拍）'] = {int(c): float(first[c]) for c in range(NC) if cnt[c] > 0}
    res['剥层（纯 C 声，一致抑制 k 扫）'] = peel(F)
    return F, res
F, res = experiment(); R['主实验（学时调谐）'] = res
# 学用之别：用时收束到 B 一次（可塑性开），再默认
w_before = F.w.copy(); W_before = F.W.copy()
cntB, _ = F.present(rates(SOUND['C'], 0), tune_vec(SOUND['B']), learn=True)
dw_use = F.w - w_before
after = default_use(F, '')
# 一轮学习的沉积量参照（在同一场的副本上再学一轮 B）
import copy
G = copy.deepcopy(F); w0 = G.w.copy()
for r in range(REPS): G.present(rates(SOUND['B'], r), tune_vec(SOUND['B']))
R['学用之别'] = {'用时收束到 B 一次所出': fired(cntB), '这一次用时收束的沉积量（入口深度最大增量）': round(float(np.abs(dw_use).max()), 4),
    '一轮学时收束（20 次）的沉积量（最大增量）': round(float(np.abs(G.w - w0).max()), 4), '收束后再默认': after}
# 对照
_, r1 = experiment(tuned=False); R['对照·学时不调谐（同样带杂散输入）'] = r1
_, r2 = experiment(seq=('B', 'C')); R['对照·删去学 A'] = {k: r2[k] for k in ('学后深度', '剥层（纯 C 声，一致抑制 k 扫）')}
_, r3 = experiment(plastic=False); R['对照·冻结可塑性'] = r3
json.dump(R, open(os.path.join(HERE, 'tone_field.json'), 'w'), ensure_ascii=False, indent=1)
if __name__ == '__main__':
    for k, v in R.items(): print('==', k); print(json.dumps(v, ensure_ascii=False))
~~~

【资源 073 起】源码　tone\_field\_retrieve.py　SHA-256 0227cd307bc5b87b5e77908df97ae3baa38b82ab3ee960195630bd6d18fe0afc

~~~python
"""脉冲沉积场：旧识层而出——信源 C 冲入时，由（外部程序模拟的）调谐律把收束指向 B 区或 A 区
出旧识须在信源 C 冲入时以调谐收束到 B 的范围，出 A 则收束到 A 的范围；调谐律此处以外部程序模拟，不训。
基底、学习与参数同 tone_field（前缀执行，不改）。
调谐律（外部程序）：给定目标区（B = {6, 12, 18}，A = {6}），对目标区外各频道每子步施抑 g（抑其周），区内不抑（活其内）；g 由 0 逐步加大。
测量：每个 g 下所出频道；所出恰为目标区的最小 g（g*，调谐所需之力）；区内各道首放与放电数。
"""
import os, json, copy
import numpy as np
HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
src = open(os.path.join(HERE, 'tone_field.py'), encoding='utf-8').read().split('R = {}')[0]
sc = {'__file__': os.path.join(HERE, 'tone_field.py')}; exec(compile(src, 'tone_field.py', 'exec'), sc)
Field, learn, rates, SOUND, NC, P, H = (sc[k] for k in ('Field', 'learn', 'rates', 'SOUND', 'NC', 'P', 'H'))
def readout_tuned(F, rate, region, g, sub=10, beats=30):
    rate = rate * 1.2; lam = P['leak'] ** (1 / sub); v = np.zeros(NC); first = np.full(NC, -1.0); cnt = np.zeros(NC); sF = np.zeros(NC)
    inh = np.full(NC, g); inh[list(region)] = 0.0
    for n in range(beats * sub):
        v = lam * v + (F.w * rate - inh) / sub + F.W @ sF - H * sF.sum()
        v = np.maximum(v, 0); sn = (v >= 1).astype(float); v = np.where(sn > 0, 0, v)
        first = np.where((first < 0) & (sn > 0), (n + 1) / sub, first); cnt += sn; sF = sn
    return cnt, first
def probe(F, region, gs=[round(0.05 * i, 2) for i in range(0, 41)]):
    rows = []; gstar = None
    for g in gs:
        cnt, first = readout_tuned(F, rates(SOUND['C'], 0), region, g); out = [int(c) for c in range(NC) if cnt[c] > 0]
        rows.append((g, out))
        if gstar is None and out == sorted(region): gstar = (g, {int(c): {'首放': float(first[c]), '放电数': int(cnt[c])} for c in region})
    seq = []; prev = None
    for g, o in rows:
        if o != prev: seq.append((g, o)); prev = o
    return {'所出随 g 的变化': seq, '所出恰为目标区的最小 g（调谐所需之力）': gstar[0] if gstar else None, '此时区内各道': gstar[1] if gstar else None}
def build(seq=('A', 'B', 'C'), tuned=True, plastic=True):
    F = Field(plastic); learn(F, seq, tuned); return F
R = {}
for nm, kw in (('主实验（学 A、B、C，学时调谐）', {}), ('对照·删去学 A', {'seq': ('B', 'C')}), ('对照·只学 C 三轮', {'seq': ('C', 'C', 'C')}),
               ('对照·学时不调谐', {'tuned': False}), ('对照·冻结可塑性', {'plastic': False})):
    F = build(**kw)
    R[nm] = {'深度（基音、泛音、共振峰）': [round(float(F.w[c]), 3) for c in (6, 12, 9)],
             '信源 C、不加调谐律': [int(c) for c in range(NC) if readout_tuned(F, rates(SOUND['C'], 0), list(range(NC)), 0)[0][c] > 0],
             '调谐律指向 B 区': probe(F, SOUND['B']), '调谐律指向 A 区': probe(F, SOUND['A'])}
json.dump(R, open(os.path.join(HERE, 'tone_field_retrieve.json'), 'w'), ensure_ascii=False, indent=1)
if __name__ == '__main__':
    for k, v in R.items(): print('==', k); print(json.dumps(v, ensure_ascii=False))
~~~

【资源 074 起】源码　overlap\_layers.py　SHA-256 031e20516c133e9560dc11d2fdc48e136e4c6819555b18abd5e86191f0a75378

~~~python
"""交叠史逐层显现构造式 · 核算：同一交叠史上的逐层显现（脉冲沉积场，前缀执行 tone_field、tone_field_retrieve，不改）
读出判据（不含位置标签）：入口深度剖面的水平集 Λ_k = {c：w_c ≥ d_k}，d_k 为剖面中「显著高于初值」的互异深度层（相邻层差 > 0.02 合并）；
所出内容 := 包含全部实际放电位置的最小水平集；无放电为 ⊥。水平集与形成段的对应由形成史记录核（某段学时调谐所及频道 = 该水平集），不进入判据。
三项独立结果：形成顺序（A、B、C 学序）；放电先后（首放时刻，深者先得其流）；内容显现顺序（各调谐档下的所出）。
调谐律以外部程序模拟（指向目标区，区外施抑 g），不在场内训成；故「场内控制」一项不满足，事件资格只作机制层读回。
"""
import os, json, copy
import numpy as np
HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
def pre(name, stop):
    p = os.path.join(HERE, name); src = open(p, encoding='utf-8').read().rsplit(stop, 1)[0]; sc = {'__file__': p}; exec(compile(src, name, 'exec'), sc); return sc
s4 = pre('tone_field.py', '\nR = {}'); s5 = pre('tone_field_retrieve.py', '\nR = {}')
Field, learn, rates, SOUND, NC, P, tune_vec = (s4[k] for k in ('Field', 'learn', 'rates', 'SOUND', 'NC', 'P', 'tune_vec'))
readout_tuned = s5['readout_tuned']
CH = SOUND['C']
def levels(F):
    vals = sorted({round(float(x), 3) for x in F.w if x > P['w0'] + 0.02}, reverse=True); ls = []
    for v in vals:
        if not ls or ls[-1] - v > 0.02: ls.append(v)
    return [(d, [int(c) for c in range(NC) if F.w[c] >= d - 0.02]) for d in ls]
def content(F, out):
    if not out: return None
    for k, (d, s) in enumerate(levels(F), 1):
        if set(out) <= set(s): return k
    return '超出各水平集'
def seg_map(F, hist):
    m = {}
    for k, (d, s) in enumerate(levels(F), 1):
        m[k] = [h for h, chans in hist if sorted(chans) == s] or ['无形成段（来源记录不成立）']
    return m
def read(F, rate, region=None, g=0.0):
    cnt, first = readout_tuned(F, rate, region if region is not None else list(range(NC)), g)
    out = [int(c) for c in range(NC) if cnt[c] > 0]
    return out, {int(c): float(first[c]) for c in out}
def build(seq=('A', 'B', 'C'), tuned=True, plastic=True, direct=None):
    F = Field(plastic); hist = []
    if direct is not None: F.w = direct.copy(); return F, hist
    for name in seq:
        for r in range(20): F.present(rates(SOUND[name], r), tune_vec(SOUND[name]) if tuned else None)
        hist.append((name, sorted(SOUND[name])))
    return F, hist
def gstar(F, region):
    seq = []; prev = None; gs = None
    for i in range(0, 41):
        g = round(0.05 * i, 2); out, _ = read(F, rates(CH, 0), region, g)
        if out != prev: seq.append((g, out)); prev = out
        if gs is None and out == sorted(region): gs = g
    return gs, seq
def report(F, hist):
    w_before = F.w.copy()
    r = {'入口深度（C 各道）': {c: round(float(F.w[c]), 3) for c in CH}, '水平集': levels(F), '水平集 ↔ 形成段': seg_map(F, hist)}
    for nm, rt in (('宽带声', np.full(NC, 0.3)), ('带杂散 C 声', rates(CH, 0))):
        out, first = read(F, rt); r[f'默认（{nm}）'] = {'所出频道': out, '所出水平集': content(F, out), '首放（拍）': first}
    for nm, reg in (('指向 B 区', SOUND['B']), ('指向 A 区', SOUND['A'])):
        g, seq = gstar(F, reg); out, first = read(F, rates(CH, 0), reg, g if g is not None else 2.0)
        r[f'调谐律{nm}'] = {'所需之力 g*': g, '所出随 g 的变化': seq, '所出水平集': content(F, out), '首放（拍）': first}
    r['读出窗内深度不变'] = bool(np.allclose(F.w, w_before))
    return r
R = {}
F, hist = build(); R['正例（学 A、B、C，学时调谐）'] = report(F, hist)
fw = F.w.copy()
# 三项独立结果
R['三项独立结果'] = {'形成顺序': [h for h, _ in hist], '放电先后（宽带声默认，首放拍）': R['正例（学 A、B、C，学时调谐）']['默认（宽带声）']['首放（拍）'],
    '内容显现顺序（默认 → 指向 B → 指向 A）': [R['正例（学 A、B、C，学时调谐）'][k]['所出水平集'] for k in ('默认（宽带声）', '调谐律指向 B 区', '调谐律指向 A 区')]}
for nm, kw in (('对照·删去学 A（重放 B、C）', {'seq': ('B', 'C')}), ('对照·只学 C 三轮', {'seq': ('C', 'C', 'C')}),
               ('对照·学时不调谐', {'tuned': False}), ('对照·冻结可塑性', {'plastic': False})):
    G, h = build(**kw); R[nm] = report(G, h)
G, h = build(direct=fw); G.W = F.W.copy(); R['对照·匹配入口直写（同终态 w、W，无形成史）'] = report(G, h)
# 撤调谐律（阻断）：同正例，不施调谐 → 所出复为默认
R['阻断调谐律'] = {'所出': read(F, rates(CH, 0))[0], '所出水平集': content(F, read(F, rates(CH, 0))[0])}
# 用时调谐一次（可塑开）后默认不变
H = copy.deepcopy(F); w0 = H.w.copy(); H.present(rates(CH, 0), tune_vec(SOUND['A'], 0.5), learn=True)
R['用时收束一次（可塑开）'] = {'沉积量（最大增量）': round(float(np.abs(H.w - w0).max()), 4), '其后默认所出水平集': content(H, read(H, np.full(NC, 0.3))[0])}
json.dump(R, open(os.path.join(HERE, 'overlap_layers.json'), 'w'), ensure_ascii=False, indent=1)
if __name__ == '__main__':
    for k, v in R.items(): print('==', k); print(json.dumps(v, ensure_ascii=False)[:1500])
~~~

【资源 076 起】源码　overlap\_layers\_more.py　SHA-256 d087297814df0c709e1defbf340a60af71b0030398cf9538cbf83f7079be7d12

~~~python
"""交叠史逐层显现构造式 · 补充核算（前缀执行 overlap_layers，其前缀 tone_field、tone_field_retrieve，均不改）
(1) 形成承接证据：每段学习逐次记录实际感受器输入、场元放电与 w、W 的写前写后；以实际沉积（本段 w 增量 > 0.01 的频道）定各段的实际沉积集，
    水平集 ↔ 形成段按实际沉积集对应（学时 SOUND 目标集只作施抑处方，不作来源）。
(2) 单次连续调谐轨迹：同一次读出内，施抑强度 g 由 0 起逐子步线性上升（0 → 0.8，共 30 拍），记录各时窗（每 2 拍）实际放电的频道，看所出是否连续地经 B 再到 A。
(3) 固定读出核验 w 与 W 不变；申报显著层阈值 0.02（高于初值）与合并容差 0.02。
(4) 成对可塑量：用时收束一次（30 拍、可塑开）与一轮学时（20 次 × 30 拍）分别记步数与 w、W 的最大与总更新量；分签默认读出与真实写回。
(5) g* 报为「扫描网格（步长 0.05）上首个命中点」；宽带默认为独立探针，逐层因果对照一律用带杂散 C 输入。
"""
import os, json, copy
import numpy as np
HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
p = os.path.join(HERE, 'overlap_layers.py'); src = open(p, encoding='utf-8').read().rsplit('\nR = {}', 1)[0]
sc = {'__file__': p}; exec(compile(src, 'overlap_layers.py', 'exec'), sc)
Field, rates, SOUND, NC, P, tune_vec, levels, content, read, H = (sc[k] for k in ('Field', 'rates', 'SOUND', 'NC', 'P', 'tune_vec', 'levels', 'content', 'read', 's4'))
H = sc['s4']['H']; CH = SOUND['C']
def build_logged(seq=('A', 'B', 'C')):
    F = Field(True); segs = []
    for name in seq:
        w0, W0 = F.w.copy(), F.W.copy(); inp = np.zeros(NC); fired = np.zeros(NC)
        for r in range(20):
            rt = rates(SOUND[name], r); inp += rt > 0
            cnt, _ = F.present(rt, tune_vec(SOUND[name])); fired += cnt
        dw = F.w - w0; dW = F.W - W0
        segs.append({'段': name, '施抑处方（学时目标区）': sorted(SOUND[name]), '实际受输入的频道（次数）': {int(c): int(inp[c]) for c in range(NC) if inp[c] > 0},
                     '实际放电的频道（总数）': {int(c): int(fired[c]) for c in range(NC) if fired[c] > 0},
                     'w 写前→写后（变动 > 0.001 者）': {int(c): (round(float(w0[c]), 4), round(float(F.w[c]), 4)) for c in range(NC) if abs(dw[c]) > 0.001},
                     'W 最大增量（行←列）': (lambda i: (int(i[0]), int(i[1]), round(float(dW[i]), 4)))(np.unravel_index(np.argmax(dW), dW.shape)),
                     '实际沉积集（w 增量 > 0.01）': [int(c) for c in range(NC) if dw[c] > 0.01]})
    return F, segs
def cum_map(F, segs):
    """水平集 ↔ 形成段：按实际沉积集的累积交叠计——某水平集等于『自该段起各段实际沉积集之交』所共同覆盖的频道。"""
    m = {}
    for k, (d, s) in enumerate(levels(F), 1):
        hit = []
        for i, sg in enumerate(segs):
            inter = set(sg['实际沉积集（w 增量 > 0.01）'])
            for later in segs[i:]: inter &= set(later['实际沉积集（w 增量 > 0.01）'])
            if sorted(inter) == s: hit.append(sg['段'])
        m[k] = hit or ['无对应形成段']
    return m
def ramp(F, rate, region, gmax=0.8, sub=10, beats=30, win=2):
    rate = rate * 1.2; lam = P['leak'] ** (1 / sub); v = np.zeros(NC); sF = np.zeros(NC); out = []; cur = np.zeros(NC)
    total = beats * sub
    for n in range(total):
        g = gmax * n / total; inh = np.full(NC, g); inh[list(region)] = 0.0
        v = lam * v + (F.w * rate - inh) / sub + F.W @ sF - H * sF.sum()
        v = np.maximum(v, 0); sn = (v >= 1).astype(float); v = np.where(sn > 0, 0, v); cur += sn; sF = sn
        if (n + 1) % (win * sub) == 0:
            fired = [int(c) for c in range(NC) if cur[c] > 0]
            out.append({'时窗末（拍）': (n + 1) // sub, '此刻 g': round(g, 3), '本窗放电频道': fired, '所出水平集': content(F, fired)}); cur = np.zeros(NC)
    return out
R = {}
F, segs = build_logged(); R['形成承接证据（逐段）'] = segs
R['显著层申报'] = {'阈值（高于初值 0.7）': 0.02, '合并容差': 0.02, '水平集': levels(F)}
R['水平集 ↔ 形成段（按实际沉积集的累积交叠）'] = cum_map(F, segs)
wF, WF = F.w.copy(), F.W.copy()
out, first = read(F, rates(CH, 0)); R['冻结读出（带杂散 C 声）'] = {'所出': out, '所出水平集': content(F, out), '首放': first, 'w 不变': bool(np.array_equal(F.w, wF)), 'W 不变': bool(np.array_equal(F.W, WF))}
out, _ = read(F, np.full(NC, 0.3)); R['独立探针·宽带默认'] = {'所出': out, '所出水平集': content(F, out)}
R['单次连续调谐轨迹（目标 A 区，g 0 → 0.8）'] = ramp(F, rates(CH, 0), SOUND['A'])
R['单次连续调谐轨迹（目标 B 区，g 0 → 0.8）'] = ramp(F, rates(CH, 0), SOUND['B'])
# 成对可塑量
U = copy.deepcopy(F); w0, W0 = U.w.copy(), U.W.copy(); U.present(rates(CH, 0), tune_vec(SOUND['A'], 0.5), learn=True)
L_ = copy.deepcopy(F); lw0, lW0 = L_.w.copy(), L_.W.copy()
for r in range(20): L_.present(rates(SOUND['A'], r), tune_vec(SOUND['A']))
def amt(a, b): d = np.abs(a - b); return {'最大': round(float(d.max()), 4), '总和': round(float(d.sum()), 4)}
R['成对可塑量'] = {'用时收束一次': {'步数（拍）': 30, 'w 更新': amt(U.w, w0), 'W 更新': amt(U.W, W0),
                                   '其后默认所出（真实写回后，带杂散 C 声）': content(U, read(U, rates(CH, 0))[0])},
                   '一轮学时收束（同目标 A）': {'步数（拍）': 20 * 30, 'w 更新': amt(L_.w, lw0), 'W 更新': amt(L_.W, lW0),
                                   '其后默认所出（真实写回后，带杂散 C 声）': content(L_, read(L_, rates(CH, 0))[0])}}
D, dsegs = build_logged(('B', 'C')); R['删去学 A：水平集 ↔ 形成段（实际沉积）'] = {'水平集': levels(D), '对应': cum_map(D, dsegs)}
json.dump(R, open(os.path.join(HERE, 'overlap_layers_more.json'), 'w'), ensure_ascii=False, indent=1)
if __name__ == '__main__':
    for k, v in R.items(): print('==', k); print(json.dumps(v, ensure_ascii=False)[:2500])
~~~
